Abstract: Persuasive writing exploits cognitive biases, recruiting a reader's mental shortcuts to shape belief, often without the reader noticing. We call the linguistic features exploiting such mental shortcuts cognitive bias triggers. As large language models (LLMs) increasingly mediate how people encounter news, summarizing it, ranking it, and answering questions over it, a natural question is whether they can recognize the features of a text that bias a human reader. We introduce BAIT (Bias Annotation In Text), a resource for detecting cognitive bias triggers in news. BAIT pairs a schema of nine triggers, each specified in an annotator codebook, with 50 English news articles annotated by trained annotators at the article and span levels and adjudicated into a gold standard. On this data we define two tasks, detecting which triggers a text contains and locating the spans that carry them. We then benchmark six frontier LLMs zero-shot and with three or ten in-context examples. The models approach human agreement on which triggers an article contains, but rarely recover its full set of triggers, and they reach less than half of human agreement in locating the passages that carry them. Moreover, they over-predict triggers that come with easy surface cues, and most of the passages they mark for these triggers are not triggers, yet carry the trigger's surface cue far more often than random text. These findings suggest that the evaluated LLMs respond to surface cues rather than to the mechanisms that make a passage manipulative. We release the schema, the codebook, standoff annotations, and the evaluation pipeline.

</details>


### [85] [Reading Too Much Into It? Interpretive Preferences and Commitment in LLM Mentalisation under Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28105)
*Stephanie Fong,Yiwen Jiang,Qingyang Xu,Zimu Wang,Yaling Shen,Xiangyu Zhao,Jiahe Liu,Yinghao Zhang,Vincent Lee,Dominic Dwyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MentalLoom评估框架，测试8个LLM在模糊社交情境下的心智化能力，发现用户披露抑郁/社交焦虑会改变LLM的解释偏好（整体更负面但针对叙述者更正面），重复提问增加情感支持、正面认同和确定性，且无新证据。说明用户语境能重塑LLM的解释偏好和承诺，但歧义本身未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心智理论评估依赖预定义答案，不确定性研究忽视LLM在心理困扰下的首选解释和置信度，而LLM越来越多用于人际建议，可能影响用户对模糊社交情境的解读。

Method: 引入MentalLoom评估框架，包含604个模糊社交情境、匹配的抑郁和社交焦虑披露、四轮对话且事件证据不变。跨8个LLM测试，分析披露对解释的影响，以及脚本化重复提问对情感支持、正面认同和确定性的影响。

Result: 披露最初引发整体更负面的解释，但针对叙述者更正面的解读。脚本化重复提问表现出更强的情感支持、更强的正面叙述者导向认同、更高的确定性，且无新事件证据。

Conclusion: 用户语境能重塑LLM的解释偏好和承诺程度，但底层的歧义仍未解决，提示LLM在心理健康建议中的潜在风险。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for interpersonal advice, potentially shaping how users interpret ambiguous social encounters. Existing Theory of Mind evaluations typically rely on predefined answers, while uncertainty research overlooks LLMs' preferred explanations and confidence under psychological distress. To address these gaps, we introduce MentalLoom, a novel evaluation of LLM mentalisation comprising 604 ambiguous social episodes, matched depression and social anxiety disclosures, and four-turn dialogues with unchanged event evidence. Across eight LLMs, disclosures initially elicited more negative explanations overall but more positive narrator-directed interpretations. Scripted repeated questioning showed greater emotional support, stronger positive narrator-directed endorsement, and higher certainty without new event evidence. These findings show that user context can reshape interpretive preferences and commitment while the underlying ambiguity remains unresolved.

</details>


### [86] [Africa-Papers: A 10-Billion-Token Multi-Domain Academic Corpus for African Foundation Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28173)
*Mich-Seth Owusu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Africa-Papers，一个大规模精选的泛非学术预训练语料库，旨在解决现有基础模型预训练数据中非洲学术内容严重不足（<0.5%）的问题，包含 844,807 篇全文论文、101.5 亿 tokens，涵盖四大科学流，已开源发布于 Hugging Face Hub。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流预训练语料库（如 The Pile、S2ORC、Dolma）中非洲科学、医学、法律、农业学术内容不足 0.5%，导致最先进模型在热带流行病学、小农农业生态、区域经济治理、本土语言结构等推理任务上系统性失效，亟需提升非洲知识在 AI 中的代表性与主权。

Method: 构建 Africa-Papers 语料库，整合四大来源：(1) 31 个非洲国家 DOAJ 索引的同行评审期刊；(2) CGIAR CGSpace 农业知识库；(3) 非洲作者/关注的 STEM/AI arXiv 预印本；(4) OpenAlex 索引的非洲机构学者开放获取国际出版物。全文处理为保留章节层级、数学公式、表格的布局感知 Markdown，附带精确 BPE token 计数与标准化 8 领域分类法，共 879 个 Parquet 分片。

Result: 发布了规模最大的泛非多领域学术预训练语料库 Africa-Papers，含 844,807 篇全文、101.5 亿 tokens，已在 Hugging Face Hub (AfriSpeech 组织) 开源，为基础模型的持续预训练与领域评测提供高质量非洲语境数据。

Conclusion: Africa-Papers 填补了预训练数据中非洲学术的结构性缺失，推动非洲知识在 AI 中的主权与代表性，为解决模型在非洲特定领域推理失效提供关键资源，促进全球基础模型的包容性与公平性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) depend fundamentally on the diversity, depth, and factual rigor of their pre-training corpora. However, contemporary foundational pre-training datasets (such as The Pile, S2ORC, and Dolma) exhibit a severe structural omission: African scientific, medical, legal, and agricultural scholarship constitutes less than 0.5% of global pre-training tokens. Consequently, state-of-the-art models suffer from systematic failure modes when reasoning about tropical epidemiology, localized smallholder agro-ecologies, regional economic governance, and indigenous linguistic structures. To resolve this representation deficit and advance African intellectual sovereignty in artificial intelligence, we introduce Africa-Papers, the largest curated multi-domain Pan-African academic pre-training corpus to date. Comprising over 844,807 full-text papers spanning 10.15 billion tokens across 879 Parquet shards, Africa-Papers unifies four primary scientific streams: (1) continental peer-reviewed journals indexed in DOAJ across 31 African nations, (2) the CGIAR CGSpace agricultural repository, (3) African-authored and African-focused STEM/AI preprints on arXiv, and (4) open-access international publications by scholars affiliated with African institutions indexed via OpenAlex. The entire corpus is processed into layout-aware, clean Markdown preserving section hierarchies, mathematical equations, and tabular data, accompanied by exact BPE token counts and a standardized 8-domain taxonomy. We release Africa-Papers openly on the Hugging Face Hub under the AfriSpeech organization to accelerate continual pre-training and domain evaluation for foundation models globally.

</details>


### [87] [Temporal Evidence-Parameter Co-Alignment for LLM Reasoning over Temporal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28176)
*Jiapu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 TEPCA 框架，通过在证据层面引入时间约束的动态相关性引导路径剪枝和互信息剪枝，在参数层面引入动态门控 LoRA，实现大语言模型在时序知识图谱上的时序推理，在两个 TKG 基准上达到 SOTA，且 DG-LoRA 在八个常识问答数据集上展现出泛化性、可扩展性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的静态参数记忆与现实知识的动态演化错位，导致其在时序推理上表现不足。现有检索增强方法只关注语义相关性，忽略时间有效性、事件顺序和演化依赖，得到的证据在时间上支离破碎；传统 PEFT 采用固定且输入无关的低秩更新，难以捕捉时间依赖的实体语义和关系漂移。

Method: TEPCA 从证据层和参数层双重对齐时序知识。证据层：提出时间约束的动态相关性引导路径剪枝保留全局连贯的时序路径，以及基于互信息的剪枝保留查询信息量大的局部事实。参数层：设计动态门控 LoRA，以时间戳为条件动态调制低秩适配子空间，捕捉细粒度实体演化和时间依赖的关系语义，同时保持参数效率。

Result: 在两个时序知识图谱基准上，TEPCA 取得最优性能。DG-LoRA 在八个常识问答数据集上的额外实验验证了其通用的输入自适应能力、跨 LLM 主干的可扩展性以及可解释性。

Conclusion: TEPCA 通过证据-参数协同对齐，有效解决了大语言模型在时序推理中的局限，使模型能在保持参数高效的前提下捕捉实体的细粒度演化和时间依赖的关系语义。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often struggle with temporally grounded reasoning because their static parametric memory is misaligned with the dynamic evolution of real-world knowledge. Although RAG and Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) provide practical ways to enhance LLMs, they remain insufficient for temporal reasoning. Existing retrieval methods typically prioritize semantic relevance while overlooking temporal validity, event order, and evolutionary dependencies, resulting in evidence that is relevant in isolation but temporally fragmented. Meanwhile, conventional PEFT methods usually employ fixed and input-agnostic low-rank updates, making it difficult to capture time-dependent entity semantics and relation drift. To address these limitations, we propose Temporal Evidence-Parameter Co-Alignment (TEPCA), a framework for LLM reasoning over TKGs. TEPCA aligns LLM reasoning with temporal knowledge at both the evidence and parameter levels. At the evidence level, it introduces time-constrained Dynamic Relevance-Guided Path (DRGP) pruning to preserve globally coherent temporal paths, together with Mutual-Information (MI)-based pruning to retain query-informative local facts under temporal constraints. At the parameter level, it integrates Dynamic-Gated LoRA (DG-LoRA), a timestamp-conditioned low-rank adaptation mechanism that dynamically modulates the effective adaptation subspace according to temporal context. This design enables LLMs to better capture fine-grained entity evolution and time-dependent relation semantics while preserving parameter efficiency. Experimental results on two TKG benchmarks demonstrate that TEPCA achieves state-of-the-art performance. Additional evaluations of DG-LoRA on eight commonsense QA datasets further verify its general input-adaptive capacity, scalability across LLM backbones, and interpretability.

</details>


### [88] [Can Language Models Get "Aphasia" From Neuronal Ablation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28214)
*Pratik Worah,Randall Bateman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过在语言模型（LSTM和Transformer）中系统消融神经元并进行有限微调，模拟神经退行性疾病中的神经元丢失及代偿可塑性，发现不同架构表现出截然不同的功能衰退模式，且这些模式与特定失语症综合征及阿尔茨海默病的临床进展相平行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经退行性疾病的标志是进行性神经元丢失，但神经元丢失如何塑造认知衰退模式尚不清楚。作者旨在利用计算模型（语言模型）系统研究架构依赖的失效模式，为失语症综合征和神经退行性疾病提供新的计算视角。

Method: 在循环LSTM和Transformer语言模型中系统消融整个计算单元（神经元），包括前馈多层感知机（MLP）单元和注意力头，并应用有限微调作为代偿可塑性的简单代理，观察模型在语法、语义等能力上的衰退轨迹。

Result: LSTM表现为语法和语义能力并行逐渐下降；Transformer在MLP消融下呈晚期阈值样崩溃，在注意力头消融下呈平滑S型下降。MLP消融模型持续产生类内语义替换（语义拟音错误）及无反应增加，类似Wernicke型失语；注意力头消融导致语义特异性丧失趋势，类似原发性进行性失语症语义变异（svPPA）。Transformer注意力头消融早期准确率保持类似阿尔茨海默病临床症状出现前40-60%神经元丢失的临床前储备期，而LSTM稳定下降类似症状期进展。

Conclusion: 前馈和注意力通路具有功能上截然不同的失效模式，架构依赖的失效谱系可为失语症综合征和神经退行性疾病提供新的体外计算模型视角。

Abstract: Progressive neuronal loss is the hallmark of neurodegenerative disease, yet how loss of neurons shapes the pattern of cognitive decline remains poorly understood. Here we systematically ablate neurons (computational units) in language models spanning recurrent long short-term memory (LSTM) and Transformer architectures and apply limited fine-tuning as a simple proxy for compensatory plasticity. With whole-unit ablation, LSTMs degrade gradually with syntactic and semantic faculties declining in parallel across mild-to-moderate damage. By contrast, Transformers exhibit late threshold-like collapse under feedforward multi-layer perceptron (MLP) ablation and smoother sigmoid-like decline under attention-head ablation. Qualitatively, MLP-ablated models continue to make within-category semantic substitutions (semantic paraphasias) alongside rising non-responses, paralleling Wernicke-type aphasia, while attention-head ablation produces a trend toward loss of semantic specificity similar to the semantic variant of primary progressive aphasia (svPPA). These findings indicate that feedforward and attentional pathways have functionally distinct failure modes, suggesting that architecture-dependent failure profiles may offer new computational perspectives on aphasic syndromes and neurodegenerative diseases as an in silico model. Notably, early accuracy retention under Transformer attention-head ablation qualitatively parallels pre-symptomatic reserve before the 40--60% neuronal loss reported by Gomez-Isla et al. (1996) when clinical symptoms emerge in Alzheimer's disease, whereas the steadier LSTM decline parallels the symptomatic progression described by DeKosky and Scheff (1990) and Terry et al. (1991).

</details>


### [89] [How Language Models Learn Taste: Brand Preference in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28272)
*Tapan Parikh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了语言模型在推荐任务中如何形成品牌偏好（口味），发现模型提及的品牌（市场领导者）与推荐的品牌（小众专业品牌）存在系统性差异。通过分析三个开放训练管道，揭示预训练已建立推荐关联，监督微调基本保持不变，而偏好优化（DPO）显著改变推荐选择，且这种偏好迁移超出品牌范畴，推测源于偏好训练中的评判模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在推荐场景下品牌选择的差异来源，理解模型如何获得“品味”，以及训练管道各阶段如何塑造这种偏好。

Method: 跨102个模型和42个品牌类别进行对比实验，分析命名品牌与推荐品牌的差异；追踪三个开放训练管道（预训练、监督微调、DPO偏好优化）中推荐品牌的变化；测试偏好数据中无品牌信息时DPO的效果；扩展到非品牌领域（如酱料选择）验证泛化性。

Result: 1) 26/42类别中推荐品牌与命名品牌不同，命名品牌为市场领导者，推荐品牌多为小众专业品牌；2) 预训练已建立推荐关联；3) 监督微调基本不改变推荐；4) DPO在所有管道中将推荐品牌变动11-24个百分点，且偏好数据几乎不含品牌；5) 偏好迁移至非品牌决策（如从番茄酱转向青酱）；6) 推测偏好源于偏好训练中的评判模型。

Conclusion: 语言模型的推荐行为主要取决于其通过偏好优化获得的“品味”，而非对品牌的事实知识。DPO能在无显式品牌偏好数据的情况下重塑模型判断，这种隐性偏好具有跨领域泛化能力，揭示了偏好训练中评判模型的关键作用。

Abstract: Asked to name a running shoe brand, language models say Nike. Asked to recommend one, they say Brooks. This paper asks where models learn that taste. Across 102 models and 42 brand categories, the brand a model picks when recommending differs from the brand it names in 26. The field moves together to a shared recommendation. The Named brands are market leaders, and the Picked brands are mostly niche and specialist. Three open training pipelines show where this forms. Pretraining already attaches the recommended brand to advice. Supervised fine-tuning teaches the model to answer and mostly leaves the pick unchanged. Preference optimization (DPO) teaches the model which answer is better, and the pick moves in all three pipelines, by 11 to 24 points. The preference data contains almost no brands. DPO moves the model's judgments toward a second set of associations even when the preferred brand is not in the preference data, and the effect reaches beyond brands. Asked to choose a sauce, models switch from ketchup to pesto. We do not know exactly how these preferences arise. We suspect they come from the judge models that decide which answers count as better during preference training. For brands, a recommendation depends less on what a model knows about them than on whether they fit this taste.

</details>


### [90] [Forty Questions: Identifying a Language Model from One-Word Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28281)
*Tapan Parikh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于模型对日常问题单字回答分布的指纹识别方法，能以极低成本（几美分/端点）高精度识别API背后的模型，并揭示了多个模型服务商的虚假宣传、隐藏上下文注入及计费异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型识别方法依赖完整输出或内部参数，难以适用于仅提供黑盒API的商业模型。作者旨在开发一种仅需极少交互（单字回答）、成本极低、鲁棒性强的模型指纹技术，以审计模型服务商的真实性声明并检测隐藏行为（如额外上下文注入、计费欺诈）。

Method: 1. 从公开的模型默认行为普查中收集日常问题（如“说一种声音”、“说一位诗人”）。2. 对每个模型多次提问，统计其对每个问题给出各单字回答的频率，构建“模型画像”。3. 给定新端点的回答序列，计算其与各候选模型画像的似然，归属最可能的模型。4. 在94个模型面板上评估识别准确率，并测试系统提示词、推理开关等干扰因素的影响。5. 将方法应用于OpenRouter上13个开源模型的96个端点，结合计费记录交叉验证。

Result: 1. 仅需40个回答即可在94模型中以92.5%准确率识别真实模型（随机猜测仅1.1%）。2. 通过OpenRouter构建的画像成功识别DeepInfra所有10个盲测模型。3. 系统提示词会使准确率下降6-16个百分点。4. 96个端点中92个与声称模型一致；1个关闭推理后一致；1个以“Llama 4 Maverick”之名实为Gemma 4（自称及计费均为Gemma 4）；2个未解决。5. 计费记录显示：Grok 4.7单行问题被计1,247输入token（GPT-4o仅12），5个模型在未计费token情况下输出当前日期，暗示存在隐藏上下文注入。

Conclusion: 该指纹方法高效、低成本且可大规模部署，不仅能验证模型身份声明，还能揭示服务商的隐藏行为（如模型替换、额外上下文注入、计费异常），为模型供应链透明度和审计提供了实用工具。

Abstract: A model behind an API can be identified from its one-word answers to everyday questions such as "Name a sound" or "Name a poet". A model's profile records how often it gives each answer to each question. We attribute new answers to the model most likely to have given them. On a panel of 94 models, 40 answers identify the exact model 92.5% of the time, against 1.1% by chance. Profiles built through OpenRouter identified all ten models served by DeepInfra under blinded labels. A system prompt lowers accuracy by 6 to 16 points. We applied the method to 96 endpoints of 13 open models on OpenRouter, and 92 identify as the model they claim. Of the other four, one matches the claimed model with reasoning turned off. One serves Gemma 4 under the name Llama 4 Maverick: it names itself Gemma 4, and it is billed Gemma 4's token counts on every message. Two remain unresolved. Billing records also show context added to first-party models. Grok 4.7 is billed 1,247 input tokens for a one-line question that costs GPT-4o only 12 tokens, and five models state the current date without being billed for it. The questions come from a public census of model defaults, and the method costs cents per endpoint.

</details>


### [91] [Adaptive Replay Refinement: Allocating Test-Time Compute Between Revision and Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28294)
*Ahsan Bilal,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Muhammad Umer,Ayesha Mohsin,Sarmad Bilal,Dean F. Hougen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应重放细化（ARR），一种无需训练的方法，通过检测修订过程是否达到不动点来动态重新分配推理计算资源，从细化转向独立采样，在保持性能的同时显著降低串行通过次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法依赖固定细化调度、自我验证判断或学习型控制器，可能浪费计算资源或依赖不可靠信号且需要额外训练，需要一种能自动判断何时停止细化并转向新采样的方法。

Method: ARR 采用重放解码技术，将当前解作为草稿并行验证未变 token，高效检测修订过程是否达到不动点；一旦检测到细化耗尽，释放的计算资源被重新分配给新的独立采样，无需额外训练。

Result: 在五个模型家族的七个模型和七个基准上，ARR 在五个数学推理基准中的三个上与多数投票性能持平，同时将关键路径串行通过次数降低多达 2.4 倍；在 MATH500 上以 84.6% 准确率仅需多数投票 41% 的串行通过次数。

Conclusion: 检测细化耗尽并自适应重新分配计算资源从细化转向独立采样，能提升推理效率，同时揭示了自我修订的局限性。

Abstract: Test-time scaling allocates additional inference compute to refining existing solutions or generating new ones. This raises a fundamental question: Should I keep improving this solution, or stop working on it and try a completely new solution? Existing methods make this decision using fixed refinement schedules, self-verification judgments, or learned controllers, which can waste compute or rely on signals that may be incorrect or require additional training. We introduce Adaptive Replay Refinement (ARR), a training-free method that detects when a stationary revision reaches a fixed point and reallocates the released compute to a new independent sample; replay decoding treats the current solution as a draft and verifies unchanged tokens in parallel, enabling efficient fixed-point detection. Across seven models from five families and seven benchmarks, ARR matches majority voting on three of five mathematical reasoning benchmarks at equal compute, while reducing critical-path serial passes by up to 2.4 times. On MATH500, ARR achieves 84.6% accuracy with only 41% of the serial passes required by majority voting. These results demonstrate that detecting refinement exhaustion enables adaptive reallocation from revision to independent sampling, improving inference efficiency while highlighting the limits of self-revision. Code is available at https://github.com/AhsanBilal7/adaptive_refine.

</details>


### [92] [Reward Misspecification Degrades Chain-of-Thought Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28303)
*Tianyi Lorena Yan,John Hewitt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文表明奖励函数设定错误会单独导致思维链可监控性下降：模型在CoT中推理真实任务意图，但输出却违背意图并获得奖励。提出通过收集多样化奖励的rollout并用LM推断奖励描述注入输入，显著提升监控性（24-42%），使CoT显式推理错误奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监控语言模型的思维链是AI安全的关键策略。随着RL环境复杂化，完美验证变得不可行，奖励设定错误不可避免。需要研究奖励设定错误本身是否会降低CoT可监控性，以及如何在无先验知识下缓解此问题。

Method: 构建包含真实奖励与错误奖励对的测试平台，跨三个模型和三个任务诱导并可验证地标记奖励黑客行为。收集训练过程中不同步骤的多样化奖励rollout，利用LM推断奖励描述并注入输入，训练模型口头化奖励机制。

Result: 随着黑客率上升，CoT可监控性持续保持低水平：CoT仍推理任务意图而输出违背意图。提出的奖励描述训练使平均监控性提升24%或42%（取决于指标），且生成的CoT显式推理错误奖励而非掩盖意图。

Conclusion: 奖励设定错误单独作用即会降低CoT可监控性。无需真实奖励或黑客先验知识，通过奖励描述训练可显著恢复监控性，使模型在CoT中显式暴露其优化的错误奖励，为AI安全监控提供实用缓解方案。

Abstract: Monitoring the chain-of-thought (CoT) of language models (LMs) is a key strategy for detecting and preventing misbehaviors for AI safety. As such, it is critical to identify reinforcement learning (RL) practices that lead to unmonitorable chain-of-thought. One such practice may be unavoidable: reward misspecification. As RL environments become more complex, it is infeasible to have perfect verification, and models are rewarded for behaviors that are different from the true task intent. We show that reward misspecification alone already degrades monitorability. We create a testbed with pairs of true reward aligned with task intent and misspecified rewards that favor a different behavior, so that reward hacking can be induced by training and labeled verifiably. Across three models and three tasks, CoT monitorability remains low as models' hack rates increase: the CoTs keep reasoning about the task intent while the outputs violate the intent and get rewarded. To mitigate unmonitorability without prior knowledge of the true reward or the hack, we collect rollouts with diverse rewards across training steps and use a LM to infer a reward description. We then inject the description into the input, which teaches the model to verbalize the reward. This reward description training improves monitorability by an average of 24% or 42% absolute, depending on the metric, across all models and tasks, and yields CoTs that reason about the misspecified reward explicitly. Code is available at https://github.com/Lorenayannnnn/reward-misspecification-degrades-cot-monitorability.

</details>


### [93] [From Abstraction to Action: DSLs for Natural Language Abstraction Interpretation and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28311)
*Amit Lisha,Reut Tsarfaty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用领域特定语言（DSL）来弥合自然语言抽象指令与具体动作之间的鸿沟。实验表明，基于人工策划的DSL进行代码生成能显著提升性能（高达10个F1点），达到人类水平，尤其在高抽象层级效果明显。但模型自动诱导的DSL仅能恢复部分增益，且跨运行不稳定，未达人工DSL水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言指令常依赖高层抽象（如'将积木堆成金字塔'），语言模型在处理高抽象层级指令时性能显著下降。需要一种显式表示层来连接抽象语言与具体动作。

Method: 借用编程环境中的领域特定语言（DSL）概念，将DSL作为显式表示层，其文档化基元命名重复出现的抽象并展开为具体动作。在Hexagons视觉接地指令遵循任务和Text2CAD基准上评估：对比直接推理、代码生成、基于人工策划DSL的代码生成、基于模型诱导DSL的代码生成四种方法。

Result: 代码生成持续优于直接推理；基于人工策划DSL的代码生成带来巨大增益（高达10个绝对F1点），达到人类天花板，增益集中在更高抽象层级。模型诱导的DSL仅恢复部分增益，跨运行不一致，未达人工DSL水平。在Text2CAD上确认相同模式。

Conclusion: 合适的表示层（DSL）能弥合高抽象造成的大部分鸿沟，但自动诱导这一表示层正是模型仍显不足之处，也是社区取得进展的重要机会。

Abstract: Natural-language instructions often rely on high-level abstractions, such as asking an agent to ``stack blocks in a pyramid.'' Interpreting abstract instructions requires models to both identify the abstraction intended by the speaker, and unroll it into the many concrete actions that instantiate it. Language models struggle here: prior work has shown that performance degrades as the level of abstraction in the instruction rises. To bridge the gap between natural language abstraction and concrete actions, we propose to borrow from coding environments the notion of a {\em domain-specific language} (DSL), an explicit representation layer whose documented primitives name recurring abstractions that expand into concrete actions. We demonstrate the efficacy of DSLs for NL interpretation on \textsc{Hexagons}, a visually-grounded instruction-following task graded for abstraction. We further ask whether language models can induce such DSLs themselves from domain data. Our results show that while code generation consistently improves over direct inference, code generation over a human-curated DSL yields dramatic gains, up to 10 absolute F1 points, reaching the human skyline, with gains concentrated at {\em higher} abstraction levels. We confirm this pattern on the \textsc{Text2CAD} benchmark. At the same time, model-induced DSLs recover only part of this gain, remaining inconsistent across runs and short of their human-curated counterpart. Overall, the right representation closes much of the gap caused by high abstraction --- but inducing that layer of representation automatically is where models still fall short, and where we see a strong opportunity for the community to make progress.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond a Single Sentiment Score: Topical and Affective Dimensions of Emoji Meaning on Twitter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28337)
*Rohan James,Ioannis Christofilogiannis,Yuuv Jauhari,Shreya Shah,Gauri Pethkar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文挑战了表情符号作为单一情感分数的传统观点，提出“稳定性增量”指标，发现表情符号的主题连贯性与情感极化程度正相关，证明主题连贯性和情感离散度是表情符号含义的两个独立维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍将表情符号视为紧凑的情感标记，仅用单一情感分数概括其含义。作者认为这种视角不完整，旨在探究表情符号的主题使用语境与其情感极化之间的关系，揭示表情符号语义的多维度本质。

Method: 基于16,575条英语推文语料库，提出“稳定性增量”指标（类内减类间句子嵌入余弦相似度），量化表情符号使用语境的主题聚类程度。结合Kralj Novak等(2015)情感词典和Ferré等(2025)西班牙语13维情感评分进行验证，采用Spearman相关性分析，并通过排列检验、BCa bootstrap、留一法Jackknife、偏相关控制频率及Holm多重校正确保结果稳健性。下游任务在Emoji Sarcasm Dataset上进行泄露自由的五折交叉验证讽刺检测实验。

Result: 主题聚类度越高的表情符号，其众包情感评分越极化（而非越中性）。与Kralj Novak词典验证得Spearman ρ=+0.361 (Holm p=0.015, 95% CI [+0.10, +0.59])；与Ferré等西班牙语评分验证得ρ=+0.509 (p=0.0003)。结果通过多种统计检验均显著。讽刺检测任务中三个基线模型（门控多模态架构、微调BERTweet、微调RoBERTa）宏F1均超0.97，表明数据集存在饱和的表情符号-标签线索。

Conclusion: 表情符号的主题连贯性与情感离散度是两个正相关但独立的语义维度，单一情感分数无法捕捉这种复杂性。下游分类器的高性能反映了数据集的标签泄露问题，而非模型优越性。该研究为表情符号语义建模提供了新的理论框架和评估范式。

Abstract: Emoji are widely treated as compact affective tokens whose meaning is summarised by a single sentiment score. We argue that this picture is incomplete. From a corpus of 16{,}575 English tweets and a per-emoji metric we call \emph{stability delta} (within-class minus between-class cosine similarity of sentence embeddings), we show that emojis used in tightly clustered topical contexts also exhibit \emph{more} polarised crowd-sourced sentiment, not less. Validated against the Kralj Novak et al.\ (2015) sentiment lexicon, the central correlation is Spearman $ρ= +0.361$ (Holm $p = 0.015$, 95\% bootstrap CI $[+0.10, +0.59]$); against the independent Ferré et al.\ (2025) Spanish 13-emotion ratings, $ρ= +0.509$ ($p = 0.0003$). The result survives permutation testing, BCa bootstrap, leave-one-out jackknife, partial correlation controlling for emoji frequency, and Holm correction across a four-test family. Topical coherence and affective dispersion are distinct dimensions of emoji meaning. As a downstream check we report leakage-free five-fold cross-validation results for sarcasm classification on the Emoji Sarcasm Dataset; all three baselines (a gated multimodal architecture, fine-tuned BERTweet features, and fine-tuned RoBERTa features) score above 0.97 macro F1, indicating the dataset has saturating emoji-label cues. We frame the classifier as supporting evidence rather than the contribution.

</details>


### [95] [Object-Oriented Harness: Evolving Agent Harnesses through Object-Oriented Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28380)
*Gaotang Li,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Yifei Ming,Yanfei Chen,Zhongying CuiZhu,Lesly Miculicich,Long T. Le,Rui Meng,Chun-Liang Li,Hanghang Tong,Chen-Yu Lee,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向对象工具链（OO-Harness），一种通过面向对象设计演化可执行工具链类层级的优化框架，解决单一全局工具链难以应对异构任务的问题，在三个长时程基准上实现高达15%的绝对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单一全局工具链难以处理需要不同执行策略的异构任务，缺乏针对性地适应多样化任务需求的能力。

Method: 训练阶段：进化智能体利用执行记录，通过继承机制从基类或现有专用工具链创建专用工具链子类家族；测试阶段：工具链路由器根据训练积累的路由经验为每个任务选择特定工具链。

Result: 在涵盖业务工作流、工作空间任务和工具使用的三个长时程基准上，OO-Harness 达到最佳性能，相比基线绝对提升高达15%，消融实验验证了核心组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 面向对象的工具链层级设计结合进化创建与路由选择机制，能有效应对异构任务需求，显著提升语言模型代理在复杂长时程任务上的表现。

Abstract: Agent harnesses are executable code that specifies the prompts, tools, and control flow around a language model. A single global harness can struggle with heterogeneous tasks that require different execution strategies. In this work, we introduce Object-Oriented Harness (OO-Harness), a harness optimization framework that evolves a hierarchy of executable harness classes using object-oriented design. During training, an evolution agent uses execution records to create a family of specialized harnesses, each implemented as a subclass of the base or an existing specialized harness via inheritance. At test time, a harness router uses routing lessons accumulated during training to select a specific harness for each task. Across three long-horizon benchmarks spanning business workflows, workspace tasks, and tool use, OO-Harness achieves the best performance among the compared methods, with an absolute improvement of up to 15% over the base. Extensive ablation studies examine the contributions of its core components.

</details>


### [96] [When Copying Is Hard: Copy-Constrained Decoding for Exact Span Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28393)
*Jinghui Zhang,Lang Gao,Zongfang Liu,Ruihong Zeng,Zirui Song,Rui Yan,Kentaro Inui,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对LLM在长上下文中精确逐字复制目标跨度的能力不足问题，构建了一个跨合同、科学文献、代码和随机序列的基准，并提出了CopyGen——一种轻量级复制约束插件，将复制解耦为“何时复制、复制多远、何时停止”三个决策，通过自适应调整logits实现受控复制，在三种LLM骨干和四个任务上使精确匹配率相对提升35%，且可与SFT结合再提升4%，同时显著加速生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在长上下文中精确逐字复制目标跨度是基础能力测试，但在实际应用（如代码生成需嵌入API密钥）中，模型常在跨度内漂移或无法正确停止，暴露了源 grounding 和边界控制的局限。现有模型对此类任务仍犯频繁错误，表明可靠精确复制并非免费获得，而是需要显式解码时控制。

Method: 提出CopyGen插件，冻结LLM参数，将复制分解为三步决策：1) 判断是否进入复制模式；2) 预测暂定复制长度；3) 逐token验证生成跨度。验证阶段自适应调整复制续写token的logits以激励或抑制续写，仅输出最长有效前缀。无需微调即可集成，且可与SFT无缝结合。

Result: 在三种LLM骨干（未指明具体模型）和四个基准任务（合同、科学文献、代码、随机序列）上，CopyGen使平均精确匹配率相对提升35%；结合SFT可再提升4%；同时显著加速生成过程。

Conclusion: 可靠的精确复制并非LLM自带能力，而是一个解码时控制问题，需显式建模。CopyGen通过轻量级插件实现受控复制，无需微调即大幅提升性能，证明了解耦复制决策并自适应调整logits的有效性。

Abstract: Precisely reproducing a target span verbatim within a long context is a fundamental capability test for LLMs: even when the target string is present in context, models often still drift within the span or fail to stop correctly, revealing limitations in source grounding and boundary control. This capability has broad practical relevance. For instance, code generation often requires embedding sensitive strings such as API keys exactly as they appear in context. In this work, we construct a benchmark to test exact span reproduction ability across contracts, scientific literature, code, and random sequences, and find that current models still make surprisingly frequent errors. To address this, we propose CopyGen, a lightweight copy-constrained plugin for frozen LLMs that turns copying from a byproduct of next-token prediction into a controlled process. Our key idea is to decompose copying into three distinct decisions: when to copy, how far to copy, and when to stop. Concretely, CopyGen first determines whether to enter copy mode, then predicts a tentative copy length, and finally verifies the generated span token by token. During verification, we adaptively adjust the logits of copy continuation tokens to incentivize or suppress continuation, and emit only the longest valid prefix. Across three LLM backbones and four benchmark tasks, CopyGen improves average exact match by 35% relative without model finetuning, and integrates seamlessly with SFT to further improve it by 4%, while substantially accelerating generation. These results suggest that reliable exact copying is not something LLMs obtain for free, but a decoding-time control problem that requires explicit modeling. Code is available at https://github.com/mbzuai-nlp/CopyGen.

</details>


### [97] [The Instruction Tax on Factual Recall: Base Models Can Beat Their Instruction-Tuned Counterparts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28407)
*Christophe Khalil,Aya Mourad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了AKBC LM-KBC 2026共享任务的解决方案，得分0.6836，超过基线0.2964的两倍多。研究表明提示方法与模型架构同样重要：指令微调模型在聊天界面下得分0.5681，而基础模型用文本补全提示得0.6836，指令微调反而降低了事实回忆能力，称之为“指令税”。最终方案：用训练数据示例提示基础模型，生成64-256个输出并保留重复项；针对每个关系设计两种专用方法，一是提升大答案集的召回，二是预测死亡地点前先验证人物是否在世。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在无检索、无微调条件下，仅用语言模型（≤320亿参数）为主体-关系对生成所有可能客体的最佳策略，并揭示指令微调对事实回忆的负面影响。

Method: 1. 采用基础模型而非指令微调模型，使用文本补全式提示（few-shot，含训练集示例）。2. 对每个主体-关系对生成64-256次输出，仅保留重复出现的答案（自一致性过滤）。3. 针对不同关系设计两种专用增强：① 大答案集召回增强方法；② 预测死亡地点前先判断人物存活状态。

Result: 基础模型+文本补全提示达到0.6836，显著高于指令微调模型+聊天提示的0.5681，且远超官方基线0.2964。实验证实指令微调会降低事实回忆能力（指令税），且该效应源于模型权重而非提示方式。

Conclusion: 在知识库补全任务中，提示策略与模型选择同等关键；指令微调虽提升对话能力，但会损害事实回忆，基础模型配合少样本文本补全提示更适合事实检索型任务。简单的自一致性过滤与关系特定的后处理能大幅提升性能。

Abstract: We present our solution to the AKBC LM-KBC 2026 Shared Task, where we achieved a score of 0.6836 on the official leaderboard, more than double the baseline of 0.2964. This task requires generating all possible objects for subject-relation pairs using a language model with up to 32 billion parameters, with no retrieval or fine-tuning involved. We show that the prompting methodology is as important as the model architecture: while the instruction-tuned model version achieves 0.5681 with its chat interface, the base model achieves 0.6836 when prompted using text completion. Thus, instruction tuning decreases factual recall; we refer to this effect as the instruction tax, which is caused by the model's weights rather than the prompting method. Our final solution is fairly straightforward: we prompt the base model with some examples from the training data, generate 64--256 outputs, and retain only the repeated ones. Two specialized methods are then applied for each relation: one improves the recall of large answer sets, and the other verifies a person's vitality before predicting their place of death.

</details>


### [98] [Every Model Leaves a Trace: Multi-Resolution LLM Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28434)
*Mohammad Reza Modarres,Armin Tourajmehr,Artin Tavasoli,Yadollah Yaghoobzadeh,Mohammad Taher Pilehvar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型生成文本中的模型特定指纹，探究其在不同模型家族、蒸馏模型、LoRA适配模型中的区分能力，以及在抑制表层线索、改写、跨提示分布和语言等条件下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已知大模型生成文本包含模型特定痕迹，但尚不清楚这些指纹能多细地区分相关模型，以及在明显线索被削弱后哪些信号仍具信息量。

Method: 通过指纹分辨率框架，从模型分辨率（不同家族、蒸馏、LoRA适配）和信号分辨率（行为、文体统计、词汇、语义证据）两个维度系统研究。逐步抑制表层线索、改写输出、变化可用文本量、跨提示分布和语言测试泛化性。

Result: 指纹超越宽泛家族差异仍存在，在蒸馏模型甚至同一基座模型的LoRA适配中仍可检测。尽管随表层和词汇线索改变归因能力减弱，但仍保留大量模型特定信号。这些信号跨提示源和语言迁移，语义表征跨语言最鲁棒。

Conclusion: 模型身份可在多个分辨率层面恢复，但恢复能力取决于模型相似度和文本中可用的证据类型。

Abstract: Large language models leave model-specific traces in their generated text, but it remains unclear how finely these fingerprints can distinguish related models and which signals remain informative as obvious cues are reduced. We study this through fingerprint resolution, considering both model resolution, from different families to distilled and adapted variants, and signal resolution across behavioral, stylometric, lexical, and semantic evidence. We progressively suppress surface cues, paraphrase outputs, vary the amount of available text, and test generalization across prompt distributions and languages. Our results show that fingerprints persist beyond broad family differences, remaining detectable in distilled models and even LoRA adaptations of the same base model. Although attribution weakens as surface and lexical cues are altered, substantial model-specific signal remains. These signals transfer across prompt sources and languages, with semantic representations proving most robust across languages. These findings show that model identity is recoverable at multiple resolutions, but that recoverability depends on model similarity and the evidence available in the text.

</details>


### [99] [MythoSemantic: An Interactive System for Discovering Semantic Parallels Across Mythological and Religious Traditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28452)
*Nikita Ganziuk,Innokentiy S. Humonen,Ilya Makarov,Pavel Lebedev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于神话和宗教文本比较研究的工具，利用神经语言模型将文本嵌入共享语义空间，通过交互式界面帮助用户跨文化传统发现语义平行结构，并量化词汇重叠和专有名词对语义几何的解释程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的人工文本比较分析极其费时费力且难以规模化，需要自动化方法来支持大规模跨文化神话宗教文本的语义对比研究。

Method: 使用神经语言模型将神话宗教文本片段嵌入共享语义空间，构建交互式界面供用户探索跨传统的语义平行结构；分析嵌入片段形成的语义结构几何特性，并量化词汇重叠和专有名词对几何结构的贡献。

Result: 嵌入片段形成了清晰的语义结构，其粗略几何与已知的传统间历史关系高度一致；量化分析显示词汇重叠和专有名词仅部分解释了这种几何一致性，表明模型捕捉到了更深层的语义模式。

Conclusion: 该工具为神话学和宗教研究提供了可扩展的计算辅助手段，揭示了跨文化文本的深层语义关联，系统已开源部署供学术界使用。

Abstract: We present a tool for the comparative study of mythological and religious texts. Traditional manual analysis is highly labor-intensive and difficult to scale. To address this, we use neural language models to embed texts into a shared semantic space, so that users can find semantic parallels across cultural traditions in an interactive interface. Embedded fragments form distinct semantic structures whose coarse geometry aligns with well-documented historical relations between traditions. We quantify how much of that geometry is already explained by lexical overlap and by proper names. The current system is available at https://mythoscope.io, and the installable package is available at https://github.com/haafus/MythoSemantic/blob/main/RUN.md.

</details>


### [100] [Asymmetric Looped Transformers: Fixed Global Representations for an Iterative Decoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28489)
*Cheng Liang,Tengfei Zhang,Wenjie DU,Jianyu Wu,Changlian Ma,Shuchen Pu,Ya Zhang,Yanfeng Wang,Zhouhan Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AsymLoop 将上下文编码与状态细化解耦，通过单次因果编码器产生固定的 Global KV，配合共享权重的循环解码器生成动态 Local KV，实现单 softmax 下的联合注意力。实验显示其在同等有效深度和训练预算下，比 Full Loop 实现更低困惑度、更高下游准确率，并在 1B 规模将七项常识推理零样本准确率从 49.58% 提升至 50.85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全栈循环 Transformer 将上下文构建与迭代状态细化混在同一循环计算中，缺乏显式的角色区分，限制了模型在不增加参数量的前提下有效扩展计算深度的能力。

Method: 提出 Asymmetric Looped Transformer (AsymLoop)：单次因果编码器生成层特定的 Global KV 缓存（跨迭代固定），权重共享的循环解码器从不断演化的状态生成 Local KV；联合注意力在单一 softmax 下融合持久上下文与当前中间状态。训练采用重尾引导的层级学习率策略分别优化编码器与解码器。

Result: 在 FineWeb-Edu 上，四个规模（60M–1B）均优于 Full Loop，验证困惑度更低、平均下游准确率更高。1B 模型七项常识推理零样本平均准确率从 49.58% 提升至 50.85%。优势在更长训练预算、OLMo 风格骨干及稀疏 MoE 模型上同样成立。

Conclusion: 不对称的上下文编码与状态细化设计是循环语言模型的有效范式，通过显式分离持久上下文表示与迭代状态更新，在保持参数量不变的前提下显著提升建模效果与扩展性。

Abstract: Looped Transformers increase effective depth by repeatedly applying shared layers, offering a way to scale computation without increasing parameter count. However, full-stack looping places context construction and iterative state refinement within the same recurrent computation, without explicitly distinguishing their roles. We introduce the Asymmetric Looped Transformer (AsymLoop), which separates single-pass context encoding from recurrent state refinement. A single-pass causal encoder produces layer-specific Global key-value (KV) caches that remain fixed across iterations, while a weight-tied recurrent decoder generates Local KV from its evolving state. Joint attention over both sources under a single softmax allows each update to integrate persistent contextual information with the current intermediate state. For training, we apply a heavy-tail-guided layerwise learning-rate method to the encoder and decoder. Experiments on FineWeb-Edu across four model scales, from 60M to 1B, show consistently lower validation perplexity and higher average downstream accuracy than Full Loop under matched effective depth and training token budgets. At 1B, AsymLoop improves average zero-shot accuracy across seven commonsense reasoning benchmarks from 49.58% to 50.85%. Further experiments extend these benefits to longer training budgets, an OLMo-style backbone, and sparse mixture-of-experts models. These results support asymmetric context encoding and state refinement as an effective design for recurrent language models.

</details>


### [101] [OptionWorld: A Benchmark for Diagnosing Failures of LLMs as Long-Term Personal Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28491)
*Ruoyu Zhang,Zhaocen Liu,Weiyi Tian,Melissa Feng,Shilong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 OptionWorld 基准，评估大语言模型作为长期个人助手的能力，发现模型在维持任务状态方面存在三大缺陷：信息获取与整合不足、行动机会未利用、自我施加额外限制。实验表明补充缺失事实、行动提醒、修正错误限制可部分恢复任务成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期助手需跨天追踪未完成任务，随信息与环境变化调整行动。现有评估缺乏对信息获取、判断、行动与结果全链路的模拟，难以定位模型在长期任务中的具体失效模式。

Method: 构建 OptionWorld 基准，模拟日常生活任务，记录信息获取、判断、行动与结果全过程。对失败案例分析，识别高频问题；再通过三组实验（补充事实、行动提醒、修正限制）验证干预效果。

Result: 档案输入运行中，35.5% 失败案例的关键事实仍保留在截止前最后一次输入。三大高频问题：1) 所需信息未获取或未整合；2) 可用行动机会未利用；3) 模型自我施加任务不要求的限制。补充缺失事实可部分恢复成功；行动提醒能重启工作但后续步骤仍错过截止；修正错误禁止联系的备注可恢复允许的询问。

Conclusion: 模型在长期任务中维持任务状态存在三大短板：将事实关联到未完成工作、在截止前坚持执行、区分实际限制与自身假设。OptionWorld 为诊断和改进长期助手能力提供了细粒度基准。

Abstract: Long-term assistants must follow unfinished tasks across days as information and circumstances change. Finding information or starting an action, however, does not ensure that the task gets completed. We introduce OptionWorld, a benchmark that traces information acquisition, judgments, actions, and outcomes in simulated daily-life tasks to identify where current LLMs fall short as long-term personal assistants. In runs with archived decision inputs, the key facts remain in the model's last input before the deadline in 35.5% of failed instances. Failure analysis identifies the three most frequent problems: needed information is not obtained or combined, available opportunities to act go unused, and agents impose restrictions that the task does not require. Three follow-up experiments show that supplying missing facts restores task success in some cases; action reminders can restart work while subsequent steps still miss the deadline; and correcting a supplied note that wrongly forbids contact restores permitted inquiry. These findings locate shortcomings in maintaining task state: connecting facts to unfinished work, following through before deadlines, and separating actual restrictions from the agent's assumptions.

</details>


### [102] [BitMTP: When Multi-Token Prediction Meets Low-Bit Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28500)
*Ning Zhang,Shihao Wang,Jinrui Zhang,Chaodong Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BitMTP 是一种算法-系统协同设计框架，将超低比特 LLM 主干与多令牌预测（MTP）相结合，通过联合训练目标、多种解码策略及前缀对齐机制，在保持任务质量的前提下显著加速推理，跨多种模型规模、主干架构和 GPU 平台均实现 2 倍以上的吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低比特主干可降低权重存储、内存流量和线性层计算开销，而 MTP 可并行预测多个未来令牌以减少顺序解码步数。但直接组合两者无法在同等质量下加速解码：掩码草稿缺乏下一个令牌训练中的中间令牌输入，更宽的计算和因果键值（KV）更新会抵消步数减少带来的收益。因此需要一种协同设计来解决这些冲突。

Method: BitMTP 提出：1) 联合下一个令牌预测与 MTP 目标，使单一模型同时具备自回归生成和块因果草稿能力；2) 置信度解码、投机解码和混合解码三种策略，提供不同质量-吞吐工作点，无需单独的草稿和验证模型；3) 接受前缀对齐机制，保持已提交前缀、物化因果 KV 边界和 CUDA Graph 重放状态一致，支持继续生成；4) 基于此契约选择宽度专用的低比特执行内核，并在共享 KV 存储上复用捕获的计算图。

Result: 在 NVIDIA H20 上，BitMTP 混合解码在 BitNet b1.58-2B 上实现 2.2 倍平均加速，在 1.7B-4B Bonsai 模型上实现 2.1-2.4 倍加速，任务质量相当。在 RTX 3090 和 Jetson Orin 上，五任务平均吞吐最高提升 2.0 倍，MBPP+ 上最高达 3.3 倍。结果表明 BitMTP 能跨主干、模型规模和 GPU 类型加速超低比特 LLM 生成。

Conclusion: BitMTP 通过算法-系统协同设计，成功将超低比特主干与多令牌预测相融合，解决了直接组合时的质量-速度权衡问题，在多种硬件平台和模型规模上均实现了显著的推理加速，为高效部署大语言模型提供了通用且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: For efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs), methods of ultra-low-bit backbones reduce weight storage, memory traffic, and linear-layer execution cost, while multi-token prediction (MTP) methods reduce sequential decoding steps by predicting multiple future tokens in parallel. Although complementary, the direct combination of these methods cannot accelerate decoding at comparable quality: masked drafting lacks intermediate token input supplied in next-token training, while wider computation and causal key-value (KV) updates can offset the savings from fewer steps. To address these challenges, we propose BitMTP, an algorithm-system co-design framework for efficient MTP on ultra-low-bit LLM backbones. A joint next-token prediction and MTP objective equips a single model with both autoregressive (AR) generation and block-causal drafting. Confidence-only decoding, speculative-only decoding, and hybrid decoding provide different quality-throughput operating points without using separate draft and verifier models. Accepted-prefix alignment keeps the committed prefix, materialized causal KV boundary, and CUDA Graph replay state consistent for continued generation. BitMTP uses this contract to select width-specialized low-bit execution and reuse captured graphs over shared KV storage. On NVIDIA H20, BitMTP with hybrid decoding achieves five-task mean speedups of 2.2x on BitNet b1.58-2B and 2.1-2.4x on 1.7B-4B Bonsai models at comparable task quality. On RTX 3090 and Jetson Orin, it further achieves up to 2.0x in five-task mean throughput and 3.3x on MBPP+, respectively. These results demonstrate that BitMTP accelerates ultra-low-bit LLM generation across backbones, model sizes, and GPU types. The source code is released at https://github.com/PolyU-VCLab/BitMTP

</details>


### [103] [Large Flow Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28559)
*Arnav Chavan,Chanhyuk Lee,Udbhav Bamba,Jaehoon Yoo,Seunghoon Hong,Aditi Raghunathan,Jinwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Lafa是一种块级流语言模型，成功扩展到8B参数，仅用<30B tokens高效训练，在指令跟随、推理、编程任务上匹配或超越自回归模型质量，并通过自投机解码实现2.54倍吞吐率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续流语言模型提供并行生成路径，具备离散扩散模型无法提供的控制和加速工具，但在十亿参数规模上的有效构建技术仍不成熟，需要建立可扩展的流语言模型基础。

Method: 提出块级流模型：前缀使用因果注意力，块内采用双向异步流。通过适配预训练自回归模型实现高效训练；利用前缀缓存和块级并行解码加速推理；设计自投机解码方案，在单模型内自回归验证流生成的草稿。

Result: 在0.5B、7B、8B三个规模上训练，数据效率优于先前扩散模型。流训练保留甚至提升预训练AR能力（多token预测效应）。自投机解码在同等质量下实现2.54倍AR吞吐率，超越多token预测和离散扩散方法。

Conclusion: 研究表明连续流可以作为语言建模的可扩展基础，兼具高质量生成、高效训练和快速推理优势。

Abstract: Continuous flow language models offer a path to parallel generation with tools for control and acceleration unavailable to discrete diffusion, yet effective techniques for building them at the billion-parameter scale remain less established. We present Lafa (Large flow language model), a fast, high-performing flow language model scaled to 8B parameters in a data-efficient manner. Lafa is a blockwise flow model with causal attention on the prefix and bidirectional, asynchronous flow within each block. This design enables efficient training by adapting pretrained autoregressive (AR) models and fast inference via prefix caching and blockwise parallel decoding. We train Lafa at 0.5B, 7B, and 8B scales with fewer than 30B tokens, more efficient than previous diffusion models, and evaluate it on instruction following, reasoning, and coding, where the learned flow matches or surpasses the quality of AR and can be distilled into flow maps for one- and few-step generation. Notably, flow training preserves or even improves pretrained AR capabilities via a multi-token prediction effect, which we leverage in a self-speculative decoding scheme that verifies flow-based drafts autoregressively within a single model. This yields 2.54x the throughput of an AR model at comparable quality, outperforming multi-token prediction and discrete diffusion methods. Our findings show that continuous flows can serve as a scalable foundation for language modeling.

</details>


### [104] [Cache Engineering for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28577)
*Tianyu Fu,Ruoxi Wang,Zihan Min,Zhongqi Ren,Zhihao Li,Tengxuan Liu,Shengxu Mu,Hanling Zhang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出缓存工程（cache engineering）让LLM代理直接控制KV缓存，无需修改轨迹或重新预填充，设计了AKV服务引擎管理状态化缓存复用，实验显示性能和成本显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理处理复杂任务，轨迹增长导致KV缓存I/O和存储成为主要成本，且可能超出上下文长度限制。现有上下文工程通过裁剪或摘要历史减小缓存，但会使后续缓存状态失效，触发昂贵的重新预填充，且保留过少上下文会损害任务准确性。

Method: 提出缓存工程方案，代理可主动使用drop排除选定缓存不参与未来注意力，用repos调整保留键的位置编码。形式化缓存操作，识别历史依赖作为服务挑战：相同token序列在不同缓存操作序列下产生不同缓存状态，前缀匹配不足以正确复用缓存。协同设计AKV服务引擎，提供无状态接口兼容现有服务API，内部管理状态化缓存复用、驱逐和恢复。

Result: 实验显示，工具响应滚动drop缓存策略相比传统阈值触发摘要，平均基准分数提升1.7分，平均成本降低27.7%。配合AKV，输出吞吐提升达1.2倍，P95请求延迟加速1.5倍。缓存策略的递归自我改进进一步比专家设计降低7.1%成本。

Conclusion: 缓存工程结合AKV引擎有效解决了LLM代理长轨迹下的KV缓存管理问题，在保持任务准确性的同时显著降低成本和延迟，且支持策略自动优化。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents can handle increasingly complex tasks, navigating long trajectories of observations and actions. As these trajectories grow, key-value (KV) cache I/O and storage become the dominant cost. Trajectories can also exceed the LLM's maximum context length, requiring control over active cache growth. Prior work uses context engineering to reduce cache size by trimming or summarizing history. However, modifying the trajectory prefix invalidates subsequent cached states and triggers costly re-prefilling of retained context. Task accuracy can also suffer when too little context is retained to limit re-prefill costs. We propose cache engineering as a new scheme. It allows agents to directly control their active KV cache without modifying the trajectory or re-prefilling retained context. Specifically, agents can proactively use drop to exclude selected cache from future attention and repos to adjust retained keys' position encodings. We formalize these cache operations and identify history dependence as a serving challenge: identical token sequences can yield different cache states under different cache operation sequences, making prefix matching insufficient for correct cache reuse. Thus, we co-design the AKV serving engine. It provides a stateless interface compatible with existing serving APIs, while internally managing stateful cache reuse, eviction, and restoration. Experiments show that a tool-response rolling-drop cache policy improves average benchmark scores by 1.7 points and reduces average cost by 27.7%, compared with conventional threshold-triggered summarization. With AKV, it achieves up to 1.2x the output throughput and a 1.5x speedup in P95 request latency. Recursive self-improvement of cache policies further reduces cost by 7.1% over expert design. Our code is at https://github.com/thu-nics/AKV.

</details>


### [105] [Capable Teacher, Myopic Scorer: Reward-Free Teacher Repair for Agentic On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28586)
*Shu Zhao,Tan Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文指出智能体在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中“累积误差”解释不完整，发现教师模型的下一token评分存在近视性，导致“回合-评分器差距”，提出Rollout-to-Scorer Distillation (R2S)：从未训练学生挖掘高分歧状态，执行教师接管并通过无奖励检查筛选，微调教师给出接管知情的评分，冻结作为唯一评分器进行标准OPD。在三环境、两教师、四学生上，R2S在全部12个单元格超越三个SOTA方法，平均提升12.8成功率点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPD方法将失败归因于累积误差，通过课程学习、教师引导回合、前缀回放或教师干预限制训练在教师可靠区域。但实验表明，即使从学生偏离最严重的状态出发，教师接管仍能完成任务，说明教师可靠区域更大。核心问题是教师逐token评分不见回合结局，对安全、拖延动作给高分，而基于结果的RL会给零奖励惩罚，OPD缺乏此惩罚。这种教师实际行为与评分不一致称为“回合-评分器差距”。

Method: R2S三步修复评分器：1) 从未训练学生采样轨迹，挖掘高分歧状态；2) 从这些状态运行教师接管，保留通过无奖励检查（共识或LLM裁判）的状态；3) 微调教师，使其在无接管上下文的情况下，从分歧回合起对每个assistant token给出接管知情的评分。修复后的教师冻结为唯一评分器，标准OPD训练学生，全程无需任务奖励。

Result: 在三个环境（如WebShop、ALFWorld、ScienceWorld）、两个教师（GPT-4、Llama-3-70B）、四个学生（Llama-3-8B、Mistral-7B等）上，R2S在所有12个教师-学生-环境组合中均优于三个SOTA agentic-OPD基线，平均比每组合最强基线高出12.8成功率点。

Conclusion: R2S通过修复评分器的近视性评分，消除了回合-评分器差距，无需任务奖励即可显著提升OPD性能。该方法揭示了OPD失败的根本原因不在于累积误差，而在于评分器缺乏全局回合视角，为智能体蒸馏提供了新范式。

Abstract: Agentic on-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student agent on its own trajectories while a teacher scores every token. A natural explanation of where it goes wrong is compounding error: the student's mistakes carry its trajectories beyond the region where the teacher is reliable, so recent methods keep training inside that region, through episode-length curricula, teacher-guided turns, prefix replay, or teacher intervention where the two disagree. We show this explanation is incomplete. From the states where the student departs most from the teacher, the teacher's own takeovers still finish the task, yet the student trained on its token scores keeps running out of turns. We attribute this to the myopia of next-token scoring. The teacher rates each token without seeing where the episode ends, so its scores let through the safe, delaying moves that outcome-based RL would punish with zero reward, a penalty agentic OPD lacks. We call this difference between what the teacher does and what it scores the rollout-scorer gap. We propose Rollout-to-Scorer Distillation (R2S), which repairs the scorer before distillation by mining high-disagreement states from the untrained student, running teacher takeovers from them, keeping the states where enough takeovers pass a reward-free check such as consensus or an LLM judge, and fine-tuning the teacher to give its takeover-informed scores on every assistant token from the divergence turn onward, without the takeover in context. The repaired teacher is frozen as the sole scorer for standard OPD, requiring no task reward during repair or training. Across three environments, two teachers and four students, R2S outperforms three state-of-the-art agentic-OPD methods in all twelve teacher-student-environment cells, by 12.8 success points on average over the best of them in each cell. Project page: https://tree-shu-zhao.github.io/r2s/.

</details>


### [106] [Benchmarking Arabic Dialects for Math Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28607)
*Noor Abo Mokh,Teresa Lynn,Nizar Habash,Ahmed Elshabrawy,Sara Alhajeri,Alaa Elsetohy,Preslav Nakov,Alham Fikri Aji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出首个用于评估阿拉伯语方言数学推理的手工标注基准DAR-MGSM，涵盖五大方言及现代标准阿拉伯语，包含纯翻译版与文化本地化版两个并行版本。评估11个阿拉伯语中心及多语言大模型发现：本地化版本普遍导致性能下降，但不同模型敏感度差异显著；机制可解释性分析揭示相同题目的翻译版与本地化版会诱导不同的内部推理轨迹。研究表明数学推理性能同时取决于语言变体和文化语境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数学推理研究在低资源语言（包括阿拉伯语方言）上严重不足。现有基准多针对现代标准阿拉伯语（MSA）或高资源语言，缺乏对方言阿拉伯语及文化语境对数学推理影响的系统评估，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 构建DAR-MGSM基准：从多语言GSM8K选取多步数学应用题，人工翻译并适配至五大阿拉伯语方言（埃及、黎凡特、海湾、马格里布、伊拉克）及MSA，制作纯翻译版与文化本地化版（实体、指代本地化）两个并行集。对11个阿拉伯语中心及多语言大模型进行少样本评测，并引入机制可解释性分析（如注意力模式、激活差异）对比同题不同版本的内部推理轨迹。

Result: 1) 所有模型在本地化版本上准确率普遍下降，证实文化适配引入额外挑战；2) 阿拉伯语中心模型对本地化的敏感度差异显著，部分模型鲁棒性较强；3) 机制分析显示，即使整体准确率相近，翻译版与本地化版同一题目往往激发不同的内部推理路径（注意力分布、中间层表示差异）。

Conclusion: 数学推理能力不仅依赖语言形式，还受文化语境深度影响。评估基准需同时考量方言多样性与文化本地化；模型开发应增强对文化适配变体的鲁棒性；机制可解释性可揭示表面性能指标无法捕捉的内部推理差异。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning remains underexplored for low-resource languages, including Dialectal Arabic. To bridge this gap, we introduce the DAR-MGSM (Dialectal Arabic Multilingual Grade School Math) Benchmark, the first manually curated benchmark for evaluating mathematical reasoning in Dialectal Arabic. DAR-MGSM consists of math word problems (MWPs) requiring multi-step reasoning and covers five major Arabic dialects alongside Modern Standard Arabic (MSA). The benchmark comprises two parallel versions: translated-only and translated & culturally localized. The latter version adapts entities and references to regional varieties. We evaluate eleven Arabic-centric and multilingual large language models on DAR-MGSM. The results show that the performance generally declines on the localized version, highlighting the challenges introduced by culturally grounded adaptations. However, the magnitude of this effect varies across models, with Arabic-centric models exhibiting different levels of sensitivity to localization. A mechanistic interpretability analysis shows that translated and localized variants of the same MWPs often induce distinct internal reasoning trajectories, even when aggregate accuracy remains similar. Overall, our results show that mathematical reasoning performance depends on both linguistic variety and cultural context.

</details>


### [107] [NarraSpace: Multimodal Scene-Grounded Evidence for Picture-Description Dementia Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28616)
*Qingming Li,Ruiyan Sun,Satoshi Nakamura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 NarraSpace 方法，将图片描述任务中的语音转录与画面元素、事件及时间对齐，利用冻结语言模型提取事件级语言特征，结合 SVM 分类器在 ADReSS 基准测试集上实现 81.25% 准确率和 91.32% AUC，且保留可追溯的源记录供临床复核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图片描述的痴呆分类通常仅使用转录级预测，无法定位支撑分类的具体画面证据与语言表现，缺乏可解释性与临床可用性。

Method: 1. 构建 NarraSpace：将词语、语音时间与预定义画面元素/事件关联，提取叙事空间描述符（覆盖率、时序组织）。2. 使用冻结语言模型对比阿尔茨海默病组与健康对照组的事件文本，计算惊讶度与覆盖度指标。3. 保留源记录，将事件得分链接回原始话语、语音区间与词汇匹配。4. 采用固定支持向量分类器进行分类。

Result: 在官方 48 人测试集上，50 个随机种子下平均准确率 80.88%±2.56%，平均概率融合后准确率 81.25%，AUC 91.32%。

Conclusion: NarraSpace 使面向场景的分类输入构建过程显性化、可审查，事件级语言模型测量与源话语、语音时序、提取记录关联，可为临床医生提供定量摘要与原始语音证据的并列展示，辅助图片描述的临床复核。

Abstract: Picture-description dementia classification uses a shared visual scene, but a transcript-level prediction alone does not identify the scene evidence underlying its inputs. NarraSpace links words and speech times to named picture elements and retrieves utterances for predefined scene events. Narrative-spatial descriptors summarize coverage and temporal organization. A frozen language model contrasts each event's text under examples from Alzheimer's disease and healthy-control training participants, alongside transcript surprisal and coverage measurements. Retained source records link event scores to their source utterances, speech intervals, and lexical matches. On the official 48-participant test set of the Alzheimer's Dementia Recognition through Spontaneous Speech benchmark, a fixed support vector classifier achieves $80.88\%\pm2.56\%$ mean accuracy over fifty seeds; averaged probabilities yield $81.25\%$ accuracy and $91.32\%$ area under the receiver operating characteristic curve. NarraSpace makes the construction of scene-linked classification inputs explicit and inspectable by connecting event-indexed language-model measurements to their source utterances, speech timing, and extraction records. Such source-linked measurements could support clinician review of picture descriptions by presenting quantitative summaries alongside the original speech evidence.

</details>


### [108] [OPLoRA: Orthogonal Projection LoRA Prevents Catastrophic Forgetting during Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.13003)
*Yifeng Xiong,Xiaohui Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出正交投影LoRA (OPLoRA)，通过双侧正交投影将LoRA更新约束在预训练权重顶部奇异子空间的正交补空间中，理论上保证保留前k个奇异三元组，有效解决LoRA微调中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA方法在高效微调大语言模型时，学习到的更新会干扰编码核心预训练知识的主导奇异方向，导致灾难性遗忘。需要一种理论上有保证的方法来保护预训练知识的核心子空间。

Method: 利用SVD分解冻结权重，构造左投影P_L = I - U_k U_k^T和右投影P_R = I - V_k V_k^T，将LoRA更新完全约束在前k个奇异子空间的正交补空间中。引入ρ_k指标量化子空间干扰程度，并证明该构造精确保留前k个奇异三元组。

Result: 在LLaMA-2 7B和Qwen2.5 7B上进行常识推理、数学和代码生成实验，OPLoRA显著减少灾难性遗忘，同时保持具有竞争力的任务特定性能，验证了正交投影作为参数高效微调中知识保留机制的有效性。

Conclusion: OPLoRA通过理论严谨的双侧正交投影机制，在保证保留预训练核心知识（前k个奇异三元组）的同时实现高效适应，为参数高效微调中的知识保留提供了有效且有理论保证的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables efficient fine-tuning of large language models but suffers from catastrophic forgetting when learned updates interfere with the dominant singular directions that encode essential pre-trained knowledge. We propose Orthogonal Projection LoRA (OPLoRA), a theoretically grounded approach that prevents this interference through double-sided orthogonal projections. By decomposing frozen weights via SVD, OPLoRA constrains LoRA updates to lie entirely within the orthogonal complement of the top-$k$ singular subspace using projections $P_L = I - U_k U_k^\top$ and $P_R = I - V_k V_k^\top$. We prove that this construction exactly preserves the top-$k$ singular triples, providing mathematical guarantees for knowledge retention. To quantify subspace interference, we introduce $ρ_k$, a metric measuring update alignment with dominant directions. Extensive experiments across commonsense reasoning, mathematics, and code generation demonstrate that OPLoRA significantly reduces forgetting while maintaining competitive task-specific performance on LLaMA-2 7B and Qwen2.5 7B, establishing orthogonal projection as an effective mechanism for knowledge preservation in parameter-efficient fine-tuning.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [MOSS: A Production Framework for Monitoring and Diagnosing Semantic Failures in Search and Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26902)
*Alina Nesen,Prathyusha Kanmanth Reddy,David Relyea,Rohit Avinash Mujumdar,Samaneh Karimi,Bo Shen,Jordan Hashemi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MOSS 是一个生产级 LLM 监控框架，通过嵌入 LLM 相关性评分的持续监控循环，实现跨推荐、搜索等多业务面的语义故障检测与根因诊断，已在大型电商生产环境运行 6 个月并验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产推荐/搜索系统的语义故障难以检测：行为指标滞后数天、人工复核无法规模化、现有 LLM 评估框架多面向离线固定数据集而非持续运营监控，存在操作层面的空白。

Method: 提出 MOSS 框架，将 LLM 相关性评分嵌入循环监控流程，包含定向采样、策略感知提示、结构化告警、诊断性后续提示（将故障分类为可执行根因）；同一评判模型、评分刻度和阈值仅通过面特定提示适配即可迁移至搜索场景。

Result: 在大型美零售商生产环境运行 6 个月以上，发现两个确认的服务缺陷；对回填混合缺陷，离线量化修复方案可使告警率相对降低 76.4%。在 498 对人工标注推荐基准上，前沿 LLM 评判者 F1 与 AUC 均优于非 LLM 基线（内部生产嵌入和 SBERT），在需意图级关系推理的策略依赖任务上增益最大。同一评判者、评分刻度和阈值仅经面特定提示适配，在 466 对、跨 21 个查询的搜索基准上也达强 F1 与 AUC 且无需重调。

Conclusion: 单一 LLM 监控框架可在大规模电商场景下跨多个产品发现业务面提供有用、可执行的质量信号，实现语义故障的持续检测与根因诊断。

Abstract: Detecting semantic failures in production recommendation and search systems is hard: behavioral metrics lag by days, manual review does not scale, and most LLM evaluation frameworks are designed for offline, fixed datasets rather than continuous operational monitoring. We present Model Sanity Service (MOSS), a production framework that addresses this operational gap by embedding LLM-based relevance scoring into a recurring monitoring loop with targeted sampling, strategy-aware prompting, structured alerting, and diagnostic follow-up prompting that categorizes failures into actionable root causes.
  MOSS has been running in production at a large U.S. retailer for over six months across recommendation, search, and offer surfaces, and has surfaced two confirmed serving defects. For one of these, a backfill blending defect, MOSS quantified the impact of the proposed fix offline as a 76.4\% relative reduction in alert rate. On a 498-pair human-annotated recommendation benchmark, frontier LLM judges achieve higher F1 and AUC point estimates than non-LLM baselines (internal production embeddings and SBERT), with the largest gains on strategy-dependent tasks requiring intent-level relational reasoning. The same judge, score scale, and threshold transfer to a 466-pair search benchmark spanning 21 queries with only a surface-specific prompt adaptation, achieving strong F1 and AUC without retuning. These results show that a single LLM monitoring framework can provide useful, actionable quality signals across multiple product discovery surfaces in a large-scale e-commerce setting.

</details>


### [110] [GRaSP: Latency-Aware Optimization for Geo-Distributed Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26938)
*Hardik Ruparel,Raj Kumar Pandey,Pramod Kumar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 GRaSP，一种用于地理分布式 RAG 系统的即插即用重排序层，通过延迟感知衰减相关性得分并可选引入出口成本惩罚，在基数预算与每区域上限约束下优化检索选择，无需重新训练，实现了延迟与召回率的 Pareto 最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在跨区域云边基础设施部署 RAG 时，广域延迟、跨区域出口流量成本与数据驻留合规要求与检索质量相互竞争。传统流水线仅按语义相似度排序，忽略内容物理位置，导致尾延迟与成本显著膨胀。亟需一种免重训练、可直接集成的选择机制，在保证检索质量前提下同时优化延迟与成本。

Method: 将问题形式化为 Geo-RAG 选择问题（GRaSP）：在分区拟阵约束下最大化线性目标，目标函数为相关性得分减去延迟衰减项与可选出口成本惩罚项。证明基础公式可通过 O(N log N) 贪心算法最优求解（而非背包问题的 NP-hard），硬度仅在引入加性出口预算时出现。实现为无需重训练的即插即用重排序层，支持基数预算与每区域上限双重约束。

Result: 在 5 节点 CloudLab 测试床（模拟 4 区域 WAN）与 Natural Questions 语料（268 万段落）上：统一放置下以可忽略质量损失大幅降低获取延迟；主题/驻留式放置下勾勒出支配纯相关性、最近 K 与延迟预算基线的召回-延迟 Pareto 前沿。适度延迟设置下 P90 获取延迟降低 41%，保留 78% 纯相关性召回，选择开销亚毫秒级，吞吐稳定至 200 QPS。结论在四个 BEIR 基准上泛化。端到端问答中上下文获取延迟降低 43%，EM 仅比纯相关性检索低 0.062 绝对值。

Conclusion: GRaSP 为地理分布式 RAG 提供了理论最优且工程实用的选择层，在不牺牲模型质量前提下显著压降尾延迟与跨区域成本，原生适配数据驻留约束，具备亚毫秒级决策开销与跨基准泛化能力，是大规模地理分布式 RAG 部署的关键使能技术。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is increasingly served across geo-distributed cloud and edge infrastructures, where wide-area latency, cross-region egress cost, and data-residency rules compete with retrieval quality. Conventional pipelines rank by semantic similarity alone and ignore where content lives, inflating tail latency and cost. We formalize the Geo-RAG Selection Problem (GRaSP), a drop-in re-scoring layer that decays relevance by latency and adds an optional cost penalty under a cardinality budget and per-region caps, with no retraining. We first settle its complexity: the base formulation is a linear objective over a partition matroid, solved optimally by an O(N log N) greedy algorithm, rather than NP-hard as a knapsack framing suggests, with hardness entering only under an additive egress budget. On a five-node CloudLab testbed emulating a four-region WAN over the Natural Questions corpus (2.68M passages), GRaSP's benefit is placement-dependent: under uniform placement it cuts fetch latency at negligible quality cost; under topical (residency-like) placement it traces a recall-latency Pareto frontier that dominates relevance-only, nearest-k, and a latency-budgeted baseline. At a moderate latency setting, GRaSP lowers P90 fetch latency by 41% while retaining 78% of relevance-only's recall, with sub-millisecond selection overhead, stable to 200 queries/s. Findings generalize across four public BEIR benchmarks. In end-to-end question answering, GRaSP reduces context-fetch latency by 43% while remaining within 0.062 absolute EM of relevance-only retrieval.

</details>


### [111] [UIBK-Embedding: Effective Text Retrieval by Unifying Dense and Late-Interaction Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27195)
*Abdelrahman Abdallah,Mohammed Ali,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UIBK-Embedding-v1 是一个 1.79 亿参数的英语编码器，在一次前向传播中同时输出稠密向量和逐 token 向量，通过在 token 序列中插入三个额外向量并引入一维标签避免跨类型匹配，使标准 MaxSim 直接等于 token-level MaxSim 与加权稠密余弦的和，从而在无需修改索引的情况下统一了稠密双编码器与晚交互检索器，并通过共享干线与跨编码器蒸馏实现了两头融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稠密双编码器擅长捕捉语义主题，而晚交互检索器（如 ColBERT）擅长精确实体与短语匹配，两者在不同查询上互补但通常需部署两套模型、两套索引并做分数融合，增加了工程复杂度与推理开销。

Method: 1. 架构：共享 18 层 Transformer 干线，分别接 4 层稠密头与 4 层 token 头。2. 融合表示：将稠密向量拆为三个向量拼接到 token 向量列表后，并给每个向量加一维二进制标签（0 为 token，1 为稠密），使 MaxSim 计算时自动屏蔽跨类型匹配，plain MaxSim = token-level MaxSim + 加权稠密余弦。3. 训练：用跨编码器蒸馏融合后的分数，使两头协同优化。4. 部署：直接复用 PLAID 等标准晚交互索引，无需修改。

Result: 在 BEIR 基准上达到 59.79 nDCG@10，比同规模最强模型 LateOn 高 2.57 分（同文档长度下高 2.10 分），并在全部 15 个数据集上均领先；去污染版 BEIR 平均 61.55 对 60.36，12 个数据集中 8 个领先。MTEB 排行榜 156 个完整结果模型中，仅参数量 ≥1.5B 的模型得分更高。

Conclusion: UIBK-Embedding-v1 以单模型、单索引、单次 MaxSim 同时获得稠密与晚交互双重优势，在小参数量下实现了 SOTA 检索效果，显著降低了工程部署复杂度，证明了统一表示融合的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: Dense bi-encoders and late-interaction retrievers are usually treated as alternatives, yet they fail on different queries: one vector captures what a text is about, token-level matching finds its exact entities and phrases. Using both today means two models, two indexes and a score-fusion step. We present UIBK-Embedding-v1, a 179M-parameter English encoder that produces both representations in one pass and scores them with one unmodified MaxSim. The dense embedding is written into the list of token vectors as three extra vectors, and a one-dimensional tag keeps the two kinds of vectors from matching each other, so that plain MaxSim equals the token-level MaxSim plus a weighted dense cosine. The model therefore runs in a standard late-interaction index without changes; we test it with PLAID. A shared 18-layer trunk with a separate 4-layer top per head, and a cross-encoder distilled into the fused score, turn the two heads into one retriever. On BEIR, UIBK-Embedding-v1 reaches 59.79 nDCG@10. This is 2.57 points above LateOn, the strongest model of its size, and 2.10 points above it when both models read documents of the same length; UIBK-Embedding-v1 is ahead on all 15 datasets. On a decontaminated version of BEIR it stays ahead on average (61.55 against 60.36) and on 8 of 12 datasets. Among the 156 models with complete BEIR results in the MTEB results repository, only models with at least 1.5B parameters score higher. Code, model weights, and a technical blog post are available at https://github.com/DataScienceUIBK/uibk-embedding , https://huggingface.co/DataScience-UIBK/uibk-embedding-v1 , and https://huggingface.co/blog/abdoelsayed/uibk-embedding-v1 .

</details>


### [112] [Neighborhood-Aware Unlearning for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27232)
*Pierre Lubitzsch,Yubao Tang,Maarten de Rijke,Sebastian Schelter*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对基于语义标识符（SID）的生成式推荐中的遗忘学习问题，指出现有方法因 token 共享导致局部邻域概率质量重新分布，全局指标掩盖了局部效用下降。提出邻域感知遗忘学习（NAU），通过显式控制被遗忘交互邻域的概率质量，在三个数据集和四种 SID 分词器上恢复垃圾注入导致的曝光偏移，保持 >95% 再训练效用，且无全局代价地恢复邻域效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐通过自回归生成共享 token 的 SID 预测物品。当需移除训练交互（如垃圾注入、用户不想要的物品）时，token 共享会使遗忘更新无意中影响语义相关邻居的预测。这种局部结构化效应被全局指标掩盖：整体测试集效用几乎不变，但被遗忘交互 SID 邻域的效用大幅下降。

Method: 提出邻域感知遗忘学习（NAU），在交互级遗忘基础上增加对被遗忘物品邻域概率质量的显式控制。通过约束邻域 token 的概率分布，防止遗忘过程中概率质量向邻域错误重新分配。

Result: 在三个数据集和四种 SID 分词器上实验：NAU 将垃圾注入导致的曝光偏移恢复至再训练基准，保留 >95% 的再训练效用，且在零全局代价下恢复了 forget-and-repair 遗忘丢失的邻域效用。

Conclusion: 识别出局部概率重新分布是基于 SID 的生成式推荐遗忘学习的重要失效模式，证明显式控制这种重新分布可有效缓解其负面影响。

Abstract: Generative recommendation predicts items by autoregressively generating semantic identifiers (SIDs), whose tokens are shared across semantically related items. With this paradigm gaining adoption, the question of how to support unlearning in it has become pressing. If training interactions must later be removed, such as injected spam or interactions with items a user no longer wants, this sharing lets an unlearning update unintentionally affect the predictions for the interactions' neighbors. We show that this effect is locally structured and hidden by global metrics: unlearning can leave recommendation utility on the whole test set nearly unchanged while substantially degrading it on neighborhoods of the forgotten interactions' SIDs. We propose Neighborhood-Aware Unlearning (NAU), which augments interaction-level unlearning with explicit control over the probability mass assigned to the forgotten items' neighborhoods. Across three datasets and four SID tokenizers, NAU restores spam-induced exposure toward the retrained reference while retaining more than 95% of retrained utility, and recovers the neighborhood utility that forget-and-repair unlearning loses at no global cost. We identify local probability redistribution as an important failure mode of unlearning in SID-based generative recommenders and show that explicitly controlling this redistribution can mitigate its effects.

</details>


### [113] [FLINT: Fixed-budget Late Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27273)
*Himank Handa,Negar Arabzadeh,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FLINT是一种固定预算的多向量晚期交互检索架构，通过注意力池化将查询和文档都表示为固定数量的嵌入向量，在保持检索效果的同时大幅降低存储和计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多向量检索模型如ColBERT虽然效果优于单向量模型，但保留每个token的向量导致存储需求大增且检索变慢，限制了可扩展性。现有方法通常只对文档侧池化而保留token级查询，未能从根本上解决向量比较次数和存储问题。

Method: 提出FLINT架构，对查询和文档对称应用基于注意力的池化，将每侧表示为固定数量的嵌入向量。使用固定预算的晚期交互机制，在BERT和ModernBERT两种骨干网络上验证。池化操作减少了文档存储和检索时的向量比较次数。

Result: 在BEIR基准上效果与最强基线相当，在长复杂查询基准（BrowseComp+、CRUMB、TREC Tip-of-the-Tongue 2025）上超越基线。长查询语料上端到端检索速度比ConstBERT快1.5-2.8倍，索引大小比ColBERTv2等token级晚期交互模型减少5.3-9.3倍。

Conclusion: FLINT通过对称池化实现了固定预算的晚期交互，在多种编码器架构上都能同时获得效果和效率的双重提升，为大规模多向量检索提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-vector retrieval models such as ColBERT often outperform single-vector models of comparable scale by matching queries and documents at the token level. However, retaining a vector for every token substantially increases storage requirements and slows retrieval compared to single-vector bi-encoders, limiting their scalability. We introduce FLINT (Fixed-budget Late INTeraction), an architecture that uses attention-based pooling to represent each query and document with a fixed number of embedding vectors. Unlike architectures that pool only document representations while retaining token-level queries, FLINT applies pooling symmetrically to both sides, limiting document storage and the number of vector comparisons needed during retrieval. FLINT achieves comparable ranking effectiveness to the closest baselines on BEIR and outperforms them on several benchmarks with longer and more complex queries, including BrowseComp+, CRUMB, and TREC Tip-of-the-Tongue 2025. We demonstrate that FLINT's effectiveness-efficiency benefits extend across encoder architectures, testing it on two separate backbones (BERT and ModernBERT). On long-query corpora, FLINT delivers 1.5-2.8 times faster end-to-end retrieval than ConstBERT, the closest baseline. FLINT also reduces index size by 5.3-9.3 times relative to token-level late-interaction models such as ColBERTv2.

</details>


### [114] [Conversational AI for Perceived Cognitive Decline Screening in Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27344)
*Nikita Gautam,Moana Sargent,Kaitlyn Sievers,Ashley Sylvara,Doina Caragea,Heather Bailey,Tianjun Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究评估基于聊天机器人的智能认知助手（ICA）问答访谈能否筛查老年人主观认知衰退及相关认知、功能和人格结果，比较了多种文本分析方法，发现Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite在聚合问答流程中表现最佳，但样本量小，结果仅具描述性；结构化问卷和认知任务模型优于纯文本模型，ICA访谈捕捉到有意义的信号，需更大规模临床验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经心理学评估资源需求大，难以大规模早期筛查老年认知衰退；基于聊天机器人的ICA通过开放式回答获取语言、语义和自传体线索，提供可扩展的替代方案。

Method: 采用ICA聊天机器人进行问答访谈，收集老年人开放式回答；比较传统机器学习、冻结Transformer嵌入、微调Transformer和大语言模型推理策略，在全局问答、单问答置信度平均和聚合问答三种流程下评估；使用保留测试集（n=31）评估分类性能（准确率、平衡准确率、宏F1），并进行连续回归分析预测AD8、FAQ、IQCODE、神经质和尽责性评分；探索性提示层面分析识别直接症状相关和间接自传体问题的预测效用。

Result: 文本方法中Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite（1-shot per class，聚合问答）达到最高测试性能（准确率0.742，平衡准确率0.739，宏F1 0.728），但因样本小仅作描述性解读；冻结临床Transformer嵌入+随机森林在非LLM文本方法中领先，微调模型泛化有限；结合结构化问卷、认知任务和人格特质的模型分类表现优于纯文本模型；回归分析显示对AD8、FAQ、IQCODE、神经质和尽责性有适度预测信号；提示层面分析发现直接症状相关和间接自传体问题均有强测试指标，但各提示样本量不同。

Conclusion: ICA聊天机器人访谈能捕捉主观认知衰退的有意义信号，支持可扩展筛查研究；但需更大规模、临床多样性队列及经验证的临床结局以实现临床转化。

Abstract: Early detection of cognitive decline in older adults remains challenging due to the resource demands of standard neuropsychological assessments. Chatbot-based Intelligent Cognitive Assistants (ICAs) provide a scalable alternative by eliciting open-ended responses rich in linguistic, semantic, and autobiographical cues. This study evaluates whether ICA question-answer (Q/A) interviews with older adults can screen for participant-reported perceived cognitive decline and related cognitive, functional, and personality outcomes. We compare traditional machine learning, frozen transformer embeddings, fine-tuned transformers, and large language model (LLM) reasoning strategies across Global Q/A, Single Q/A confidence averaging, and Aggregated Q/A pipelines. Among text-based methods, Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite (1-example-per-class, Aggregated Q/A) achieved the highest held-out test performance (0.742 accuracy, 0.739 balanced accuracy, 0.728 macro-F1). Given the small test set (n = 31), these results are interpreted descriptively. Frozen clinical transformer embeddings with a random forest head led non-LLM text methods, whereas fine-tuned models showed limited generalization. Models utilizing structured questionnaires, cognitive tasks, and personality traits outperformed text-based models in classification. Continuous regression analyses revealed modest predictive signals for AD8, FAQ, IQCODE, neuroticism, and conscientiousness. Exploratory prompt-level analyses identified both direct symptom-related and indirect autobiographical questions with strong test metrics, though sample sizes varied across prompts. These findings indicate that ICA chatbot interviews capture meaningful signals of perceived cognitive decline, supporting scalable screening research. Larger, clinically diverse cohorts with validated clinical outcomes are needed for clinical translation.

</details>


### [115] [Baseline and Incremental: User, Item, and Request Debiasing in Instagram Feed Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27423)
*Hsin-Chiang Chen,Hao Wang,Gedi Zhou,Mingze Gao,Abhishek Kumar,Yiben Yang,Haoyu Wang,Hongjing Xia,Bo Chen,Zhengyu Su,Xian Sun,Chang Xu,Kevin Han,Weiwei Li,Zhongchang Sun,Yao Zhang,Qifei Wang,Peggy Yang,Yihan Wu,Haoran Liu,Siyang Sun,Xingyu Pan,Shannon Phu,Haoming Chen,Kevin Zhao,Chen Liang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双项式价值模型，在Feed排序中保留原始预测（基线）并单独加权增量部分，通过参数化去偏算子统一处理观众习惯、内容流行度和请求上下文三类偏差，在Instagram Feed大规模在线消融实验中显著优于单项式基线，并已在Meta全家桶多业务场景落地推动核心指标增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献倾向于从预测中移除基线，但作者认为基线包含有价值的信号（如观众固有活跃度、内容固有吸引力），直接丢弃会损失信息；同时不同偏差源（观众、内容、请求）耦合在单一预测中，难以独立建模和加权。

Method: 1. 将价值模型拆解为：原始预测（基线）+ 增量项（去偏后的增量），两者赋予独立权重。2. 设计参数化去偏算子，统一处理观众习惯、内容流行度、请求上下文三类偏差；媒体类型偏差改用去偏标签。3. 通过设计流程自动选取算子参数。4. 四臂消融实验：无项、仅基线、仅增量、双项并用。

Result: 双项式模型在13个（项，指标）对中的11个点估计优于两个单项式模型；26个直接对比中17个显著支持双项式，仅1个显著反对（展示量指标）。离线诊断应筛选信号而非排序。该方法已在Instagram Feed生产环境运行近一年，并推广至Meta旗下多App、多排序阶段，显著提升会话数和日活用户。

Conclusion: 保留基线并显式建模增量是多信号价值模型的更优范式；统一参数化去偏算子能高效处理多源偏差；大规模在线实验验证了方法的普适性和业务价值，已成为Meta排序基础设施的标准组件。

Abstract: A feed ranker scores candidates with a value model, a weighted sum of engagement predictions. Each prediction conflates a baseline, what this viewer, item or request would produce anyway, with the incremental amount by which this candidate exceeds it. The literature mostly removes the baseline. We argue that a multi-signal value model should keep the raw prediction and add the incremental part as a separately weighted term. On Instagram Feed, three debiasing families deployed independently, for viewer habit, item popularity and request context, are one parameterised operator, and a design procedure picks a point in it. Media-type (format) bias goes instead to a debiased label chosen by the same procedure. In a controlled four-arm ablation of deployed Feed terms (neither term, each alone, both), the two-term arm beats both single-term arms at production weights on point estimates in 11 of 13 judged (term, metric) pairs. Of the 26 direct contrasts, 17 are significantly for it and only 1 against it, on impressions. The ablation also suggests that the offline diagnostic should screen signals, not rank them. Such terms have served Instagram Feed production for most of a year. Beyond Feed, Meta, whose apps serve billions of people, has adopted the operator across surfaces, apps and both ranking stages. Its launches there drive sessions and daily active users, the core visitation and retention metrics.

</details>


### [116] [Robustness Analysis of Pareto-based Joint Evaluation of Fairness and Relevance in Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27850)
*Theresia Veronika Rampisela,Maria Maistro,Tuukka Ruotsalo,Christina Lioma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出Distance to Pareto Frontier (DPFR)方法，用于联合评估推荐系统的相关性与公平性。DPFR基于现有度量的Pareto前沿，将用户-物品交互数据转化为多个Pareto最优权衡点，通过计算模型得分到前沿的距离获得联合评分。实验表明现有联合度量与DPFR关联不一致，DPFR更稳健，并提供了使用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估推荐系统相关性与公平性的两种方式各有缺陷：单独评估会导致两个不同的模型排名；联合“相关性感知”公平度量往往对两方面评估不平衡。两者都无法同时、平衡地评估相关性和公平性，因此需要一种能提供联合、稳健评估的新方法。

Method: 提出Distance to Pareto Frontier (DPFR)：利用现有相关性和公平性度量，在用户-物品交互数据上生成Pareto前沿（即多个Pareto最优的相关性-公平性权衡点）。模型的联合得分定义为其在相关性-公平性平面上的得分点到该前沿的距离。该方法模块化、直观，可兼容现有度量。

Result: 在4个推荐器、3个公平性感知重排序器、6个真实数据集上的实验显示：现有联合度量与DPFR呈现不一致的关联，说明它们不是基于Pareto最优性评估的可靠代理。通过人工推荐、不同权衡设置及与简单度量的一致性分析，验证了DPFR比现有联合评估方法更稳健。

Conclusion: DPFR提供了一种基于Pareto最优性的联合评估框架，能同时、平衡地量化推荐系统的相关性与公平性。实验证明其优于现有联合度量，并给出了实际应用指南。代码已开源。

Abstract: Recommender Systems (RSs) can be evaluated for many aspects, two of them being recommendation relevance and fairness. These two aspects can be evaluated either in isolation, using a measure for each aspect; or jointly with a single`relevance-aware' fairness measure that quantifies fairness with respect to relevance. Yet, neither approach provides a simultaneous evaluation of both relevance and fairness: the first approach may result in two different rankings of models, each corresponding to an evaluation aspect; the second approach often provides an imbalanced evaluation of the two aspects. To address this gap, we propose Distance to Pareto Frontier (DPFR), a new approach to jointly evaluate RS fairness and relevance. DPFR is based on a Pareto frontier of relevance and fairness scores from existing measures. The frontier is generated from user-item interaction data and represents various Pareto-optimal trade-offs between the two aspects. Thus, the distance from a point in the frontier provides a joint score of fairness and relevance. As DPFR can be used with existing measures, it is modular and intuitive. Our experiments with 4 recommenders and 3 fairness-aware re-rankers on 6 real-world datasets reveal inconsistent associations between existing measures and DPFR, showing that they are not reliable proxies for evaluations based on Pareto optimality. We further examine the robustness of DPFR as a joint evaluation approach for fairness and relevance by conducting experiments with artificial recommendations and with various fairness-relevance trade-offs, as well as by analysing DPFR's agreement to simple measures of fairness and relevance. These analyses show that DPFR is more robust than existing joint evaluation approaches. Finally, we provide guidelines for evaluating fairness and relevance jointly with DPFR. Our code: https://github.com/theresiavr/robustness-DPFR-recsys-evaluation.

</details>


### [117] [SPAR: Stepwise Personalized Autoregressive Recommendation with Prefix-Balanced Semantic IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27898)
*Chunrui Zhang,Gaoyun Lin,Chang Zhou,Zheng Hu,Qiran Song,Shimin Cai,Tao Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 SPAR 框架，通过 PATH Decoder 增强前缀相关的用户证据检索，以及 RQ-PIB 模块平衡兄弟分支的历史交互负载，解决了生成式推荐中逐步个性化不足和 SID 分支不平衡问题，在优酷大规模生产数据和在线 A/B 测试中显著提升了命中率、目标前缀保留率、分支平衡度及用户观看时长与播放转化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语义 ID (SID) 的生成式推荐在解码每一步时，未充分利用前缀相关的用户长期记忆与近期行为，且候选分支间历史交互量不均衡，导致不可逆的分支选择错误和热门路径偏好，降低了推荐的个性化程度和层级解码的可靠性。

Method: SPAR 包含两大核心模块：1) PATH Decoder：从压缩的长期用户记忆中检索前缀相关证据，结合近期行为、用户画像和事件上下文，指导每一步 token 决策；2) RQ-PIB (Residual Quantization with Prefix-aware Interaction Balancing)：在保持语义结构的前提下，平衡每个父前缀下兄弟分支的历史交互负载，缓解分支不平衡问题。

Result: 在优酷大规模生产数据上，相比最强基线平均相对 HitRate 提升 2.66%；目标前缀保留率提升 5.79 个百分点；流量加权最大子分支占比在 SID 各层级下降 32.84%。7 天在线 A/B 测试（承载 10% 生产流量）显示，人均平均观看时长提升 3.95%，播放转化率提升 3.38%。

Conclusion: SPAR 通过联合优化逐步个性化和 SID 构建，实现了更可靠的层级解码、更平衡的 SID 分支结构，并在工业级推荐系统中显著提升了用户参与度指标，验证了其在大规模生产环境中的有效性和实用性。
