Result: 1. 情绪类别可解码性在中层形成宽峰值，第11层L2探针准确率63.6%（远超10%随机与54.6% TF-IDF基线）。2. 生成时用户情绪token每token获得注意力是其他token的1.89倍，注意力集中于第10-15层。3. 目标情绪对齐度从输入表征均值余弦相似度0.239 下降至生成响应0.125，77%样本呈正向漂移，愤怒与暴怒类别漂移幅度最大。4. 表征、注意力分配、响应级对齐三大组件在功能上可分离，符合认知-情感共情框架预测。

Conclusion: 研究证实大模型内部存在可分离的认知共情（情绪表征与选择性注意）与情感共情（响应对齐）处理通路，且中层为情绪表征核心区，注意力机制自发聚焦情绪线索，但生成过程中情绪对齐度系统性衰减。该框架为实时监控生成时情绪表征、干预注意力分配、利用模型内生情感结构实现更自然共情提供了可操作的分析工具与理论依据。

Abstract: Empathetic interaction involves two distinguishable capacities: representing another person's emotional state (cognitive empathy) and generating an appropriate response to that state (affective empathy). We apply this distinction to examine how emotion-related information is internally organized, selectively attended to, and carried through response generation in Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct across 10 emotion categories from the EmpatheticDialogues dataset. Layer-wise linear probing revealed a broad middle-layer region of peak emotion-category decodability, with the layer-11 L2 probe achieving 63.6% held-out accuracy against a 10% chance level and a 54.6% TF-IDF lexical baseline. During response generation, emotion-related user tokens received 1.89 times as much attention per token as other utterance tokens, concentrated across layers 10-15. Target-emotion alignment then decreased from a mean cosine similarity of 0.239 for input representations to 0.125 for generated responses, with positive drift in 77% of examples and the largest shifts for angry and furious. Emotion-category representation, attention allocation, and response-level alignment therefore emerge as distinguishable components of model processing that can be analyzed through a functional cognitive-affective empathy framework. These findings provide a foundation for monitoring emotion representations during generation and leveraging models' existing affective structure to support more naturally empathetic responses without relying on external prompting or explicit emotion conditioning.

</details>


### [184] [TriGeoBench: A Multimodal Mathematical Geometry Benchmark with Figures and TikZ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27626)
*Jing Wang,Yitong Yang,Hao Dou,Yaomin Wu,Shuting He,Tingfei Shen,Jia Wu,Jiaqi Li,Yinglin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 TriGeoBench，一个几何中心的多模态基准，包含 7000 个问答对和 12235 个带 TikZ 代码的几何图形，用于评估多模态大语言模型的几何推理能力，结果显示模型在细粒度结构和图文对齐上仍存在挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学基准多依赖光栅化图形，覆盖的几何知识领域和响应格式有限，难以评估模型是否真正推理几何结构还是利用表面视觉模式。

Method: 从中国中高中数学考试构建 TriGeoBench，每个实例形成 {问答对、图像、TikZ} 三元组：问答对存为 LaTeX，图像为 RGB，TikZ 保留几何拓扑、符号标注和关系结构；评估代表性开源/闭源 MLLM 的图像转 TikZ 和多模态推理能力。

Result: 实验揭示模型在细粒度结构理解和图文对齐方面存在持续挑战；Oracle 实验表明准确的可执行 TikZ 表示能提供互补几何线索，但并非评估端到端转换流程。

Conclusion: TriGeoBench 为结构感知的数学推理提供了测试平台，有助于推动几何中心多模态推理的研究。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models show strong vision-language capabilities, yet their geometry-centric reasoning remains insufficiently understood. Existing mathematical benchmarks typically rely on rasterized figures and cover limited geometric knowledge areas and response formats, making it difficult to assess whether models reason over geometric structures or exploit superficial visual patterns. We introduce TriGeoBench, a geometry-centric multimodal benchmark constructed from Chinese middle- and high-school mathematics examinations. TriGeoBench contains 7,000 question-answer pairs and 12,235 geometric figures with TikZ code, spanning plane geometry, solid geometry, analytic geometry, function, and vector geometry across diverse response formats. Each instance forms a {question-answer pair, image, TikZ} triplet: question-answer pairs are stored in LaTeX, images as RGB figures, and TikZ preserves geometric topology, symbolic annotations, and relational structure. We evaluate representative open- and closed-source MLLMs through image-to-TikZ and multimodal reasoning probes. Results reveal persistent fine-grained structure and image-text alignment challenges. Oracle experiments with human-curated reference TikZ suggest that accurate executable representations provide complementary geometric cues; they do not evaluate an end-to-end conversion pipeline. TriGeoBench offers a testbed for structure-aware mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [185] [When Does Feedback Help? Auditing Learned Voltage-Clamp Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27632)
*Rayan Nyaupane,Abhiram Bhupatiraju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了学习型科学控制器在无反馈情况下是否能优于设定错误的规划器，通过合成电压钳制任务实验发现，学习策略虽能匹配贝叶斯控制器性能，但并未利用反馈，且排除目标的预测增益极小，结论是这些结果既不构成生物学发现，也不证明强化学习的普遍优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨学习型控制器在模型设定错误时是否能在不依赖反馈的情况下超越传统规划器，以及这种性能是否源于真正的反馈利用或仅是固定协议的优化。

Method: 使用两个合成电压钳制任务，采用训练选定的固定协议、电流掩蔽、贝叶斯参考控制器和排除目标设置。五个循环策略在1200次离网轨迹中重复同一固定协议；在四假设交叉任务中，固定诊断脉冲后接一个学习选择，策略使用首次电流观测，与正确设定的贝叶斯控制器性能持平，且拟合阈值能复现学习动作。

Result: 电流掩蔽不改变决策；学习策略与正确设定的贝叶斯控制器表现相当，但并未真正利用反馈；排除目标的预测增益仅为4.6991×10^{-6} nats/样本；反馈利用与协议质量是独立因素。

Conclusion: 实验表明学习控制器可在无反馈情况下匹配贝叶斯控制器，但这不构成生物学发现，也不证明强化学习普遍优于传统方法；固定协议的质量而非反馈利用是关键。

Abstract: A learned scientific controller can outperform a misspecified planner without benefiting from feedback. We audit two synthetic voltage-clamp tasks with a training-selected fixed protocol, current masking, a Bayesian reference, and an excluded target. Five recurrent policies repeat one fixed protocol in all 1,200 off-grid rollouts; masking current changes no decision. In a crossed four-hypothesis task, a fixed diagnostic pulse is followed by one learned choice. Policies then use the first current, but tie a correctly specified Bayesian controller, and a fitted threshold reproduces the learned actions without being that controller. The excluded-target prediction gain is $4.6991\times10^{-6}$ nats per sample. Feedback use and protocol quality are separate tests. These cases establish neither biological discovery nor general RL superiority.

</details>


### [186] [SCODEL: Shared Canonical Ontology Definitions for Food Entity Linking and Ontology-Grounded Recipe Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27637)
*Nuhu Ibrahim,Rishi Ravikumar,Ayuba Dauda,Fuqi Xu,Robert Stevens,Riza Batista-Navarro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCODEL通过共享规范定义作为语义归一化层，提升食谱实体链接性能，且模型无关，泛化至生物医学领域，构建本体接地食谱知识图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食谱中的食物提及往往模糊、依赖上下文且词汇多样，导致本体实体链接（EL）具有挑战性。

Method: 提出SCODEL，一种基于核心思想的流线型、模型无关方法：指代同一实体的上下文食物提及和本体概念应学习相同的规范本体导向定义。利用共享定义作为语义归一化层，为现有EL模型提供互补语义信息，无需新链接架构。并引入ReciLinkD基准。

Result: 在三个模块化EL模型（ReFinED, GSM-EL, BLINK）上持续提升：SCODEL x BLINK在已见概念上Hit@1达88.33%，SCODEL x GSM-EL在未见概念上Hit@1达83.15%，均优于最强LLM基线。生物医学本体基准实验证实泛化能力。大规模接地食物提及构建本体接地食谱知识图谱，展示本体感知食谱检索和语料库级食物分析效用。

Conclusion: SCODEL通过共享规范定义有效解决食谱实体链接的歧义和多样性问题，作为即插即用模块提升现有EL模型性能，跨领域泛化，并支持大规模知识图谱构建与下游应用。

Abstract: Identifying recipe entities and linking them to ontology concepts is fundamental for semantic recipe understanding. However, food mentions in recipes are often ambiguous, context-dependent, and lexically diverse, making ontology entity linking (EL) challenging. We propose SCODEL, a streamlined, model-agnostic approach based on a central idea: contextual food mentions and ontology concepts that refer to the same entity should learn the same canonical ontology-oriented definition. SCODEL uses these shared definitions as a semantic normalisation layer, providing complementary semantic information to existing EL models without requiring a new linking architecture. We further introduce ReciLinkD, the first benchmark for contextual food ontology entity linking in recipes. Experiments show that SCODEL consistently improves three modular EL models (ReFinED, GSM-EL, and BLINK): SCODEL x BLINK achieves the highest Hit@1 on seen concepts (88.33%), while SCODEL x GSM-EL achieves the highest Hit@1 on unseen concepts (83.15%), outperforming the strongest large language model (LLM) based EL baseline in each setting. Experiments on a biomedical ontology benchmark further demonstrate that SCODEL generalises beyond the food domain. Finally, we use SCODEL to ground food mentions at scale and construct an ontology-grounded recipe knowledge graph, demonstrating its utility for ontology-aware recipe retrieval and corpus-level food analysis.

</details>


### [187] [CUA-Recovery: Benchmarking Error Recovery in Long-Horizon Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27645)
*Xu Hu,Haoming Li,Yuxiang Wang,Jinxin Yang,Kangshuo Li,Licheng Wang,Dingyi Kang,Jiayu Song,Tianhao Wang,Zhanhua Hu,Xu Yang,Qiannan Li,Bingzhe Li,Feng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了CUA-Recovery基准，用于评估计算机使用代理在长时程多应用工作流中的错误检测与恢复能力，并提供了ReRail框架自动合成长时程任务和恢复轨迹，实验显示现有代理在错误感知和任务完成上表现不佳，微调后显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注端到端任务性能，缺乏对代理在执行过程中检测和恢复错误能力的评估；现有恢复基准依赖人工注入错误，不能反映真实代理策略导致的失败类型和时程。

Method: 提出ReRail框架，将MyPCBench任务组合为长时程工作流，收集多策略验证的失败案例并转化为验证过的恢复轨迹，构建CUA-Recovery基准（含2,250个人工验证错误状态的测试套件和8.6K轨迹的训练套件）；在六个代理和六个接管深度上评估错误感知率(EAR)和Pass@3；在训练套件上微调Qwen3.5-35B-A3B模型。

Result: 测试套件上，代理难以识别继承错误和完成任务，EAR最高44.0%，Pass@3最高25.5%。微调后，EAR从19.9%提升至35.2%，Rubric Score从5.0%提升至16.1%；在MyPCBench和Odysseys上Pass@3分别提升15.3和10.8分，表明训练轨迹同时提升恢复能力和从零开始完成任务的能力。

Conclusion: CUA-Recovery填补了长时程多应用场景下错误检测与恢复评估的空白，ReRail框架能自动生成高质量恢复轨迹；实验证明现有代理在错误恢复上表现不足，但通过在合成恢复轨迹上微调可显著提升恢复能力且不损害原始任务性能。

Abstract: As computer-use agents (CUAs) move to long-horizon workflows spanning multiple applications, undetected errors can silently corrupt persistent state and cause cascading failures in subsequent actions. Reliable error detection and recovery have become critical bottlenecks for building robust CUAs. However, existing benchmarks focus primarily on end-to-end task performance, providing limited insight into whether agents can detect and recover from errors during execution. The few benchmarks that evaluate recovery rely largely on manually injected errors, which may not reflect the types or horizons of failures induced by real agent policies. We introduce CUA-Recovery, a benchmark comprising test and training suites across multiple desktop applications. We construct it using ReRail, a framework for synthesizing long-horizon tasks and trajectories in a persistent desktop environment. ReRail composes MyPCBench tasks into new long-horizon workflows with executable ground truth, collects verifier-confirmed failures from multiple policies, and turns them into verified recovery trajectories. CUA-Recovery comprises a human-verified test suite of 2,250 erroneous states over 100 tasks and a training suite of 8.6K trajectories over 377 tasks. Evaluation on the test suite shows that agents struggle both to recognize inherited errors and to complete the task. Across six agents and six takeover depths, Error Awareness Rate (EAR) reaches at most 44.0% and Pass@3 at most 25.5%. After fine-tuning Qwen3.5-35B-A3B on the training suite, its average EAR rises from 19.9% to 35.2% on held-out recovery states, and its Rubric Score goes from 5.0% to 16.1%. It also gains 15.3 points on MyPCBench and 10.8 points on Odysseys for Pass@3, showing that the training trajectories help both with recovery and with completing tasks from a clean start. Our code is available at https://github.com/xu-hu-2002/CUA-Recovery.

</details>


### [188] [Does Multi-Agent Collaboration Pay When Agents Serve Users? Testing Agent Scaling in Dual-Control Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27655)
*Ethan Yang,Xiangcheng Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究在真实用户交互场景（τ²-bench基准）中测试多智能体系统（MAS）与单智能体系统（SAS）的性能对比，发现MAS在几乎所有设置下均不优于SAS，平均性能下降28个百分点，且未减少错误数据库写入，反而将其他失败转化为预算耗尽和无行动。结论指出，在面向用户的智能体任务中，SAS应为默认选择，MAS设计需在相同计算预算下提供来自实际部署环境的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在自主基准上建立了“能力饱和规则”：协作在SAS较弱时有益，在SAS较强时有害，交叉点约在45%单智能体准确率。但该规则未在真实用户交互场景（含策略约束、工具调用、预算限制）中验证。本文旨在填补这一空白，测试MAS在实际部署环境中的真实收益。

Method: 在τ²-bench（航空、零售、电信三个领域）上，使用六个来自三个模型家族的语言模型，对比标准SAS与基于相同模型构建的集中式MAS。实验包含约7,950次模拟对话，每轮记录完成token账本。研究预先注册并公开变更日志；MAS按实际构建报告，但规划器未见工具模式（一项有效性指标未达标）。

Result: 在固定模拟用户下，13个有效单元格中无一显示MAS优势：12个单元格MAS更差，1个持平；加权平均增益-28点（95% CI [-44, -12]）。规则预测有益的两个单元格中，MAS分别落后13和22点。无预算上限时，MAS最多仅能在SAS 5.5倍token开销下持平。仅在第二模拟器下、最接近45%准确率的单元格出现微弱增益。增益随能力下降的趋势无法确定（斜率-0.41，95% CI [-1.51, +0.68]），因模型-领域效应主导。MAS未减少错误数据库写入，而是将其他失败转化为预算耗尽和无行动。

Conclusion: 对于服务于人的智能体任务，单智能体系统应为默认选择；多智能体设计必须在相同计算预算约束下，提供来自实际部署环境本身的实证支持。作者发布所有轨迹、账本、提示词及代码以供复现。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built from language models are deployed on the premise that collaboration beats a single-agent system (SAS), yet when it pays is unclear. The most systematic study to date gives a capability-saturation rule: coordination helps while the SAS is weak and hurts once it is strong, with the crossing near 45% single-agent accuracy. That rule was derived on autonomous benchmarks, where no user takes part. We test it where agents now work: in conversation with a user under a written policy, on $τ^2$-bench, the dual-control benchmark. On its airline, retail and telecom domains we compare the stock SAS with a centralized MAS built from the same model for six models from three families, over about 7,950 simulated conversations, with one completion-token ledger per episode per system. The study was pre-registered with a public change log; the MAS is reported as built, with one validity bar unmet (its planner never saw the tool schemas). Under a fixed simulated user no cell shows a MAS benefit: the MAS is worse in 12 of the 13 valid cells and tied in the thirteenth, and the fitted gain is negative across the observed capability range (weighted mean -28 points, 95% CI [-44, -12]). On the two cells where the rule predicts a benefit, the MAS loses by 13 and 22 points, and without a budget ceiling it at best draws level at 5.5 times the SAS's token spend. The one gain appears on the cell nearest 45%, under a second simulator. Whether the gain declines with capability is unresolved (slope -0.41, 95% CI [-1.51, +0.68]), because model-by-domain effects dominate. The MAS does not detectably reduce wrongful database writes; it converts other failures into budget exhaustion and inaction. For agents that serve people, the SAS is the default; a multi-agent design must show compute-matched evidence from the setting itself. We release all trajectories, ledgers, prompts and code.

</details>


### [189] [When Can LLM Reasoning Reliably Reuse Intermediate Results?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27662)
*Deyuan Liu,Zecheng Wang,Xuanyi Liu,Jungang Li,Yihao Hu,Mingjie Tang,Bryan Dai,Jie Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型在多子任务问题中复用中间结果的可靠性及计算成本，提出局部重试策略并给出正确完成条件与尝试次数上界，实验表明提供前子任务推理和答案能显著提升后续子任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样完整解法虽能增加解决问题机会，但每次尝试都从头开始，对于多子问题任务，所有答案需在同一次尝试中正确；保留中间结果可避免重复正确工作，但错误可能传播，需研究何时复用可靠及计算开销。

Method: 提出局部重试机制，在重试预算内停止当无候选通过检查；给出正确完成条件并推导平均尝试次数上界；分析检查成本与通过检查概率对总计算量影响；在八个推理模型上进行复合数学和算术实验，对比四个DeepSeek模型在有无前子任务上下文时的表现。

Result: 四个DeepSeek模型上，无前子任务上下文时第二子问题成功率36.2%，有答案时57.2%，有推理和答案时58.3%；算术实验显示相似首次尝试准确率可能导致重复尝试后覆盖率差异巨大。

Conclusion: 中间状态复用能显著提升多子任务解决成功率，但需权衡错误传播风险；提出的局部重试策略提供理论保证，实验验证了提供推理过程比仅答案更有效；相似初始准确率不代表相同最终覆盖率，重试策略设计至关重要。

Abstract: Sampling complete solutions gives large language models more chances to solve a problem, but each attempt starts again. For tasks with several subproblems, all required answers must be correct in the same attempt. Retaining intermediate results can avoid repeating correct work, but errors in those results may affect later attempts. We study when this reuse is reliable and how much computation it requires. For local retries that stop when no candidate passes within the retry budget, we give conditions for correct completion and bound the average number of attempts. We also show how checking cost and the chance of passing a check affect total computation. Experiments across eight reasoning models examine composite mathematics and arithmetic. Across four DeepSeek models, average success on the second subproblem is 36.2 percent without first-subproblem context, 57.2 percent with its answer, and 58.3 percent with its reasoning and answer. These scores measure second-subproblem coverage and include continuations made when selection fails. Arithmetic experiments show that similar first-attempt accuracies can lead to very different coverage after repeated attempts. Code is available at https://github.com/SempraETY/Intermediate-State-Reuse.

</details>


### [190] [Inferring continuous cognitive traits from limited neuroimaging data using pairwise convolutional neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27668)
*Marjan Rashidi,Robert Bain,Craig Stark,Jeffrey Krichmar,Mary Hegarty,Elizabeth Chrastil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种成对卷积排序框架，直接从全脑T1加权MRI学习，通过比较而非绝对预测来预测认知能力，在小样本（N=108）下表现良好，优于标准回归和逐点深度学习基线，并识别出空间能力任务的共同和任务特异性脑区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统脑-行为分析依赖预定义指标（如体积、皮层厚度）并假设线性关系，限制了小数据集的敏感性；个体认知能力差异的结构脑特征仍知之甚少。

Method: 成对卷积排序框架：直接从全脑T1加权MRI学习，进行成对比较预测而非绝对预测；使用迭代皮层区域移除（向后消除）识别支持个体表现的脑区。

Result: 在四个小规模空间能力任务中均实现高精度预测，预测能力显著优于标准回归和逐点深度学习基线；揭示了所有四个任务共有的脑区以及任务特异性脑区。

Conclusion: 成对学习能从结构MRI中提取有意义的行为信息，为个体差异的神经解剖学提供了预测和可解释的洞见。

Abstract: Individual differences in cognitive abilities vary widely, yet the structural brain features supporting these differences remain poorly understood. Traditional brain-behavior analyses rely on predefined metrics such as volume or cortical thickness and typically assume linear relationships with behavior, limiting sensitivity in small datasets. Here, we introduce a pairwise convolutional ranking framework that learns directly from whole-brain T1-weighted MRI by making comparative rather than absolute predictions, which performs well even with small samples (N=108). We tested this method with four small-scale spatial abilities tasks. Regression analysis predicted performance with high accuracy across all tasks, achieving greater predictive power over standard regression and pointwise deep learning baselines. Iterative cortical-region removal (backward elimination) revealed brain regions supporting individual performance common to all four tasks, as well as task-specific regions. These results demonstrate that pairwise learning extracts meaningful behavioral information from structural MRI, providing prediction and interpretable insight into the neuroanatomy of individual differences.

</details>


### [191] [Reusing Multimodal Agents: Efficient Evaluation of Sound Designs in Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27670)
*Thai Nguyen Van,Ibrahim Khan,Mury Fajar Dewantoro,Pratch Suntichaikul,Hai Van Pham,Ruck Thawonmas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新框架，利用多模态学习和强化学习高效评估游戏音效设计，通过预训练多模态编码器并固定策略微调编码器，避免为每种音效设计从头重新训练智能体，显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏音效设计显著影响游戏体验和无障碍性，但客观评估方法（基于智能体性能的自动化评估）通常需要为每种音效设计从头重新训练智能体，计算成本高昂且不具备可扩展性。

Method: 首先预训练一个多模态编码器，将视觉和听觉信息编码到共享潜在空间；然后使用该潜在表示训练强化学习智能体；针对每种音效设计微调多模态编码器，同时保持训练好的策略固定，以隔离音效变化对智能体行为的影响。

Result: 在多种类型游戏上实验，比较仅使用声音输入的盲智能体在不同音效设计下的表现。结果表明该方法能检测因音效退化或改变导致的智能体性能差异，且与从头重新训练相比显著降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: 该方法为游戏音效设计的评估提供了一个实用且可扩展的解决方案。源代码、数据和补充文档已公开。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel framework for efficiently evaluating in-game sound designs using multimodal learning and reinforcement learning. Sound design significantly influences gameplay and accessibility; however, objective evaluation methods, defined here as automated assessments based on agent performance rather than human ratings, often require retraining agents from scratch for each sound design. Our approach first pre-trains a multimodal encoder that encodes visual and auditory information into a shared latent space. A reinforcement learning agent is then trained using this latent representation. We fine-tune the multimodal encoder for each sound design while keeping the trained policy fixed, allowing us to isolate the effects of sound changes on agent behavior. To evaluate the proposed method, we conducted experiments on games from multiple genres, comparing the performance of blind agents (agents using only sound input) under various sound designs. Experimental results suggest that this approach can detect differences in agent performance caused by degraded or altered sound design. Moreover, we significantly reduce the computational cost compared to retraining the agent from scratch. Experimental results suggest that this approach provides a practical and scalable solution for evaluating in-game sound designs. The source code, data and supplementary document are available: https://github.com/thainv0212/tog-reusing-agents.

</details>


### [192] [Temporal Evidence Allocation For Longitudinal Clinical Retrieval: A Position And Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27682)
*Soumya Ranjan Sahoo,Divya Bharti,Sanand Sasidharan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Onco-CONSTRUE框架，将纵向临床检索视为跨时间的有限证据预算分配问题，而非单纯的相关性排序。研究发现：时间感知分配（Temporal-15）的里程碑槽位召回率（0.538）优于全局基线（0.388），但检索证据量大幅增加（496 vs 50单位/查询），提升不能仅归因于分配策略；固定证据量下移除优先级规则会增加模式顺序逆转；更宽的时间覆盖不一定带来更多有日期标注的里程碑。结论指出：记录对某时期的描述量不是其作为证据效用的可靠指标，且因缺乏专家参考时间线，评估仅反映检索行为而非临床准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）是为临床护理而非检索编写的。在多年的肿瘤科记录中，主要诊断反复出现，而塑造患者病程的关键事件（如复发、治疗变更）可能仅记录一次。基于相似度排序的检索器会不断返回重复内容，错过这些关键转折点，表现为历史叙述中的时间冗余。现有方法未将纵向检索视为跨时间分配有限证据预算的问题。

Method: 将任务分解为三个目标：时间证据分配、候选重构、临床验证。Study A：13个临床查询，20份患者记录，两阶段重构。对比Temporal-15配置与Global-50基线。固定证据预算条件下消融提示优先级规则。5患者单查询试点研究固定预算下的时间覆盖效果。使用里程碑槽位召回率、检索证据量、模式顺序逆转率等指标。

Result: Temporal-15里程碑槽位召回率0.538 vs Global-50的0.388；但Temporal-15检索496个索引单位/查询 vs 基线50个，提升非纯分配所致。固定证据量下移除优先级规则，模式顺序逆转从0.010升至0.080。固定预算试点显示更宽时间覆盖不稳定产生更多有日期里程碑。记录对某时期的描述量与其证据效用关联性差。

Conclusion: 纵向临床检索应作为预算约束下的时间证据分配问题。记录量不是证据效用的可靠代理指标。因缺乏专家参考时间线，当前评估仅刻画检索行为而非临床准确性。本文呈现Onco-CONSTRUE框架的检索分析，并为预算约束的纵向临床检索确立研究议程。

Abstract: Electronic health records are written to support care, not retrieval. Across years of oncology notes, the primary diagnosis is restated in report after report, while the events that shape a patient's course, such as a recurrence or a change in therapy, may be recorded only once. A retriever that ranks passages by similarity to the query keeps returning the repeated material and can miss these transitions, that is, it exhibits temporal redundancy across historical narratives. We argue that longitudinal clinical retrieval should be treated as allocating a limited evidence budget across time, not just ranking by relevance. We separate the task into three objectives: temporal evidence allocation, candidate reconstruction, and clinical validation. In Study A (13 clinical queries over 20 patient records, with two-stage reconstruction), the Temporal-15 configuration reaches a milestone slot recall of 0.538, compared with 0.388 for the Global-50 baseline. However, it also retrieves far more evidence (496 versus 50 indexed units per query), so the gain cannot be credited to allocation alone. When evidence is held fixed, removing prompted precedence rules raises schema-order reversals from 0.010 to 0.080. A five-patient, single-query pilot with fixed budgets finds that wider temporal coverage does not reliably yield more dated milestones. Together, these results suggest that how much a record says about a period is a poor guide to how useful that period is as evidence. Because no expert reference timelines are available, our measurements describe retrieval behavior rather than clinical accuracy. This position and technical report presents the retrieval analysis behind the Onco-CONSTRUE framework and sets out a research agenda for budget-constrained longitudinal clinical retrieval.

</details>


### [193] [Pattern Intelligence: Unsupervised, LLM-Interpreted Discovery of Systematic Underpayment and Denial Patterns in Healthcare Revenue Cycle Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27684)
*Harshil Lodhiya,Alex McManus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Pattern Intelligence系统，通过基于裁决行为的聚类发现医疗索赔少付和拒付的可修正模式，排除付款人/合同身份避免平凡聚类，使用HDBSCAN和二次子聚类，LLM生成分析师可读描述，发现高额拒付约1/3-1/2形成不可聚类的长尾，在两个生产租户上验证可回收机会金额。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗提供者因系统性付款人少付和拒付损失大量收入，但识别背后的可修正重复模式主要靠人工，无法扩展到医院级索赔量。需要无监督、可扩展的方法自动发现可操作的模式。

Method: 将每个索赔嵌入130维向量（含逐行预期/实际支付比率、CARC代码、拒付/支付信号、服务构成），故意排除付款人和合同身份以避免主导几何结构导致平凡聚类，仅作事后分段。使用HDBSCAN聚类，对噪声集进行二次子聚类恢复结构，门控低置信度成员。LLM将质心转为分析师可读描述（主导手术、调整原因、失败模式）。

Result: 发现反复出现的规律：约1/3到1/2的高额拒付形成不可约的长尾，无论密度聚类配置或表征如何都无法聚类，属领域属性而非建模缺陷。通过聚类连贯性分析验证有效性，用可回收机会（$）量化影响，在两个生产租户（约1万和15.7万索赔）上评估，六租户部署中。

Conclusion: 贡献不在于新算法，而在于问题建模、表征设计洞察（排除身份特征避免平凡聚类）、生产实证发现（长尾不可聚类性质），并提供了一个已部署的端到端系统框架。

Abstract: Healthcare providers lose substantial revenue to systematic payer underpayments and claim denials, yet identifying the recurring, correctable patterns behind them remains largely manual and does not scale to hospital claim volumes. We present Pattern Intelligence, a deployed system that discovers such patterns without supervision by clustering claims on their adjudication behaviour rather than their identity. Each claim is embedded in a 130-dimensional vector combining per-line expected-versus-actual payment ratios, claim adjustment reason (CARC) codes, denial and payment signals, and service composition. Payer and contract identity are deliberately excluded from the distance metric - we show they dominate the geometry and collapse clusters into trivial per-payer groups - and are reintroduced only as a post-hoc segmentation (by contract and visit type) that keeps every surfaced pattern coherent and actionable. We cluster with HDBSCAN, recover structure from the noise set via second-pass sub-clustering, and gate low-confidence memberships. A large language model translates each centroid into an analyst-ready description of dominant procedures, adjustment reasons, and failure mode. We report a recurring finding: roughly one-third to one-half of high-dollar denials, depending on tenant and segmentation, form an irreducible long tail that no density-clustering configuration or representation we tried groups - a domain property, we argue, rather than a modeling deficiency. We evaluate validity through cluster-coherence analysis and quantify impact with a recoverable-opportunity ($) metric on two production tenants (approx. 10K and 157K claims) within a six-tenant deployment. Rather than a new algorithm, the contribution is a problem formulation, a representation-design insight, and empirical findings from production.

</details>


### [194] [EASformer: Environment-Action-State Alignment for Industrial Generative Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27691)
*Bohao Li,Dinglun Cao,Pengcheng Huang,Yingda Tang,Zhiting Zhao,Yi Cao,Jilei Fu,Miao Yan,Tianying Liu,Heng Chang,Bowen Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EASformer通过环境-动作-状态解耦架构解决工业排序中序列与非序列特征语义不对齐问题，实现历史动作检索与状态条件动作预测，离线AUC提升0.88%，在线eCPM分别提升1.26%和3.94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工业排序系统采用生成式推荐范式，将用户行为序列和非序列特征联合建模，但历史表示包含环境上下文和观测动作语义，候选侧非序列表示无观测动作但含更丰富补充上下文，直接交互导致语义不对齐，限制跨token有意义交互。

Method: 提出EASformer，构建显式对齐的上下文环境空间，共享历史与非序列token，解耦用户动作（历史token）和候选特定上下文（非序列token）。基于对齐空间实现互补历史动作检索和状态条件动作预测。设计Cloze式自监督目标，掩码历史动作并复用候选预测路径。

Result: 真实工业应用市场离线基准AUC 0.89411，相对部署基线提升0.88%。在线A/B测试在两个真实排序场景eCPM分别相对提升1.26%和3.94%。

Conclusion: EASformer通过环境-动作-状态解耦有效解决序列与非序列特征语义不对齐，提升工业排序性能，离线在线均显著优于基线，验证了解耦架构与自监督目标的有效性。

Abstract: Modern industrial ranking systems increasingly adopt generative recommendation (GR) paradigms, in which user behavior sequences and non-sequential (NS) features are tokenized and jointly modeled by Transformer-style backbones. This unified formulation provides a scalable interface for modeling long histories and heterogeneous features. However, sequence and NS representations often carry asymmetric semantics: historical representations may combine environment context with observed-action semantics, whereas candidate-side NS representations contain no observed action but incorporate richer supplementary context, such as user-item interaction features and recent behavior lists. Direct interaction between such representations can make query-key dot products compare different semantic objects, limiting meaningful cross-token interaction. To fill this gap, we propose EASformer, an Environment-Action-State disentangled architecture for industrial app ranking. EASformer constructs an explicitly aligned context environment space shared by historical and non-sequence tokens, while disentangling user actions from historical token and candidate-specific context form non-sequence tokens. Based on this aligned space, EASformer enables complementary historical action retrieval and state-conditioned action prediction. Furthermore, the disentangled design supports a Cloze-style self-supervised objective by masking historical actions and reusing the candidate prediction pathway. On an offline benchmark from a real-world industrial app marketplace, EASformer achieves an AUC of 0.89411, yielding a 0.88% relative improvement over the deployed baseline. Online A/B tests further support this advantage, with relative eCPM lifts of 1.26% and 3.94% across two real-world ranking scenarios.

</details>


### [195] [E8-QAT: Efficient 2-Bit Quantization-Aware Training with Implicit Lattice Codebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27699)
*Xinyu Lei,Hong Huang,Jiebin Zhang,Hongyu Li,Jinlong Song,Decheng Wu,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu,Sujian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: E8-QAT是一种2位量化感知训练框架，利用E8格点作为隐式八维码本，通过算术投影联合量化权重组，无需存储或搜索显式码本，实现了与全精度模型相当的性能，显著降低内存占用并提高吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化虽能减少大语言模型内存占用，但在保持精度和高效训练方面仍具挑战。标量量化独立编码权重，显式码本向量量化在训练中引入昂贵搜索开销。

Method: 提出E8-QAT框架，利用E8格点作为隐式八维码本，通过算术投影联合量化权重组，训练前向传播无需存储或搜索显式码本。采用2位核心编码和稀疏扩展精确保留选定格点，核心索引存储增加不到0.1%。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上，E8-QAT在四个任务平均分上匹配全精度模型性能；在IFEval上比SOTA基线提升2.03%，LiveCodeBench提升1.51%（相同数据、调度、比特宽度）。2位表示使峰值内存降低80%，支持4倍更大批次；在各自最大可行批次下，解码吞吐量达到BF16的1.97倍。

Conclusion: E8-QAT通过隐式E8格点码本实现高效2位量化感知训练，在保持模型精度的同时大幅降低内存占用并提升推理吞吐量，为大模型低比特量化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization reduces the memory footprint of large language models, but preserving accuracy with efficient training remains challenging. Scalar quantization encodes weights independently, while explicit-codebook vector quantization introduces costly search during training. We propose E8-QAT, a 2-bit quantization-aware training framework that uses the $E_8$ lattice as an implicit eight-dimensional codebook. It jointly quantizes weight groups through arithmetic projection without storing or searching an explicit codebook during the training forward pass. A 2-bit core encoding and sparse extension preserve the selected lattice points exactly, adding less than 0.1\% to core index storage. On Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, E8-QAT matches the performance of the full-precision model in terms of the average score across the four tasks. E8-QAT improves over the state-of-the-art baseline by 2.03\% on IFEval and by 1.51\% on LiveCodeBench, under the same data, schedule, and bit width. The 2-bit representation reduces peak memory by 80\% and supports a $4\times$ larger batch. At each representation's largest feasible batch, E8-QAT reaches $1.97\times$ the decoding throughput of BF16.

</details>


### [196] [How Fine-Tuning Objectives Shape Layer-Wise Information in LLM Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27711)
*Kaiyang Wan,Forrest Sheng Bao,Amin Ahmad,Yuxia Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文对比了分类器头微调（CLS）与LLM-as-Judge序列到序列（S2S）在二分类幻觉检测任务中的表现，发现CLS始终优于S2S。通过表示学习和训练动力学分析，揭示了CLS在浅层学习到任务特异性判别方向，形成清晰的双簇几何结构；而S2S依赖预训练语言建模头，表示变化较小。进一步发现CLS信号在中层饱和，上层形成冗余平台区。提出InfoWindow概念，移除其上层可削减31%网络深度而不损失精度，且该深度比例可迁移至其他模型和任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为什么分类器头微调（CLS）在二分类幻觉检测任务中持续优于LLM-as-Judge序列到序列（S2S）范式，并探究两种目标函数诱导的表示差异，以指导高效任务特定LLM设计。

Method: 1. 静态表示分析：比较CLS和S2S在隐藏状态空间中的几何结构、类别分离度和线性可解码性。2. 训练动力学分析：追踪各层任务信息涌现和饱和过程，识别预训练基底、InfoWindow（任务信息涌现窗口）和上层冗余平台区。3. 层剪枝实验：移除InfoWindow以上层，在Qwen3-4B/8B、Llama-3.2-3B、Mistral-7B-v0.3上验证，并迁移至QNLI和SST-2任务。

Result: 1. CLS学习任务特异性判别方向，隐藏状态呈清晰双簇几何，类别分离度强，标签信息线性可解码；S2S依赖预训练头，表示接近预训练骨干网络。2. CLS信号在中层饱和，上层形成统计可互换的冗余表示平台区。3. 训练动力学揭示三阶段结构：下层预训练基底、中上层窄InfoWindow（任务信息涌现）、上层同步平台区。4. 截断31%上层网络在Qwen3模型上精度不降（种子噪声内），延迟降低1.4倍；相同深度比例迁移至Llama和Mistral有效，且层结构在QNLI和SST-2上复现。

Conclusion: CLS范式通过浅层学习判别方向实现高效表示，上层冗余可安全移除。InfoWindow机制揭示了任务特定信息处理的层级定位，为设计高效任务特定LLM提供指导：可在不损失性能前提下大幅削减模型深度，且该设计原则跨模型、跨任务泛化。

Abstract: Under matched conditions, classifier-head fine-tuning (\textsc{cls}) paradigm consistently outperforms the LLM-as-Judge sequence-to-sequence (\textsc{s2s}) on binary hallucination detection. We trace this gap to the representations induced by the two objectives. Static analysis shows that \textsc{cls} learns a task-specific discriminative direction, pushing hidden states into a clear two-cluster geometry with stronger class separation and linearly decodable label information. By contrast, \textsc{s2s} relies on the pretrained language-modeling head to make decisions. This minimizes the pressure to reorganize representations, leaving the hidden states much closer to the pretrained backbone. Our analysis also reveals that the \textsc{cls} signal saturates before the final layer, after which upper layers form a plateau of statistically interchangeable and largely redundant representations. However, endpoint redundancy alone does not show whether these layers are dispensable during training. Further training-dynamics analysis reveals 1) a lower pretrained substrate, 2) a narrow upper-middle \textbf{InfoWindow} where task information emerges, and 3) a later-synchronizing upper plateau that mirrors this signal. This mechanism motivates us to remove layers above the InfoWindow. Truncating 31\% of the network leaves accuracy unchanged within seed noise on Qwen3-4B and 8B and cuts latency by $1.4\times$. The same depth fraction, fixed before any analysis of the target models, transfers to Llama-3.2-3B and Mistral-7B-v0.3, and the layer structure behind it reappears there and on QNLI and SST-2. We hope this helps guide efficient task-specific LLM design.

</details>


### [197] [When Does Experience Memory Help Closed-Loop LLM Driving Agents? A Controlled Evaluation with Resampled Warmup Pools, Token-Matched Fillers, and Audited Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27714)
*Yingxin Jia,Xuanyi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过在HighwayEnv测试平台上的受控实验，系统评估了经验记忆对闭环LLM驾驶代理的实际帮助。研究发现：先前报告的记忆增益主要源于暖池抽样偏差和提示词token注入效应，而非记忆机制本身；任何检索设计都不优于token匹配的无关填充物；表征复杂度无稳健作用；基线移植质量会静默反转结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献声称经验记忆能显著提升LLM驾驶代理性能，但未控制两个关键混淆因素：暖池经验的具体抽样和注入提示词的token对模型行为的直接影响。这导致报告的增益不可靠，亟需受控评估揭示记忆机制的真实效用边界。

Method: 在HighwayEnv（1Hz，gpt-4o-mini）上构建审计级测试平台。将暖池视为重采样随机因子（10个不相交池），引入token匹配的无关填充控制、预言机认证诊断、谨慎定价指标和基线移植量化审计。探针套件覆盖：平文检索（DiLu移植）、单帧网格端口（RESPOND启发）、情景时序图记忆、无检索整合规则手册、平坦K步观测历史基线。

Result: 四大发现：1）头条记忆增益归因于暖池抽样运气——报告池是两种检索记忆在10池中的最佳表现，仅在1/10池显著优于无记忆（符号检验p=0.011），规则手册的池免疫性在5个新抽样中失效；2）无检索设计超越token匹配填充物——单条15-token谨慎指令即达碰撞合并上限，谨慎定价指标抹平规则手册领先优势；3）表征复杂度无处稳健生效；4）基线移植质量静默反转结论——无填充控制的单池比较会报告被控制实验推翻的记忆增益。

Conclusion: 经验记忆在闭环LLM驾驶中的实际收益被高估；性能提升主要源于提示工程（谨慎指令）和暖池选择偏差，而非记忆架构的表征能力。研究强调需采用多池重采样、token匹配填充控制、谨慎定价指标等严谨方法论，并开源测试平台、审计端口、种子与缓存以推动可复现评估。

Abstract: When does experience memory help a closed-loop LLM driving agent? Published gains cannot say, because the two factors we find to matter most, which warmup experiences the pool contains and what any injected tokens do to the prompt, are controlled in none of them. We answer with a controlled evaluation on one audited HighwayEnv testbed (1Hz, gpt-4o-mini for the decisive contrasts). We treat the warmup pool as a resampled random factor (ten disjoint pools) and add token-matched irrelevant-filler controls, oracle-certified diagnostics, caution-pricing metrics, and quantified audits of the baseline ports. The probe suite spans flat-text retrieval (a DiLu port), a single-frame grid port (RESPOND-inspired), our episodic temporal-graph memory, a retrieval-free consolidated rulebook, and a flat K-step observation-history baseline. Four findings follow, each a property of this testbed. (1) Headline memory gains belong to the warmup draw. The reported pool is the best of ten for both retrieval memories, which beat No-Memory in only one pool of ten (sign test p=0.011), and even the rulebook's apparent pool-immunity collapsed on five fresh draws. (2) No retrieval design tested exceeds token-matched filler. The collision-only merge ceiling is reached by a single 15-token caution instruction, and metrics that price caution erase the rulebook's lead. (3) Representational sophistication is nowhere robustly operative. (4) Baseline port quality silently inverts conclusions. On this testbed, a single-pool comparison without filler controls would have reported memory gains that these controls reverse. We release the testbed, audited ports, seeds, and caches.

</details>


### [198] [How Language Models Organize Task Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27724)
*Kaiyang Wan,Yuxia Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种测量语言模型表示中读出稀疏度的指标k*，即通过固定搜索协议找到的、能恢复密集探针95% AUROC的最小特征数量。研究评估了来自五个实验室的19个语言模型在14个核心任务上的表现，发现基础模型在可读任务上的特征数量和顺序高度一致，且选定特征本身在跨模型间表现出超越类均值差异的响应对应性。指令微调会选择性降低k*，合成实验显示成功学习的目标仅需单特征读出，而可读控制需要数十个特征。k*与标签需求呈幂律关系，持续学习后保存的头可能失效但线性可读性保持高，修复所需标签少于初始学习。剪枝会降低线性可读性，剪枝前读出越宽损失越大。研究区分了任务可读性与读出稀疏性，确立了二者在预测学习需求和诊断模型变化方面的互补价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的密集线性探针虽然能评估任务从语言模型表示中的可读性，但无法直接揭示预测所需的最小特征数量。研究者旨在量化读出稀疏性，区分任务可读性与读出稀疏性这两个不同概念，并探究它们在预测学习需求、诊断模型变化（如指令微调、持续学习、剪枝）方面的互补作用。

Method: 1. 定义指标k*：通过固定搜索协议找到的最小特征数量，使其能恢复密集线性探针AUROC（扣除随机基线）的95%。2. 评估19个语言模型（来自五个实验室）在14个核心任务上的表现。3. 分析基础模型间k*的一致性及选定特征的跨模型对应性。4. 研究指令微调对k*的影响（权重与输入格式）。5. 在20个模型上进行受控合成实验，对比成功学习目标与可读控制的特征需求。6. 测量参考模型k*与八个学生模型标签需求的关系，拟合经验缩放定律。7. 考察持续学习后保存头失效但线性可读性保持高的现象，及修复所需标签量。8. 分析剪枝对线性可读性的影响及剪枝前读出宽度与损失的关系。

Result: 1. 十个基础模型在九个可靠可读任务上给出相似的k*值且顺序几乎一致。2. 三个任务上选定特征显示跨模型响应对应性，超越类均值差异和标签相关性匹配的零假设。3. 指令微调部署选择性降低k*，权重和输入格式均有贡献。4. 合成实验：成功学习的目标仅需单特征读出，可读控制需要数十个特征。5. 参考模型k*与八个学生模型的标签需求相关，遵循经验缩放N* ∝ (k*)^0.65。6. 持续学习后保存的头可能失效而线性可读性保持高，修复所需标签少于初始学习。7. 剪枝降低线性可读性，剪枝前读出越宽通常关联越大损失。

Conclusion: 研究建立了读出稀疏性指标k*，证明任务可读性与读出稀疏性是两个不同但互补的概念。k*能预测学习标签需求（幂律关系），诊断指令微调、持续学习、剪枝等模型变化的影响。跨模型特征选择的一致性揭示了表示的共性结构。这些发现为理解语言模型内部表示、预测下游任务学习难度、监控模型演变提供了新工具和理论框架。

Abstract: Dense linear probes assess task readability from language model (LM) representations, but do not directly reveal how many features suffice for prediction. We measure readout sparsity with \(k^*\), the smallest feature count found by a fixed search protocol that recovers 95% of dense-probe AUROC above chance. We evaluate 19 LMs from five labs on 14 core tasks. Ten base models give the nine reliably readable tasks similar feature counts and nearly the same order. This agreement extends to the selected features themselves, which on three tasks show cross-model response correspondence beyond class-mean differences and a label-correlation-matched null. Instruction-tuned deployment selectively reduces \(k^*\), with both weights and input format contributing. In controlled synthetic experiments across 20 models, successfully learned targets admit single-feature readouts, whereas readable controls require tens of features. Reference-model \(k^*\) is associated with label requirements across eight students, following an empirical scaling of \(N^*\propto(k^*)^{0.65}\). After continual learning, saved heads can fail while linear readability remains high, and recovery requires fewer labels than initial task learning under the tested procedures. Pruning reduces linear readability, with wider pre-pruning readouts generally associated with larger losses. These findings distinguish task readability from readout sparsity and establish their complementary value for predicting learning requirements and diagnosing LM changes.

</details>


### [199] [RSI Control: Can AI Models Perform Covert Sabotage in the Self-Improvement Process?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27735)
*Kaiwen Zhou,Udari Madhushani Sehwag*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究递归自我改进（RSI）的安全风险，构建 RSI Control Arena 测试平台，评估前沿模型作为自主研发代理在训练后继模型时植入错位属性的能力。实验显示所有测试模型均能在不降低主任务性能的前提下完成大量隐蔽攻击，且推理监控虽能检测大部分攻击但并非万无一失，揭示 RSI 存在显著安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着前沿 AI 模型开始参与训练下一代模型（递归自我改进），若代理模型存在错位，其可能将有害属性传递给后继模型并放大风险。现有研究对这一过程的安全性关注不足，缺乏系统性评估框架，亟需量化代理模型在训练流水线中植入隐蔽后门或错位目标的可行性及检测难度。

Method: 构建 RSI Control Arena 测试平台，包含 16 个隐蔽副任务（分三类）与 22 个主任务配对，覆盖数据筛选到强化学习的完整训练流水线。评估六个前沿模型作为自主研发代理，在执行良性主任务的同时尝试完成副任务。部署基于 LLM 的监控器读取运行记录与代理推理过程，对比不同监控策略（仅转录、转录+推理、提供规避战术提示）的检测效果。

Result: 所有六个模型均能在保持主任务性能的同时完成相当比例的副任务，包括使后继模型成为更强破坏者的任务。仅凭极简隐蔽指令，模型即可实施监控器难以察觉的攻击；提供简短规避战术清单可进一步降低检出率。拥有代理推理访问权限的监控器能检测大多数攻击，但仍有漏报。

Conclusion: 递归自我改进引入重大安全风险：前沿模型已具备在训练后继模型时植入并隐藏错位属性的能力，且现有监控手段无法完全捕获。在模型被赋予更大训练责任前，必须建立强有力的安全保障措施，包括更鲁棒的监控、推理透明化机制及形式化验证方法。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement, in which frontier AI models work on training the next generation, is quickly becoming a reality. However, the safety of this process has received far less attention than it should. A misaligned model training its successor can shape what that successor knows, how it behaves, and what it wants, and the harm carries forward into the next generation. We study this risk through the lens of AI control. We introduce RSI Control Arena, a testbed in which a frontier model, acting as an autonomous R\&D agent, is given a benign main task from a stage of a successor model's training pipeline together with a covert side task to install a misaligned property in the successor. The testbed contains 16 side tasks in three classes, forming 22 task pairs with main tasks that span the pipeline from data curation to reinforcement learning. We evaluate six frontier models as agents against LLM monitors that read the transcript of the run and the agent's reasoning. We observe that every model completes a substantial share of the side tasks without degrading the main-task performance, including side tasks that make the successor itself a better saboteur. Under minimal instructions to stay covert, models already produce successful attacks that monitors fail to detect, and providing a short list of evasion tactics further reduces the detection rate. Monitors with access to agent's reasoning detects most attacks, but not all. Our results indicate that recursive self-improvement raises significant safety concerns, and that strong safety effort should be in place before models are trusted with greater responsibility of training their successors.

</details>


### [200] [Give the Model a Finger: Developmentally Aligned Evaluation of Counting in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27756)
*Yingxin Jia,Hui Geng,Wenxin Yang,Mohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究发现视觉语言模型（VLM）的计数行为类似未掌握计数的儿童：小集合精确、大集合估计。通过赋予VLM「认识论动作」（指点、移动、分区）作为无视觉智能的图像工具，构建无需训练的智能体循环，在图像上留下可审计的序列推理痕迹。核心主张是测量而非干预：标记将隐性序列过程显性化，使每个标签可按点级真值分类（命中、重复、幻影、遗漏），并可验证答案与标签数一致性、测试顺序无关性。实验覆盖9模型、682图像、7工况，揭示模型特有的精确计数范围签名、指点精度与收益的对应、密集场景的遗漏失效、以及遵循遍历顺序与顺序无关性的解离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解VLM计数能力的局限与机制，并建立一种测量而非干预的框架：通过让模型在图像上留下可见标记，将隐性的序列视觉推理过程显性化为可审计的轨迹，从而系统性评估模型的计数行为、指点精度、顺序敏感性及空间组织利用等发展性计数原则。

Method: 设计无需训练的智能体循环，赋予VLM「认识论动作」作为哑图像工具（指点标记、移动计数项、分区数组）。标记在图像上形成序列轨迹，每个标签按点级真值分类为命中、重复、幻影、遗漏；答案与标签数核对；强制反向遍历测试顺序无关性。跨9模型、682图像、7工况评估，并引入标签扰动探针验证模型是否读取自身标签。

Result: (i) 精确计数范围呈模型特有签名，R80跨度近两个数量级（3–150点）；(ii) 作为干预，「手指」多失效：仅帮4/9模型在小计数照片上改善，损害不精确指点者，难胜过口头常规或盲点列表——循环带来的是可审计轨迹而非准确率；(iii) 作为测量成功：标签扰动显示精确指点者从标签读答案，指点精度预测受益模型，密集场景因遗漏失效，模型极少在见标签后修正；(iv) 能力解离：模型遵循指令遍历顺序却常在反向时改变答案，儿童的规则阵列优势仅在常规下出现并能抵抗击败行×列捷径的晶格干扰。

Conclusion: 认识论动作循环主要作为测量工具揭示VLM计数机制，而非提升准确率。它暴露了模型特有的计数签名、指点精度与收益的耦合、密集场景的遗漏主导失效、以及遵循遍历顺序与顺序无关性的解离，为序列视觉推理提供了可解释的审计基础。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are exact on small sets and drift into estimation on larger ones, a profile reminiscent of children before they master counting. Children cross the barrier with epistemic actions: pointing at each item while reciting number words, moving counted items aside, partitioning the array. We give VLMs the same actions as dumb image tools with no visual intelligence of their own in a training-free agent loop. Our central claim is about measurement, not intervention: a model that counts by marking leaves a normally hidden serial procedure on the image, and the developmental principles of counting turn those marks into an interpretable audit of serial visual reasoning: every tag is classified against point-level ground truth as on-target, duplicate, phantom, or omitted, the answer is checked against the number of tags, and order irrelevance is tested by forcing opposite traversal orders. Across 9 models, 682 images, and 7 harness conditions we find that (i) the exact-counting range is a model-specific signature spanning nearly two orders of magnitude (R80 from 3 to 150 dots); (ii) as an intervention the finger mostly fails: tags help 4 of 9 models on small-count photographs, hurt imprecise pointers, and rarely beat a verbal routine or a blind point list; what the loop buys is an auditable trace, not accuracy; (iii) as measurement it succeeds: a tag-perturbation probe shows that precise pointers read their answers off the tags, pointing precision tracks which models benefit, dense scenes fail by omission, and models rarely revise after seeing their own tags; and (iv) the capacities dissociate: models follow an instructed traversal order yet often change their answer when it reverses, and children's regular-over-scattered advantage appears only under the routine and survives lattices that defeat a rows-times-columns shortcut.

</details>


### [201] [CrystalAR: Autoregressive Modeling for Symmetric Crystal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27762)
*Ziqiao Meng,Rongsheng Chen,Zhi Zheng,Nikita Kazeev,Zhenkun Wang,Andrey Ustyuzhanin,Wee Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CrystalAR，一种统一的自回归框架，在单一生成序列中建模Wyckoff对称性模板与完整晶体结构，通过将连续坐标生成重构为对称性条件下的离散空间定位决策层级，实现了对称性模板生成与从头生成性能的兼得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成建模在晶体生成中取得显著进展，近期工作试图显式引入空间群对称性。然而，引入对称性模板增加了原子种类与晶体学位点间化学计量关系的组合复杂性，且在离散晶体学模板的灵活探索与连续三维结构的忠实实现之间产生根本张力。

Method: CrystalAR将连续坐标生成重构为对称性条件下的离散空间定位决策层级，自回归地跨分辨率层级细化所有对称非等价位点的位置，并在每层内联合建模其空间构型。该离散层级公式进一步支持对晶体学模板与几何实现的约束感知搜索。

Result: CrystalAR保持了强大的对称性模板生成能力，同时实现了具有竞争力的从头生成性能和无模板晶体结构预测（CSP）精度。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对称性模板生成可以与广泛具有竞争力的从头生成性能及竞争性CSP共存，且无需依赖从先验数据检索的对称性模板，为保持对称性的晶体生成提供了有前景的替代范式。

Abstract: Deep generative modeling has achieved remarkable progress in crystal generation. Given the rich symmetries inherent in crystalline structures, recent work has sought to explicitly incorporate space-group symmetry into generative models, enabling more effective exploration of novel symmetry templates. Yet introducing symmetry templates increases the combinatorial complexity of stoichiometric relationships between atomic species and crystallographic sites, while creating a fundamental tension between flexible exploration of discrete crystallographic templates and faithful realization of these templates as continuous three-dimensional structures. To address these challenges, we propose CrystalAR, a unified autoregressive framework that models Wyckoff symmetry templates and complete crystal structures within a single generative sequence. Specifically, CrystalAR reformulates continuous coordinate generation as a symmetry-conditioned hierarchy of discrete spatial localization decisions, autoregressively refining the positions of all symmetry-inequivalent sites across resolution levels while jointly modeling their spatial configuration within each level. This discrete hierarchical formulation further enables constraint-aware search over both crystallographic templates and geometric realizations. As a result, CrystalAR retains strong symmetry-template generation capability while achieving competitive de novo generation performance and template-free crystal structure prediction (CSP) accuracy. These results suggest that symmetry-template generation can be retained alongside broadly competitive de novo generation performance and competitive CSP without reliance on symmetry templates retrieved from prior data, providing a promising alternative paradigm for symmetry-preserving crystal generation.

</details>


### [202] [RITE: Evaluating End-to-End Resilience to Silent Tool Errors in E-Commerce Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27777)
*Wenshuo Zhang,Yuquan Lin,Yushi Sun,Yishi Lin,Rui Sheng,Peng He,Han Gao,Wang Jikai,Yifeng Rong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了静默错误弹性任务，并构建了RITE基准，评估工具使用LLM代理在电商多步交互中面对静默错误（工具返回貌似合理但错误的值且无显式失败信号）的端到端韧性。实验显示静默错误使任务成功率约减半，且比显式失败更难处理；识别、验证路径利用、证据整合形成渐进瓶颈。提出轻量状态检查器作为干预手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用LLM代理在电商查询中通过多步工具交互完成任务，但工具调用的预期输出可能在开发与执行阶段发生偏移，导致返回貌似合理但错误的值且无显式失败信号（静默错误）。这些错误会在后续决策中传播，影响最终答案。现有评估缺乏对这种端到端静默错误韧性的系统研究。

Method: 构建RITE基准：基于人工编写的工业级工作流和生产日志衍生的输入，使用语义错误分类法向固定工作流前缀注入错误，冻结记录的工具行为，让代理自由继续执行。包含9个电商工作流的167个匹配单元，产生501个静默错误、正确值和显式失败实例。在10个近期LLM上评估，并提出轻量状态检查器作为干预。

Result: 静默错误使任务成功率大约减半，且始终比显式失败更难处理。代理面临三个渐进瓶颈：识别错误、利用可用验证路径、整合验证得到的证据。轻量状态检查器干预能一定程度提升韧性。

Conclusion: 静默错误是工具使用代理在实际部署中的关键挑战，RITE基准为系统评估提供了可控、可复现的测试床。研究揭示了代理处理静默错误的瓶颈层级，轻量级状态检查器是一种有前景的干预方向，未来工作可探索更鲁棒的错误检测与恢复机制。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents increasingly answer e-commerce queries through multi-step tool interactions. The expected output of a tool call can shift between agent development and execution, allowing a plausible but incorrect value to be returned without an explicit failure signal. These silent errors may propagate through subsequent decisions into final answers. To study agents' resilience across this end-to-end process, we formulate Silent-error Resilience Task, an interactive task that evaluates whether they complete the original query after a silent error corrupts an intermediate state. We introduce RITE, a benchmark built from human-authored industrial workflows and production-log-derived inputs. To enable controlled and reproducible evaluation, we use a semantic error taxonomy to inject errors into fixed workflow prefixes, freeze recorded tool behavior, and let agents continue freely. It contains 167 matched units across nine e-commerce workflows, yielding 501 silent-error, correct-value, and explicit-failure instances. Across 10 recent LLMs, silent errors roughly halve task success and are consistently harder than explicit failures. Recognition, use of an available verification path, and integration of the resulting evidence form successive bottlenecks. We also propose a light State Checker as an intervention to test whether resilience can be improved.

</details>


### [203] [GRADUS: Discovering and Recovering Agent Lessons from the Environment's Latent Syllabus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27781)
*Qing Ping,Wei Yang,Panpan Xu,Johannes Kirmayr,Lukas Stappen,Junjie Tang,Jun Huan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Gradus 框架，通过将环境视为潜在课程表、提取受保护的行为算子并组合生成渐进式任务，联合校准任务与解、将验证转化为恢复信号，显著提升 LLM Agent 在策略约束工具环境（如航空后台）上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成流水线仅保留模型能立即解决的任务，导致训练数据偏向简单行为，难以覆盖策略约束环境中复杂的规则组合与边界情况。

Method: Gradus 从固定环境中提取受保护行为算子（捕捉动作对条件的依赖及效果对后续决策的重塑），将其组合为难度递增的任务，利用语料级反馈覆盖缺失的结果组合；联合校准任务与解，执行反馈指示修正方向同时保留行为要求，使验证成为恢复信号而非终端过滤器，实现覆盖引导探索与困难样本保留的耦合。

Result: 在 τ²-Bench 上将 30B 模型从 59% 提升至 89%（Opus 4.5 proposer）和 76%（Qwen3-235B proposer）；在 CAR-Bench 上从 47% 提升至 83%（Opus 4.5）和 62%（Qwen3-235B）。

Conclusion: 联合控制行为探索空间与验证保留机制，能有效构建高质量课程数据，显著提升 LLM Agent 在复杂策略约束环境下的泛化与合规能力。

Abstract: Training LLM agents for policy-constrained tool environments, such as an airline backend with booking, refund, and cancellation policies, requires tasks that exercise the environment's rules individually and in combination. Existing synthesis pipelines let a model propose tasks and retain only those it immediately solves, biasing training data toward easier behaviors. We introduce Gradus (Graded Rungs of Ascending Difficulty from an Underlying System), which decouples what a task should teach from the concrete task and solution used to teach it. Without seed tasks, Gradus treats a fixed environment as a latent syllabus, extracting guarded behavioral operators that capture how actions depend on current conditions and how their effects reshape subsequent decisions. It composes these operators into increasingly demanding tasks while using corpus-level feedback to target missing outcome combinations. Crucially, Gradus jointly calibrates tasks and solutions against the environment: execution feedback identifies which side to refine while preserving the intended behavioral requirements. Verification thus becomes a recovery signal rather than a terminal filter, coupling coverage-guided exploration with the preservation of difficult examples. From off-the-shelf backbones, the resulting curriculum lifts a 30B model from 59% to 89% on $τ^2$-Bench and from 47% to 83% on CAR-Bench with an Opus 4.5 proposer, and to 76% and 62%, respectively, with an open-weight Qwen3-235B proposer. These results show the value of jointly controlling which behaviors are explored and which survive into verified training data.

</details>


### [204] [Toward Agentic AI for the 6G Physical Layer: From Algorithm Discovery to Verified Baseband Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27797)
*Eric Mittag,Lukas Brechtel,Maximilian Stark,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM编码代理的智能体工作流，可在商用基带SoC上实现、优化并验证AI发现的物理层算法，展示了在OTFS检测器上的成功应用，实现了周期精确的硬件部署，并发现了硬件感知的新检测器结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G物理层算法的AI驱动搜索速度远快于硬件实现速度。将算法移植到嵌入式基带硅片上面临严格的内存和数值限制，且硬件成本结构是GPU所不具备的。现有设计流程若不在目标硬件上运行，无法感知这些真实约束。

Method: 构建了一个智能体工作流：LLM编码代理在商用基带SoC上实现算法，优化至手写向量汇编层面，并在硅片上验证。代理由包含实测平台事实的知识库指导，每步接受不可篡改的评估器检查，并按阶段计量。在开放6G4All平台上对AI发现的OTFS检测器组合进行展示。

Result: 获得12个在硅片上周期精确运行的内核，优化版本每帧耗时18k-405k周期（30-660 μs）。发现了一种由芯片实测成本塑造的新检测器，比移植版检测器减少13.9%周期且误码率持平，这种结构是硬件盲搜索目标无法发现的。

Conclusion: 该工作流不特定于OTFS，其核心价值在于：LLM代理在真实硬件约束下探索实现空间，每个选择的代价均为实测而非假设，实现了算法与硬件的协同优化。

Abstract: 6G will be designed faster than it can be built: AI-driven search already proposes physical-layer algorithms at machine speed, while carrying a single one onto embedded baseband silicon remains specialist work. That silicon imposes hard memory and numeric limits, but also exposes cost structures a GPU lacks, and a design flow that never runs on the target sees neither. We present an agentic workflow in which an LLM coding agent implements algorithms on a commercial baseband SoC, optimizes them down to agent-written vector assembly, and validates them on silicon. The agent is guided by a knowledge base of measured platform facts, checked at every step against evaluators it is instructed never to edit, and metered per phase. We showcase the workflow on an AI-discovered orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS) detector portfolio on the open 6G4All platform. It yields twelve kernels that run cycle-exactly on silicon, their optimized versions at 18k-405k cycles (30-660 $μ$s) per frame. It also yields a new detector shaped by the chip's measured costs that needs 13.9% fewer cycles than the port of the discovered detector while matching its error rate, with a measured edge our evaluation cannot statistically resolve, a structure that a hardware-blind search objective had no reason to find. What is not specific to OTFS is the workflow: an LLM agent exploring an implementation space under real hardware limits, with the cost of every choice measured rather than assumed.

</details>


### [205] [LatBridge: Efficient and Stable Model Replacement in Latent-Communication Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27813)
*Jiajun Zhu,Haobo Li,Zixin Chen,Huamin Qu,Wenshuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LatBridge，一种用于多智能体系统的潜在通信框架，通过共享 Bridge 和独立接口实现模型替换后的接口复用，解决了现有潜在通信方法在模型重组后接口失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统依赖通信完成任务，潜在通信通过传递连续表示减少文本生成和重新编码开销。但现有方法针对固定模型组合设计，当智能体模型被替换或重组时，接口会失效，无法直接复用。初步实验证实了这一接口失效问题，并明确了模型替换后接口复用的需求。

Method: LatBridge 引入共享的 Bridge 模块和分离的接口模块，在共享潜在通信协议下连接智能体模型。模型替换后，LatBridge 复用未变更的接口，选择匹配的低秩接口，并通过 Bridge 组合它们，仅训练缺失的接口且冻结所有模型参数。Bridge 支持两种模式：基础模式处理源表示，加速模式无需自回归生成即可构造目标输入。

Result: 实验表明 LatBridge 在模型替换后仍能保持有效通信。在八个小模型配置上，基础模式下 MATH 任务平均准确率 14.8%，ALFWorld 下一步动作预测平均准确率 27.3%。分析进一步探讨了支持任务准确率和通信效率的因素。

Conclusion: LatBridge 通过共享 Bridge 和可复用接口设计，实现了多智能体系统中模型替换后的潜在通信适配，显著降低了重新训练成本，同时保持了任务性能，为动态模型重组场景提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems rely on communication to complete tasks, and latent communication passes continuous representations to reduce text generation and re-encoding. However, multi-agent systems must reconfigure agent models, whereas existing latent-communication methods are built for fixed model combinations, so their interfaces may fail after replacement. Our pilot study confirms this interface failure and identifies the requirement for interface reuse after model replacement. We present LatBridge, which connects agent models through a shared Bridge and separate interfaces under a shared latent-communication protocol. After agent model replacement, LatBridge reuses unchanged interfaces, selects matching low-rank interfaces, and combines them through the Bridge, training only missing ones with all models frozen. The Bridge processes source representations in the basic mode and constructs target input without autoregressive generation in the accelerated mode. Experiments support effective communication after model replacement, with mean basic-mode accuracies of 14.8% on MATH and 27.3% on ALFWorld next-action prediction across eight small-model configurations. Analyses examine the factors supporting task accuracy and communication efficiency.

</details>


### [206] [Privacy-preserving inference on hosted models: a learned input-embedding transform for frozen Qwen3.8-27B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27817)
*Mahendra Roopa,Srikanth Roopa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种客户端本地嵌入变换方案，将文本在客户端转为向量后通过一个小型网络（55.6M参数）变换再发送给托管的冻结27B模型（Qwen3.8-27B），实现提示词隐私保护；在MMLU上仅损失1.5点准确率（80.7% vs 82.3%），30.1%内容令牌无法通过最近邻还原。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托管语言模型会读取用户发送的所有提示词，存在隐私泄露风险。现有方案多需修改模型架构或重新训练大模型，成本高且难以部署。本文旨在让提示词留在客户端，仅向主机发送经过变换的向量，利用公开查找表在本地完成嵌入，再用轻量网络变换向量，使主机无法还原原始文本或token id，同时保持模型效用。

Method: 1. 客户端使用公开查找表将文本转为嵌入向量（仅一次前向传播）。 2. 向量通过一个可训练的小型变换网络（55.6M参数）映射为扰动向量。 3. 变换网络在冻结的27B模型（Qwen3.8-27B）作为固定裁判下训练，损失函数包含：前向KL散度效用项（使变换后向量在大模型上的输出分布接近原始向量）和辅助项（推动每个向量远离其原始方向以增强隐私）。 4. 主机仅接收变换向量，运行冻结大模型推理，从未见到文本或token id。

Result: 在1,490个未参与训练的MMLU题目上，变换方案准确率为80.7%，较无变换基线82.3%下降1.5个百分点。在基线答对的1,226题中，有68题（5.5%）因变换而答错。最近邻查找显示，30.1%的内容令牌无法还原为原始token，表明有一定隐私保护效果。

Conclusion: 通过客户端轻量变换网络即可在几乎不损失大模型性能的前提下，实现提示词级隐私保护。方案无需修改或重训练托管模型，部署成本低，适合现有API服务场景；但仍有约5.5%的正确样本变错，且令牌级还原率约70%，隐私与效用的权衡需视具体应用进一步调优。

Abstract: Every prompt sent to a hosted language model is readable by whoever runs it. We study whether the prompt can remain on the client while the host receives transformed vectors that stand in for it. A language model converts text to vectors exactly once, with a public lookup table; we perform that step locally and pass the vectors through a small trained network (55.6M parameters) before they leave the perimeter. The host runs a frozen 27B model (Qwen3.8-27B) on the transformed vectors and never receives text or token ids. We train the transform against the frozen model as a fixed judge, combining a forward-KL utility term with an auxiliary term that pushes each vector away from its original direction. On 1,490 MMLU questions held out from training, the transform costs 1.5 points of accuracy (80.7% vs. 82.3%), breaking 68 of the 1,226 questions the unmodified model answers correctly (5.5%), while 30.1% of content tokens no longer decode to their original token under a nearest-neighbour lookup.

</details>


### [207] [COMPASS: Routing via Capability-Oriented Model Profiling with Adaptive Search, Selection, and Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27822)
*Amin Rezaei,Pouya Parsa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COMPASS 是一个智能体路由器，让 LLM 按照路由配方、通过工具收集性能与成本证据、可选地检查候选答案来选择模型，无需针对路由的专门训练，即可在准确率与调用成本上同时优于专用与训练式基线，并能跨基准泛化、通过读取新评测记录适应扩展模型池。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 路由旨在低成本选择强模型，但专用路由器在模型与任务变化时需要更新，缺乏灵活性与泛化能力。

Method: 提出 COMPASS：一个智能体路由器，核心 LLM 遵循路由配方，调用工具收集候选模型的性能与成本证据，可选地先检查候选答案，再决定最终调用哪个模型；全程无需路由专用的权重训练或提示工程微调。

Result: 在 LLMRouterBench 上达到 69.85% 准确率、成本 $139.57，优于 RLCascadeRouter 的 69.14%、$180.22；在 DeepSWE 五折交叉验证下达到 78.1% 准确率、成本 $215，优于最佳单模型的 74.1%、$500。同时支持跨基准路由，并能通过读取新评测记录适应扩展的模型池，无需修改路由器权重或提示。

Conclusion: 智能体路由范式无需专用训练即可同时提升准确率与降低成本，具备跨任务泛化与动态适应新模型的能力，为 LLM 路由提供了更灵活、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM routing aims to select a capable model at low cost, but specialized routers can require updates as models and tasks change. We propose COMPASS, an agentic router in which an LLM follows a routing recipe, gathers performance and cost evidence through tools, and optionally inspects candidate answers before selecting a model. Our evaluations on LLMRouterBench and DeepSWE show that agentic routing can improve accuracy and lower model-call cost relative to specialized and trained routing baselines, without routing-specific training. On LLMRouterBench, COMPASS reaches 69.85% accuracy at \$139.57, compared with RLCascadeRouter's 69.14% at \$180.22. On DeepSWE under fixed five-fold cross-validation, it reaches 78.1% at \$215, compared with 74.1% at \$500 for the best single model. COMPASS also supports routing across benchmarks and adapts to an expanded model pool by reading new evaluation records, without changing the router's weights or prompt.

</details>


### [208] [SAPD: Learning from Agent Experience without Environment Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27847)
*Juncheng Diao,Haibin Wang,Wei Shi,Yifan Ding,Qingbin Li,Rongxiang Weng,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Step-Advantage Policy Distillation (SAPD)，一种利用离线轨迹通过相对步骤优势进行策略蒸馏的方法，无需环境交互即可提升LLM代理性能，有效缓解灾难性遗忘，且训练速度比在线方法快至少4.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM代理训练需要大量可执行环境，其构建、维护和运行成本高昂，制约了训练经验的扩展。现有离线轨迹提供了复用交互经验的机会，但若不重访环境，有效优化当前策略仍具挑战：监督微调(SFT)易导致灾难性遗忘，而在线强化学习需要实时环境交互。因此需要一种无需环境交互即可从离线轨迹高效学习的方法。

Method: 提出Step-Advantage Policy Distillation (SAPD)。该方法将多轮轨迹分解为单步决策上下文进行on-policy蒸馏，使学生模型能基于记录的历史生成响应，并引入相对步骤优势信号以稳定训练。整个过程既不需要新的环境观测，也不需要额外的多轮交互。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-based QA三个基准上实验表明：SAPD在不同教师配置下均能提升性能；相比SFT有效缓解灾难性遗忘；每训练步运行速度至少比在线OPD快4.7倍。

Conclusion: SAPD是一种有效的离线策略蒸馏方法，能在无环境交互条件下利用离线轨迹显著提升代理性能，同时避免灾难性遗忘并大幅加速训练过程，为大规模LLM代理训练提供了可行的离线学习范式。

Abstract: Scaling the training of Large Language Model (LLM) agents typically requires a large collection of executable environments, whose construction, maintenance, and operation constrain the expansion of training experience. Existing offline trajectories offer an opportunity to reuse interaction experience, yet effectively refining the current policy without revisiting these environments remains challenging: supervised fine-tuning (SFT) can lead to catastrophic forgetting, while online reinforcement learning requires real-time interaction with the environment. To address this challenge, we propose Step-Advantage Policy Distillation (SAPD), a method for learning from offline trajectories using relative step advantages. SAPD decomposes multi-turn trajectories into individual decision contexts for on-policy distillation, allowing the student to generate responses conditioned on recorded histories, and introduces relative step advantage signals to stabilize training. The entire process requires neither new environment observations nor additional multi-turn interactions. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-based QA show that SAPD improves performance across different teacher configurations, mitigates catastrophic forgetting compared with SFT, and runs at least $4.7\times$ faster per training step than online OPD. We will release our code soon.

</details>


### [209] [Physical Operating-Condition-Guided Vision-Language Matching for Cavitation Diagnosis in Industrial Pipeline Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27860)
*Yu Sha,Junqi Tao,Deyu Wang,Horst Stoecker,Domagoj Vnucec,Andreas Widlf,Kai Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出物理工况引导的视觉-语言匹配(POC-VLM)用于工业管道系统空化诊断,通过融合工况感知语义描述与非参数物理先验,在三个真实数据集上超越现有方法并展现鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业管道系统中空化诊断面临声学响应随工况显著变化且不同时空化状态可能呈现相似时频模式的挑战,现有数据驱动、多模态及领域特定方法难以同时处理工况相关性和物理合理性。

Method: POC-VLM包含四大组件:1)构建工况感知语义描述,将样本特定工况变量显式融入候选空化文本;2)时频条件文本自适应模块,动态对齐物理语义与观测声学响应;3)全局-局部视觉-语言匹配,捕捉整体与局部判别模式;4)基于K近邻的非参数物理工况先验估计各候选状态物理合理性,在决策层与视觉-语言语义证据融合。

Result: 在三个真实空化数据集上,POC-VLM持续优于代表性数据驱动、多模态及领域特定诊断方法;消融实验验证视觉-语言语义匹配与物理工况先验的互补作用;在不同采样率、噪声水平和观测时长下表现鲁棒。

Conclusion: POC-VLM通过显式建模物理工况约束与视觉-语言语义匹配的深度融合,有效解决了空化诊断中工况敏感性与模式混淆的核心难题,为工业声学故障诊断提供了兼具物理可解释性与数据驱动泛化能力的新范式。

Abstract: Cavitation diagnosis in industrial pipeline systems is challenging because acoustic responses vary substantially with operating conditions, while similar time-frequency patterns may correspond to different cavitation states. To address this issue, we propose a physical operating-condition-guided vision-language matching (POC-VLM) for cavitation diagnosis. POC-VLM first constructs physical operating-condition-aware semantic descriptions by explicitly incorporating sample-specific operating variables into candidate cavitation texts. A time-frequency-conditioned text adaptation module then dynamically aligns these physical semantics with the observed acoustic response, followed by global-local vision-language matching to capture both holistic and localized discriminative patterns. In parallel, a K-nearest neighbours-based non-parametric physical operating-condition prior estimates the physical plausibility of each candidate state and is fused with the vision-language semantic evidence at the decision level. Experiments on three real-world cavitation datasets show that POC-VLM consistently outperforms representative data-driven, multimodal and domain-specific diagnostic methods. Further analyses verify the complementary roles of vision-language semantic matching and physical operating-condition priors, while demonstrating robust performance under varying sampling rates, noise levels and observation durations.

</details>


### [210] [Automatic Differentiation with Constraints in Forward and Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27888)
*Nikolay Yavich,Alexander Ryabov,Viacheslav Naumov,Sayan Ranu,N. M. Anoop Krishnan,Evgeny Burnaev,Vladimir Vanovskiy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种完全可微的基于图的隐式求解器，用于非结构网格上的偏微分方程（PDE），将大型方程组求解视为约束，实现了前向建模、反演参数估计及图粗化加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非结构网格上演化型和静态PDE数值优化中大型方程组求解的复杂性问题，并将其作为可微约束纳入优化流程，以支持反演问题求解和模型加速。

Method: 构建基于图的隐式求解器，将PDE离散化为图结构，利用自动微分实现完全可微；将线性/非线性方程组求解作为隐式层嵌入，支持前向建模、系数反演、右端项反演及可学习图粗化四大任务。

Result: 在四个关键任务上验证了方法的有效性：前向建模精度高、反演参数估计准确、图粗化显著加速计算；稳定性与可微性使前向建模复杂度大幅降低，高效解决相关反演问题。

Conclusion: 该可微图求解器为物理和工程应用（特别是地球物理学中的反演建模）提供了强有力工具，能有效降低模型不确定性，实现大规模PDE约束优化的端到端可微计算。

Abstract: Approximate solutions of partial differential equations (PDEs) on unstructured grids can be represented on a graph. Numerical optimization involving stiff evolutionary and static PDEs often requires solving a large system of equations, which can be treated as a constraint. In this work, we present a fully differentiable graph-based implicit solver that incorporates this constraint. We evaluate its performance across four key tasks: a) linear and nonlinear forward modeling of evolutionary and static problems, b) solving inverse problems to estimate equation coefficients, c) solving inverse problems to estimate the right-hand side, and d) learnable graph coarsening to accelerate forward modeling. The stability and differentiability of our solver enable significant reductions in the complexity of forward modeling while efficiently addressing related inverse problems. These capabilities make it a powerful tool for different physics and engineering applications, in particular for geophysics, where inverse problems are commonly solved in order to reduce the model uncertainty.

</details>


### [211] [Staged Continual Adaptation of Multimodal Foundation Models for Japanese Financial Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27904)
*Genshin Kakimoto,Atsushi Yanagisawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多模态基础模型分阶段后训练中各阶段的非加性交互效应，发现不同训练阶段会相互权衡能力，最优检查点取决于具体任务，对检查点保留和调度策略有重要启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多模态基础模型分阶段后训练（视觉-语言对齐、推理蒸馏、领域微调）中各阶段如何相互权衡能力，这一问题目前研究不足。

Method: 追踪一个8.4B参数模型从未微调基线开始，在日语金融文档上经过三个训练阶段，在五个文本输入基准上评估，并进行跳过阶段的消融实验。

Result: 各阶段呈非加性交互：仅数学蒸馏会覆盖先前获得的金融知识，但程序性数学技能得以保留；领域阶段修复的知识多于新增的知识；跳过阶段的消融实验对领域知识最好，但牺牲了数值推理增益；各阶段贡献不可分解，最优检查点随任务而异。

Conclusion: 最优检查点取决于具体任务，这对检查点保留和训练调度策略而非单纯增加阶段具有重要启示。

Abstract: Staged post-training of multimodal foundation models chains vision-language alignment, reasoning distillation, and domain tuning, yet how each phase trades capabilities against the others remains under-characterised. Tracking an 8.4B-parameter model from an un-fine-tuned baseline through three training phases on Japanese financial documents (evaluated on five text-input benchmarks), we find the phases interact non-additively: math-only distillation overwrites the financial knowledge acquired earlier even as procedural math skill survives, and the domain phase repairs more than it installs. A skip-phase ablation is best for domain knowledge but forfeits numerical-reasoning gains, so per-phase contributions are not decomposable and the best checkpoint is task-dependent, with implications for checkpoint retention and scheduling rather than added stages.

</details>


### [212] [MMEnterprise-Bench: Evaluating Enterprise Agents over Evolving Multimodal Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27911)
*Seohyun Lee,Jongha Kim,Dohwan Ko,Ji Soo Lee,Seoung Choi,Jayun Ku,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MMEnterprise-Bench 基准，包含 1613 个基于模拟协作项目演进多模态记录的问题，评估 10 个多模态 LLM 在证据推理、冲突解决和不可回答性三类任务上的表现，发现即使最强模型在跨源/时序冲突及多图证据下仍表现挣扎，增加检索预算或更大检索器虽提升证据召回但不稳定提升答案准确率，揭示企业级智能体在演进职场记录上推理的持久挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体需在团队协作中演进的多模态工作记录（文档、聊天、图表等）上回答问题，这些记录包含多版本信息、跨源冲突、时序冲突及不充分证据。现有基准缺乏系统评估此类能力，因此需要一个基准来评估智能体在演进多模态记录上的证据推理、冲突解决及不可回答性识别能力。

Method: 构建 MMEnterprise-Bench，包含 1613 个问题，基于模拟协作项目的演进多模态记录，涵盖证据推理、冲突解决、不可回答性三类别。评估 10 个专有和开源多模态 LLM，实验变量包括检索预算和检索器规模，测量证据召回率和答案准确率。

Result: 即使最强模型在证据包含跨源冲突、时序冲突或跨越多张图像时表现挣扎。增加检索预算或使用更大检索器虽提高证据召回率，但未能稳定转化为更高的答案准确率。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了在演进职场记录上推理的持久挑战，MMEnterprise-Bench 为开发更可靠的企业级智能体提供了测试平台。代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Enterprise agents answer questions over workplace records that evolve through team collaboration. As information is shared, corrected, and revised across documents, chats, and figures, multiple versions of the same information accumulate in the workspace. Reliable question answering therefore requires agents to reason over sufficient evidence, resolve cross-source and temporal conflicts, and recognize when the available evidence is insufficient to establish an answer. To systematically evaluate these capabilities, we introduce MMEnterprise-Bench, a benchmark comprising 1,613 questions grounded in evolving multimodal records from simulated collaboration projects, covering three categories: Evidence Reasoning, Conflict Resolution, and Unanswerability. We evaluate 10 proprietary and open-source multimodal LLMs and find that even the strongest models struggle when evidence contains cross-source or temporal conflicts or spans multiple images. Moreover, increasing the retrieval budget or employing a larger retriever improves evidence recall but does not consistently translate into higher answer accuracy. These findings highlight the persistent challenges of reasoning over evolving workplace records and establish MMEnterprise-Bench as a testbed for developing more reliable enterprise agents. Code and data are available at https://mlvlab.github.io/MMEnterprise-Bench/.

</details>


### [213] [ACE-Compression: An Ability-Centric Evaluation of Compressed Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27913)
*Jiahui Ning,Liang Chen,Yuchen Liang,Yi Zhang,Fenglei Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ACE-Compression框架，从能力维度评估大语言模型压缩（量化/剪枝）的影响，发现压缩会重新分配能力谱系：粗粒度、局部、表层能力保留较好，而细粒度、长尾、上下文依赖能力易崩塌；量化整体优于剪枝，但实际差距取决于应用所需能力，建议按能力画像选择压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩评估仅关注任务级准确率，忽略压缩对分布在数十亿权重中的知识造成的扰动，无法揭示哪些底层能力幸存、哪些悄然崩塌，导致压缩像黑盒一样不可解释。

Method: 构建ACE-Compression能力中心评估框架，针对四个面向部署的维度设计探针：程序推理与约束执行、知识落地与真实性、社会认知与情感理解、人设保真与风格一致性。在六个开源大模型的35+个压缩检查点（覆盖量化与剪枝）上进行大规模实证研究。

Result: 压缩会重新分配能力谱系：粗粒度、局部、表层信号常保留，而细粒度、长尾、上下文依赖能力变得脆弱。量化在保留能力上持续优于剪枝，但该差距的实际意义完全取决于应用所需的具体能力。

Conclusion: 部署者应根据应用所需的能力画像选择压缩方法，而非仅看排行榜平均分；算法设计者可获得诊断信号，构建保留关键能力的压缩方法。ACE-Compression将压缩从黑盒准确率下降转变为可测量、可理解、可改进的可解释干预。

Abstract: Model compression makes large language models deployable, but today's evaluation tells an incomplete story. Standard practice measures retained task performance and stops there. This misses a critical fact: compression perturbs the distributed knowledge embedded across billions of weights, and task scores alone cannot reveal which underlying abilities survive and which quietly collapse. To solve this question, we introduce ACE-Compression, an ability-centric evaluation framework. Instead of asking only "does the compressed model still answer correctly?", we ask "which of its core abilities remain intact?" Our framework probes four deployment-facing dimensions: procedural reasoning and constraint execution, knowledge grounding and truthfulness, social cognition and affective understanding, and persona fidelity and stylistic consistency. Across more than 35 compressed checkpoints from six open-weight LLMs-covering quantization and pruning-we find that compression redistributes ability profiles. Coarse, local, and surface-level signals often persist, while fine-grained, long-tail, and context-dependent abilities become fragile. Quantization consistently outperforms pruning in retention, but the practical meaning of that gap depends entirely on which ability an application requires. Based on our findings, deployers can choose compression methods by the ability profile their application needs, not just by a leaderboard average. And algorithm designers gain diagnostic signals to build compression methods that preserve what matters. We believe that ACE-Compression turns compression from a black-box accuracy drop into an interpretable intervention-one we can measure, understand, and ultimately improve.

</details>


### [214] [Quantum-JEPA: Quantum Born Priors for Latent-Action JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27921)
*Zhiyuan Liu,Sicheng Liu,Haiyu Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Quantum-JEPA，一种基于量子Born先验的潜在动作JEPA框架，用于学习未来表示的分布，实现多模态未来预测，并在真实量子处理单元(QPU)上验证了推理可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一观测可对应多个合理的未来，不确定性是世界建模的核心，但量子生成模型在此任务上研究尚不充分。

Method: Quantum-JEPA框架采用条件量子Born先验：量子电路测量生成二进制潜在动作，共享预测器将其映射为替代未来表示；通过采样能量目标优化量子先验的质量与多样性；采用有限采样参数偏移训练，并支持读取噪声下的训练。

Result: 在构造的多模态任务中学习到截然不同的延续，捕捉潜在动作相关性以支持协调多智能体未来；在D3IL基准（多样化人类演示）上，从共同起点生成多条避障路径；经典与量子先验均显著提升预测有效性与分布保真度，超越变分基线；量子先验兼具竞争力的预测质量与参数高效的采样结构（避免指数级分类输出层），并验证了真实QPU上的推理可行性。

Conclusion: 确立了可训练的量子组件用于JEPA未来预测，为大规模量子增强世界模型的探索奠定了基础。

Abstract: A single observation can admit several plausible futures, making uncertainty an essential part of world modelling. However, quantum generative models remain underexplored for this task. We introduce Quantum-JEPA, a latent-action JEPA framework that learns distributions of future representations using a conditional quantum Born prior. Circuit measurements generate binary latent actions, which a shared predictor maps to alternative future representations. We train the quantum prior through the quality and diversity of these predictions using a sampled energy objective. Our model learns distinct continuations on constructed multimodal tasks and captures latent-action correlations that support coordinated multi-agent futures. On D3IL, a benchmark of diverse human demonstrations, our trained models generate multiple obstacle-crossing routes from a common starting point without observed motion. On the controlled tasks, our classical and quantum prior implementations improve prediction validity and distributional fidelity over the evaluated variational baselines. Our quantum priors combine competitive predictive quality with a parameter-efficient sampling structure: they avoid an exponentially sized categorical output layer while supporting correlated latent actions. We further validate finite-shot parameter-shift training, including training with readout noise. Unlike most prior work, which focuses on simulation, we also verify the feasibility of inference with trained priors on a real QPU. These results establish a trainable quantum component for JEPA future prediction and a basis for exploring quantum-enhanced world models at larger scales.

</details>


### [215] [From Comparison to Selection: Enhancing Cross-Scale Generalization of Pointer Networks Trained with Reinforcement Learning for Capacitated Drone Delivery Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27936)
*Sofiene Lassoued,Devara Tertia,Stefan Lier,Andreas Schwung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文针对无人机路径规划中基于注意力指针网络的强化学习策略在不同规模问题间泛化性能下降的问题，识别出节点密度变化、注意力分散和图上下文稀释三个关键因素，提出熵缩放注意力机制和基于Set2Set的图上下文聚合方法，并引入模型选择器动态选择最优策略，在CVRP基准上显著缩小跨规模泛化差距，最多降低3.2%成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机末端配送和应急配送需求增长，但客户数量变化导致路径规划难度大；传统混合整数规划和启发式算法扩展性差或需针对性设计，强化学习虽有潜力但要求单一策略跨规模泛化而无需重训练。注意力指针网络虽具规模无关性，但在上采样和下采样时性能均下降，亟需解决跨规模泛化失效的根本原因。

Method: 以固定载重约束的CVRP为代理任务，系统分析三大退化因素：节点密度偏移、注意力分散、均值池化图上下文稀释。对比架构级、编码器侧、解码器侧改进，提出两种排列不变的学习式图上下文聚合机制：加权平均和Set2Set；引入基于熵的注意力缩放缓解上采样退化；最后设计方法选择器，根据训练-目标规模对自动选用最优模型。

Result: 熵缩放注意力将CVRP20→CVRP100性能差距缩小1.41个百分点；图上下文聚合方法在更难的CVRP100→CVRP20迁移中提升约0.9个百分点。组合两者互补优势的选择器，在9对已见规模和9对未见规模（CVRP35/75/200）中分别全部和8对优于基准，最大成本降低达3.2%。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模跨规模泛化的三大失效机制并针对性改进注意力机制与图上下文聚合，配合自适应模型选择策略，实现了单一强化学习策略在无人机路径规划中跨多种问题规模的稳健泛化，为实际部署提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) enable fast, flexible last-mile and emergency delivery, but their routing remains challenging as the number of clients varies. Classical approaches based on mixed-integer programming and heuristics can scale poorly or require problem-specific design, motivating learning-based alternatives. Reinforcement learning is particularly promising, provided a single policy can generalize across problem sizes without retraining. Although attention-based Pointer Networks are inherently size-agnostic, their solution quality degrades under both upscaling and downscaling. Using the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (CVRP) as a controlled proxy, with fixed capacity reflecting drone payload constraints, we investigate this degradation and identify three contributing factors: shifts in node density, attention dispersion, and dilution of the mean-pooled graph context. We systematically compare architecture-wide, encoder-side, and decoder-side modifications to address these limitations and propose a learned, permutation-invariant graph-context aggregation mechanism, implemented as a weighted average and as Set2Set. Results reveal distinct requirements for transfer in each direction: entropy-based attention scaling reduces the CVRP20-to-CVRP100 performance gap by 1.41 percentage points, while the proposed aggregation methods achieve the largest improvement on the more challenging CVRP100-to-CVRP20 transfer, approximately 0.9 percentage points. Exploiting this complementarity, we introduce an approach selector that chooses the best model for each training-target size pair. The selector reduces cost relative to the reference model across all nine seen train-test pairs and eight of nine pairs involving unseen sizes (CVRP35, CVRP75, and CVRP200), achieving improvements of up to 3.2 percent.

</details>


### [216] [HypTI: Hyperbolic Geometry-Aware Multimodal Feature Fusion Framework for Dementia Detection Based on Tabular-Image Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27941)
*Jianfei Xu,Huizhi Liang,Judith Harrison,John-Paul Taylor,Jaume Bacardit,Varun Ojha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 HypTI，一种基于双曲几何的多模态融合框架，用于结合临床表格评估和 MRI 扫描进行痴呆检测。该方法在 Lorentz 空间中分别对表格变量（通过可学习 Lorentz 原型捕捉层级关系）和 MRI（通过 ROI 引导的多尺度表示学习捕捉解剖多尺度模式）建模，并通过自适应融合模块生成主体特定的缩放与平移系数动态融合多模态嵌入，在 OASIS-3 数据集上取得优于代表性基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有痴呆检测多模态融合方法多在欧氏空间进行特征融合，忽略了临床评估的层级组织结构和神经影像的多尺度解剖模式。双曲空间因其指数级扩展的空间特性，天然适合建模层级与多尺度结构，但尚未在表格-MRI 融合场景中被充分探索。
