First, agentic orchestration extends single-agent execution through task delegation and parallel collaboration among subagents with isolated contexts, enabling complex tasks to be decomposed and addressed concurrently and supporting efficient execution while reducing interference across concurrent subagents. Second, agentic verification strengthens long-horizon reliability through progress checking, evidence validation, and reflective strategy revision, allowing the agent to recognize unproductive search paths and pursue alternative approaches. Third, open scientific discovery extends agentic capabilities beyond answering predefined questions to conducting exploratory research, connecting scientific inquiry with interactive analysis and inspectable research artifacts. We showcase these capabilities through complicated agentic tasks and multidisciplinary scientific applications. Besides, we hope Sage-A1.5 provides the community with an accessible foundation for open scientific discovery, offering a practical path from long-horizon task execution to collaborative, verifiable, and inspectable scientific exploration.

</details>


### [248] [Beyond the Sequentiality Bottleneck: A Hybrid Diffusion-Autoregressive Framework for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28584)
*Wei Cai,Jian Zhao,Yuchen Yuan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CoRAL 框架，结合扩散语言模型规划器与自回归执行器，通过奖励引导去噪训练，实现高效且高质量的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型存在“顺序瓶颈”，推理速度慢；扩散语言模型虽支持并行但缺乏语义连贯性。需要一种混合方法兼顾效率与推理质量。

Method: 采用非对称规划器-执行器架构：DLM 规划器并行生成 K 条推理轨迹，ARM 执行器选择最优轨迹并合成最终答案；引入奖励引导去噪训练规划器，将基于结果的奖励融入扩散过程，引导朝向目标导向推理。

Result: 在视觉-语言基准上，CoRAL 显著优于先前扩散基方法，且与强自回归模型性能竞争，同时实现更高效率和更好质量。

Conclusion: CoRAL 通过异步协作解决了自回归模型的顺序瓶颈和扩散模型的连贯性不足，证明混合架构可兼顾推理效率与质量，为大模型推理提供新范式。

Abstract: Autoregressive models (ARMs) face a "Sequentiality Bottleneck" in reasoning, while diffusion language models (DLMs) offer parallelism but lack semantic coherence. To addresses both limitations, we propose Collaborative Reasoning Asymmetric Learning (CoRAL), a hybrid framework via an Asymmetric Planner-Executor (APE) architecture: a DLM Planner generates K diverse reasoning trajectories in parallel, while a large ARM Executor selects the optimal plan and synthesizes the final answer. To train the Planner effectively, we introduce Reward-Guided Denoising for CoT (RDC), which integrates outcome-based rewards into the diffusion process to steer it toward goal-directed reasoning. Experiments on vision-language benchmarks show that CoRAL significantly outperforms prior diffusion-based methods and achieves competitive performance against strong ARMs, achieving both superior efficiency and quality.

</details>


### [249] [What Do RTL Repair Benchmarks Actually Measure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28595)
*Yeyin Eva Zhu,Rishabh Ranawat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了 RTLRepairDataset（5,784 个训练对）和 RTLRepairBench（459 个保留测试用例，含 374 个句法变异和 85 个行为变异），并配套提供支持仿真和形式等价性检查的评测工具。通过该基础设施，作者发现了两个测量混淆因素：1) 仿真评分与形式评分对“提供规范”的效应给出相反符号，且差异主要源于复位和首周期比较语义；2) 在 274 个挖掘出的模型失败案例中，仅规范基线与规范条件修复的得分差异在 -8.5 到 +18.3 个百分点之间，说明常规修复得分无法隔离模型是否真正利用了故障 RTL。据此，作者提出评测控制建议：报告初始化、复位、首周期和未知值语义；披露预言机覆盖率和未判决情况；并纳入仅规范基线。相关资源已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RTL 修复基准缺乏对行为修复的严格度量，且评测过程中存在仿真与形式验证评分不一致、无法区分模型是否真正利用规范与故障代码等混淆因素，导致修复能力评估不可靠。

Method: 构建大规模训练集 RTLRepairDataset 和保留测试集 RTLRepairBench（含句法与行为变异），开发支持仿真和形式等价性检查的评测工具链；对六个模型进行实验，分析仿真与形式评分的差异来源，挖掘模型失败案例并对比仅规范基线与规范条件修复的得分差异，提出评测控制协议。

Result: 发现两个关键测量混淆：1) 仿真与形式评分对‘提供规范’的效应符号相反，复位感知重评分显示差异几乎完全归因于复位和首周期比较语义；2) 仅规范基线与规范条件修复的得分差异在 -8.5 到 +18.3 个百分点之间，表明常规修复得分不能反映模型是否利用了故障 RTL。

Conclusion: 作者发布了 RTL 行为修复基准及配套资源，并提出了一套诊断评测得分贡献因素的协议：要求报告初始化、复位、首周期、未知值语义；披露预言机覆盖率与未判决情况；必须包含仅规范基线。这些资源与审计方法共同为行为级 RTL 修复提供了可靠的基准与评估框架。

Abstract: RTL repair benchmarks should measure whether a model can fix broken RTL given a specification. To this end, we construct and hash-bind RTLRepairDataset, 5,784 (broken, fixed) training pairs; RTLRepairBench, 459 held-out cases with 374 syntactic and 85 behavioral mutations; and an evaluation harness supporting simulation and formal equivalence. We then use this infrastructure to identify two measurement confounds. First, simulation and formal scoring give opposite signs for the apparent effect of providing a specification in one evaluated cohort; reset-aware rescoring attributes almost all of the simulation-formal disagreement to reset and first-cycle comparison semantics. Second, on 274 mined model failures, the specification-only minus specification-conditioned recovery point estimate ranges from -8.5 to +18.3 percentage points across six models. Thus, a conventional repair score need not isolate whether the model used the faulty RTL. We translate these findings into evaluation controls: report initialization, reset, first-cycle, and unknown-value semantics; disclose oracle coverage and undecided cases; and include a specification-only baseline. Together, the resources and audit provide both a benchmark for behavioral repair and a protocol for diagnosing what contributes to its measured score. We release the benchmark, the training set, construction code, sanitized aggregate evidence, and reproduction materials at https://github.com/Evaaaz/RTLRepairBench.

</details>


### [250] [Evolving Agent Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28601)
*Saaket Agashe,Yuzhe Yang,Gonzalo Gonzalez-Pumariega,Bhuvan Chandra Koduru,Chih-Lun Li,Jiachen Yang,Ang Li,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agent S4 是一种自主计算机使用科学家，能从任务经验中进化智能体接口。在 OSWorld V2 基准测试中，配合 Qwen3.8-27B 模型达到 21.3% 二分得分，是同模型固定接口的 3.8 倍，超越 GPT-5.5 (13.0%) 和 Claude Opus 4.8 (20.6%)，成本仅为其 4.3% 和 1.5%。在 ScienceBoard 上成功率从 41.4% 提升至 58.6%。证明接口可从经验进化，自主接口设计是模型和框架进步之外的互补改进源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算机使用智能体受限于人工构建的接口及使用知识，无法跟上新应用、工作流和模型的发展。智能体可能逐单元格输入电子表格而非单行代码，或打印两千行文件查找单个函数。论文主张接口设计应成为智能体自身能力。

Method: 元智能体在合成训练任务上检查任务智能体的轨迹（无成功标签），构建并修订接口及使用知识存储库。基于种群的进化根据未见验证任务的表现选择进一步开发的存储库，偏好能泛化至已见任务之外的变更。

Result: OSWorld V2：Qwen3.8-27B + 进化存储库达 21.3% 二分得分，为开源计算机使用智能体新 SOTA，是固定接口基线的 3.8 倍；超越 GPT-5.5 (13.0%) 和 Claude Opus 4.8 (20.6%)，成本分别仅约 4.3% 和 1.5%。ScienceBoard：同模型下成功率从 41.4% 提升至 58.6%。

Conclusion: 有用接口可从经验中进化，自主接口设计是除模型与框架进步外的互补改进来源。代码已在 https://github.com/simular-ai/agent-s 开源。

Abstract: Computer-use agents are limited by the interfaces humans give them. An agent may type a spreadsheet cell by cell when a single line of code would do, or print all two thousand lines of a file through a shell to find one function. Both the interfaces and the knowledge of when to use each are built by hand, which cannot keep pace with new applications, workflows, and models. We argue that interface design should itself be an agent capability. We introduce Agent S4, an autonomous computer-use scientist that builds Evolving Agent Interfaces from task experience. A meta-agent examines task agents' trajectories on synthetic training tasks, without success labels, and constructs and revises interfaces and usage knowledge in a repository. Population-based evolution then selects which repositories to develop further by their performance on validation tasks the meta-agent never sees, favoring changes that generalize beyond the seen tasks. On OSWorld V2, Agent S4, with Qwen3.8-27B and the evolved repository of interfaces, sets a new state of the art in open-source computer-use agents, reaching 21.3% binary score, 3.8 times the same model with fixed interfaces. This is above both GPT-5.5 (13.0%) and Claude Opus 4.8 (20.6%), at about 4.3% and 1.5% of their respective costs. On ScienceBoard, the evolved interfaces improve the success rate from 41.4% to 58.6% with the same model. These results suggest that useful interfaces can be evolved from experience, making autonomous interface design a source of improvement complementary to advances in models and harnesses. We open-source our code at https://github.com/simular-ai/agent-s.

</details>


### [251] [Value-Rich, Differently Organized: Measuring Value Expression and Organization in AI-Generated Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28604)
*Maoyuan Li,Haoyuan Li,Zhongsheng Wang,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Schwartz价值理论的诊断度量框架，评估LLM生成辩论中的价值表达与组织，发现AI生成辩论虽价值表达丰富但组织方式不同，提示评估需兼顾价值存在与组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成辩论评估主要关注论证质量，忽视了生成论证的价值结构，缺乏系统度量价值表达与组织的方法。

Method: 基于Schwartz基本10价值理论，将辩论表示为论证单元、话语、立场、完整辩论多层级的价值分布，提出价值覆盖、一致性、扩散度、跨轮动态等指标，区分价值表达（调用价值维度广度）与价值组织（价值跨轮分布与维持）。

Result: 多个生成系统实验显示，AI辩论整体价值表达丰富，激活的价值维度不少于人类参考，但话语层面价值扩散度更高，盲测评委能区分价值清晰度与价值聚焦度，表明价值组织模式存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 辩论评估不应仅关注价值是否出现，还需考察价值如何在对话中组织与维持；提出的框架为价值层面的细粒度诊断提供了工具。

Abstract: Current evaluation of LLM-generated debate largely focuses on argumentative quality, leaving the value structure of generated arguments underexamined. We introduce a diagnostic metric framework for comparing how values are expressed and organized in human-reference and AI-generated debates. The framework separates value expression, the range of recognizable values a debate invokes, from value organization, how those values are distributed and sustained across turns. Grounded in Schwartz's Basic-10 value theory, it represents debates as multi-level value distributions over argumentative units, utterances, stances, and full debates, enabling metrics for value coverage, alignment, diffusion, and cross-turn dynamics. Across multiple generation systems, AI-generated debates are broadly value-expressive, often activating as many or more value dimensions than matched human references, but they organize these values differently. Their utterances are more locally value-diffuse, and source-blind judges distinguish value clarity from value focus. These findings show that debate evaluation should consider not only value presence, but also value organization. Code and supplementary materials are available at https://github.com/Maoyuan-li/Value-Expression-and-Organization.

</details>


### [252] [Semantic Observability for Autonomous Systems Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28611)
*Jitendra Solanki,Anand Todkar,Ziran Min*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Semantic Observability，一个自主多智能体框架，将异构日志流统一为属性图数据库，结合有向环图编排的多智能体系统与因果图算法，实现故障根因的自动定位与代码级修复建议，显著降低 MTTR 与 LLM Token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代微服务部署产生海量异构、多格式的可观测性日志，分布在物理和逻辑分区基础设施中。级联故障时，SRE 依赖手动关键词过滤和碎片化的时间关联，难以快速隔离根因，导致平均修复时间（MTTR）过长。

Method: 1. 将物理拓扑、逻辑依赖、时间事件序列建模为属性图数据库中的属性节点和有向关系，消除扁平非结构化日志的语义鸿沟。2. 基于有向环图（DCG）编排状态化多智能体系统，自主遍历拓扑，应用因果图算法与时间回溯，精准划定故障影响范围。3. 将剪枝后的子图输入 LLM 推理引擎，生成上下文感知的代码级根因分析（RCA）与可执行修复方案。

Result: 理论上证明该框架能消除环境遥测噪声、降低 MTTR、压缩 LLM Token 消耗、防止结构性幻觉。提供基于 LogHub 和 Train-Ticket 基准的开源验证框架，为后续量化评估与生产基准测试奠定基础。

Conclusion: Semantic Observability 通过统一知识图谱与多智能体因果推理，实现了从海量异构日志到可执行根因分析的端到端自动化，为大规模微服务故障诊断提供了新范式，后续将发布详尽的量化评估与生产基准结果。

Abstract: Modern distributed microservice deployments generate massive volumes of heterogeneous, multi-format observability logs across physically and logically partitioned infrastructure. Isolating root causes during cascading failures remains an arduous task for Site Reliability Engineers (SREs), bottlenecked by manual keyword filtering and disjointed temporal correlation across monitoring dashboards. We introduce Semantic Observability, an autonomous multi-agent framework that unifies disparate log streams into a dynamic, queryable Property Graph Database. By modeling physical infrastructure topologies, logical software dependencies, and chronological event sequences as attributed nodes and directed relations, it bridges the semantic gap inherent in flat, unstructured log repositories. Building on this unified substrate, we deploy a stateful multi-agent system orchestrated through Directed Cyclic Graphs (DCGs). The system autonomously navigates the topology, applying causal graph algorithms and temporal back-traversal to isolate the precise blast radius of anomalies. The pruned subgraph is then interpreted by a Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning engine to generate contextualized, code-level Root Cause Analyses (RCA) and actionable remediations. This paper establishes the foundational system architecture, formal relational ontology, and multi-agent control loops of Semantic Observability. We show conceptually how the framework eliminates ambient telemetry noise, reduces Mean Time to Resolution (MTTR), compresses LLM token consumption, and prevents structural hallucinations. We also specify an open-source validation framework on standard benchmarks (LogHub and Train-Ticket), laying the groundwork for a forthcoming companion paper on exhaustive quantitative evaluation and production benchmarking.

</details>


### [253] [Adaptive Multi-Token Prediction: How far should we go?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28619)
*Emile Anand,Mohammed Rehan Parwani,Xinyuan Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应多token预测（AMTP），通过在线学习动态选择最优推测horizon，无需重训练即可在保持验证器不变的情况下实现比固定horizon高达10%的吞吐量提升，解码速度比标准解码快达4.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多token预测（MTP）虽能通过单次前向传递验证多个草稿token加速解码，但最优推测horizon随上下文动态变化：较长草稿在可靠时摊销收益大，但被拒绝后会浪费计算。与自回归草稿可逐token决定停止不同，MTP必须在前向传递前固定horizon，因此需要一种无需重训练、保持验证器不变的在线自适应horizon选择机制。

Method: 将horizon选择建模为在线控制问题，引入轻量级控制器AMTP：1）用验证反馈学习各上下文下草稿可信程度；2）定义接受存活率（acceptance survival）表征各horizon下预期接受token数；3）每轮预测草稿可能存活长度并选取单位时间预期token数最大的horizon；4）理论证明在线梯度下降可实现次线性遗憾界，进而得到壁钟完成时间界。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct配合Medusa头、Meta MTP模型、Qwen3-4B-Inst-2507-MTP五种草稿-目标配对上，AIME 2025和GSM8K评测显示：AMTP比标准解码快达4.1倍，在10个设置中有8个匹配或超越固定窗口，吞吐量增益高达10%。

Conclusion: AMTP通过在线梯度下降实现上下文感知的horizon自适应选择，理论有次线性遗憾保证，实验验证了在多种模型和任务上的显著加速效果，为MTP解码提供了实用的自适应控制方案。

Abstract: Multi-token prediction (MTP) can accelerate language-model decoding by drafting several tokens and verifying them in a single target-model pass. However, the best speculation horizon changes with context: longer drafts offer greater amortization when reliable, but can waste compute on suffixes discarded after a rejection. Unlike autoregressive drafting, where a stopping rule can decide token by token, MTP must fix the horizon before drafting, as all heads fire in the same forward pass. We therefore formulate horizon selection as an online control problem, using verification feedback to learn how far the draft can be trusted in each context, and introduce Adaptive Multi-Token Prediction (AMTP): a lightweight controller that requires no retraining and leaves the exact verifier unchanged. To guide horizon selection, we characterize the expected number of tokens committed at each horizon via acceptance survival, the probability that a draft prefix is fully accepted. Before each round, AMTP predicts how far the draft is likely to survive and picks the horizon with the highest expected tokens per second, learning freely from every verification pass. Theoretically, we show that any online learner's prediction error translates into throughput regret against the best horizon for each context, and in turn into a bound on wall-clock completion time. With online gradient descent, this regret is sublinear. Across five draft-target pairings built on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct with Medusa heads, Meta's MTP models, and Qwen3-4B-Inst-2507-MTP, evaluated on AIME 2025 and GSM8K, AMTP decodes up to 4.1x faster than standard decoding and matches or outperforms the fixed window in 8 of 10 settings, with throughput gains of up to 10%.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [254] [High-dimensional layer-state trajectories reveal the internal dynamics of transformer inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26866)
*Heng Zhao,Yufei Wang,Ruidian Song,Weisheng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将有序层状态轨迹视为经验过程，发现层顺序定义了可测量的计算，层顺序效应在随机初始化中更强，表明是架构兼容的脚手架而非训练特定规律。通过局部转换执行器和置信度门控，实现了对内部轨迹的选择性控制，且不影响非闭包输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机制性研究将功能定位在参数、注意力头或神经元，但这些部分无法说明单次推理如何展开。因此需要研究层顺序是否定义了可测量的计算过程。

Method: 将有序层状态轨迹作为经验过程，测试层顺序是否定义可测量计算。在Qwen、Llama、Gemma检查点上比较实际顺序与端点保留打乱顺序的对齐度。使用直接隐藏状态作为机制坐标，任务定义坐标使候选竞争可计算。尝试加性干预失败后，使用局部转换执行器选择性控制内部轨迹，并引入置信度门控。

Result: 实际顺序产生的路径比端点保留打乱顺序与端点弦对齐度高1.60-2.24倍。顺序效应迁移到词汇任务，但在同架构的随机初始化中更强，表明是架构兼容的脚手架。直接隐藏状态比词汇回读提供更强的机制坐标。局部转换执行器跨检查点选择性控制内部轨迹。置信度门控驱动17/87个保留的Qwen闭包轨迹跨越全词汇表top-1候选边界，而203个非闭包top-1输出保持不变。安全匹配的动作重分配表现相似，增益在于选择性门控和有界执行器家族而非精细剂量分配。

Conclusion: 层顺序揭示了Transformer推理的一个可观察、可计算且行为上有后果的过程，但未建立LLM动力学的完整理论或答案正确性的控制。

Abstract: Mechanistic studies of large language models localize function in parameters, attention heads or neurons, but those parts do not specify how one inference unfolds. We treat the ordered layer-state trajectory as the empirical process and test whether layer order defines a measurable computation. Across representative Qwen, Llama and Gemma checkpoints, realized order produced paths 1.60--2.24 times as aligned with their endpoint chord as endpoint-preserving shuffles. The order effect transferred to a lexical task, but was stronger in three random initializations of the same Qwen architecture, identifying an architecture-compatible scaffold rather than a training-specific law. Direct hidden states provided stronger mechanism coordinates than vocabulary readbacks, and a task-defined coordinate made candidate competition computable. After additive interventions failed, local transition actuators selectively controlled internal trajectories across checkpoints. A confidence gate then drove 17 of 87 held-out Qwen closure trajectories across the full-vocabulary top-1 candidate boundary while leaving all 203 non-closure top-1 outputs unchanged. Safety-matched action reassignment performed similarly, locating the gain in selective gating and the bounded actuator family rather than fine-grained dose assignment. Layer order exposes an observable, computable and behaviourally consequential process of transformer inference without establishing a complete theory of LLM dynamics or control of answer correctness.

</details>


### [255] [Steerable Pluralistic Policy Alignment from Anonymized Crowd Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26914)
*Akansha Kalra,Ellen R. Novoseller,Vernon J. Lawhern,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Preference-PCA Pluralistic Learning (P3L)，一种无需标注者身份即可从异构众包反馈中联合学习轨迹嵌入和共享偏好方向基的多元对齐框架，实现快速个性化奖励学习和策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多元对齐方法通常假设拥有标注者身份标签，且依赖强大的预训练表示来建模个体偏好差异，这限制了在真实匿名众包反馈场景下的应用，且计算开销大。

Method: P3L通过PCA分解从异构众包反馈中联合学习轨迹嵌入和共享偏好方向基，无需标注者身份。测试时利用学到的共享基快速进行个性化奖励学习和下游策略优化。

Result: 在多个具身控制任务实验中，P3L实现了可控的个性化对齐，泛化到分布外新用户，达到有竞争力的RL和测试性能，同时大幅降低计算成本，支持可变的自适应查询预算。

Conclusion: P3L为多元对齐提供了一个实用的新范式：无需身份标签即可从匿名众包数据中提取共享偏好结构，实现高效、可泛化的个性化对齐，显著降低了部署门槛和计算开销。

Abstract: Pluralistic alignment requires AI systems to learn from diverse populations whose preferences may reflect heterogeneous or even conflicting objectives, and to personalize their behavior to individual users. Existing approaches in pluralistic alignment often assume access to annotator identities and rely on powerful pretrained representations to model individual preference variations. We propose Preference-PCA Pluralistic Learning (P3L), a novel framework for pluralistic alignment that jointly learns trajectory embeddings and the shared basis of preference directions via PCA decomposition from heterogeneous crowd feedback without requiring annotator identities. At test time, our approach enables rapid and accurate personalized reward learning and downstream policy optimization. Experiments across multiple embodied control tasks demonstrate that P3L enables steerable personal alignment that generalizes beyond the offline dataset to out-of-distribution new users and attains competitive RL and test performance, while substantially reducing computational cost and supporting variable adaptation query budgets.

</details>


### [256] [Expert-Guided Rubrics as Dense Reward Signals for Reinforcement Learning in Non-Verifiable Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26932)
*Ashish Bansal,Hossam Amer,Keerat Kaur Guliani,Parinita Edke,Lesley Matheson,Leizer Teran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合奖励信号方法，将少量专家判断转化为密集奖励并结合检索指标，用于GRPO训练非可验证领域的LLM检索代理，显著提升Recall@3和MRR@3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融等非可验证领域，LLM代理需理解用户意图并重写查询，但缺乏自动二元奖励；专家判断稀缺且为分级评分，导致GRPO在稀疏奖励下陷入优化停滞。

Method: 将固定少量专家判断转化为密集奖励，融合检索相关指标（如召回率、MRR）构建混合奖励信号，使其兼容GRPO训练流程。

Result: 实验显示该方法在Recall@3上最高提升6%，MRR@3上提升6.7%，优于稀疏奖励基线。

Conclusion: 通过少量专家投入构建密集混合奖励，可有效解决非可验证检索任务中GRPO训练信号稀疏问题，显著提升代理性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents in non-verifiable domains such as financial services, where they must interpret user intent, rewrite queries, and retrieve information under domain-specific constraints. Although reinforcement learning (RL) can train such agents from the consequences of their actions, recent successes largely rely on verifiable tasks with automatically computed binary rewards. In non-verifiable retrieval tasks, assessing query quality requires scarce subject-matter expert (SME) judgment that is inherently graded rather than binary. With Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), sparse rewards yield no useful learning signal when all sampled outputs within a group receive the same outcome, causing optimization to stall. We introduce a method that converts a small, fixed investment of SME judgment into a dense reward and combines it with retrieval-based metrics to form a hybrid, GRPO-compatible training signal. Experiments show improvements of up to 6\% in Recall@3 and 6.7\% in MRR@3 compared with sparse-reward baselines.

</details>


### [257] [Beyond Policy Discrepancy: Capability-Aware Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26950)
*Xuanfeng Yang,Le Gui,Jiaming Wang,Xuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 CA-MOPD，一种能力感知的多教师在线策略蒸馏方法，通过合并专家初始化和基于能力差距的动态监督强度调整，提升学生模型在数学、代码生成和指令跟随等任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于差异的分配方法可能在学生已匹配或超越教师性能时仍分配强监督，因为行为不匹配不一定意味着能力缺失。需要一种考虑学生已学习能力的方法。

Method: CA-MOPD 先将强化学习专家合并为强学生初始化，然后利用验证器估计的教师-学生能力差距调整领域级蒸馏强度。监督强度随能力差距缩小而减少，随学生能力退化而增加，同时保留教师提供的稠密 token 级指导。

Result: 在六个基准测试（数学、代码生成、指令跟随）上，CA-MOPD 平均得分 32.22，分别比 naive MOPD、Open-MOPD 和合并初始化高出 0.86、0.83 和 0.74 分。对照实验进一步验证了合并初始化和能力差距引导的优势。

Conclusion: CA-MOPD 通过能力感知的动态监督分配和专家合并初始化，有效解决了多教师蒸馏中监督强度分配不当的问题，显著提升了学生模型在多领域任务上的泛化性能。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) integrates domain specialists into a shared student, but determining how strongly each teacher should supervise remains challenging. Existing discrepancy-based allocation can assign strong supervision even when the student already matches or exceeds the teacher's task performance, since behavioral mismatch does not necessarily imply a remaining capability deficit. We propose Capability-Aware Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (CA-MOPD), which combines specialist merging with capability-guided allocation to account for what the student has already learned. CA-MOPD first merges reinforcement-learning specialists into a strong student initialization, then uses verifier-estimated teacher--student capability gaps to adjust domain-level distillation strength. Supervision decreases as capability gaps close and increases when estimated student capability regresses, while dense token-level guidance remains provided by the teachers. Across six benchmarks spanning mathematics, code generation, and instruction following, CA-MOPD achieves an average score of 32.22, outperforming naive MOPD, Open-MOPD, and the merged initialization by 0.86, 0.83, and 0.74 points, respectively. Controlled comparisons further support the benefits of merged initialization and capability-gap guidance under matched training budgets.

</details>


### [258] [Dynamic System Inspired Continuous Large Language Model for AI4Science Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26959)
*Zhaoxin Fan,Zheng Zhu,Yuqing Ma,Faguo Wu,Jian Yang,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Continuum-LM，一种连续深度语言模型，通过将预训练的残差变换复用为动力学算子，利用常微分方程（ODE）和高阶数值积分实现隐藏状态的连续演化，在不增加主干参数的情况下扩展计算深度。在八个科学任务上，该模型在 0.6B 和 1.7B 规模下分别比离散基线提升 7.77 和 10.96 个百分点的精确匹配准确率，且在 0.6B 规模下优于 Ouro 风格的循环控制基线。结果表明，连续隐状态演化是科学语言模型除参数扩展外的一个实用设计维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学基础模型处理连续现象，却仍依赖离散残差计算。作者探究：预训练的残差变换能否在不扩大主干网络的前提下，支持额外的潜在计算？即通过参数复用实现计算深度的可扩展性。

Method: 提出 Continuum-LM：将预训练残差变换作为动力学算子，隐藏状态通过常微分方程（ODE）连续演化，采用高阶数值积分求解器（如 Runge-Kutta），通过求解器阶数和步长控制计算量。模型在八个科学任务上训练，对比离散基线及 Ouro 风格循环控制。

Result: Continuum-LM 训练稳定，在 0.6B 和 1.7B 规模下平均精确匹配准确率分别提升 7.77 和 10.96 个百分点。在 0.6B 规模下，在匹配参数量和残差函数评估预算的条件下，优于 Ouro 式循环控制基线。

Conclusion: 连续隐状态演化通过参数复用实现了可扩展的计算深度，是科学语言模型在参数规模扩展之外的一个实用且有效的设计维度。

Abstract: Scientific foundation models increasingly operate on continuous phenomena, yet still use discrete residual computation. We ask whether pretrained residual transformations can support additional latent computation without expanding the backbone. To test this possibility, we introduce Continuum-LM, which reuses these transformations as dynamical operators in a continuous-depth language model. Its hidden states evolve through an ordinary differential equation evaluated by higher-order numerical integration, with solver order and step size controlling computation. Across eight scientific tasks, Continuum-LM trains stably and improves mean exact-match accuracy by 7.77 and 10.96 percentage points over discrete counterparts at the 0.6B and 1.7B scales, respectively. At 0.6B, it also outperforms an Ouro-style recurrent control under matched parameter and residual-function evaluation budgets. Together, these results support extensible computational depth through parameter reuse, identifying continuous hidden-state evolution as a practical design dimension for scientific language models beyond parameter scaling.

</details>


### [259] [Transformers Learn Two Competing Sorting Algorithms, and Only One Length-Generalizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26974)
*Khashayar Gatmiry,Nathan W. Henry,Chloe H. Su,Emmanuel Abbe,Samy Bengio,Melanie Weber,Jonathan A. Kelner,Peter L. Bartlett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究两层Transformer在排序任务上的训练动态，发现两种机制不同的解决方案：单层注意力方案无法长度泛化，双层注意力方案完美长度泛化。通过初始化可控制学习哪种方案。在寻找最小值任务中出现相反模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer层数对长度泛化能力的影响，理解注意力层如何协作实现算法推理，以及初始化如何引导学习不同的机制解。

Method: 在固定长度序列上训练两层Transformer至近零损失，分析不同初始化下的收敛解，考察注意力模式、层间专业化及泛化行为，对比排序与最小值任务。

Result: 发现两类解：1) 仅使用第一层注意力，无法长度泛化；2) 双层协作，第二层利用第一层上下文缩小搜索空间，完美长度泛化。稀有输入类型导致泛化差异。最小值任务中双层方案反而泛化更差。

Conclusion: 层间专业化机制决定长度泛化能力，初始化可引导学习路径。注意力层协作方式随任务而异，非单调有益。揭示了Transformer算法推理的内在机制。

Abstract: We show that two-layer transformers trained to near-zero loss on a sorting task converge to one of two mechanistically distinct solution classes depending on their initialization. One solution class consists of solutions that do not use the second attention layer to sort, but also fail to length-generalize. The other, which uses both attention layers, length-generalizes perfectly, despite only being trained to sort sequences of a fixed length. By carefully choosing the weight initialization, we demonstrate that we can steer the model toward learning one solution or the other. We identify input types that are rare at the training length but increasingly common at longer sequence lengths as a source of this disparity in length-generalization, and find that layer-wise specialization of the second class of solutions allows them to handle these rare sequences more accurately. In the second class, we discover a mechanism by which the second layer attention can use the contextual information gathered by the first layer attention to reduce its search space. On the related task of finding the minimum of a sequence, we find a similar two solution-type phenomenon, but with the opposite generalization pattern, where a different form of layer-wise specialization causes the two-layer solution to length-generalize worse than the single-attention solution.

</details>


### [260] [Latent Projection for Latent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26979)
*Yoo Sung Jang,Michael Ryoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种后处理方法，通过拟合受限状态动态矩阵 A，利用其特征基将规划成本限制在低不确定性子空间，显著提升了潜在世界模型在导航任务中的规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型规划时常用 L2 距离作为成本，但潜在表示包含噪声：防止坍缩的正则化项会在无信息方向上引入方差，导致规划成本不可靠。

Method: 利用 SIGReg 将潜在边缘分布驱动为标准高斯，假设潜在序列为高斯-马尔可夫过程，转移为线性：z'|z ~ N(Az, I - AA^T)。拟合状态动态矩阵 A 并约束其为正规矩阵，固定特征基跨时间步，从而得到每个潜在方向的不确定性。规划时将 L2 成本限制在前 k 个低不确定性特征方向组成的子空间。仅需拟合单个受限矩阵 A，编码器、预测器、展开和规划器均保持不变。

Result: 在六个环境中评估，2D 导航任务 TwoRoom 和 PointMaze 成功率较普通 L2 成本提升高达 +54.7 个百分点（在更困难规划设置下）。

Conclusion: 通过后处理拟合受限线性动态矩阵，可有效剔除潜在空间中的噪声方向，构建稳定的低不确定性子空间用于规划成本计算，无需修改原有模型组件，显著提升规划性能。

Abstract: Latent world models let an agent plan in latent space. The planner searches for an action sequence that minimizes a cost, and that cost is often the $\ell_2$ distance between the predicted terminal latent and the goal latent. The latent, however, is partly noise: the regularizer that prevents collapse during world model training promotes variance in the directions carrying no signal. We show that the same regularizer also supplies the structure to prune that noise in the latent. SIGReg drives the latent marginal to a standard Gaussian at every step, and assuming further that the latent sequence is a Gauss-Markov process, the transition is linear with its covariance pinned by the map itself, $z' \mid z \sim \mathcal{N}(Az,\, I - AA^\top)$. Fitting the state dynamics $A$ therefore yields the covariance at no additional cost, and in its eigenbasis each latent direction carries its own uncertainty. Furthermore, constraining $A$ to be a normal matrix fixes that basis across timesteps, so one eigenbasis measures the uncertainty over an arbitrary number of steps and serves as a stable basis to compute the planning cost on. We then restrict the $\ell_2$ cost to the $k$-dimensional subspace the state dynamics carry forward with the least uncertainty. The correction is post hoc: the encoder, the predictor, the rollout, and the planner are all left untouched, and only a single constrained matrix, $A$, is fit. We evaluate across six environments and show large gains on the 2D navigation tasks, TwoRoom and PointMaze, raising success rate over the plain $\ell_2$ cost by up to $+54.7$ points under a harder planning setting. Code is available at https://github.com/yjang43/lp2.

</details>


### [261] [CoASFL: Coupled Consistency Optimization for Asynchronous Split Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26992)
*Junhao Cheng,Yunming Liao,Hongli Xu,Yang Xu,Zuan Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoASFL，一种针对异步分割联邦学习（SFL）中水平不一致性和垂直不一致性的框架，通过动态激活合并与一致性感知模型聚合，在异构边缘环境下显著加速训练并提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步 SFL 虽然缓解了系统异质性导致的拖延者瓶颈，但引入了两个耦合的不一致性维度：客户端间的水平不一致性、客户端与服务器间的垂直不一致性；统计异质性进一步加剧这些不一致，导致激活表示偏差、模型发散和严重的客户端漂移。

Method: 提出 CoASFL 框架，包含两大核心机制：1) 动态激活合并机制，从异构客户端构建更一致的激活序列以缓解水平不一致；2) 一致性感知模型聚合机制，根据客户端参与频率和进度差距动态调整聚合权重以缓解垂直不一致。两机制联合协调激活层面和模型层面的一致性，抑制异步训练中的不一致性传播。

Result: 在 80 设备异构边缘测试床上的大量实验表明，CoASFL 持续优于最先进的 SFL 方法，训练加速 1.61× 到 3.74×，最终模型精度最高提升 3.26%。

Conclusion: CoASFL 通过显式建模和缓解异步 SFL 中的双重不一致性，有效解决了异构边缘环境下的模型收敛与精度问题，为大规模资源受限设备上的分割联邦学习提供了实用的高性能解决方案。

Abstract: Split Federated Learning (SFL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for training large-scale deep models on resource-constrained edge devices. Although asynchronous SFL alleviates the straggler bottleneck caused by system heterogeneity, it inevitably introduces two coupled dimensions of inconsistency: horizontal inconsistency across clients and vertical inconsistency between clients and the server. These inconsistencies, further exacerbated by statistical heterogeneity, result in biased activation representations, model divergence, and severe client drift. To address these challenges, we propose CoASFL, an inconsistency-aware asynchronous SFL framework that incorporates a dynamic activation merging mechanism and a consistency-aware model aggregation mechanism. Specifically, CoASFL constructs more consistent activation sequences from heterogeneous clients to alleviate horizontal inconsistency, and dynamically adjusts aggregation weights according to client participation frequencies and progress gaps to mitigate vertical inconsistency. By jointly coordinating activation-level and model-level consistency, CoASFL effectively suppresses inconsistency propagation during asynchronous training. Extensive experiments on an 80-device heterogeneous edge testbed demonstrate that CoASFL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art SFL approaches, accelerating training by 1.61$\times$ to 3.74$\times$ and improving final model accuracy by up to 3.26\% under heterogeneous scenarios.

</details>


### [262] [CorteXforge: Scenario-Based RF Dataset Generation on Real Testbeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26999)
*Andréa Joly,Leonardo S Cardoso,Jean-Marie Gorce*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CorteXforge，一种基于场景的框架，用于在可编程软件无线电（SDR）测试平台上生成真实且可复现的射频（RF）数据集，自动将高层 RF 场景转化为在 SLICES-RI/CorteXlab 平台上执行的同步实验，输出完全标注的 SIGMF 数据集，捕捉硬件损伤和传播效应。通过 ModForge 数据集验证，跨域实验揭示合成到真实的性能差距，并表明合成预训练对 OTA 数据适应仍有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在 RF 信号分析中依赖大规模标注数据集，但合成数据集以物理真实性换取可控性，而不受控的空中实测虽提供真实性但复现性有限，因此需要一种兼具真实性与可复现性的数据集生成方法。

Method: 提出 CorteXforge 框架：将高层 RF 场景自动翻译为在 SLICES-RI/CorteXlab 可编程 SDR 测试平台上执行的同步实验，生成完全标注的 Signal Metadata Format (SIGMF) 数据集，捕获硬件损伤与传播效应。

Result: 利用 CorteXforge 生成的 ModForge 空中实测数据集进行自动调制识别验证。跨域实验显示存在合成到真实的性能差距，但合成数据预训练对适应 OTA 数据仍具益处。

Conclusion: CorteXforge 为可复现 RF 数据集生成提供了实用基础，并为未来的域泛化研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Deep learning for radio frequency (RF) signal analysis relies on large annotated datasets, yet synthetic datasets trade physical realism for controllability, while uncontrolled over-theair measurements offer realism but limited reproducibility. We present CorteXforge, a scenario-based framework for generating realistic and reproducible RF datasets on programmable software defined radio (SDR) testbeds. High-level RF scenarios are automatically translated into synchronized experiments executed on the SLICES-RI/CorteXlab platform, producing fully annotated Signal Metadata Format (SIGMF) datasets that capture hardware impairments and propagation effects. We validate CorteXforge through automatic modulation classification using ModForge, an over-the-air (OTA) dataset generated with the framework. Cross-domain experiments reveal a synthetic-to-real performance gap and show that synthetic pretraining remains beneficial for adaptation to OTA data. CorteXforge provides a practical foundation for reproducible RF dataset generation and future research on domain generalization.

</details>


### [263] [ORBIT: Autoregressive Time Series Generation via Next Frequency-Band Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27000)
*Chunyi Hou,Xiangfei Qiu,Hanyin Cheng,Chenchen Sun,Yisheng An,Yang Shu,Jilin Hu,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ORBIT，一个统一的深度生成框架用于时间序列合成，包含两个核心组件：Frequency-Hierarchical VQ-VAE (FH-VQ-VAE) 和 Frequency-Band Autoregressive Transformer (FBAR)。FH-VQ-VAE 通过将潜在时序表示分解为不同频带并采用递增 token 预算的残差量化学习层次化表示；FBAR 引入低到高频的自回归生成方向，先构建全局时序结构再逐步融入细粒度细节。该设计有效解耦异质时序模式并缓解生成时的动态干扰，在多个真实基准上达到 SOTA 性能且推理效率更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度时间序列生成模型虽取得显著进展，但仍面临两大核心挑战：(1) 学习异质时序模式的层次化表示；(2) 协调异质时序动态的生成过程。传统方法难以有效解耦不同时频模式，且生成时不同动态间存在相互干扰，限制了生成质量与效率。

Method: 提出 ORBIT 框架，包含两大创新组件：(1) FH-VQ-VAE：将潜在时序表示分解为多个频带，对每个频带应用残差向量量化，并为高频带分配更大 token 预算，实现层次化、解耦的表示学习。(2) FBAR：采用低频到高频的自回归生成顺序，先生成低频全局结构，再逐步条件生成高频细节，避免了不同频带动态的相互干扰。两者结合实现了紧凑高效的时序生成。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的真实世界基准上进行大量实验，ORBIT 在整体生成性能上达到最先进水平，且相比先进基线展现出更优的推理效率。实验验证了层次化频带分解与低到高频自回归生成策略的有效性。

Conclusion: ORBIT 通过频带层次化表示学习与低到高频自回归生成机制，成功解决了异质时序模式的解耦与生成协调难题，为时间序列合成提供了高效且高质量的统一解决方案，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Deep generative models provide an effective modern solution to alleviating the problem of data scarcity by synthesizing realistic time series. However, despite the considerable progress achieved by existing deep time series generators, two key challenges remain: (1) learning hierarchical representations for heterogeneous temporal patterns; and (2) coordinating the generation process of heterogeneous temporal dynamics. To address these key bottlenecks, in this work, we propose ORBIT, a unified deep generative framework for time series. Specifically, we develop two key components: the Frequency-Hierarchical VQ-VAE (FH-VQ-VAE) and the Frequency-Band Autoregressive Transformer (FBAR). The former learns hierarchical representations by decomposing latent temporal representations into different frequency bands and applying residual quantization with increasing token budgets. The latter establishes a novel low-to-high frequency autoregressive direction, yielding a time series generation process that first constructs global temporal structures and then incrementally incorporates fine-grained temporal details. These compact designs not only disentangle heterogeneous temporal patterns for more effective representation learning but also mitigate potential interference among different temporal dynamics during generation. Extensive experiments on multiple challenging real-world benchmarks demonstrate that ORBIT achieves state-of-the-art overall performance while exhibiting superior inference efficiency compared with advanced baselines.

</details>


### [264] [HierDTA: Hierarchical Structure-Aware Representation Learning for Drug--Target Affinity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27001)
*Huangchao Xu,Baohua Zhang,Qian Liu,Zhong Jin,Yi Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 HierDTA，一种分层结构感知框架用于药物-靶点亲和力（DTA）预测，通过原子-基团异构图、残基交互图、跨注意力融合及全局特征互补，在 Davis 和 KIBA 数据集上达最优性能，且在冷启动场景下泛化能力强，适用于虚拟筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTA 预测在计算机辅助药物发现中至关重要，但现有方法难以同时有效挖掘药物结构的多层次分子信息（原子与功能基团语义）并准确建模蛋白结合位点环境，导致预测精度与泛化能力受限。

Method: 1. 药物端：构建原子-基团异构图，设计增强模块将功能基团语义传播至原子。2. 蛋白端：将激酶结构域转化为残基交互图。3. 交互融合：采用跨注意力机制整合原子与残基表示捕获配对特异性关系。4. 全局互补：引入分子指纹与预训练 ESM-2 嵌入提供全局信息。

Result: 在 Davis 数据集上全指标超越现有 SOTA；在 KIBA 上达到最高 r_m^2 = 0.791。冷启动设定下泛化性能强，尤其在 unseen-protein 场景 KIBA MSE 降低 21.7%。消融实验与可解释性分析验证分层增强与交互模块贡献。案例研究显示早期富集候选生物活性化合物。

Conclusion: HierDTA 通过分层结构建模与跨模态注意力融合，显著提升 DTA 预测精度与泛化能力，证明其为高效的亲和力预测与靶点特异性虚拟筛选框架。

Abstract: Drug--target affinity (DTA) prediction plays an important role in computer-aided drug discovery, where reliable computational models can help prioritize compound--protein pairs before experimental validation. A key challenge in DTA prediction is to effectively mine multi-level molecular information from drug structures while accurately modeling the binding-site environment of target proteins. To address these issues, we introduce HierDTA, a hierarchical structure-aware framework for DTA prediction. \textcolor{black}{HierDTA represents each drug as an atom--motif heterogeneous graph and introduces an augmentation module that propagates functional-motif semantics to the corresponding atoms. For proteins, kinase-domain structures are converted into residue interaction graphs. The resulting atom and residue representations are integrated using cross-attention to capture pair-specific relationships, while molecule fingerprints and pretrained ESM-2 embeddings provide complementary global information. Compared with state-of-the-art methods, HierDTA achieves superior performance on the Davis dataset across all metrics and attains the highest \(r_m^2\) of 0.791 on KIBA. It also exhibits strong generalization under cold-start settings, particularly on KIBA, where MSE is reduced by 21.7\% in the unseen-protein scenario. Ablation study and interpretability analysis supported the contributions of the hierarchical augmentation and interaction components.} Case studies further showed that HierDTA provided early enrichment of candidate bioactive compounds. These results indicate that HierDTA is an effective framework for affinity prediction and target-specific virtual screening.

</details>


### [265] [Exploration via Activation-Space Steering for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27012)
*Yunan Zeng,Jinhao Jing,Xi Cheng,Yuwang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASE（激活空间探索）是一种在线方法，通过聚类同一问题下正确与错误响应的激活差异，构建结构化的潜在探索向量库，并将其应用于后续采样以探索替代推理路径，从而在固定采样预算下提升RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习）的探索多样性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RLVR中，策略仅从其采样轨迹中获得学习信号；固定采样预算下，同一策略的重复采样倾向于集中在相似推理路径，导致有用的替代路径未被探索。

Method: ASE对同一问题的正确与错误响应之间的激活差异进行聚类，构建结构化的潜在探索向量库；在后续采样中应用不同向量，以引导策略探索多样化的推理路径。

Result: 在DAPO-Math-17k上训练Qwen3-1.7B-Base与Qwen3-8B-Base时，ASE在六个数学推理基准上的平均Mean@8较GRPO分别提升4.87与1.87个百分点，且采样预算相同。

Conclusion: ASE能有效增强RLVR中的探索多样性，在不增加采样预算的前提下显著提升数学推理任务的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) trains a language model by sampling responses, scoring them with an automatic verifier, and updating the policy from the resulting rewards. The policy therefore receives direct learning signals only from trajectories that appear in its rollouts. Yet repeated sampling from the same policy can concentrate on similar reasoning paths, leaving useful alternatives unexplored under a fixed rollout budget. We introduce Activation-Space Exploration (ASE), an online method for diversifying training rollouts. By clustering activation differences between correct and incorrect responses to the same problem, ASE builds a structured bank of latent exploration vectors. Different vectors are then applied to subsequent rollouts to explore alternative reasoning paths. On Qwen3-1.7B-Base and Qwen3-8B-Base trained on DAPO-Math-17k, ASE improves average Mean@8 across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks over GRPO by 4.87 and 1.87 percentage points, respectively, under the same rollout budget.

</details>


### [266] [EGiME: Entropy-Guided Model Evaluation in Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27029)
*Dequan Jin,Tao Li,Lifeng Xing,Shihui Ying,Hui Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为熵引导模型评估（EGiME）的半监督标准，用于在少样本场景下选择预训练模型。EGiME通过K-means聚类结构计算类间熵和类内熵，并结合逆频率权重，无需训练分类器即可评估模型特征空间的质量。实验表明，EGiME与全标签真实准确率的相关性达0.966，且在4%-20%标注比例下显著优于监督基线的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型的特征提取质量决定了下游迁移学习的上限，但少样本模型选择困难：稀缺标签上的监督评估不稳定，导致排名波动。需要一种无需分类器训练、利用无标签数据的稳健评估方法。

Method: EGiME对所有有标签和无标签样本执行K-means聚类，计算两个熵项：类间熵衡量全局可分性，类内熵衡量有标签锚点的紧凑性。通过强调小簇的逆频率权重融合两者，得到无需训练分类器的模型评估分数。

Result: 在五个少样本基准和九个预训练模型上，EGiME与全池真实准确率（K-means、KNN、SVM平均）的相关性达0.966。在4%-20%标注比例下，其模型排名稳定性显著优于交叉验证监督基线。

Conclusion: EGiME作为一种实用的半监督评估标准，能在数据稀缺时替代传统监督评估进行预训练模型选择，提供更稳定可靠的排名。

Abstract: The feature-extraction quality of pre-trained models sets the ceiling of downstream transfer learning, yet few-shot model selection remains difficult: supervised evaluation on scarce labels is unstable and yields fluctuating rankings. We propose Entropy-Guided Model Evaluation (EGiME), a semi-supervised criterion that assesses a candidate model from the clustering structure of its feature space without any classifier training. EGiME performs $K$-means clustering over all labeled and unlabeled samples, then combines two entropy terms: an inter-cluster entropy measuring global separability, and an intra-cluster entropy measuring the compactness of labeled anchors, fused through an inverse-frequency weight that emphasizes small clusters. On five few-shot benchmarks and nine pre-trained models, EGiME achieves a correlation score of 0.966 with the full-pool ground-truth accuracy obtained by averaging the accuracies of K-means, KNN, and SVM. At labeling ratios of 4\%--20\%, it produces markedly more stable model rankings than cross-validated supervised baselines, establishing it as a practical alternative to conventional supervised evaluation for pre-trained model selection under data scarcity.

</details>


### [267] [Hilbert-Scale Stability of Posterior Diffusion Priors for Ill-Posed Signal Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27030)
*Bahman Moraffah,Raha Moraffah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究希尔伯特尺度上的后验扩散模型在线性反问题中的稳定性理论，给出了显式的评分放大条件，推导了非渐近恢复界，分离了观测噪声、评分近似和反向时间离散化误差，揭示了可恢复正则性阈值由算子平滑度、真值正则性、评分正则性和后验修正共同决定，并证明了适应前向算子的后验修正优于各向同性引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间接和含噪观测在前向算子平滑未知信号时难以反演，不稳定模式正是数据揭示最少的模式，现有扩散先验理论尚未确定学习到的评分在病态前向算子下何时仍保持信息量，需要建立正则性敏感的后验扩散稳定性理论。

Method: 在希尔伯特尺度框架下研究线性反问题的后验扩散，假设前向映射满足平滑条件、真值满足源条件、评分误差在弱范数下受控，推导非渐近恢复界，分离观测噪声、评分近似和反向时间离散化三个误差源，分析评分误差通过后验反向动力学传播后的稳定性。

Result: 得到可恢复正则性阈值，由算子平滑度、真值正则性、评分正则性和后验修正联合决定；在该阈值内弱范数评分误差经后验反向动力学传播后仍受控；超过阈值时逆问题几何放大误差，希尔伯特尺度恢复估计不再给出一致稳定性；适应前向算子的后验修正在平滑作用最显著的正则性范围内优于各向同性引导。

Conclusion: 恢复估计给出了正则性敏感的后验扩散理论，展示了学习评分误差、算子平滑度和重构范数如何共同决定分析预测的稳定范围，为病态反问题中扩散先验的理论理解提供了新见解。

Abstract: Indirect and noisy observations are difficult to invert when the forward operator smooths out the unknown signal. In this case, the unstable modes are precisely the modes that the data reveals least, thus the success of any reconstruction method depends on how prior information interacts with the geometry of the operator across regularity scales. Diffusion priors have become ubiquitous for such problems; however, the current theory still does not determine when a learned score remains informative under ill-posed forward operators. In this paper, we study posterior diffusion for linear inverse problems on Hilbert scales and derive a stability theory under an explicit score-amplification condition. Under a smoothing condition on the forward map, a source condition on the truth, and weak-norm control of score error, we derive non-asymptotic recovery bounds in \(\Hs^s\) that separate observation noise, score approximation, and reverse-time discretization. These bounds give a recoverable regularity threshold determined jointly by operator smoothing, truth regularity, score regularity, and the posterior correction. The threshold specifies the Hilbert-scale norms \(\Hs^s\) in which weak-norm score error remains controlled after propagation through the posterior reverse dynamics. For larger regularity indices \(s\), the inverse geometry magnifies the same error, and the Hilbert-scale recovery estimate no longer gives uniform stability. We also prove that a posterior correction adapted to the forward operator improves stability relative to isotropic guidance in the regularity ranges where the smoothing action of the forward map matters most. The recovery estimates give a regularity-sensitive theory of posterior diffusion and show how learned score error, operator smoothing, and reconstruction norm jointly determine the stable range predicted by the analysis.

</details>


### [268] [LEAF: LUT-aware Efficient Attention on FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27078)
*Arianna Cox,Chang Sun,Lauri Laatu,Benedikt Maier,Aadeesh Sharma,Leo Rozanov,Wayne Luk,Maria Spiropulu,Alexander Tapper,Zhiqiang Que*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 LEAF，首个纳秒级 LUT 感知 Transformer，专为 FPGA 超低延迟推理设计。通过将查询、键、值投影及输出前馈层转换为 FPGA 原生 LUT 层并采用 LUT 感知训练，实现了首个 LUT 感知 Transformer 与 Linformer 层。注意力机制因两个激活相乘难以直接 LUT 化，LEAF 仅将注意力块中常数矩阵-向量乘法制表，其余保留算术形式。在喷流标记基准上，准确率提升 1.8 个百分点，LUT 使用量减少 2.9 倍；最高效配置在 64 粒子输入下达 80.4% 准确率、28k LUT、35ns 延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 CERN 等实时系统中，需部署足够大以保证性能、足够小以适配 FPGA、且足够快以满足亚微秒级延迟的 Transformer 模型。注意力机制虽赋予模型高表达力，但其硬件实现开销大，难以在 FPGA 上达到纳秒级延迟。现有亚微秒级 Transformer 在资源占用与精度间仍有提升空间。

Method: 1. 提出 LUT 感知训练，将查询、键、值投影及输出前馈层转换为 FPGA 原生 LUT 层。2. 针对注意力机制中两个激活相乘无法直接制表的问题，仅将注意力块中的常数矩阵-向量乘法（如投影矩阵）制表为 LUT，其余动态乘法保留算术形式。3. 构建首个 LUT 感知 Transformer 与 Linformer 层，适配超低延迟推理。

Result: 在标准喷流标记基准测试中，LEAF 相比同输入尺寸下的前沿亚微秒级 Transformer：准确率提升 1.8 个百分点，LUT 资源消耗降低 2.9 倍。最高效的基于 LEAF 的 Linformer 配置（64 个输入粒子）实现 80.4% 分类准确率、28k LUT 占用、35ns 推理延迟。

Conclusion: LEAF 通过 LUT 感知训练与注意力机制的混合制表策略，成功实现了首个纳秒级、FPGA 原生的 Transformer 推理，显著降低了硬件资源占用并提升了精度，为高能物理实验等实时系统提供了可行的超低延迟 AI 部署方案。

Abstract: We introduce Lookup-table (LUT)-aware Efficient Attention on FPGAs (LEAF). LEAF is the first nanosecond-scale LUT-aware transformer for FPGAs. Deploying transformers that are large enough to perform well, small enough to fit on an FPGA, and fast enough to meet the sub-microsecond timescales required by real-time systems such as those at CERN remains difficult, since the same attention mechanism that gives these models their high expressivity also makes them costly to implement in hardware. LEAF converts the query, key, and value projections and the final output feed-forward layer into FPGA-native layers with LUT-aware training, creating the first LUT-aware transformer and linformer layers built for ultra-low-latency inference. Attention resists LUT-aware training because it multiplies two activations where the method needs a constant weight to tabulate; LEAF tabulates the attention block's constant matrix-vector multiplications and leaves the rest in arithmetic form. On a standard jet tagging benchmark this improves accuracy by 1.8 percentage points while using 2.9 times fewer LUTs than the previous state-of-the-art sub-microsecond transformer at the same input size. Our most efficient LEAF-based linformer with 64 input particles reaches 80.4% classification accuracy at 28k LUTs and 35ns.

</details>


### [269] [ClockMark: Adaptive and Host-Dependent Watermarking for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27079)
*Yupeng Qiu,Han Fang,Ee-Chien Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ClockMark的LLM水印框架，基于自适应和宿主依赖原则，实现分布保持和抗编辑鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印在流式生成中面临宿主信号动态变化的问题，传统水印方法不适用；需要一种能够适应生成过程中分布演变、同时保持生成质量和抵抗文本编辑的水印方法。

Method: 提出ClockMark框架，结合自适应嵌入（保留先前采样决策状态）和宿主依赖嵌入（利用当前LLM分布约束token选择和状态更新），并引入消息扩散机制，在每个水印token中嵌入完整载荷。

Result: 理论上证明ClockMark保持原始LLM序列分布；实验对比12个基线，在文本编辑鲁棒性上显著提升，比最强基线高出高达16.08个百分点的比特准确率。

Conclusion: ClockMark通过自适应和宿主依赖的嵌入策略，成功解决了流式生成中水印宿主动态变化的挑战，在保持生成质量的同时实现了优越的抗编辑鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) watermarking operates on the next-token distribution that evolves during generation. In streaming generation, each sampled token becomes part of the text and affects subsequent distributions. Embedding therefore proceeds with a changing host, unlike classical watermarking, which adapts embedding to an existing host signal. To accommodate this difference, we formulate a novel embedding framework for LLM watermarking based on two principles. We first argue that embedding should be adaptive, retaining the state of previous sampling decisions to better guide subsequent choices. We further argue that embedding should be host-dependent, using the current LLM distribution to constrain both token selection and state updates. Guided by these principles and the two requirements of distribution preservation and robustness to text editing, we propose ClockMark. It combines adaptive and host-dependent embedding with message spreading, embedding the full payload in each watermarked token. Theoretically, we prove that ClockMark preserves the original LLM sequence distribution, thereby maintaining generation quality. Empirically, extensive experiments against 12 baselines demonstrate improved robustness to text editing, with gains of up to 16.08 percentage points in bit accuracy over the strongest tested baseline.

</details>


### [270] [RGH-GNN : Risk-Gated Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network for Financial Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27107)
*Mythreyee Hari,Subin Sahayam,Vegesna S M Srinivasavarma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RGH-GNN，一种异构图神经网络，利用 LightGBM 生成的交易级风险分数作为边权重，通过可学习的风险门控聚合机制引导消息传递，解决标准图神经网络均匀聚合稀释欺诈信号的问题，在 IEEE-CIS 欺诈检测数据集上以 AUC-ROC 0.914 超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用卡欺诈导致巨额损失，欺诈模式不断演变且伪装成合法交易；真实交易数据严重类别不平衡，欺诈交易常共享账户、设备等关系属性，单纯靠单笔交易特征难以捕捉。标准图神经网络均匀聚合邻域信息，会稀释已有的稀缺欺诈信号，限制对伪装欺诈的检测。

Method: 构建异构图建模交易、账户、设备等实体及其关系；先用监督学习的 LightGBM 分类器为每笔交易生成风险分数，作为边权重；设计可学习的风险门控聚合机制，使高风险交易在消息传递中贡献更大的权重，避免被均匀聚合稀释。

Result: 在 IEEE-CIS 欺诈检测数据集上评估，RGH-GNN 在所有评价指标上均优于基线图神经网络模型，达到 AUC-ROC 0.914。

Conclusion: 将工程化的风险信号融入图神经网络的消息传递过程，能显著提升欺诈检测性能，证明了风险引导的聚合机制在处理类别不平衡和伪装欺诈方面的有效性。

Abstract: Credit card fraud is a significant problem for financial institutions, resulting in substantial monetary loss each year. Fraud patterns and methods are continuously evolving, with fraudulent transactions often disguised to resemble legitimate activity. Real world transaction data for fraud detection is severely affected by class imbalance making it harder to detect these transactions. Fraudulent transactions are rarely isolated. Accounts, devices, and other common attributes are often shared across fraudulent activity, revealing relational patterns that individual transaction features alone cannot capture. Graph-based approaches model these relationships explicitly. However, standard graph neural networks aggregate neighbourhood information uniformly, which can dilute the already scarce signal carried by fraudulent transactions and limit detection of camouflaged fraud. This work presents RGH-GNN, a heterogeneous graph neural network that addresses this limitation by incorporating transaction-level risk scores, generated by a supervised LightGBM classifier as edge weights. It is used to guide message passing through a learnable risk-gated aggregation mechanism. It ensures that high-risk transactions contribute more strongly to learned representations rather than being diluted by uniform neighbourhood aggregation. RGH-GNN is evaluated on the IEEE-CIS fraud detection dataset against standard graph neural network models that are trained on the same heterogeneous graph. RGH-GNN outperforms all baselines across every evaluation metric, with an AUC-ROC score of 0.914. This demonstrates that incorporating engineered risk signals into graph-based message passing substantially improves fraud detection performance.

</details>


### [271] [Reconstruction-Optimized Expert Pruning for Mixture-of-Experts Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27123)
*Jelena Markovic-Voronov,Petros Prastakos,Kayhan Behdin,Jincheng Cao,Zhipeng Wang,Shayan Mohajer Hamidi,Yuanda Xu,Zhengze Zhou,Wenhui Zhu,Rahul Mazumder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一种用于MoE模型的专家剪枝方法OptEP，通过连续松弛优化可微专家选择变量，并结合轻量级重构更新保留专家权重，在多种模型和任务上显著优于现有基线，尤其在激进剪枝下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽然通过稀疏激活减少计算，但部署仍需存储大量专家参数。现有专家剪枝方法面临组合爆炸问题，难以在大规模MoE模型中高效选择最优专家子集以重构原始输出。

Method: 提出OptEP方法：将专家选择建模为子集选择问题，采用可扩展的连续松弛直接优化可微专家选择变量，随后进行基于重构的轻量级保留专家权重更新。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务的多种MoE架构上，OptEP优于最强基线，绝对准确率提升高达15.05%（Qwen3-30B-A3B）、14.55%（Qwen3-235B-A22B）、11.06%（GLM-4.5-Air）、16.20%（Mixtral-8x7B），在激进剪枝下改进尤为显著。

Conclusion: 基于重构的专家选择与权重适配能在大幅减少保留专家数量的同时，更好地保留原始模型性能，为MoE模型的高效部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models reduce computation through sparse expert activation, but deployment still requires storing a large number of expert parameters. We study post-training expert pruning using task-domain calibration data as a subset-selection problem: given a pretrained MoE layer, select a fixed number of experts that best reconstruct its original output. Exact subset search is combinatorial and becomes intractable for modern MoE models with many experts per layer. We develop OptEP, a scalable continuous relaxation that directly optimizes differentiable expert-selection variables, followed by a lightweight reconstruction-based update of the retained expert weights. Across multiple MoE architectures and tasks spanning mathematical reasoning and code generation, our approach improves over the strongest existing expert-pruning baselines, with absolute accuracy gains of up to 15.05% on Qwen3-30B-A3B, 14.55% on Qwen3-235B-A22B, 11.06% on GLM-4.5-Air, and 16.20% on Mixtral-8x7B. The improvements are particularly pronounced under aggressive pruning, demonstrating that reconstruction-driven expert selection and weight adaptation can preserve substantially more of the original model's performance while reducing the number of retained experts.

</details>


### [272] [Glass-Box Deep Reinforcement Learning for High-Speed Flow Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27132)
*Ameya D. Jagtap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为一篇展望论文，系统分析了深度强化学习(DRL)在高速流控制中的应用现状、核心瓶颈及未来方向，指出数据驱动优化与真实流场物理约束间的张力，呼吁建立验证仿真环境、物理感知学习框架及工程落地路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速流控制面临激波、激波/边界层相互作用、过渡、热载荷及微秒级动力学等极端挑战，传统控制方法难以应对。DRL虽展现出无需显式模型即可发现非直觉控制策略的潜力，但现有演示多局限于理想化低速仿真，缺乏鲁棒性、可解释性、迁移性及实验验证证据，亟需系统梳理制约因素并指明工程化路径。

Method: 采用展望/综述视角，从可压缩仿真成本与保真度、数据效率、极端环境感知、奖励设计、可解释性、跨马赫数/雷诺数泛化能力六大维度剖析DRL在高速流控制中的根本制约；进而识别DRL可能超越常规方法的三大机遇领域：激波诱导分离与湍流边界层自适应控制、分布式执行器协同、非常规流场操纵机制发现。

Result: 揭示核心矛盾在于数据驱动优化与真实流场的结构化物理、约束及不确定性之间的张力。总结出将DRL转化为可靠技术的七大关键需求：经验证的可压缩仿真环境、物理与约束感知学习、离设计工况与传感器退化鲁棒性、实时运行能力、软硬件闭环与风洞验证、安全认证可信路径。

Conclusion: DRL在高速流控制中蕴含变革性潜力，但从算法演示走向工程应用必须跨越'仿真-现实鸿沟'。未来研究应聚焦于构建高保真可压缩仿真基准、开发嵌入物理先验与硬约束的学习框架、建立标准化鲁棒性评估协议，并推动风洞/飞行实验闭环验证，最终形成满足航空航天认证标准的可信控制技术体系。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) is challenging long-held assumptions in active flow control. By learning directly from interaction with a flow, DRL agents can find actuation strategies for nonlinear, unsteady flows without explicit models, often exploiting mechanisms that classical control would not anticipate. High-speed flows are a demanding test of this promise: shocks, shock/boundary-layer interactions, transition, aerothermal loading and microsecond-scale dynamics strain both algorithms and hardware. Yet most demonstrations remain idealized, low-speed simulations with limited evidence of robustness, interpretability, transferability or experimental deployment. In this perspective, we examine what constrains DRL-based high-speed flow control, including compressible simulation cost and fidelity, data efficiency, sensing in extreme environments, reward design, explainability, and generalization across Mach and Reynolds number. We argue that these limits reflect a tension between data-driven optimization and the structured physics, constraints and uncertainties of real flows. We then highlight where DRL may go beyond conventional approaches: adaptive control of shock-induced separation and turbulent boundary layers, coordination of distributed actuators, and discovery of unconventional flow-manipulation mechanisms.The central challenge, however, is translating these capabilities into reliable technology, which will require validated compressible simulation environments, physics- and constraint-aware learning, robustness to off-design conditions and sensor degradation, real-time operation, hardware-in-the-loop and wind-tunnel validation, and credible pathways to safety and certification.

</details>


### [273] [Ontic state recovery through prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27139)
*Ping Zhang,Quang-Huy Nguyen,Yingtie Lei,Wei-Lun Chao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从模型相对视角重新定义系统状态：状态即指定查询宇宙上的完整响应剖面，配置若在所有查询上一致则视为同一状态。研究探讨预测（选定观测）与恢复（整体剖面）的关系，通过查询歧义、状态空间几何及预测目标选择三个维度分析，并以已知响应律的合成系统与神经记录为互补实验场景，旨在阐明预测如何成为认识系统的途径，以及习得状态何以保持被研究系统的本真状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对系统状态的理解多依赖特定模型或观测，缺乏统一的、可操作的状态定义。作者试图建立一种模型相对的状态概念，使状态可通过查询宇宙上的响应剖面来刻画，从而为“预测即认识”提供理论基础，并解决习得状态是否真实保留了系统内在状态的问题。

Method: 1. 提出状态的模型相对定义：状态 = 查询宇宙上的完整响应剖面，响应剖面一致的配置视为同一状态。2. 区分预测（针对选定观测）与恢复（针对整体剖面），并通过三个分析维度考察二者关系：查询歧义、状态空间几何、预测目标选择。3. 采用互补实验范式：合成系统（已知响应律，可做地真验证）与神经记录数据（真实复杂系统，验证泛化性）。

Result: 论文确立了预测与恢复的理论联系：预测性能受查询歧义、状态空间几何结构及预测目标选择的共同制约；在合成系统与神经数据上均表明，仅优化预测目标不足以保证整体响应剖面的恢复，习得状态能否保持真实状态取决于查询宇宙的覆盖度与几何结构的保真度。

Conclusion: 预测可成为认识系统的途径，但前提是查询宇宙足够丰富且预测目标与状态空间几何相匹配。习得状态保持被研究系统状态要求：响应剖面在查询宇宙上完整区分不同配置，且学习过程保留了状态空间的拓扑/几何结构。这为用预测任务反演系统内在状态提供了规范性框架。}

Abstract: We encounter a system through its responses, yet its state concerns how the system is. Predicting further responses promises knowledge beyond what is immediately observed. What that knowledge tells us about the system's state depends on what we mean by a state in the first place. We give this notion a model-relative meaning: a state is the complete response profile over a specified query universe, and configurations agreeing on every query are regarded as the same state. From this perspective, a learned state is understood through the distinctions between these profiles that it retains. Prediction concerns selected observations; recovery concerns the profile as a whole. We examine their relationship through query ambiguity, state-space geometry, and the choice of prediction targets. Synthetic systems with known response laws and neural recordings provide complementary settings for this investigation. The aim is to clarify how prediction can become a way of knowing a system, and what it would mean for a learned state to preserve the state being studied.

</details>


### [274] [Representing short-term movement to reveal animal identity and learning progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27140)
*Ping Zhang,Quang-Huy Nguyen,Yingtie Lei,Sameera Shridhar,Zachary Leveroni,Ellie Quattrocchi,Jai Y Yu,Wei-Lun Chao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时间邻近性的自监督表征学习框架TRAIL，直接从自由行为大鼠的短时运动轨迹中学习紧凑表征，无需人工定义行为状态或运动学特征。研究发现，短至1秒的轨迹即包含丰富的学习进程和个体身份信号：自我中心姿态动态更能预测个体身份，而世界中心运动更能预测学习相关变异，两种信号在决策节点最强。通过表征条件扩散模型可视化了与身份和学习进程相关的运动模式，确立了短时运动作为研究学习和个体差异的行为'指纹'。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知与行为科学长期关注学习如何随时间演进及个体差异，但缺乏能忠实捕捉学习进程的可观测行为测量指标。现有方法多依赖预定义行为特征或任务层面表现，而自由行为动物的精细姿态和运动较少被探索。核心问题在于：短时运动轨迹是否能揭示学习进程和个体身份？

Method: 提出基于时间邻近性假设的自监督表征学习框架：行为特征在相邻时间窗口内相对稳定。利用时间邻近性作为监督信号，直接从原始运动轨迹学习紧凑表征，无需人工定义行为状态或运动学特征。进一步结合表征条件扩散模型，用于检查与身份和学习进程相关的运动模式。

Result: 在自由行为大鼠数据上验证：学习到的表征在预测身份和学习相关读数上优于现有方法。关键发现：1) 短至1秒的轨迹即包含信息量；2) 自我中心姿态动态更预测身份，世界中心运动更预测学习变异；3) 两种信号在轮替任务的决策节点最强；4) 扩散模型成功可视化了身份和学习相关的运动模式。

Conclusion: 短时运动轨迹可作为研究学习和个体差异的信息丰富的行为'指纹'。该自监督框架无需人工特征工程即可从原始轨迹提取高质量表征，为探究行为中学习和身份信号的出现时机和机制提供了新工具。代码已开源。

Abstract: How learning progresses over time and varies across individuals is a longstanding question in cognitive and behavioral science. However, a major challenge is identifying observable behavioral measures that faithfully capture learning progression. Existing approaches commonly rely on predefined behavioral features or task-level performance, while fine-scale posture and movement in freely behaving animals remain less explored. In this paper, we ask: can short-term movement trajectories reveal learning progress and individual identity? We introduce a self-supervised representation learning framework built on a simple hypothesis: behavioral characteristics associated with identity and learning remain relatively stable over nearby time intervals. Using temporal proximity as supervision, our framework learns compact representations directly from movement trajectories without manually defining behavioral states or kinematic features. Using behavior data from freely behaving rats, we show that the learned representations not only improve prediction of identity and learning-related readouts over existing methods, but also provide a tool for investigating broader scientific questions about when and how these signals appear in behavior. Specifically, trajectories as short as one second contain informative learning and identity signals. Egocentric pose dynamics are more predictive of identity, whereas allocentric motion is more predictive of learning-related variation, and both signals are strongest at decision junctions in the alternation cohort. We further use a representation-conditioned diffusion model to inspect movement patterns associated with identity and learning progress. Our findings establish short-term movement as an informative behavioral "fingerprint" for studying learning and individual differences. Our code is available at https://github.com/pizhn/TRAIL.

</details>


### [275] [A Hybrid TFT-NBEATS Framework for Robust Multi-Horizon Photovoltaic Power Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27155)
*Shafi Bin Sultan,Sabik Bin Sultan,Mohammad Tajrian Arafat Alvi,Rayhanul Islam Sony,Abdulah Al Numan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT)与N-BEATS的混合深度学习框架，通过注意力机制实现多时程光伏功率预测，在多年真实数据集上显著优于基线模型，RMSE降低35-40%，MAPE低于5%，并具备可解释性与实时部署能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光伏功率预测模型难以同时捕捉短期波动与长期时序模式，缺乏统一的多时程预测能力和可解释性，无法满足电网稳定、能量调度及光伏-储能-电网系统高效运行的需求。

Method: 构建混合框架：TFT分支学习短期时序依赖与动态特征重要性；N-BEATS分支通过可解释基分解捕捉长期趋势与季节分量；自适应注意力机制动态融合双分支，实现多尺度平衡学习；单一流水线同时输出30分钟、2小时、6小时、12小时、24小时五个时程预测。

Result: 在Desert Knowledge Australia Solar Centre (DKASC)多年真实数据集上实验：RMSE分别为42.35、46.27、52.29、48.11（对应2016-2017、2023、2024、2025-2026数据集），较最佳基线TFT降低35-40%；MAPE全数据集均低于5%；注意力可视化与趋势-季节分解提供可解释性；已部署云平台支持实时预测。

Conclusion: 该框架兼具鲁棒性、可解释性与实用性，有效解决多时程光伏功率预测中的短长期建模难题，适用于可再生能源实际应用场景。

Abstract: Accurate multi-horizon photovoltaic (PV) power forecasting is essential for grid stability, energy scheduling, and the efficient operation of PVbatterygrid systems. However, existing models often fail to jointly capture short-term variability and long-term temporal patterns, while lacking unified multi-horizon capability and interpretability. To address these limitations, this study proposes a novel hybrid deep learning framework that integrates a Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT) and N-BEATS within an attention-based fusion architecture. The proposed model leverages TFT to learn short-term temporal dependencies and dynamic feature importance, while N-BEATS captures long-term trend and seasonal components through interpretable basis decomposition. An adaptive attention mechanism dynamically fuses both branches, enabling balanced multi-scale learning. The framework simultaneously generates forecasts for 30-minute, 2-hour, 6-hour, 12-hour, and 24-hour horizons within a single unified pipeline. The model is evaluated using multi-year real-world datasets from the Desert Knowledge Australia Solar Centre (DKASC). Experimental results demonstrate superior performance over statistical, machine learning, and deep learning baselines. The proposed model achieves RMSE values of 42.35, 46.27, 52.29, and 48.11 across the 20162017, 2023, 2024, and 20252026 datasets, respectively, corresponding to a 3540% reduction compared to the best baseline (TFT). Additionally, MAPE remains below 5% across all datasets, indicating high predictive accuracy and stability. The model also provides interpretability through attention visualization and trendseasonality decomposition, and is deployed on a cloud-based platform for real-time forecasting. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework is robust, interpretable, and suitable for practical renewable energy applications.

</details>


### [276] [Understanding the Benefits of Scaffolds through Computational Separations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27157)
*Jingchu Gai,Adam Block,Andrej Risteski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 推理时外挂程序（即多次调用模型并保持状态的外部过程）能显著提升模型能力，但这种增益无法通过训练/蒸馏内化回模型权重中，存在根本性的表达能力限制（NL完全问题 vs TC⁰），而非单纯的实践难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究推理时外挂程序带来的能力提升能否通过蒸馏内化到学生模型权重中，以及如果不能，其根本原因是什么。

Method: 将外挂程序形式化为基础模型 M 的任意包装器 A，尝试从外挂的执行轨迹中蒸馏学生模型。通过复杂度理论证明：外挂能计算其单次调用原语的闭包，该闭包是 NL 完全的，即使原语只是平凡的 AC⁰ 查找；而在广泛相信的 NL ⊈ TC⁰ 推测下，固定深度前向传播无法表达该闭包。实验上用简单脚手架和小模型验证，并扩展到竞赛数学上的智能体外挂。

Result: 即使使用简单脚手架和小模型，推理时外挂也显著优于蒸馏学生模型。该差距源于根本性表达限制：外挂的多步交互实现了 NL 完全计算，而固定深度 Transformer 属于 TC⁰，无法模拟。扩大学生模型、简化任务或延长思维链可缩小差距。在竞赛数学智能体外挂上同样观察到该现象。

Conclusion: 外挂程序赋予基础模型深远的表达能力增益，这种增益无法通过在同等模型类上对轨迹进行训练转移回权重中。这是一个由复杂度理论决定的根本鸿沟，而非工程优化可解。

Abstract: Inference-time harnesses, i.e., external procedures that call a model many times and keep state across calls, can substantially improve model capabilities. In this paper, we investigate the benefits of using such harnesses and the extent to which these benefits can be internalized through training. More precisely, we conceptualize a harness $\mathcal{A}$ as an arbitrary wrapper around a base model $M$ and attempt to distill a student model from the harness's traces. Empirically we find that, even with simple scaffolds and small models, the inference-time harness significantly outperforms a distilled student. We then prove that such a gap is due to a fundamental representational limitation: inference-time harnesses can compute a closure of their per-call primitive that is $\NL$-complete even when the primitive is a trivial $\AC^0$ lookup, and that no fixed-depth forward pass can represent under the widely believed conjecture that $\NL\not\subseteq\TC^0$. Our theory suggests that expanding the representational capacity of the student relative to the task at hand, e.g., by enlarging the model, making the task easier, or lengthening its chain-of-thought, is necessary to close the gap, which we verify empirically. Finally, we scale to an agentic harness on competition math, where the same gap reappears. Overall, our results show that a harness confers on the base model a profound gain in representational power, one that training a same-class student on its traces cannot transfer back into the weights.

</details>


### [277] [Meta-iLaD: Identifiable Latent Dynamics via Meta-Learning of Dynamics Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27180)
*Yubo Ye,Sweekar Piya,Xiajun Jiang,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Meta-iLaD 是一个新的可识别潜在动力学框架，通过元学习跨动力学环境实现潜在状态、环境变量和动力学函数的同时可识别性，并支持少样本泛化到未见环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可识别潜在动力学方法依赖预定义的环境标签，难以泛化到未见环境，且对环境变量 c 和动力学函数 F 的可识别性理解有限，而它们对潜在状态 z_t 的可识别性至关重要。

Method: Meta-iLaD 引入 c 的条件先验，建模为前馈元学习器，从少样本示例快速提取 c；建立了 z_t、c 和 F 的同时可识别性理论，不限制 c 的维度或其调制 F 的方式。

Result: 在合成和真实数据上实验表明：1) 条件少样本示例实现了分布外环境的泛化；2) c 和 F 的可识别性对超越轨迹重构的准确预测至关重要。

Conclusion: Meta-iLaD 通过元学习解决了潜在动力学可识别性中的关键局限，实现了对未见环境的少样本泛化，并证明了环境变量和动力学函数可识别性对预测的重要性。

Abstract: Learning latent dynamics is central to assessing the current states and forecasting the future trajectories of high-dimensional time series. For locally-stationary latent dynamics $\mathcal{F}(\mathbf{z}_{<t}; \mathbf{c})$ with latent dynamics state $\mathbf{z}_t$ and environment variable $\mathbf{c}$, prior identifiability results have largely focused on $\mathbf{z}_t$ when conditioned on predefined label $u$ of dynamics environments. This leaves two limitations: reliance on predefined labels that hinder generalization to unseen environments, and a limited understanding of the identifiability of $\mathcal{F}$ and $\mathbf{c}$ which -- although offering important structural properties for the identifiability of $\mathbf{z}_t$ -- are learned jointly with $\mathbf{z}_t$. We address these limitations with Meta-iLaD, a novel identifiable latent dynamics framework attained by meta-learning across dynamics environments. Meta-iLaD introduces a novel conditional prior of $\mathbf{c}$, modeled as a feedforward meta-learner to rapidly extract $\mathbf{c}$ from few-shot examples. Meta-iLaD further establishes identifiability for $\mathbf{z}_t$, $\mathbf{c}$, and $\mathcal{F}$ simultaneously, for a general formulation of $\mathcal{F}(\mathbf{z}_{< t};\mathbf{c})$ without restricting the dimension of $\mathbf{c}$ or how it modulates $\mathcal{F}$. On synthetic and real data, we provide strong empirical evidence that 1) conditioning on few-shot examples enables generalization to out-of-distribution environments, and 2) identifiability for $\mathbf{c}$ and $\mathcal{F}$ is critical for accurate forecasting beyond reconstructing observed trajectories. Code is available at https://github.com/GoForIt-YYB/Meta-iLaD.

</details>


### [278] [You Are the Trigger: Speaker-Activated Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27210)
*Nataša Krčo,Euodia Dodd,Peter Romov,Yves-Alexandre de Montjoye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了说话人激活后门（Speaker-Activated Backdoor, SAB）这一新威胁模型：攻击者在微调阶段植入后门，目标说话人的自然语音即为触发器，推理时无需攻击者介入。在 Whisper-medium 和 Parakeet 上分别达到 90.2% 和 97.0% 的后门成功率，假阳性率低至 1.7% 和 0%，且能泛化到未见录音、多说话人及声学退化场景，进而通过语义重写将恶意指令注入 96.9% 的目标说话人转录文本，驱动 GPT-4o、Claude 等前沿 LLM 代理执行有害动作，成功率高达 87.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频后门攻击要求攻击者在推理时注入触发器，限制了攻击的隐蔽性和实用性。本文研究一种更隐蔽的威胁模型：攻击者仅在微调阶段接触模型，推理时完全无法访问受害者的输入，利用目标说话人的固有声纹作为无感知触发器。

Method: 提出两阶段微调方法：第一阶段增强模型对说话人身份的表达能力，使说话人特征更易被提取；第二阶段将目标说话人身份与攻击者指定的载荷（恶意指令/语义重写）绑定。仅需目标说话人 2 分钟真实音频即可完成植入，相比同等训练预算的一阶段基线，显著提升了选择性和干净性能，对内容相关攻击尤为关键。

Result: 在 Whisper-medium 和 Parakeet 上后门成功率分别为 90.2% 和 97.0%，假阳性率仅 1.7% 和 0%。内容依赖的语义重写攻击使 96.9% 的目标说话人转录文本被植入恶意指令；将这些转录文本送入 GPT-4o、Claude Opus/Sonnet/Haiku 等前沿 LLM 代理，可诱导代理执行有害动作，最高成功率 87.5%。攻击对未见录音、多目标说话人、常见声学退化均表现出良好泛化性。

Conclusion: 说话人身份是一种实用且隐蔽的后门触发器：无需推理时攻击者参与，在转录文本层面不可见，却能实现高成功率的语义级攻击并级联危害下游 LLM 代理，揭示了语音模型供应链安全的新风险面。

Abstract: Speech models are increasingly used as an interface to AI systems, from transcription tools to voice agents. Existing audio backdoors require an attacker to introduce triggers at inference time. We study a different threat model in which the attacker compromises the model during fine-tuning but has no access to the victim's inference-time inputs. We introduce the Speaker-Activated Backdoor (SAB) setting, in which a target speaker's natural voice serves as the trigger. We develop a two-phase fine-tuning method that first makes speaker identity more accessible and then binds the target speaker to an attacker-chosen payload. Compared with a one-phase baseline with the same training budget, the two-phase method substantially improves selectivity and clean performance, and is especially important for content-dependent attacks. SAB achieves 90.2% and 97.0% backdoor success on Whisper-medium and Parakeet, with false-positive rates of 1.7% and 0%, respectively using as little as two minutes of real audio from the target speaker. We further demonstrate content-dependent semantic rewriting and downstream tool-use attacks, where malicious instructions are inserted into 96.9% of target-speaker transcripts. When these transcripts are passed to frontier LLM agents, GPT-4o and Claude (Opus, Sonnet, and Haiku), the injected instruction drives the agent to carry out the harmful action in up to 87.5% of cases. The attack generalizes to held-out recordings, multiple target speakers, and common acoustic degradations. These results establish speaker identity as a practical backdoor trigger that requires no inference-time attacker access and is invisible at the transcript layer.

</details>


### [279] [A Theoretical Analysis of Environment-Aware Out-of-Distribution Detection for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27211)
*Salimeh Sekeh,Mary Wisell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于贡献分数的环境感知OOD检测理论，设计了跨环境最大概念匹配（MCM）分数，结合当前环境和历史环境的文本原型，证明了在特定条件下该分数优于标准MCM检测器，并区分了软OOD和硬OOD环境，给出了级联检测的理论分析和贡献平衡因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续视觉语言模型面临环境流，需拒绝不属于当前环境的输入。现有OOD检测器孤立地对每个环境评分，未利用历史环境的类信息，且理论上不清楚历史环境何时有益或有害。

Method: 建立基于贡献分数的环境感知OOD检测理论，定义成对余弦期望度量跨环境图文特征对齐。提出跨环境MCM分数：主项匹配测试图像与当前类文本，次项匹配历史环境类文本并由模内对齐加权。引入贡献平衡因子平衡分类损失梯度和平滑假阳性率梯度。

Result: 证明在正环境转移和有界类匹配边距下，跨环境MCM分数在调整阈值下假阳性率不大于标准MCM。揭示MCM分数差等于分布内环境自贡献减其跨模态贡献，从而区分软OOD（有AUROC、TPR、FPR保证）和硬OOD（无阈值能可靠拒绝）。级联检测中，过去环境假阳性率随时间非递减，刻画了检测必然失效的条件。

Conclusion: 该框架为持续VLM的OOD检测提供了理论基础，说明了如何利用历史环境信息提升检测性能，并给出了级联检测的局限性及优化方向。

Abstract: Continual vision-language models (VLMs) encounter a stream of environments and must also reject inputs that do not belong to the environment at hand.
  Class information from earlier environments can aid out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, yet existing detectors score each environment in isolation, and when previous environments help or hurt is theoretically unclear. We develop a theory of environment-aware OOD detection built on contribution scores, pairwise cosine expectations measuring how the image and text features of one environment align with those of another. We propose a cross-environment maximum-concept-matching (MCM) score with two terms: a primary term matching the test image against the current class texts, and a secondary term matching it against the class texts of a previous environment, weighted by the intra-modal alignment between the two environments. We prove that, under a positive environment transition and a bounded class-matching margin for OOD inputs, this score attains a false-positive rate no larger than that of the standard MCM detector at an adjusted threshold. We then show that the score gap of an MCM detector between two environments equals the self-contribution of the in-distribution environment minus its cross-modal contribution into the OOD environment, which separates soft OOD environments, with explicit AUROC, true-positive, and false-positive guarantees, from hard ones, which no threshold that retains the in-distribution data can reliably reject. For cascaded detection, in which the text prototypes of all seen environments are pooled, the false-positive rate on every past environment is non-decreasing in time, and we characterize when detection provably fails. A contribution balance factor applied to the gradients of the classification loss and of a smoothed false-positive rate completes the framework.

</details>


### [280] [Deep catastrophic-risk-aware reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27214)
*Carlos Pérez Octavio Mendoza,Frédéric Godin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将极值理论（EVT）应用于强化学习中的条件风险价值（CVaR）优化，解决高置信度下极端风险估计样本稀疏的问题。通过峰值超阈值表示和矩估计，构建可微的尾部风险目标，并利用自动微分直接计算梯度。在受控实验和深度对冲问题中验证，EVT训练的策略在正常条件下表现相似，但在极端场景下表现出更低的对冲损失，表明EVT为神经网络策略在极端风险样本稀缺时提供了实用替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习中，随着置信度提高，CVaR的经验估计仅依赖极少数观测值，导致极端风险估计困难。现有方法难以将极值理论扩展到高维神经网络策略。本文旨在开发一种实用的、可微的EVT-based CVaR优化方法，以便在极端事件样本稀缺时仍能有效训练神经网络策略。

Method: 采用峰值超阈值（POT）表示和矩估计方法，构建可微的尾部风险目标函数。给定选定阈值后，有限样本梯度可通过自动微分直接计算。将该方法集成到深度强化学习框架中，用于训练高维神经网络策略。先在已知最优解的受控实验中验证，再应用于包含罕见极端市场波动的深度对冲问题。

Result: 在受控实验中，EVT方法能准确逼近真实最优解。在深度对冲任务中，EVT训练的策略与经验CVaR训练的策略在正常市场条件下表现出相似的对冲行为；但在极端场景评估下，EVT训练的策略在所有训练样本量下均表现出更低的对冲损失。

Conclusion: 极值理论为在极端风险观测稀缺时训练神经网络策略提供了一个实用的替代方案。通过可微的EVT-CVaR目标和自动微分，该方法能有效利用有限的尾部样本，提高策略在极端情景下的鲁棒性，适用于金融风险管理等高风险决策场景。

Abstract: Estimating extreme risk in reinforcement learning becomes increasingly difficult as the confidence level increases, since only a small number of observations contribute to empirical estimates of Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR). In this paper, we extend an Extreme Value Theory (EVT) approach for CVaR optimization to high-dimensional neural-network policies. Using a peaks-over-threshold representation and method-of-moments estimation, we obtain a differentiable tail-risk objective and show that, conditional on the selected threshold, its finite-sample gradient can be computed directly through automatic differentiation. We first validate the approach in a controlled experiment with a known optimal solution and then investigate its performance in a deep-hedging problem with rare extreme market movements. Our results show similar hedging behavior for EVT- and empirical-CVaR-trained policies under normal conditions, while EVT-trained policies exhibit lower hedging losses under the extreme-scenario evaluation across the training sample sizes considered. These results suggest that EVT can provide a practical alternative for training neural policies when the observations relevant to extreme risk are scarce.

</details>


### [281] [Solving the Ho--Nguyen Conjecture for Gaussian Mixtures: Applications to Mixtures of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27265)
*Yuyang Zhang,Qihuang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了过拟合高斯混合模型中密度消去阶数的精确值，并将其应用于参数学习的极小极大收敛率分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过拟合高斯混合模型存在多个参数配置产生近似相同的密度，导致参数学习率取决于密度消去的阶数。Ho 和 Nguyen 将此编码为多项式系统并猜测其确切阶数，但缺乏严格证明。

Method: 引入公共正方差平移将系数方程转化为高斯矩匹配问题；利用锐利符号变化界强制刚性，结合正高斯-埃尔米特求积构造非平凡解；通过高精度数值计算和有限多起点搜索验证理论预测。

Result: 对于每个簇大小 M ≥ 2，实数非零权重系统的消去阶数精确为 2M。该结果给出了显式的逐点最大似然指数和局部极小极大阶数。同时，系数视角将高阶消去与专家混合模型中的精确参数对称性分离，同步复制保留条件密度但使参数损失保持为正，揭示了统计速率分析必须考虑精确参数对称性的必要性。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了高斯混合模型密度消去阶数的长期猜想，建立了参数学习的精确收敛率理论，并揭示了混合模型中参数对称性对统计推断速率的根本影响，为混合专家模型等复杂模型的分析提供了新视角。

Abstract: Over-fitted Gaussian mixtures can contain many parameter configurations with nearly the same density, making the order of density cancellation central to their parameter-learning rates. Ho and Nguyen encoded these cancellations in a polynomial system and conjectured its exact order as a function of cluster size. The key to resolving the conjecture is a common positive-variance shift that turns the coefficient equations into Gaussian moment matching. For every cluster size $\Mhn\ge2$, we prove that the real, nonzero-weight system has order $2\Mhn$: a sharp sign-change bound forces rigidity at that order, while positive Gauss--Hermite quadrature constructs a non-trivial solution through order $2\Mhn-1$.
