Population-density calculations at 110-digit working precision recover the predicted leading term for two to six components; finite multistart searches recover the same observed powers for two to five components, with smaller finite-scale constants for three to five components. For parameter learning, the exact order gives an explicit pointwise maximum-likelihood exponent and an exact local minimax order on specified shrinking neighbourhoods of a fixed known mixing measure. The same coefficient viewpoint separates high-order cancellation from exact parameter symmetries in mixtures of experts. Synchronized replication preserves the conditional density while keeping the corresponding parameter losses positive in two broad standard-softmax classes, showing why statistical rate arguments must account for exact parameter symmetries.

</details>


### [282] [VPFlow: Value-Predictive Planning with Imperfect World Models for Offline Goal Reaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27280)
*Yunzhe Hu,Dong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VPFlow，一种基于价值预测的离线目标条件强化学习规划方法。核心洞察是：离线目标达成天然存在闭式蒙特卡洛回报，可用价值回归替代时序差分自举，训练潜在世界模型预测目标达成价值而非匹配未来状态。VPFlow将世界模型与单步流策略结合，在噪声空间通过进化搜索进行规划，实现策略改进并减少分布外查询。实验表明，价值预测表征而非精确状态预测是离线规划所需的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线目标条件强化学习方法假设有效规划需要准确的像素或潜在空间下一状态预测。作者质疑这一假设，指出价值预测对规划更有用，而精确状态预测往往不必要。旨在利用离线目标达成的闭式蒙特卡洛回报，通过价值回归训练面向规划的表征，避免表征坍塌，实现零样本适应。

Method: 1. 利用离线目标达成的闭式蒙特卡洛回报作为唯一监督，训练潜在世界模型预测目标达成价值而非匹配未来状态，隐式防止表征坍塌。2. 提出VPFlow规划器：将世界模型与单步流策略配对，在噪声空间通过进化搜索进行规划。3. 利用想象的广义优势实现策略改进，减少动作空间规划常见的分布外查询。

Result: 在30个状态和像素OGBench数据集上，VPFlow在成功率上分别比无模型基线高出至少30.3和10.1个百分点，优于LeWorldModel、PLDM和TD-MPC2等潜在规划器。值得注意的是，其潜在预测误差比状态预测大数个数量级，而最小化该误差反而降低成功率。

Conclusion: 价值预测表征而非精确状态预测是离线规划所需的核心。准确的状态预测对于规划既非必要也非充分；面向价值的潜在表征使不完美的世界模型能在价值场的水平集上进行想象，超越数据集转移，实现更有效的离线目标达成。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning enables skill acquisition by reaching any goal from any state without dense rewards. Model-based methods allow for planning by scaling test-time compute to solve complex tasks. Combining both holds appealing promise for pre-training on suboptimal demonstrations and zero-shot adaptation via goal specification and planning. Yet existing methods assume effective planning requires accurate next-state prediction in pixel or latent space. In this work, we show that value prediction is more useful for planning, whereas accurate state prediction is often unnecessary. Our key insight is that offline goal reaching naturally admits a closed-form Monte Carlo return, enabling value regression to replace temporal-difference bootstrapping. We use this return as the sole supervision for latent world models: imagined latents are trained to predict goal-reaching value rather than match future states, implicitly preventing representation collapse. These planning-oriented representations let imperfect world models imagine on the level sets of value field beyond dataset transitions. We then present VPFlow, a value-predictive planner that pairs the world models with a one-step flow policy and plans in its noise space via evolutionary search. It enables policy improvement with imagined generalized advantage and reduces out-of-distribution queries common in action-space planning. On 30 state and pixel OGBench datasets, VPFlow surpasses model-free baselines by at least 30.3 and 10.1 percentage points in success rate, respectively, and outperforms latent planners LeWorldModel, PLDM, and TD-MPC2. It achieves this despite a latent prediction error orders of magnitude larger than with state prediction, while minimizing this error lowers success rate. This demonstrates that value-predictive representations, not exact state prediction, are what offline planning requires.

</details>


### [283] [VampPrior Unleashed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27322)
*Andrew A. Stirn,Abhejit Rajagopal,David A. Knowles,Grace Huynh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对深度潜变量模型中的 VampPrior 提出改进：解耦先验与后验协方差，并引入狄利克雷过程替代固定均匀混合权重，实现了更高的模型证据和稳健的非参数聚类，在六个单细胞图谱上显著提升了 scVI 的整合与聚类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度潜变量模型（DLVMs）广泛用于单细胞基因组学等分析管道，但其先验分布受关注甚少。VampPrior 虽能逼近最优先验（聚合后验）并常获高模型证据，但存在两大缺陷：1) 先验与后验不确定性被单一摊销协方差绑定，导致高先验熵与低后验熵的生成目标冲突；2) 固定均匀混合权重导致聚类效果差。

Method: 提出两项改进并结合：1) 解耦先验与后验协方差，消除共享参数下的熵冲突，大幅提升模型证据；2) 用狄利克雷过程替代均匀混合权重，实现非参数聚类。该方法仅修改先验部分，可迁移至任意具有连续潜在空间的 DLVM。

Result: 组合方案在模型证据上超越原 VampPrior，并提供稳健的非参数聚类能力。将改进先验集成到 scVI 中，在六个单细胞图谱上均显著提升了细胞整合与聚类性能，同时大幅提高模型证据。

Conclusion: 通过解耦协方差与引入狄利克雷过程混合，本文释放了 VampPrior 的潜力，实现了更高模型证据与鲁棒聚类的双重提升，且作为通用先验改进可直接应用于现有 DLVM 框架。

Abstract: Deep latent variable models (DLVMs) underpin many modern analysis pipelines, for example in single-cell genomics, yet their prior distributions have received markedly little attention. The VampPrior approximates the optimal prior (the aggregate posterior) and often attains high model evidence. Here, we identify two key limitations of the VampPrior: it ties prior and posterior uncertainty to a single amortized covariance and clusters poorly due to its fixed uniform mixture weights. We unleash the VampPrior by decoupling its prior and posterior covariances, resolving the conflict of generating high prior and low posterior entropy with the same shared parameters and substantially improving model evidence. We then replace the uniform mixture with a Dirichlet process. When combined, our proposals provide higher model evidence than the VampPrior and robust non-parametric clustering. Augmenting single cell Variational Inference (scVI) with our prior improves single-cell integration and clustering across six atlases while significantly raising model evidence. Our approach changes only the prior and thus can be applied to any DLVM with a continuous latent space.

</details>


### [284] [Automated Brick Schema Knowledge Graph Completion Across Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27339)
*Daniel Ma,Jalil Huseynov,Xinyi Pan,Shaked Shalev,Zhang Chi,Ai Yamamoto,Sohei Arisaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将知识图谱补全(KGC)应用于Brick建筑元数据模型的自动生成，对比了三种方法在feeds、hasPart、hasPoint三种核心关系上的表现，发现CompGCN在留出建筑数据上表现最佳，但没有任何方法能在所有数据集和关系上均匀泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成完整Brick模型需要大量人工检查建筑原理图和点表，工作量巨大。Brick模型本质上是类型化、多关系的知识图谱，因此推断缺失关系天然适合建模为知识图谱补全问题，旨在实现自动化建模。

Method: 对比三种方法：梯度提升树、成对Brick关系排序(Pairwise Brick Relation Ranking)和CompGCN（基于图卷积网络的组合关系建模），重点针对feeds（供给）、hasPart（包含部分）、hasPoint（包含点位）三种占人工建模工作量大头的关系进行补全预测。

Result: 在留出的MORTAR建筑数据上，CompGCN在抗类别不平衡指标（macro-F1和AUPRC）上表现最强；但在Kaggle和Building Time Series等分布外数据集上，没有任何方法能在所有数据集和关系上实现均匀泛化。

Conclusion: 知识图谱补全是Brick模型自动生成的可行路径，CompGCN在同分布数据上效果最好，但跨数据集、跨关系的泛化能力仍是主要挑战，未来需研发更鲁棒的迁移学习或领域适应方法。

Abstract: The Brick schema represents building equipment, sensors, spaces, and the relationships between them as typed, multi-relational graphs, providing machine-readable metadata that supports generalizable control applications. Current methods for producing complete Brick models require extensive manual examination of building schematics and point lists. We observe that as Brick models are knowledge graphs, inferring missing relations is naturally posed as a knowledge graph completion problem. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first application of knowledge graph completion to Brick model generation. We compare three methods, gradient-boosted trees, Pairwise Brick Relation Ranking, and CompGCN, on the relations feeds, hasPart, and hasPoint, which account for the majority of the schematic-reading work in building a Brick model by hand. Models are evaluated on held-out MORTAR buildings and out-of-distribution Kaggle and Building Time Series data. Although CompGCN is the strongest performer on held-out buildings under metrics resistant to class imbalance, leading macro-F1 and AUPRC, no method generalizes uniformly across datasets or relations.

</details>


### [285] [LiBRA: Logit Fingerprints for Byzantine-Robust Client Selection in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27357)
*Saheed A. Tijani,Frank Jiang,Ming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 LiBRA 方法，通过比较客户端模型在伪模式上的类条件 logit 响应指纹（C² 值）而非参数向量，实现拜占庭鲁棒聚合，兼容安全聚合、异构架构，且大幅降低通信与计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拜占庭鲁棒聚合规则需直接比较客户端参数向量，这与安全聚合（隐藏参数）冲突，计算复杂度为 O(n²d)，且仅适用于同构架构客户端，限制了实际部署。

Method: LiBRA 构建每个客户端模型在针对其优化的伪模式上的类条件 logit 响应指纹（维度 C²），将 Krum、Multi-Krum、Bulyan 等规则中的不相似度度量替换为指纹间距离，仅改变评分计算方式，不读取客户端参数。

Result: 在 Fashion-MNIST 和 CIFAR-10 上，三种架构、四种后门/标签翻转攻击、i.i.d. 分区下，LiBRA 变体保持其父规则的所有鲁棒性；同构相同更新下，指纹对恶意客户端排序 AUC 与完整参数向量仅差 0.006。通信比较值减少高达 49,090 倍，天然支持异构架构，每轮每客户端需一次模式优化。

Conclusion: LiBRA 在不牺牲鲁棒性的前提下，解决了参数比较与安全聚合的冲突，大幅降低开销并支持异构联邦学习，为实际部署提供了实用的拜占庭鲁棒聚合方案。

Abstract: Byzantine-robust aggregation shields federated learning from malicious updates, but the rules that filter out suspect updates do so by comparing clients' parameter vectors. That comparison requires access to every client's update, which secure aggregation exists to withhold, costs $O(n^2 d)$ in the parameter dimension $d$, and is defined only between clients that share an architecture. We propose LiBRA, which compares clients on what their models compute rather than on the parameters that compute them, using a fingerprint of $C^2$ values for $C$ classes, the class-conditional logit response of each client's model to pseudo-patterns optimised against it. We instantiate LiBRA as variants of Krum, Multi-Krum and Bulyan that change only what the dissimilarity score is computed on. On Fashion-MNIST and CIFAR-10 with three architectures and four backdoor and label-manipulation attacks under i.i.d. partitions, each variant holds every attack its parent holds, and on identical client updates under a backdoor attack the fingerprint ranks malicious clients within 0.006 AUC of the full parameter vector. The variants thus match the robustness of the rules they extend, while their trust decision reads no client parameter, compares up to 49,090 times fewer values, and can by construction compare clients whose architectures differ, at the cost of one pattern optimisation per client per round.

</details>


### [286] [Predictive Closure: A Set-Level Characterization of JEPA's Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27364)
*Mosh Levy,Aly Lidayan,Yoav Goldberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预测闭包表征，解释JEPA中特征选择机制，表明不可预测特征若能帮助预测其他特征也会被保留，实验验证了特征间预测关系对表示学习的影响，并展示了间接引导特征选择的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA理论认为特征选择基于个体可预测性，担心不可预测特征（如红绿灯）不会被编码。但预测误差也受特征间关系影响，需考虑特征集合的整体预测闭包性质。

Method: 基于JEPA训练目标的重新表述，提出预测闭包表征，分析特征集合的选择依赖于集合属性；通过受控实验验证，包括操纵特征间预测关系、辅助目标和侧信息对特征表示的影响。

Result: 实验显示：不可预测特征在帮助预测其他特征时表征增强；改变一个特征的预测关系会影响其他特征的表征质量；针对某特征施加辅助目标或提供侧信息，可间接增强其帮助预测的其他特征的表征。

Conclusion: JEPA的特征选择取决于特征集合的预测闭包，而非单一特征的可预测性；这为理解和引导表示学习提供了新视角，支持间接特征引导策略。

Abstract: In joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPA), an encoder and a predictor are trained together to produce a representation that minimizes the error in predicting the representation of the next observation. Because the representations of both the current and the next observation are learned, it is unclear which features they will include. Earlier characterizations describe the features JEPA selects in terms of their individual predictability, raising the concern that unpredictable features, like a traffic light, will not be encoded into the representation. However, the prediction error is also affected by the relationships between features, e.g., the light's color helps predict whether the traffic will stop. We propose the predictive closure characterization, which accounts for these relationships. Based on a reformulation of the JEPA training objective, we show that selection depends on properties of feature sets. This view agrees with earlier works when features evolve independently, but makes new predictions when they do not, for instance that a feature like the traffic light can still be retained. We test this characterization in controlled experiments, which show that unpredictable features can become better represented when they help predict other features, and that changing one feature's predictive relationships can affect how well others are represented. We also demonstrate implications for steering feature selection: targeting one feature with an auxiliary objective, or supplying it to the predictor as side information, can strengthen the representation of another feature whose future values it helps predict, so steering can be indirect.

</details>


### [287] [Trail-Weighted Influence Scores for Approximate Outcome Prediction in Bayesian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27366)
*Sahil Kumar Rajput,Randhir Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于踪迹加权的证据剪枝方法，用于贝叶斯网络预测。提出通过计算从证据节点到结果节点的活跃踪迹得分（边强度乘积），丢弃低于阈值的证据。在无碰撞器的二元变量网络（链和叉）中，证明踪迹得分等于端点精确相关性，且链与叉在相同边强度下联合分布相同。给出剪枝后后验分布的误差界，当被丢弃踪迹位于结果节点的不同分支时成立，否则失效。实验显示在随机树上优于随机剪枝1.4-13倍，在七个bnlearn网络（含碰撞器和回路）中作为启发式也优于随机剪枝，但不优于互信息排序，且计算开销大于推理节省，未实现加速。基于踪迹、图和信息特征训练的模型未超越踪迹得分或互信息，但踪迹特征贡献最大。通用网络的误差界留待未来工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在贝叶斯网络预测中，证据节点过多会增加推理计算成本。现有剪枝方法（如随机丢弃、互信息排序）缺乏理论保证或计算效率。本文旨在开发一种基于踪迹强度的证据剪枝方法，既能提供理论误差界（在受限网络结构下），又能在实践中作为高效启发式应用于一般网络。

Method: 1. 定义活跃踪迹：从证据节点到结果节点的有向无环路径。2. 踪迹得分计算：各边条件概率强度（如相关系数或互信息）的乘积。3. 剪枝策略：丢弃得分低于阈值的证据节点。4. 理论分析：针对无碰撞器的二元链/叉结构，证明踪迹得分等于端点精确相关性，推导剪枝后验误差界（当被丢踪迹位于结果节点不同分支时）。5. 实验验证：在所有小树上穷举验证界；在随机树和七个bnlearn网络上与随机剪枝、互信息排序对比；训练基于踪迹/图/信息特征的模型泛化测试。

Result: 1. 理论：无碰撞器二元网络中，踪迹得分=端点精确相关性；链和叉在相同边强度下联合分布相同；剪枝误差界为各被丢踪迹项之和，仅当踪迹分属结果节点不同分支时成立，共享分支时失效。2. 仿真：随机树上阈值剪枝误差比随机剪枝低1.4-13倍。3. 真实网络：七个bnlearn网络中，保留前50%踪迹得分证据优于随机剪枝，但不显著优于互信息排序；踪迹计算开销在六个网络中超过推理节省，无加速。4. 学习模型：未见网络上未超越踪迹得分或互信息，踪迹特征贡献主导且优于通用图特征。

Conclusion: 踪迹加权剪枝在无碰撞器二元网络中具有坚实理论基础（精确相关性等价、可计算误差界），在一般网络中作为启发式有效但计算成本较高，未带来实际加速。踪迹特征是证据重要性的核心表征，优于通用图特征。通用网络的理论误差界仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We study trail-weighted pruning of evidence for Bayesian-network prediction. A traversal enumerates the active trails from each evidence node to the outcome, scores each trail by the product of its edge strengths, and drops evidence whose score falls below a threshold. For collider-free networks (chains and forks) over binary variables, we show that the trail score equals the exact correlation between a trail's endpoints, that a chain and a fork with equal edge strengths have identical joint distributions, and that the pruned posterior is within a computable sum of per-trail terms of the exact posterior when each dropped trail lies in its own branch of the outcome node. We prove this bound, check it exhaustively on all small trees, and show that it fails when dropped trails share a branch. In simulations on random trees, thresholding trail scores gives 1.4 to 13 times lower error than random pruning of the same size. We also apply the score, as an unproven heuristic, to seven bnlearn networks with colliders and loops. Keeping the top half of the active evidence by trail score gives lower error than random pruning on all seven, but it is not clearly better than a mutual-information ranking, and scoring costs more than the inference it saves on six of the seven, so we show no speedup. Models trained on trail, graph and information features and tested on networks they never saw do not beat the trail score or mutual information, but trail features carry most of what they use and clearly outperform generic graph features. A bound for general networks is left to future work.

</details>


### [288] [Shaped SAEs: Expanding the Toolkit for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27375)
*Oveys Delafrooz Noroozi,Bhagyashree Puranik,Upamanyu Madhow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种增强稀疏自编码器（SAE）语义选择性的方法：利用J空间（基于雅可比矩阵的子空间）对稠密激活进行秩降低变换，并引入了一种新的Matched Filter SAE（MF-SAE）架构，该架构联合学习仿射整形变换，采用TopK投影进行原子选择，并使用相同原子进行重构。实验在SAEBench和SAE Probes基准上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SAE将LLM的稠密激活分解为稀疏特征，但学到的字典原子往往缺乏足够的语义选择性。作者希望通过在SAE训练前对激活进行线性'整形'，保留语义相关的子空间（由雅可比矩阵大奇异值对应的特征模式张成），从而提高特征的可解释性和语义纯度。

Method: 1. J空间整形：计算从当前层到LLM输出的平均雅可比矩阵，投影到由大奇异值对应的特征向量张成的'live'子空间上，实现秩降低变换。2. MF-SAE：联合学习仿射整形变换和字典原子；原子选择基于沿原子一维子空间的TopK投影；重构使用相同的原子（匹配滤波器原理）。

Result: 在SAEBench和SAE Probes基准测试中，结合J空间整形的SAE以及MF-SAE均优于直接在稠密激活上训练的最先进SAE，展示了整形方法提升语义选择性的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过雅可比矩阵导出的子空间投影对激活进行预处理，以及提出的MF-SAE架构（联合学习整形变换、TopK选择、匹配重构），能显著提升SAE学到特征的语义选择性和可解释性，为LLM机制性解释提供了更有效的工具。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) seek to decompose the dense activations of large language models (LLMs) into sparse, potentially interpretable features using a learned overcomplete dictionary. In this work, we ask whether it is possible to enhance the semantic selectivity of the learned dictionary atoms by linearly \emph{shaping} the dense activations prior to learning an SAE. We explore rank-reducing transformations based on the recently introduced J-space corresponding to the averaged Jacobian from the current layer to the LLM output, projecting activations onto the ``live'' subspace spanned by eigenmodes of the Jacobian with larger singular values (which we expect to be more semantically relevant). We also introduce a new approach for training SAEs in which (a)~an affine shaping transformation is learned along with the dictionary atoms, (b)~the atom selection mechanism is based on the TopK projections along the one-dimensional subspace spanned by the atoms, and (c)~reconstruction is carried out using the same atoms. We call this approach a Matched Filter SAE (MF-SAE) based on analogies with communication theory. The promise of shaping is demonstrated via evaluations against dense activations and state-of-the-art SAEs on benchmarks including SAEBench and SAE Probes.

</details>


### [289] [Sample-efficient learning with Dense Associative Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27384)
*Andrew Lucas,Dmitry Krotov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上分析了稠合联想记忆（DenseAMs）在随机扩展器低密度奇偶校验码（LDPC）码字上的训练动态，发现类似 grokking 的两阶段转变：小样本下仅记忆，大样本下能学会底层稀疏约束。提出阈值学习规则，使模型仅需 O(log n) 样本即可学会指数级解空间，并在存在隐变量时仍能恢复稀疏表示，为深度潜在表示涌现提供了可解析的物理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索联想记忆模型如何从有限样本中发现数据生成的稀疏约束规则，理解类 grokking 现象的理论机制，以及隐变量如何通过稀疏化高阶相关被自动发现，旨在为深度学习中的表示学习提供物理可解释的玩具模型。

Method: 采用统计物理方法，解析研究 DenseAM 能量函数在随机 XORSAT 解（即随机扩展器 LDPC 码字）上的演化；引入带阈值的学习规则，实现稀疏哈密顿量学习；分析训练集大小、阈值、隐变量比例对模型泛化与规则发现的影响。

Result: 识别出记忆阶段与规则发现阶段及其转变；阈值学习规则使样本复杂度从多项式降至 O(log n) 即可学会 exp[O(n)] 个解，达到渐近最优；即使 O(1) 比例的比特为隐变量，模型仍能通过稀疏化观测相关恢复底层码结构，并给出保守算法学习最小潜在表示的条件。

Conclusion: DenseAM 配合阈值学习规则展示了从少量样本中高效发现稀疏生成规则的能力，且能自动推断隐变量，为深度网络中潜在表示的涌现提供了透明、物理动机清晰且可解析的理论框架。

Abstract: We analytically study the evolution of the energy function of Dense Associative Memories (DenseAMs) as they train on randomly sampled codewords of random expander low-density parity-check codes (a.k.a. solutions of random XORSAT problems). We identify two distinct regimes and a transition between them, reminiscent of grokking in neural networks. A regime of small training set size is where the model memorizes a few training examples but cannot discover the underlying rules of the entire codeword dictionary generative process. The opposite regime, corresponding to a larger training set size, is where the model has uncovered the sparse constraints that generated the entire data set after seeing only polynomially many patterns. We also design a novel class of DenseAMs with a thresholding learning rule, and show that any amount of thresholding enables the model to perform simplified sparse Hamiltonian learning, which exponentially reduces the number of training examples needed to learn the true underlying data set. In this regime, the model learns how to discover $\exp[\mathrm{O}(n)]$ solutions from only $\mathrm{O}(\log n)$ training samples, which is asymptotically optimal for any learning algorithm. When training on codewords of certain random codes, the model can even learn the underlying code when a small O(1) fraction of bits are latent variables. These latent variables are not shown to the model during learning, but will be discovered by sparsifying observed higher-order correlations. For certain random codes, we show that a simple and conservative algorithm learns a minimal latent representation for the data set. This provides a transparent, physics-motivated, and analytically tractable toy model for how deep latent representations emerge in an associative memory training.

</details>


### [290] [Fixed-Size Active Statistical Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27389)
*Erik Skalnes,Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有条件泊松采样（CPS）作为泊松采样（PS）的固定样本量替代方案，证明其在多元Z估计问题中渐近方差不劣于PS，且在预测不完美时显著更优，并发布Python包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泊松采样（PS）虽常用于加权采样以减少方差，但其样本量随机导致实际应用挑战并引入额外估计方差，亟需固定样本量的替代方法。

Method: 提出有条件泊松采样（CPS），将其作为PS在广义多元Z估计问题（含凸M估计）中的直接替代，理论分析其渐近方差性质，并对比PS性能。

Result: 理论证明CPS渐近方差不劣于PS，在预测不完美的现实场景中可显著降低方差；合成数据与真实数据实验均验证优势；同步开源Python实现包。

Conclusion: CPS是PS的优越固定样本量替代方案，消除了可变样本量的弊端，建议在方差缩减采样任务中优先采用。

Abstract: Variance reduction techniques improve the efficiency of drawing conclusions from data. Recent proposals for variance-reduction use two complementary techniques to reduce variance: incorporating machine learning predictions into estimators and weighted sampling. A popular weighted sampling algorithm is Poisson Sampling (PS), which independently flips a biased coin at each unit and includes all heads in the sample. A fundamental flaw with PS is that it has a random sample size, which presents practical challenges and introduces variance into the estimator. We show that a fixed-size alternative, known in survey sampling as Conditional Poisson Sampling (CPS), is a drop-in replacement for PS on general multivariate Z-estimation problems, which include convex M-estimation considered in prior work. Notably, we show that the asymptotic variance of CPS is no worse than that of PS, and can be substantially better in realistic scenarios where predictions are imperfect. We illustrate these advantages on both synthetic and real datasets, and release a Python package implementing CPS. In light of our contributions, there is no need to suffer a variable sample size.

</details>


### [291] [MLIPS: Multi-Layer Indexer Pool Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27438)
*Samkit Jain,Sai Kishan Pampana,Shlok Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MLIPS（多层索引器池共享），一种无需训练的稀疏注意力加速方法：生产者层生成一个超大的top-M候选池，后续消费者层各自从中选取top-K，实现候选检索的共享同时保留各层独立打分选择。在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上实现层间共享、持久解码复用和粗粒度生产者，长上下文任务获1.14–1.40×加速、14.8–27.4% GPU能耗降低，准确率波动-1.49到+0.29个百分点。同时发现预填充阶段跨查询组和层的KV位置复用模式，据此提出探索性的Top-G有界池构建，但其部署与任务精度仍待验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力虽限制每个查询只关注少量键，但每层仍需扫描全前缀以找到这些键，导致显著的计算与内存开销。现有方法未充分利用层间候选集的重叠性，亟需一种免训练、可共享候选检索且不损害各层自主选择能力的机制。

Method: MLIPS：引入生产者-消费者范式。生产者层计算一个规模为M（M>K）的top-M候选池并发布；后续多个消费者层直接从该池中各自选取top-K，保留各自的打分与选择逻辑。工程上实现了层级共享、持久解码复用（跨步复用池）和粗粒度生产者（降低生产者开销），集成于vLLM服务框架的DeepSeek-V4-Flash模型中。此外，通过采样预填充轨迹分析跨查询组和层的KV位置复用模式，提出Top-G作为预填充阶段的有界池构建启发式策略。

Result: 在四个长上下文基准套件上，设置三消费者层对一生产者层，获得1.14–1.40倍吞吐加速、14.8–27.4% GPU能耗降低；单次运行准确率变化在-1.49至+0.29个百分点之间。更大范围的共享（如更多消费者层）会暴露任务相关的质量损失。预填充轨迹分析揭示了跨查询组和层的反复选中KV位置结构，为Top-G池构建提供动机，但其部署效益、任务精度和服务收益尚未完全评估。

Conclusion: MLIPS以免训练、细粒度共享的方式有效降低了稀疏注意力的候选检索开销，在保持模型质量近似不变的前提下实现显著加速与节能。研究同时揭示了注意力工作集的跨层复用结构，为预填充阶段的池构建提供新思路，但更激进的共享策略与Top-G机制的实际部署价值仍需进一步系统性研究。

Abstract: Sparse attention limits the keys used by each query, but finding those keys can still require a full-prefix scan at every layer. We introduce \textbf{MLIPS (Multi-Layer Indexer Pool Sharing)}, a training-free method that shares candidate search while retaining each consumer's own scoring and selection. A producer publishes a top-$M$ pool larger than the attention budget $K$; subsequent layers select their own top-$K$ from that pool. Measurements across three sparse-attention model families show substantially higher coverage from broad pools than overlap between final selections. We implement layerwise sharing, persistent decode reuse, and a coarse producer in vLLM for DeepSeek-V4-Flash. Across four long-context suites, three consumer layers per producer give $1.14$--$1.40\times$ speedups and $14.8$--$27.4\%$ lower GPU energy, with observed accuracy changes of $-1.49$ to $+0.29$ percentage points in single runs. More extensive sharing exposes task-dependent quality losses. Sampled prefill traces also reveal recurring selected KV positions across query groups and layers. We characterize this shared working-set structure and use it to motivate Top-$G$, an exploratory bounded pool construction for prefill. Its deployment, task accuracy, and serving benefits remain open.

</details>


### [292] [Scaling Graph Reasoning in Language Models with Learnable Graph Positional Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27439)
*Javier Porras-Valenzuela,Arush Arora,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种增强语言推理模型(LRM)的方法，通过引入可扩展且置换等变的图位置编码来调节注意力logits，并结合后融合模块将结构信息注入隐藏状态，从而在图推理任务中实现O(N)的上下文复杂度，并在小图训练后展现出对大图(最多1000节点)的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过序列化图描述输入LRM，导致输入长度随边数增长，限制了可解决问题的规模；且即使图能放入上下文，性能也随图规模增长急剧下降。需要一种不依赖序列化邻接表、能高效融合图结构信息的方法。

Method: 1. 使用GNN+Graph Transformer计算图位置编码；2. 将位置编码用于调节注意力logits(置换等变)；3. 引入后融合模块将结构信息注入隐藏状态；4. 联合微调位置编码器和LRM的LoRA适配器，使用图论求解器生成的最优解作为监督信号；5. 消除了序列化邻接表的需求，上下文复杂度降至O(N)。

Result: 在小图训练后，模型在最多1000节点的图论任务上表现出更好的尺寸泛化能力；生成的token数少于传统LRM；对节点重标签具有鲁棒性；在小图上的性能得以保持。

Conclusion: 该方法通过图位置编码增强LRM，有效解决了图序列化带来的上下文爆炸和性能衰减问题，实现了计算高效且具有强泛化能力的图推理，为大规模图上的语言模型推理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Solving reasoning tasks supported on graphs typically requires providing a Language Reasoning Model (LRM) with a serialized description of the graph. This approach results in the input length growing with the number of edges, which limits the size of the problems that can be solved. Furthermore, even when graphs fit in context, performance sharply decays as the graphs grow. We propose to address this by augmenting LRMs with scalable and permutation equivariant graph positional encodings that modulate attention logits. An additional post-fusion-style module incorporates structural information into hidden states. We compute these positional encodings using a GNN followed by a Graph Transformer. This eliminates the need for serialized adjacencies, reducing the context burden to $O(N)$. We jointly fine-tune the positional encoder and the LRM LoRA adapters using optimal solutions generated by graph-theoretic solvers, resulting in a computationally efficient method. Trained on small graphs, the model exhibits improved size-generalization across graph-theoretic tasks on graphs with up to 1,000 nodes, and produces fewer tokens than a conventional LRM. Additionally, we observe that our model remains robust to node relabelings. Evaluations on graph-theoretic benchmarks also show that performance on small graphs is preserved.

</details>


### [293] [Learning-Theoretic Foundation for General Coded Computing: The Adversarial Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27440)
*Parsa Moradi,Hanzaleh Akbarinodehi,Mohammad Ali Maddah-Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对抗鲁棒的广义编码计算（GCC）扩展，利用平滑正则化限制对抗性工作者输出的影响，而非显式定位纠错，实现了线性主节点复杂度，并证明了最坏情况误差为 O((q/N)^{6/5})，当对抗者数量亚线性增长时误差渐近为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式计算系统中对抗性工作者返回损坏输出，严重威胁计算恢复；现有编码计算方法通过显式定位和纠错处理对抗输出，带来显著主节点计算开销，且缺乏对恢复误差随工作者数量和对抗预算缩放的理论表征。

Method: 基于广义编码计算（GCC）框架，引入平滑正则化（光滑样条）来限制对抗性输出的影响，设计编码器和解码器以最小化端到端恢复误差上界，实现线性主节点复杂度；将最坏情况误差分解为编码器近似误差、诚实结果下的解码器重构误差和对抗扰动误差三部分进行理论分析。

Result: 推导了非渐近最坏情况端到端近似误差界：在 N 个工作者中最多 q 个对抗性工作者时，通过适当选择平滑参数，最坏情况误差为 O((q/N)^{6/5})；实验在标量计算和神经网络推理上验证了理论收敛行为。

Conclusion: 提出的方法避免了显式对抗检测的开销，通过平滑正则化实现对抗鲁棒性，理论保证当对抗者数量随网络规模亚线性增长时误差渐近消失，为大规模分布式计算提供了可扩展的鲁棒编码计算方案。

Abstract: Adversarial workers pose a severe challenge in distributed computing systems by returning corrupted outputs that can substantially distort the recovered computations. Coded computing addresses this threat by introducing redundancy across worker assignments. Existing exact and approximate coded-computing methods typically handle adversarial outputs by explicitly locating and correcting or removing erroneous evaluations, which adds significant computational overhead at the master node. Moreover, existing analyses do not characterize how the recovery error scales with the number of workers and the adversarial budget.
  In this paper, we develop an adversarially robust extension of general coded computing (GCC), a learning-theoretic approximate coded-computing framework that designs the encoder and decoder by minimizing an upper bound on the end-to-end recovery error. Rather than identifying corrupted workers, the proposed method uses smoothness regularization to limit the influence of their outputs. The resulting encoder and decoder can be implemented using smoothing splines with linear master-side complexity. We derive a non-asymptotic bound on the worst-case end-to-end approximation error by decomposing it into three components: (1) the encoder approximation error, (2) the decoder reconstruction error under fully honest results, and (3) the adversarial perturbation error. For maximum of $q\geq 1$ adversarial workers among $N$ workers results in a worst-case error of order $\mathcal{O}((q/N)^{6/5})$ with appropriate selection of smoothness parameter, demonstrating that the approximation error vanishes asymptotically whenever the number of adversaries grows sublinearly with the network size. Experiments on scalar computation and neural-network inference are consistent with the predicted convergence behavior.

</details>


### [294] [Using Set Learning to Improve Set-Valued Classifiers on Long-tailed Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27443)
*Kevin Kasa,Scott C. Lowe,Graham W. Taylor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究集合值分类（SVC）在长尾/不平衡数据上的表现，提出改进的 odd-K-out (OKO) 损失函数，显著降低计算开销（从 3.2x 降至 1.36x），并在多种 SVC 方法（top-k、average-k、共形预测）上提升宏平均准确率，同时保持有竞争力的微平均准确率，且不显著增加预测集大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合值分类（SVC）通过预测变长集合来传达不确定性，但在长尾和不平衡数据集上性能显著下降，尤其是在宏平均评估下。现有 SVC 主要依赖事后校准，对训练动态如何影响下游 SVC 表现知之甚少。

Method: 系统研究神经网络训练策略（标签平滑、类别平衡等）及损失函数对不平衡数据下 SVC 的影响；改进近期提出的 odd-K-out (OKO) 损失——基于批次样本集合学习，提升样本效率，并通过算法修改将计算开销从标准交叉熵的 3.2 倍降至 1.36 倍。

Result: 在高度长尾数据集上，改进的 OKO 方法在多种 SVC 方法（top-k、average-k、共形预测）上显著提升宏平均准确率，同时保持有竞争力的微平均准确率；少数类性能提升未伴随预测集大小的实质性增加。

Conclusion: 精心设计的基于集合的训练损失能产生更适合不平衡数据的网络，改进的 OKO 损失在计算效率与 SVC 下游性能之间取得良好平衡，为长尾分类中的不确定性量化提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Set-valued classification (SVC) offers a practical approach to communicating uncertainty while maintaining accuracy by predicting variable-sized sets per input rather than fixed-sized sets (e.g. top-1 or top-5). However, set-valued classifiers (SVCs) exhibit significantly degraded performance on long-tailed and imbalanced datasets, a setting for which they are ostensibly well-suited. This becomes apparent under macro-average evaluation, which weighs all classes equally. SVCs are typically formed during a post-hoc calibration stage, so little is known about how training dynamics affect their behaviour. In this work, we investigate how different neural network training strategies and loss functions impact downstream SVC performance on highly imbalanced data. In addition to methods such as label smoothing and class-balancing, we investigate whether loss functions that operate over sets of examples in a batch, rather than treating all examples as independent, lead to better downstream SVCs. We adapt the recently proposed odd-K-out (OKO) loss function, which learns from sets of training samples, improving its sample efficiency whilst introducing algorithmic modifications that reduce its computational overhead from 3.2x to just 1.36x that of standard cross-entropy training. Experiments on highly long-tailed datasets demonstrate our modified OKO approach significantly improves macro-accuracy across multiple SVC methods - including top-k, average-k, and conformal prediction - while maintaining competitive micro-accuracy. This improved performance on minority classes occurs without substantially increasing prediction set sizes, suggesting that carefully constructed set-based training can produce networks better suited to imbalanced data.

</details>


### [295] [Decision Sufficiency of Learned Representation Interfaces for Safe Generative Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27446)
*Jipeng Li,Yanning Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出决策充分性框架，评估语言模型控制器使用稀疏自编码器特征时的性能损失，并审计了Qwen2.5、SmolLM2和Phi模型中的特征。实验表明，仅使用两个开/关特征会使最小平均损失显著增加（0.0345-0.1166），且该损失源于控制器输入信息不足，而非架构或训练问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制器依赖选定的内部特征，但性能下降无法区分是由于特征缺失信息、决策规则局限还是训练不足。需要一种方法量化表示的决策充分性，即最优控制器能否达到全状态信息下的最小平均损失。

Method: 1. 形式化定义决策充分性；2. 针对有限输出接口精确计算最优损失，并分解三种超额损失来源；3. 证明成对检查可能遗漏信息损失，给出揭示冲突所需状态数上界，表征无冲突接口所需最少输出数；4. 使用稀疏自编码器提取特征，在Qwen2.5、SmolLM2、Phi上审计；5. 在SmolLM2上用两个人工评分员评分，对比受限特征与全状态信息的控制器损失。

Result: 在SmolLM2上，将控制器输入限制为两个选定的开/关特征，最小平均损失相对全状态信息增加0.0345-0.1166，即使提供治理策略也是如此。两个独立训练的自编码器在两个评估集上均显示该增幅。成对检查无法捕捉群体层面的最优策略冲突。

Conclusion: 审计揭示的决策失败根源于控制器输入信息不足，而非架构或训练缺陷。决策充分性框架可量化表示质量，指导特征选择与控制器设计。

Abstract: A controller can guide a language model toward safe and useful responses by choosing when to assist, ask for clarification, or refuse. When the controller uses selected internal features, poor performance alone cannot distinguish missing information from limitations of its decision rules or training. We study decision sufficiency: whether the best controller using a representation can match the minimum average loss attainable with full state information, under the same strategies and evaluation criteria. For interfaces with finitely many outputs, we compute the optimal loss exactly and separate the three sources of excess loss. Pairwise checks can miss information loss: states with the same controller input may share an optimal strategy in every pair but not across the whole group. We bound the number of states needed to reveal a conflict and characterize the fewest outputs an unrestricted interface needs to avoid all conflicts. We audit selected features from sparse autoencoders, which encode model activations using relatively few active features, in Qwen2.5, SmolLM2, and Phi. On SmolLM2 responses scored by two human raters, restricting the controller to two selected on/off features increases its minimum average loss by 0.0345--0.1166 relative to full state information, even with the governing policy supplied. Both independently trained autoencoders show the increase on both evaluation sets. The audit identifies decision failures that require changing the controller's input, not merely its architecture or training.

</details>


### [296] [Projected Distillation: On Learning to Solve Hard Problems from Close, Correct Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27467)
*Shripad V. Deshmukh,Christian Bock,Kangrui Ruan,Abhijeet Awasthi,Vijay Lingam,Rein Houthooft,Manish Prajapat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了投影蒸馏，将在线策略学习视为连续谱，通过最小编辑失败尝试生成校正目标，结合对比更新与在线刷新，实现了在全失败时的近在线策略学习，理论证明遗憾随编辑规模增长，实验在数学、代码、科学、工具使用等五个领域显著优于GRPO、OPD、SPOT、SODA等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略学习虽能限制分布偏移并保留能力，但在困难问题上全失败时无奖励信号；在线策略蒸馏受限于早期错误前缀；离线策略目标（标准答案/教师解）偏离学生生成分布，会侵蚀现有能力。因此需要在在线与离线之间寻找平衡，实现最小修正的近在线策略学习。

Method: 提出投影蒸馏：将学生的失败轨迹通过最小编辑转为正确目标，使用对比损失偏好校正轨迹而非失败轨迹，并随着学生演化在线刷新校正目标。理论上证明学习遗憾随编辑规模增长：最小校正下随时域线性增长（类似DAgger），全答案替换下二次增长（类似行为克隆）。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和OLMo-2-7B两个学生模型及数学、代码、科学、工具使用五个领域实验中，投影蒸馏在专业化-保留前沿曲线下面积最高，对比GRPO和OPD在9个单元格中8个最优，对比SPOT和SODA在9个中7个最优。消融显示在线校正、对比损失、在线策略性至关重要：目标偏离在线策略会稳步恶化权衡，保留率从0.916降至0.744。

Conclusion: 投影蒸馏通过最小编辑将失败轨迹投影到正确轨迹，结合对比学习与在线刷新，实现了在全失败场景下的近在线策略学习，理论与实验均证实其在专业化能力提升与原有能力保留之间达到最优权衡，优于现有在线/离线策略蒸馏与强化学习方法。

Abstract: On-policy learning is central to post-training: training on a model's own generations limits distribution shift and helps preserve existing capabilities. On hard problems, however, on-policy RL provides no reward-based learning signal when all attempts fail. On-policy distillation provides next-token supervision from a teacher, but on hard problems, if a rollout goes wrong early, subsequent supervision is conditioned on prefixes that already contain the mistake. Correct behavior can instead come from a gold or teacher solution, but such off-policy targets can differ substantially from the model's generations and, as we show, may erode existing capabilities. We therefore treat on-policyness as a continuum and propose projected distillation, which minimally edits the student's failed attempts into correct targets. The method uses contrastive updates to favor corrections over failed attempts and refreshes these corrections as the student evolves, enabling near-on-policy learning even when all student attempts fail. Theoretically, we prove that the student's learning regret grows with the edit size: it scales linearly with the horizon under minimal correction, as in DAgger, and quadratically under full-answer replacement, as in behavior cloning. Empirically, across two students (Qwen3-8B and OLMo-2-7B) and five domains spanning math, code, science, and tool use, projected distillation gives the highest area under the specialization-retention frontier (best on 8 of 9 cells against GRPO and OPD, and 7 of 9 against SPOT and SODA). Our ablations highlight the importance of online corrections, the contrastive loss, and on-policyness: moving targets farther off-policy steadily worsens the specialization-retention trade-off, with retention falling from 0.916 to 0.744 when minimal corrections are replaced by student-agnostic gold solutions.

</details>


### [297] [Training-Free Continual Learning for Multimodal Forecasting via Prompt Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27468)
*Laurent Mombaerts,Jonathan Taws,Jacopo Gargano,Dinesh Gaddam,Kevin George,Srijan Tiwari,Shreyas Rajesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种无需重新训练的持续学习方法：通过基于结果驱动的反思演化一段文本“指导块”，并将其注入冻结的大语言模型（LLM）提示词中，用于多模态时间序列预测。实验表明，该方法在严格防泄漏基准上以极低成本（美元级）超越了时间序列基础模型（TSFM），且可迁移至更新模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，适应变化的预测系统需要重新训练，成本高昂且耗时。作者探索能否用一段可演化的文本指导块，配合冻结的 LLM 预测器，实现免训练的持续学习，以解决多模态预测（搜索指数、汇率、大宗商品 + 网络新闻）中的分布漂移问题。

Method: 构建刷新的多模态基准（每窗口配网络检索新闻），采用 GEPA 风格的提示词演化，并严格控制数据污染（预测器的知识截止早于所有评估窗口）。将演化出的约 900 词指导块嵌入 Llama-3.3-70B 的提示词中进行预测。同时分析循环动态（反射收益集中、循环内增益高估、纯误差目标抑制文本模态），并提供两个扩展：将指导编译为可执行代码并调用 TSFM 工具；消融实验验证短期预测优势及文本新闻的作用机制。

Result: 演化指导块在保留数据上实现 GM MASE 0.776，显著优于 TabPFN-TS-3 的 0.898；迁移至更新模型无性能损失；更新成本仅几美元而非 GPU 天数。循环分析显示：首次反射贡献主要收益，无监测仲裁时循环内改进高估真实增益 2-3 倍，纯误差目标会静默抑制文本模态。代码编译扩展可确定性恢复大部分增益；消融显示短期预测优势扩大，移除新闻文本不损害性能，说明文本价值已在学习过程中蒸馏为策略。

Conclusion: 持续提示词演化是一种实用的、免训练的多模态预测持续学习替代方案，能以极低成本适应分布漂移，且无需重新训练模型参数。

Abstract: Adapting a forecasting system to a changing world classically means retraining. We ask whether the task of continually learning regimes for time series forecasting can be represented by a piece of text: a guidance block, evolved by outcome-driven reflection and used in a frozen LLM forecaster's prompt. On a refreshed multimodal benchmark (weekly search indices, daily currencies, daily commodities, each window paired with web-retrieved news), we run GEPA-style prompt evolution under strict contamination control to prevent the data leakage common in timeseries benchmarks: the forecaster's knowledge cutoff predates every evaluated window. A 900-word evolved guidance carried by Llama-3.3-70B beats TimeSeries Foundation Models (TSFMs) on held-out data (GM MASE 0.776 vs. 0.898 for TabPFN-TS-3), transfers to more recent models without measurable loss, and costs dollars rather than GPU-days to update. We characterize the loop: out-of-loop gains concentrate in the first reflection, in-loop improvements overstate out-of-loop gains 2-3x unless an untouched monitor arbitrates, and error-only objectives silently suppress the text modality. We provide two extensions. First, compiling the guidance into executable code with a TSFM as a callable tool recovers most of the gain deterministically. Second, ablations show the advantage widens at short horizons and that removing the news text does not hurt the guided forecaster, consistent with the text's value being distilled into policy during learning. We argue that continual prompt evolution is a practical, training-free alternative to retraining for multimodal forecasting.

</details>


### [298] [When Parameter Gradient Flows Close in Representation Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27474)
*Shengye Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究参数化模型的表示梯度流及其移动度映射，分析了从固定初始化出发，单一移动度映射何时适用于所有自治损失函数。针对双因子矩阵表示，分类了所有可接受的联合秩；对于一般表示，建立了普通闭包与局部不变度量的等价性，并分离了不同损失族的闭包性质，发现线性损失普遍满足闭包而强凸二次损失可能失效，同时指出了光滑输出秩二且李秩至少四的情况仍未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究参数化模型中表示梯度流的移动度映射如何保留隐藏参数信息，以及从固定初始化出发，单一移动度映射何时能对所有自治损失函数生效。核心动机是理解表示学习中梯度流几何结构的闭包性质，以及不同损失族对表示动力学的影响。

Method: 采用几何分析和李代数方法：1) 对宽于输出维度且因子满行秩的双因子矩阵表示，通过联合秩分类建立闭包精确条件；2) 对具有常数环境表示秩和梯度-李秩的一般表示，利用解析几何建立普通闭包与局部不变水平度量的等价性；3) 通过端点截面技术强制移动度映射在表示像上存在局部光滑代表；4) 构造反例（延迟括号例子）区分线性损失族与平移强凸二次损失族的闭包行为；5) 设计冻结确定性实验验证工程损失构造。

Result: 主要发现：1) 双因子矩阵表示中，闭包精确发生在有效标量平衡最小联合秩或满联合秩处的双值符号谱族，中间秩均被排除，正表示流属于已知平方根族；2) 一般表示中，普通闭包在解析情形等价于局部不变水平度量；3) 线性损失在每个浸没处局部均满足闭包，但固定平移的强凸二次损失族在延迟括号例子上失效；4) 固定输出读出提供普通平方损失见证；5) 这些结果不刻画一般二次损失闭包；6) 光滑输出秩二且李秩≥4的情况仍悬而未决；7) 工程损失构造经冻结实验验证，区别于平方损失证明、通用定理及经验学习性能。

Conclusion: 本文建立了表示梯度流移动度映射闭包性的几何分类理论，揭示了双因子矩阵表示的联合秩谱系，确立了一般表示中闭包与不变度量的等价性，并通过反例展示了损失族几何性质对闭包的决定性影响。结果表明平方损失具有特殊的普适见证作用，但一般二次损失闭包特征仍未完全刻画，高秩光滑情况下的开放问题依然存在。工程损失构造的实验验证为理论分析提供了补充视角。

Abstract: The represented gradient flow of a parameterized model depends on a mobility that can retain hidden parameter information. We study when one entire represented mobility map works for every autonomous loss from one fixed initialization. For two-factor matrix representations wider than both output dimensions, with individually full-row-rank factors, we classify all admissible joint ranks: closure occurs precisely at effective scalar-balanced minimal joint rank or a two-value signed spectral family at full joint rank. All intermediate ranks are excluded; positive represented flows belong to known square-root families. For general representations with constant ambient representation and gradient-Lie ranks, ordinary closure is equivalent to a local invariant-level metric in the analytic case and specified smooth regimes. An endpoint section forces the initially unrestricted mobility map to admit a locally smooth representative on the represented image. We also separate testing families: linear losses locally admit closure at every submersion, whereas one fixed translated strongly convex quadratic family fails it on a delayed-bracket example. A fixed output readout gives an ordinary squared-loss witness. These supporting results do not characterize quadratic-only closure in general. We retain the unresolved smooth output-rank-two case with Lie rank at least four. A frozen deterministic experiment checks a separate engineered-loss construction; it is distinguished from the squared-loss proof, universal theorems and empirical learning performance.

</details>


### [299] [Orthogonal Boundary Unlearning for Dual-Use Concepts in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27490)
*Niccolò Ronchetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出正交边界遗忘 (OBU) 框架，通过奇异值分解提取双用途能力子空间，将参数更新限制在正交零空间，从而在移除危险能力（如网络战、病原体增强）的同时，实现零旁路损伤地保留良性科学效用。提供 Projected LoRA 和闭式权重手术两种实现，在四个开源基础模型上验证了强概念分离、抗上下文注入攻击和抗对抗性重学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在双用途领域（网络战利用、病原体增强等）中，合法防御科学与恶意攻击共享词汇、句法和内部表示，导致标准遗忘方法产生严重的旁路损伤（概念渗透），无法在消除危险能力的同时保留良性效用。

Method: 提出正交边界遗忘 (OBU) 框架：1) 通过成对校准查询的激活协方差矩阵进行奇异值分解，提取领域匹配的能力子空间；2) 将参数更新严格限制在该子空间的正交零空间，保证良性表示在校准子空间上经历零参数扰动。提供两种互补实现：Projected LoRA（迭代梯度微调中施加正交约束）和闭式权重手术（单步解析式直接编辑权重，无需训练）。

Result: 在 Gemma-3-4B、Qwen3.5-4B、SmolLM3-3B、Granite-4.2-3B 四个现代开源基础模型上，OBU 实现了强双用途概念分离，大幅缓解上下文注入攻击，展现持久的抗对抗性重学习能力，且未降低良性领域能力。

Conclusion: OBU 通过数学严格的正交约束，从根本上解决了双用途领域遗忘中的旁路损伤问题，提供了无需训练的闭式解和高效微调两种实用路径，为大模型安全对齐提供了可扩展、可验证的技术方案。

Abstract: Mitigating catastrophic risks from large language models requires excising hazardous operational capabilities, such as cyberwarfare exploitation and pathogen enhancement, without degrading benign scientific utility. In these dual-use domains, legitimate defensive science and malicious attacks share vocabulary, syntax, and internal model representations, causing standard unlearning methods to induce severe collateral damage (concept bleeding).
  In this work, we present Orthogonal Boundary Unlearning (OBU), a mathematically grounded framework designed to eliminate collateral damage on benign dual-use capabilities. OBU extracts a domain-matched capability subspace from paired calibration queries via singular value decomposition on empirical activation covariances, and restricts parameter updates strictly to the orthogonal null space. This guarantees that benign representations experience zero parameter perturbation along the spanned calibration subspace.
  We present two complementary realizations of OBU: Projected LoRA, which applies the orthogonal constraint during iterative gradient-based fine-tuning, and Closed-Form Weight Surgery, which directly edits model weights in a single analytical step without training. Across four modern open-weight foundation models (Gemma-3-4B, Qwen3.5-4B, SmolLM3-3B, and Granite-4.2-3B), OBU achieves strong dual-use concept separation, substantially mitigates in-context priming attacks, and demonstrates durable resistance against adversarial relearning without degrading benign domain competence.

</details>


### [300] [Self-Evolving Agents through Privileged Retrospective Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27495)
*Yuhang Zhou,Yifan Wu,Bo Peng,Mingyi Wang,Jiayi Liu,Xiangjun Fan,Lizhu Zhang,Zhuokai Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了回顾性自蒸馏(RSD)框架，使智能体能在部署后从自身经验中自我进化，无需真实标签或验证器奖励。通过多轮自蒸馏，利用模型作为教师和学生，结合跨任务先例、逐轮反思和未来后见之明等特权回顾上下文，将轨迹转化为密集监督信号。引入前沿引导的上下文-策略协同进化，进一步提升性能。在ALFWorld、ScienceWorld和SearchQA上，RSD相对基础模型提升高达59.7%，前沿引导再增44.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体旨在部署后从自身经验改进而非保持固定，但自然收集的轨迹通常缺乏真实解或验证器衍生的奖励，导致难以将经验转化为有效监督。

Method: RSD是一个多轮框架：每轮中，同一模型同时充当学生和教师。教师在特权回顾上下文（跨任务先例与教训、逐轮反思、完成轨迹的未来后见之明）条件下重新评分学生的实际动作，生成的教师分布通过在线策略自蒸馏蒸馏回策略。经验库随轮次增长，回顾上下文不断刷新，实现模型、数据、监督的协同进化。此外引入前沿引导的上下文-策略进化：更强的前沿模型提出对特权上下文构建方式的约束性编辑。

Result: 在ALFWorld、ScienceWorld和SearchQA三个基准上，使用Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-14B，RSD相对基础模型最高提升59.7%；前沿引导的上下文-策略进化在固定上下文策略基础上再提升高达44.7%。

Conclusion: 自然积累的经验可在无真实轨迹和无验证器奖励的情况下，转化为持久的模型能力，实现核心策略更新的自我监督进化。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents aim to improve from their own experience rather than remain fixed after deployment, but naturally collected trajectories often lack ground-truth solutions or verifier-derived rewards. We introduce Retrospective Self-Distillation (RSD), a multi-round framework that turns such experience into dense supervision using privileged retrospective context. For each on-policy trajectory, the same underlying model acts as both student and teacher: the teacher re-scores the student's realized actions while conditioning on cross-task precedents and lessons, together with turn-level reflection and future hindsight from the completed trajectory. The resulting teacher distribution is distilled back into the policy through on-policy self-distillation. Across rounds, the experience bank grows and the retrospective context is refreshed, allowing the model, its data, and its supervision to co-evolve. We further introduce frontier-guided context-policy evolution, where a stronger frontier model proposes constrained edits to how privileged context is constructed. Across ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and SearchQA with Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B, RSD improves performance by up to 59.7% relative to the base model, while frontier-guided context-policy evolution adds up to a further 44.7% relative improvement over a fixed context policy. These results show that naturally accumulated experience can be converted into persistent model capability without ground-truth trajectories or verifier-derived rewards for the core policy update.

</details>


### [301] [BEACON: Bridging Multimodal Semantics and Asynchronous Propagation for Cloud System Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27542)
*Zhe Li,Tengxue Zhang,Yang Shu,Tung Kieu,Lunting Fan,Yingying Zhang,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 BEACON 框架，用于云系统异常检测，通过动态可解释多模态聚合器 (DIMA) 利用信息瓶颈机制压缩指标的语义与时序动态并与日志在共享原型空间对齐，以及异步时空传播器 (ASTP) 采用多尺度时序注意力与拓扑感知聚合建模异步跨实例异常传播，在三个真实数据集上超越现有最优基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云系统异常检测方法依赖简单融合策略和图学习，忽视了多模态观测（指标、日志、依赖图）之间的语义鸿沟，以及系统实例间随时间演变的异步时空依赖关系。

Method: 提出 BEACON 框架，包含两大核心模块：1) 动态可解释多模态聚合器 (DIMA)：引入信息瓶颈机制，压缩指标的语义与时序动态信息，并将其与日志在共享原型空间中对齐，实现跨模态语义桥接。2) 异步时空传播器 (ASTP)：利用多尺度时序注意力捕获时序依赖，采用拓扑感知聚合建模空间关系，实现异常在系统实例间的异步传播建模。

Result: 在三个真实世界云系统数据集上进行大量实验，BEACON 在异常检测任务上持续优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: BEACON 通过显式建模多模态语义对齐与异步时空传播，有效解决了云系统异常检测中的语义鸿沟与异步依赖问题，显著提升了检测性能，为大规模云系统可观测性分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern large-scale cloud systems continuously generate massive and multimodal observability data, such as time-series metrics, log records, and system dependency graphs, which reflect the health state of the system. To ensure reliable cloud system operation, anomaly detection models identify abnormal states by analyzing these multimodal observations. However, existing approaches rely on simple fusion strategies and graph learning, ignoring the semantic gap among modalities and the asynchronous spatio-temporal dependencies across system instances over time. To this end, we propose BEACON, a framework to Bridge multimodal sEmantics and Asynchronous propagation for Cloud system anOmaly detectioN. Specifically, we introduce a Dynamic Interpretable Multimodal Aggregator (DIMA) that leverages an information-bottleneck mechanism to compress information from the semantics and temporal dynamics of metrics, and aligns them with logs in a shared prototype space. Furthermore, we propose an Asynchronous Spatio-Temporal Propagator (ASTP) that captures temporal dependencies with multi-scale temporal attention and models spatial relations with topology-aware aggregation. This enables asynchronous propagation of anomalies across system instances. Extensive experiments on three real-world cloud system datasets demonstrate that \textbf{BEACON} consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in anomaly detection.

</details>


### [302] [Timing-Aware and Interpretable Self-Attention for Event-Based Wearable Task Transition Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27549)
*Anda Ouyang,Siyuan Chen,Julien Epps*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件的时间感知自注意力框架用于任务转换检测，通过显式建模事件的时间戳和持续时间，在可穿戴设备数据集上超越现有基于事件的方法，性能媲美统计和深度学习模型且输入表示更紧凑，同时提供更好的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务转换检测为持续健康监测和及时情境感知干预提供基础。现有的统计特征和深度特征虽分类性能强，但依赖聚合或潜在特征，难以从行为角度解释。基于事件的特征提供紧凑且可解释的替代方案，但传统自注意力分类器主要依赖令牌顺序，未显式结合时间和持续时间信息，导致事件表示中的时间线索未被充分利用。

Method: 提出基于事件的时间感知自注意力框架：将行为信号表示为带有起始时间戳和持续时间的离散事件序列。使用参数高效的基于核的表示编码事件时间，设计新颖的类型感知持续时间嵌入根据事件持久性调节信息传播。

Result: 在半自然穿戴数据集实验中，该模型优于现有基于事件的方法，性能超越或媲美统计学和深度学习模型且输入表示更紧凑，并通过积分梯度分析提供改进的可解释性。

Conclusion: 显式建模事件时间和持续时间同时提升了分类性能和可解释性，支持在生物医学和健康信息学应用中的可靠部署。

Abstract: Task transition detection aims to identify when a person shifts from one task to another, providing a foundation for continuous health monitoring and timely context-aware interventions. While statistical and deep feature representations have demonstrated strong classification performance, they often rely on aggregated or latent features that are difficult to interpret in behavioural terms. Event-based features provide a compact and interpretable alternative by encoding behavioural and physiological patterns as discrete tokens with explicit semantic meaning, making them well suited for behavioural analysis in health informatics. Self-attention offers a mechanism for modelling interactions among such tokens; however, conventional self-attention classifiers primarily rely on token order and do not explicitly incorporate timing or duration information. As a result, informative temporal cues in event representations may not be fully exploited for task transition detection. This study proposes an event-based time-aware self-attention framework that represents behavioural signals as sequences of discrete events augmented with onset timestamps and durations. Event timing is encoded using a parameter-efficient kernel-based representation, while a novel type-aware duration embedding modulates information propagation according to event persistence. Experiments on a semi-naturalistic wearable dataset show that the proposed model outperforms existing event-based approaches, achieves performance beyond or comparable with statistical and deep-learning models with a more compact input representation, and provides improved interpretability through integrated-gradient-based analysis. These results indicate that explicit modelling of event timing and duration enhances both classification performance and interpretability, supporting reliable deployment in biomedical and health informatics applications.

</details>


### [303] [Lower Bounds for Smooth Nonconvex Zeroth-Order Optimization: The Price of Determinism under Low Curvature Trace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27550)
*Yuxing Peng,Zhiqing Tang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明了在低曲率迹（有效秩条件）下，随机化零阶方法（如高斯两点法）在高维非凸优化中可达到 O(ET) 的最优查询复杂度，而确定性方法则需要 Ω̃(d min{T, √(ET)}) 次查询，确定性带来的开销随维度 d 线性增长，且当 E 超过 T 时退化为 Ω(dT)，表明确定性方法无法享受相同的维度无关加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机化零阶方法（如语言模型微调中使用的高斯两点法）在高维任务中表现优异，常被低曲率迹（有效秩条件）解释。本文旨在探究确定性方法是否能获得相同的查询复杂度节省，从而揭示随机性在零阶优化中的本质作用。

Method: 理论分析：在 L-光滑非凸函数、精确函数值预言机、Hessian 被迹不超过 LE 的固定矩阵主导的假设下，建立随机化与确定性方法的查询复杂度上下界。引入参数 T = LΔ/ε²（梯度下降迭代数），对比高斯两点法（拒绝上升步）与自适应子空间搜索等算法，证明确定性方法的下界及随机方法的最优性。

Result: 当 ET ≤ d 时：随机化方法（高斯两点法）需 O(ET) 次查询，且证明任意随机方法需 Ω(ET)，最优至常数因子。确定性方法需 Ω̃(d min{T, √(ET)}) 次查询，自适应子空间搜索达到 O(d min{T, √(ET)}) 上界。确定性开销因子约为 d/(E+√(ET))，随 d 线性增长；若 E 超过 T 对数因子，确定性复杂度退化为 Ω(dT)，等同于无迹预算情况。下界对全域 C^∞ 函数成立，甚至允许预言机返回按秩定价的梯度投影。

Conclusion: 随机性是零阶方法利用低有效秩结构实现维度无关加速的关键。确定性方法每发现一个方向需付出约 d 次查询，迹预算仅允许约 √(ET) 个方向，导致确定性开销随维度线性放大。这从理论上解释了为何语言模型微调等高维任务必须依赖随机化零阶方法。

Abstract: Low curvature trace, an effective rank condition, is a common explanation for why randomized zeroth-order methods scale to high-dimensional tasks such as language model fine-tuning. Can deterministic methods achieve the same savings? We show that they cannot. We study finding $\varepsilon$-stationary points of $L$-smooth nonconvex functions on $\mathbb{R}^d$ from exact function values, assuming that below the starting value the Hessian is dominated by a fixed matrix with trace at most $LE$; this is the effective rank condition used to explain zeroth-order fine-tuning. When $ET\le d$, the Gaussian two-point method that such fine-tuning uses needs only $O(ET)$ calls if it rejects steps that raise $f$, where $T=LΔ/\varepsilon^2$ is the iteration count of gradient descent from initial gap $Δ$, and we prove that every randomized method needs $Ω(ET)$ calls, so this cost is optimal up to constants. In contrast, every deterministic method needs $\widetildeΩ(d\min\{T,\sqrt{ET}\})$ calls, and adaptive subspace search attains $O(d\min\{T,\sqrt{ET}\})$: a deterministic method pays about $d$ calls per discovered direction, and the trace budget allows about $\sqrt{ET}$ directions. Determinism thus multiplies the cost by a factor of order $d/(E+\sqrt{ET})$ up to logarithmic factors, which grows linearly with $d$ for fixed $E$ and $T$; once $E$ exceeds $T$ by a logarithmic factor, deterministic methods need $Ω(dT)$ calls, as without any trace budget. Our lower bounds hold for $C^\infty$ functions with the matrix bound on all of $\mathbb{R}^d$, even when each call also returns gradient projections priced by rank.

</details>


### [304] [Can Native Safety Travel? Reusing Safety Pretraining Across Models via On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27555)
*Bingqi Shang,Yang Zhang,Yiwei Chen,Changsheng Wang,Shiyu Chang,Bingquan Shen,Sijia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出原生安全复用问题，通过在线策略蒸馏将安全预训练教师的原生安全迁移至标准预训练学生，实现小到大的选择性安全迁移，显著提升学生的安全鲁棒性同时保持更高通用效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全预训练虽能嵌入深层安全性，但获取成本高、局限于原模型且常伴随通用效用下降。需探索能否将原生安全从专用教师迁移至标准学生，继承鲁棒性同时保持通用效用。

Method: 采用在线策略蒸馏：标准预训练学生在安全预训练提示上生成响应，小型安全预训练教师监督学生在自身生成轨迹上的逐token预测；引入跨分词器对齐实现跨模型家族迁移。

Result: 实验表明，1.7B学生经后续微调后攻击成功率（ASR）仅上升5.00%，远低于安全后训练的11.58%；平均通用效用48.20%显著超过对齐教师的27.42%。学生兼具更高安全鲁棒性和通用效用。

Conclusion: 原生安全可作为可复用模型属性，不再永久绑定于原预训练模型；该框架实现选择性小到大安全迁移，复用小型教师的鲁棒安全专长而不继承其效用局限。

Abstract: Safety pretraining has emerged as a promising paradigm for embedding safety deeply into a model during pretraining, yielding robustness to subsequent fine-tuning that conventional safety post-training often lacks. We refer to this safety acquired during model pretraining as native safety. Yet native safety is expensive to acquire, confined to the model in which it was pretrained, and often accompanied by lower general utility. In this work, we rethink the practical value of safety-pretrained models and introduce, for the first time, the native safety reuse problem: Can native safety be transferred from a specialized, potentially smaller, safety-pretrained "teacher" to standard-pretrained "students," enabling them to inherit its robustness while preserving their general utility? We show that native safety can "travel" through on-policy distillation. A standard-pretrained student generates responses to safety-pretraining prompts, while a small safety-pretrained teacher supervises the student's token-level predictions along its own generated trajectories; cross-tokenizer alignment further enables transfer across model families. The resulting students exhibit substantially greater safety robustness than their safety-post-trained counterparts while retaining higher general utility than the teacher: subsequent fine-tuning raises the average attack success rate (ASR) of the 1.7B student by only 5.00%, compared with 11.58% for safety post-training, and its average utility of 48.20% far exceeds the aligned teacher's 27.42%. Unlike conventional large-to-small knowledge distillation, our framework enables selective, small-to-large safety transfer: it reuses the robust safety expertise of a smaller pretrained teacher without inheriting its utility limitations. These findings establish native safety as a reusable model property rather than one permanently tied to the originally pretrained model.

</details>


### [305] [AnchorRM: Parallel Intra-Batch State Reconstruction with Anchor-Based Residual Memory for Temporal Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27558)
*Haroon Gharwi,Kai Shu,Yue Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AnchorRM锚点残差记忆机制，解决时序小批量训练中节点多次出现导致的记忆陈旧问题，实现并行计算同时保持时序因果性，显著提升训练速度和批量扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于记忆的时序图神经网络在时序小批量训练时面临计算效率与时序保真度的权衡：同一批次中节点多次出现时，后续交互会使用陈旧的批次前记忆，因为中间记忆更新尚未提交。随着批次大小增加，该问题愈发严重。

Method: AnchorRM将每个批次开始时最后提交的节点记忆作为固定锚点，利用因果上先于当前事件的同批次交互推断相对于锚点的缺失状态演化。学习的残差校准锚点以重建每个交互的事件特定节点记忆。每个事件特定残差直接从固定锚点及其因果历史预测，重建状态不依赖先前重建状态，可在批次内并行计算并保持时序因果性。

Result: 在四个时序图数据集和三个基于记忆的骨干网络上实验表明，AnchorRM实现平均6.16倍训练加速（最高10.05倍）和28.67倍批量扩展（最高40倍），同时保持具有竞争力的预测性能。

Conclusion: AnchorRM在保持竞争力预测性能的同时，大幅提升了基于记忆的时序图学习的可扩展性和训练效率。

Abstract: Memory-based temporal graph neural networks (TGN) rely on recurrent node memories to capture evolving interaction histories. However, temporal mini-batching introduces a trade-off between computational efficiency and temporal fidelity: when a node appears multiple times within the same batch, later interactions are evaluated using stale pre-batch memory because the intermediate memory updates have not yet been committed. This problem becomes more severe as temporal batch size increases. To overcome this challenge, we propose AnchorRM, an anchor-based residual memory mechanism that reconstructs event-specific intra-batch node states without executing sequential recurrent updates. AnchorRM treats the last committed node memory at the beginning of each batch as a pre-batch fixed anchor and uses causally preceding same-batch interactions to infer the missing state evolution relative to that anchor. The learned residual then calibrates the anchor to reconstruct an event-specific node memory for each interaction. Because each event-specific residual is predicted directly from the fixed anchor and its causal history, reconstructed states do not depend on previously reconstructed states and can therefore be computed in parallel within the batch while preserving temporal causality. Experiments across four temporal graph datasets and three memory-based backbones demonstrate that AnchorRM achieves an average 6.16x training speedup (up to 10.05x) and 28.67x batch scaling (up to 40x). These results demonstrate that AnchorRM substantially improves the scalability and training efficiency of memory-based temporal graph learning while maintaining competitive predictive performance.

</details>


### [306] [TRCM: Topology-Aware Route-Consistency Modeling for Robust Neural Vehicle Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27568)
*Peiheng Jian,Zhenwei Wang,Tiehua Zhang,Lei Zhang,Zhishu Shen,Ruibin Bai,Zhijun Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TopologyAware RouteConsistency Modeling (TRCM)，在自回归 CVRP 求解器中显式建模路径级客户共同分配概率，并将其作为一致性信号注入节点选择过程，结合结构保持增强与拓扑熵重加权缓解监督不平衡，显著缩小最优间隙并提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归神经组合优化模型在 CVRP 中仅通过序列节点选择隐式表示路径组织，缺乏显式的路径级客户共同分配建模，限制了解的质量与结构感知能力。

Method: TRCM 复用标准自回归构建管道中的节点表示预测成对同路径概率，将其作为路径一致性信号注入客户选择；针对同路径监督的类别不平衡与结构不均，引入结构保持增强与拓扑熵 (TE) 重加权，重点强化局部模糊客户对的学习。

Result: 相同解码预算下，CVRP50 最优间隙从 3.67% 降至 3.28%，CVRP100 从 2.11% 降至 1.99%；8 倍增强推理下 CVRP100 间隙从 1.61% 降至 1.49%；XML100 平均目标提升 0.24%，结构信息因素增益更大；500 变体需求重分配测试显示目标波动更集中。

Conclusion: 显式路径一致性建模是自回归神经路由的有效结构原则，TRCM 通过显式建模同路径概率并结合拓扑熵重加权，显著提升了解质量与鲁棒性，为神经组合优化提供了新的结构感知范式。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization has enabled fast constructive solvers for the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (CVRP), yet most autoregressive models still construct solutions through sequential node selection and represent route organization only implicitly. We investigate whether route-level customer co-assignment can be modeled explicitly and used to guide this node-level decision process. To this end, we propose TopologyAware RouteConsistency Modeling (TRCM), which reuses node representations from a standard autoregressive construction pipeline to predict pairwise same-route probabilities and injects them into customer selection as a route-consistency signal. Because sameroute supervision is both class-imbalanced and structurally uneven, TRCM combines structure-preserving augmentation with Topological Entropy (TE)- based reweighting to emphasize locally ambiguous customer pairs. Under matched decoding budgets, TRCM reduces the optimality gap from 3.67% to 3.28% on CVRP50 and from 2.11% to 1.99% on CVRP100; under 8-fold augmented inference, it reduces the CVRP100 gap from 1.61% to 1.49%. On XML100, TRCM*8Aug improves the average objective by 0.24%, with larger gains on several structurally informative factors. A controlled demand-reassignment test over 500 variants further shows more concentrated objective fluctuations. These results support explicit route-consistency modeling as an effective structural principle for autoregressive neural routing.

</details>


### [307] [Two Is Enough: Binary Classification Priors Suffice for Multi-Class In-Context Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27569)
*Zhixiong Cheng,Shiyuan Han,Jingjing Suo,Zhengli Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabICLv2 使用二分类预训练先验（类别数上限设为 2）仍能实现零样本 K 分类，且在 TabArena 和真实信贷评分数据上均显著优于默认的 10 类先验，揭示了 TFM 预训练中类别数可从架构约束降级为可调超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究表格基础模型预训练时分类先验的类别数上限对零样本泛化能力的影响，验证是否可以通过极简的二分类先验实现任意类别数的分类，从而降低预训练成本并简化架构约束。

Method: 在 TabICLv2 预训练中将分类先验的类别数上限从 10 降至 2（即每个预训练任务均为二分类），利用上下文比较算子匹配查询行嵌入与类条件嵌入进行分类，输出空间基数由上下文标签决定而非预训练标签空间；在 38 个 TabArena 分类数据集（200 轮 bootstrap）和 60 家真实信贷评分机构上评估。

Result: 二分类两阶段配方在 TabArena 上达到 1590.1 Elo，比官方默认 1568.0 高 22.1，超越 TabPFN-v2.6 (1577.1) 和 RealTabPFN-v2.5 (1521.6)；在 60 家机构中 44 家测试 AUC 提升（配对 Wilcoxon p=0.0007），KS 统计量 p=0.0002，推理成本仅增加 1.5%。

Conclusion: 分类机制不依赖固定基数输出头，而是通过上下文比较算子实现，使类别数从架构约束变为可调超参数；二分类先验结构上更廉价易缓存，指向类别数无关的 TFM 预训练新范式。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) pretrain a transformer on large-scale synthetic priors so that a single forward pass yields zero-shot tabular prediction. TabICLv2 samples the class count of its classification prior during pretraining, and the prevailing configuration caps it at ten. We report a counter-intuitive result: setting this cap directly to two---making every pretrained classification task binary---still yields weights that perform zero-shot K-way classification 2. On the 38 TabArena classification datasets (200 bootstrap rounds, anchored at default RF = 1000), the binary two-stage recipe reaches 1590.1 Elo, above the official default at 1568.0 (+22.1), above TabPFN-v2.6 (1577.1) and RealTabPFN-v2.5 (1521.6). A production-scale validation on 60 real-world credit-scoring institutions independently confirms the direction: the same checkpoint improves test AUC on 44 of 60 institutions (paired Wilcoxon p = 0.0007 for AUC, p = 0.0002 for KS) at +1.5 inference cost. Mechanistically, classification in TabICLv2 is carried not by a fixed-cardinality output head but by an in-context comparison operator matching query-row embeddings against class-conditional embeddings, so the cardinality of the output space is set by the labels present in the context, not by the label space seen during pretraining. This makes a binary prior structurally cheaper to generate and cache than a ten-class prior, demotes "number of classes" from an architectural constraint to a tunable hyperparameter, and points to a class-count-agnostic regime of TFM pretraining.

</details>


### [308] [From POMDP Theory to Deep RL with Particle Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27575)
*Kevin Tan,Ziping Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于部分可观测环境（POMDP）的模型型强化学习算法，结合序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）滤波、在线潜在动力学学习和基于QMDP近似的有限步在线规划。作者给出了端到端的遗憾界，将误差分解为模型估计误差、粒子滤波误差、蒙特卡洛展开误差和QMDP结构性偏差（由规划深度控制），并通过实验验证了理论预测的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模型的POMDP强化学习方法通常只解决流程中的单一环节（如状态推断、动力学学习或规划），并依赖理解不充分的近似；而现有的POMDP理论主要分析计算低效的理论算法，与现代深度RL实践脱节。本文旨在填补这一理论与实践的鸿沟，提供一个既实用又有理论保证的端到端算法框架。

Method: 提出的算法包含三个核心组件：(1) 使用序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）进行隐状态滤波近似；(2) 在线更新潜在动力学模型；(3) 执行d步在线规划，叶子节点用状态值函数评论家（QMDP近似）进行引导。算法在MPC框架下实例化，结合深度神经网络进行实际部署。理论上证明了遗憾界，将总误差分解为四个可控项，明确了样本量、粒子预算、展开预算和规划深度对性能的影响。

Result: 理论上：证明了端到端遗憾界，误差分解为模型估计误差、粒子滤波误差、蒙特卡洛展开误差和QMDP结构性偏差，后者可通过增加规划深度控制。实验上：在满足假设的POMDP上，每个误差项按预测速率衰减；在多个环境中展示了算法的实用性和有效性，验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 本工作为POMDP中的在线决策提供了一条兼具实用性与理论保证的路径。通过明确量化各近似误差来源及其与计算预算的关系，该框架成功弥合了POMDP理论算法与现代深度RL实践之间的差距，为后续研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning in partially observable environments requires inferring hidden states, learning latent dynamics, and planning under the resulting belief. Practical algorithms often solve one aspect of the above pipeline while relying on approximations for the others that are often poorly understood, while existing POMDP theory typically analyzes computationally inefficient theoretical algorithms far from modern deep RL practice. To fill this gap, we propose a practical end-to-end algorithm that approximately solves the hidden state filtering problem via sequential Monte Carlo (SMC), updates a latent dynamics model online, and performs approximate online planning for $d$ steps before bootstrapping leaves with a state-value critic (a QMDP approximation). We analyze each approximation and prove an end-to-end regret bound decomposing the error into model estimation error, particle filter error, Monte Carlo rollout error, and a structural bias term induced by the QMDP approximation controllable by the planning depth that we further analyze in various special cases. The resulting guarantee makes explicit how statistical sample size, particle budget, rollout budget, and planning depth affect performance. We instantiate our algorithm in an MPC-based deep RL framework, demonstrating its practicality on a variety of environments. On a POMDP that satisfies our assumptions, each term of the bound decays at its predicted rate. Our results provide a practical and theoretically grounded route for bridging theory and practice within online decision-making in POMDPs.

</details>


### [309] [Bridging Digital Deception and Physical Fidelity: A Spatio-Temporal Attack on Cognitive Radars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27585)
*Tongrun Xing,Yongjun Dai,Xuan Liao,Zhiwen Zhang,Tong Zhou,Longlong Zhang,Tian Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种物理先验驱动的分段时空攻击方法（SSTA），旨在解决认知雷达端到端处理中深度网络面临的对抗攻击跨架构迁移性差、易受物理信道畸变影响的问题。通过全局-局部特征解耦分段机制和可微分时空算子建模信道畸变，SSTA 在 9 种异构模型上实现了 10.36% 的平均黑盒攻击成功率提升，且生成波形在极端物理畸变下经匹配滤波后主瓣衰减为 0 dB，噪声底抬升严格符合恒虚警率容限，实现了数字域欺骗与物理域保真度的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着认知雷达向一维复基带序列端到端处理演进，深度网络面临严重的对抗威胁。现有攻击方法在实际电子战中存在两大瓶颈：跨架构迁移性低，以及对物理信道畸变极其脆弱，导致战术可靠性下降。因此，需要一种能同时兼顾跨架构迁移性和物理域鲁棒性的对抗攻击方法。

Method: 提出物理先验驱动的分段时空攻击（SSTA），包含两大核心模块：1) 全局-局部特征解耦分段机制，打断对抗梯度对长程序列相干性的依赖，弥合跨架构特征鸿沟；2) 将信道畸变抽象为可微分时空算子，通过变换期望（EoT）策略将其集成到动量迭代中，替代无物理语义的静态范数约束。

Result: 在涵盖 CNN、RNN 和 Transformer 的 9 种异构模型上进行全面交叉评估，SSTA 相比最先进基线将平均黑盒攻击成功率提升 10.36%。基带等效仿真验证：生成波形可在极端物理畸变下幸存，经匹配滤波后主瓣衰减为 0 dB，噪声底抬升严格控制在恒虚警率（CFAR）容限内。

Conclusion: SSTA 成功统一了数字域欺骗效果与物理域保真度，为认知电子战（CEW）安全评估提供了关键支撑，展示了在实际复杂电磁环境下实施可靠对抗攻击的可行性。

Abstract: As cognitive radars evolve towards the end-to-end processing of one-dimensional complex baseband sequences, deep networks face severe adversarial threats. However, existing attacks encounter dual limitations in practical electronic warfare: low cross-architecture transferability and severe vulnerability to physical channel distortions, which degrades their tactical reliability. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose a physical prior-driven Segmented Spatio-Temporal Attack (SSTA). First, a global-local feature decoupling segmentation mechanism interrupts the adversarial gradients' dependence on long-range sequence coherence, bridging the cross-architecture feature gap. Second, moving beyond static norm constraints devoid of physical semantics, we abstract channel distortions into differentiable spatio-temporal operators, integrating them into momentum iterations via an Expectation over Transformation (EoT) strategy. Comprehensive cross-evaluations across 9 heterogeneous models spanning CNNs, RNNs, and Transformers demonstrate that SSTA outperforms the state-of-the-art baseline by 10.36% in Average Black-Box Attack Success Rate. Furthermore, baseband equivalent simulations confirm that the generated waveforms survive extreme physical distortions and, after matched filtering, achieve zero main-lobe attenuation (0 dB) with noise floor lift strictly within Constant False Alarm Rate tolerances. SSTA successfully unifies digital-domain deception and physical-domain fidelity, providing crucial support for CEW security assessment.

</details>


### [310] [Auditing the Product, Not the Recursion: The Evaluation Evidence Behind Recursive Self-Improvement Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27610)
*Zihan Chen,Di Zhu,Lei Nico Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对114篇arXiv论文（截至2026年10月）的全文编码分析，系统评估了递归自我改进（RSI）研究中声明与证据的匹配情况。研究发现：90%的RSI研究报告了超越开发任务的产品评估，但仅60%在未用于选择的任务上评估，仅22%审计两次以上完整循环；声称改进程序更优的26项研究中，仅4项在相同起始条件和预算下对比早晚期程序。重复性和程序改进的证据远弱于产品评估。作者提出三个命题解释产品评估的局限，并主张建立声明索引报告标准，将每个声明与所需评估设计关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）承诺AI系统能持续自我优化，但现有文献缺乏对研究声明与评估证据匹配程度的系统度量。现有综述仅规定声明所需证据类型，未量化研究实际报告这些证据的频率。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过可复现的编码框架，量化RSI研究中产品声明与过程声明的证据报告现状，揭示评估实践中的系统性缺口。

Method: 采用可复现的arXiv检索框架（冻结于2026年10月），筛选114篇符合条件的研究，其中90篇自述为RSI系统。构建将声明映射至评估设计的代码本，实施独立人工编码、第三方裁决以及改进器对比的盲编码以保证编码可靠性。分析维度包括：产品评估是否超越开发任务、是否在未用于选择的任务上评估、是否审计多次完整循环、改进程序声明的对比严谨性等。另设立人工编码随机样本与成熟工作对照组进行验证。

Result: 在90篇RSI研究中：90%报告超越开发任务的产品评估（含部分/未说明分离的宽松标准），60%在未用于选择的任务上评估，仅22%审计两次以上完整循环。人工编码随机样本显示类似模式：45%在未用于选择的任务评估，14%审计多次循环。声称改进程序更优的26项研究中，23项报告相关对比，但仅4项在相同起始制品和匹配预算下对比早晚期程序。跨结果与对照组看，重复性与程序改进证据显著滞后于产品评估。三个命题揭示选择效应、共享评估器误差、系统状态变化对产品评估推断的限制。

Conclusion: RSI研究普遍存在声明与证据错位：产品评估相对完备，但重复性审计与程序改进对比的严谨性严重不足。选择效应、评估器共性偏差、系统状态漂移等因素从根本上限制了单纯产品评估的推断效力。研究呼吁建立“声明索引报告标准”，强制将每个具体声明与验证所需的评估设计（如独立测试集、多轮循环审计、受控程序对比）显式绑定，以提升该领域证据标准的科学性与可信度。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) promises AI systems, such as self-evolving LLM agents, that become better at producing further improvements. Evaluating this promise requires separating product claims about the final system from process claims about the improvement process itself. Existing surveys specify the evidence these claims require, but how often studies report that evidence remains unmeasured. We examine this question through full-text coding of 114 eligible studies in a reproducible arXiv search frame frozen in October 2026, including 90 that describe their own systems as RSI. The codebook links claims to evaluation designs. Independent human coding, third-reader adjudication, and a separate blind coding of improver comparisons assess coding reliability. Among the 90 RSI studies, 90% report product evaluation beyond development tasks under a broad criterion that includes partial or unstated separation, while 60% report evaluation on tasks unused for selection. Only 22% audit two or more reruns of the full loop. In a human-coded random sample, the RSI studies show the same contrast: 45% evaluate on tasks unused for selection and 14% audit two or more reruns. Of the 26 studies claiming a better improvement procedure, 23 report a related comparison, but only 4 compare earlier and later procedures from identical starting artifacts at a matched budget. Across these results and a comparison set of established works, evidence for repeatability and improved procedures remains less developed than product evaluation. Three propositions explain how selection effects, shared evaluator errors, and changes in system state limit what product evaluations can establish. Together, the findings support a claim-indexed reporting standard that connects each claim to the evaluation design needed to test it.

</details>


### [311] [Learning Inter-Graph Interactions Between Heterogeneous Substructures of Chemical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27614)
*Gyoung S. Na*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于复杂化学系统中有机-无机异质子结构间相互作用的图神经网络方法，并将其应用于混合太阳能电池材料的带隙预测，在1,682种材料上达到最先进精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中常见包含异质子结构的复杂化学系统，其物理性质源于异质子结构间的相互作用。现有图表示学习方法主要假设同质结构间的图级相互作用（如有机分子或无机晶体材料），缺乏针对有机-无机异质相互作用的建模能力。

Method: 提出了一种复杂化学系统的数据描述符，以及一种用于学习有机和无机化合物间异质图间相互作用的图神经网络。该方法能够建模异质子结构间的相互作用，而非传统的同质结构间相互作用。

Result: 将该方法应用于混合太阳能电池材料的带隙预测任务，在1,682种材料上通过学习异质图间相互作用，达到了最先进的预测精度。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了复杂化学系统中异质子结构间相互作用建模的空白，证明了针对有机-无机异质界面设计的图神经网络在可持续能源材料性质预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Complex chemical systems containing heterogeneous substructures are common in real-world applications. Various physical phenomena of the complex chemical systems are derived from the interactions between the heterogeneous substructures. However, existing graph representation learning methods for inter-graph interactions assumed graph-level interactions between homogeneous structures, such as organic molecules and inorganic crystalline materials. We propose a data descriptor of the complex chemical systems and a graph neural network for learning inter-graph interactions between organic and inorganic compounds. We applied the proposed method to predict the physical properties of hybrid solar cell materials containing heterogeneous substructures, which have received significant attention for sustainable energy resources. By learning heterogeneous inter-graph interactions, the proposed method achieved state-of-the-art accuracy in predicting band gaps of 1,682 hybrid solar cell materials.

</details>


### [312] [From Outcomes to Steps: Reliable Success-Aware Critic Learning for Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27623)
*Jiaxi Liu,Shixiong Kai,Zhentao Tang,Yang Li,Yuqi Cui,Mingxuan Yuan,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了SCALE，一种面向长时程语言智能体的Actor-Critic框架，通过显式预测任务最终成功概率（Success Value）来辅助传统回报估计，利用Success-GAE将成功率变化转化为多步动作信用分配，并根据预测器可靠性调节贡献，在保持token级PPO目标的同时显著提升任务成功率与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长时程语言智能体的强化学习中，仅有轨迹级监督时，传统Critic虽能准确预测累积回报，却难以分辨局部价值差异以实现精细的信用分配，尤其在稀疏反馈与函数逼近条件下更为严重。策略改进不仅需要知道未来回报，更需要知道每个中间决策是否增加了任务最终成功的可能性。

Method: SCALE在常规回报估计基础上引入显式的成功值预测：在每个交互前缀处，Critic同时输出回报值和Success Value（即从该前缀出发最终完成任务的概率）。利用Success Value的变化通过Success-GAE生成多步动作优势估计，并根据预测器的可靠性加权融合到优势函数中，同时保留token级PPO的策略目标。

Result: 在五个智能体基准和三种模型骨干上，SCALE在大多数设置下优于对比RL基线，成功率最高提升4.1个百分点。独立的Critic学习分析表明，SCALE还能改善Critic估计精度并增强训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模任务成功概率并将其变化用于信用分配，SCALE有效解决了长时程稀疏奖励下的局部价值识别难题，为语言智能体的强化学习提供了更稳健、高效的训练框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for long-horizon language agents requires identifying whether intermediate actions make eventual task success more or less likely when supervision is available only at trajectory level. The main difficulty is that a critic with aggregate return-prediction accuracy may still fail to resolve the local value differences needed to assign credit to actions, especially under sparse feedback and function approximation. Conventional critics estimate future return, but policy improvement also depends on knowing whether each intermediate decision makes task success more or less likely. We introduce SCALE, an actor-critic framework that augments conventional return estimation with explicit prediction of eventual task success. At each interaction prefix, the critic predicts a return value and a Success Value, defined as the probability that the task will eventually be completed from that prefix. SCALE converts changes in Success Value into multi-step action credit through Success-GAE and modulates its contribution according to the estimated reliability of the predictor, while retaining the token-level PPO objective. Across five agent benchmarks and three model backbones, SCALE improves task performance over the compared RL baselines in most settings, with gains of up to 4.1 percentage points in success rate. Separate critic-learning analyses show that SCALE also improves critic estimation and training stability.

</details>


### [313] [Ensemble-Aware Structural Encoding (EASE): Conditioning Protein Encoders on Peptide Conformational Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27638)
*Ruoyao Zhang,Hongxuan Li,Jie Peng,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出集合感知结构编码（EASE）框架，从肽的构象集合中提取结构信息，通过选择中心构象并融合其表示，显著提升了六项肽性质预测任务的性能，平均绝对增益约3.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质基础模型通常将肽简化为单一序列或结构，忽略了与抗菌活性、溶血性、毒性、稳定性及结合等关键性质相关的构象多样性。例如，蛋白酶抗性与氢交换测得的构象动力学相关性更强，而非平均螺旋度。因此需要一种能捕捉构象集合信息的表示方法。

Method: EASE框架：1）从肽的构象集合中选择代表主要结构状态的中心构象；2）利用置换不变算子融合各构象的个体表示，生成肽级别的整体表示；3）将该表示用于下游性质预测任务。

Result: 在六项肽性质预测任务（泄漏控制划分）上，EASE相较于匹配的单结构基线持续提升性能，两种结构编码器下平均绝对增益约3.0%。随构象数量增加至K=20，增益持续增长。

Conclusion: 单一肽构象为结构表示提供了有限基础，而EASE通过提炼并融合多个构象，能产生更具信息量的表示，有效捕捉构象动力学对肽性质的影响。

Abstract: Protein foundation models provide transferable representations for downstream peptide prediction tasks. However, existing representations typically reduce a peptide to a single sequence or structure, which may miss conformational variability relevant to key peptide properties, including antibacterial activity, hemolysis, toxicity, stability, and binding. For example, protease resistance correlates more strongly with conformational dynamics measured by hydrogen exchange than with average helicity. Therefore, we introduce Ensemble-Aware Structural Encoding (EASE), a framework for effectively extracting structural information from conformational ensembles for peptide representation. To extract useful information from noisy conformers, EASE selects central conformers representing a peptide's major structural states and fuses their individual representations with a permutation-invariant operator to produce a peptide-level representation for downstream prediction. We then evaluate our framework on six peptide property-prediction tasks under leakage-controlled splits. Against matched single-structure baselines, EASE improves performance consistently on six tasks with a mean absolute gain of about 3.0% across two structural encoders. Gains keep growing as we add conformers through K=20.
