A single peptide conformer therefore provides a limited basis for structural representation, whereas our framework distilling and fusing multiple conformers yields a more informative representation.

</details>


### [314] [BCT: Bayes-Consistent Tempering for Flow Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27641)
*Daisuke Oba,Hiroki Furuta,Naoaki Okazaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了贝叶斯一致性回火（BCT），通过在生成流的分布和动力学中显式建模温度，解决了后验诱导流语言模型中后验回火带来的两个不匹配问题，在GSM8K和数独任务上显著提升了低温生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统温度缩放作为后验诱导流语言模型的事后概率变换，导致两个不匹配：1）后验回火隐式改变了端点分布和中间噪声状态分布，但去噪器仍在原始状态上训练；2）回火后验权重与为未回火流推导的传输动力学结合。这导致降低温度在流LM中提升GSM8K性能不如离散扩散LM可靠。

Method: 提出贝叶斯一致性回火（BCT），使温度在生成流的分布和动力学中显式化。学习式实现：在回火中间状态上训练去噪器，并使用相应的回火动力学回火端点分布。同时推导了针对常规训练检查点的无训练变体。

Result: 在两种去噪器架构的超球面流LM和von Mises-Fisher流LM上，BCT在广泛温度范围内提升GSM8K生成性能，达到最强回火性能。增益延伸至数独任务，展示紧约束生成的优势。极低温度下，学习式实现比无训练变体显著更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 温度应作为生成流在训练和推理中的属性，而非事后概率调整。BCT通过显式建模温度在分布和动力学中的作用，实现了更一致、鲁棒的低温生成。

Abstract: Temperature scaling is typically applied as a post-hoc transformation of model probabilities. In posterior-induced flow language models, this creates two mismatches. First, posterior tempering implicitly changes both the endpoint distribution and the distribution of intermediate noisy states, while the denoiser remains trained on the original states. Second, the tempered posterior weights are combined with transport dynamics derived for the untempered flow. These mismatches are reflected empirically: lowering temperature improves GSM8K performance much less reliably in flow LMs than in discrete diffusion LMs. We introduce Bayes-Consistent Tempering (BCT), which makes temperature explicit in both the distributions and dynamics of the generative flow. Its learned realization trains the denoiser on tempered intermediate states and tempers the endpoint distribution using the corresponding tempered dynamics. We also derive training-free variants for conventionally trained checkpoints. Across hyperspherical flow LMs with two denoiser architectures and a von Mises--Fisher flow LM, BCT improves GSM8K generation across broad temperature ranges and achieves the strongest tempering performance. The gains also extend to Sudoku, demonstrating benefits on tightly constrained generation. At very low temperatures, the learned realization is also markedly more robust than the training-free variants, supporting temperature as a property of the generative flow across training and inference rather than a post-hoc probability adjustment. Code and model weights are available at https://daioba.github.io/bct .

</details>


### [315] [Pairwise Learning Increases Data Efficiency in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27669)
*Robert Bain,Marjan Rashidi,Elizabeth Chrastil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出成对比较框架，将回归问题重构为聚合相对排名，显著提升小样本下的数据效率，在年龄回归和分类任务上均超越基线，尤其适合标注数据稀缺的临床场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在小样本回归中容易坍缩为低方差预测，忽略个体差异；现有方法依赖大规模预训练或大量标注数据，难以在数据受限场景（如临床应用）中部署。

Method: 引入成对比较框架，将回归转化为成对相对排名的聚合学习。利用无需额外监督的成对构造，从 2.3 万张 UTKFace 图像生成超 10 亿训练比较对；同一框架直接推广到分类任务，用成对监督替代交叉熵。

Result: 在 UTKFace 年龄回归上，从头训练的轻量 ResNet10 达到 3.64 年 MAE，超越含大规模预训练的先前模型。在 STL10 分类（500-2000 标注样本）上，成对监督比同架构 10 路交叉熵最高提升 5.2% 准确率。

Conclusion: 成对监督能大幅提升数据效率，在标注数据有限时同时实现强回归与分类性能，为临床等小样本场景提供了实用的轻量化解决方案。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often fail at small-sample regression, collapsing to low-variance predictions that miss individual differences. Here, we introduce a pairwise comparison framework that reframes regression as aggregated relative rankings. This method improves data efficiency across model architectures and dataset sizes. On the UTKFace dataset (23k images), pairwise learning generates over one billion training comparisons without additional supervision. The paired ranking allows a lightweight ResNet10 trained from scratch on home hardware to achieve a 3.64-year MAE-surpassing previous models, including those with large-scale pretraining. For classification into categories, pairwise supervision consistently outperforms an otherwise identical 10-way crossentropy model on STL10 with 500-2000 labeled examples, improving accuracy by up to 5.2%. Overall, pairwise supervision enhances data efficiency and enables strong regression and classification performance when labeled data are limited, particularly in clinical settings.

</details>


### [316] [Neural Operator Splitting for Fluid Dynamics in Complex Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27671)
*Jianxiang Li,Xi Deng,Hong Chul Nam,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 Neural Operator Splitting 的框架，通过将流体动力学时间步分解为物理可解释的分步算子，并利用条件线性傅里叶神经算子（CL-FNO）分别学习每个算子，同时结合对称性采样和修正循环策略抑制误差累积，在二维和三维复杂几何流体实验中显著降低了滚动预测误差，且推理速度比蒙特卡洛求解器快约三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数值求解器和基于学习的代理模型在处理复杂多样几何下的动力学系统时仍面临挑战。传统单体神经算子难以保持分步数值格式的物理结构，且自回归滚动预测容易累积误差。

Method: 1. 采用算子分裂策略，将每个时间步分解为物理可解释的分步（如对流、扩散、压力投影），并利用底层数值求解器的中间状态分别学习各算子。2. 提出条件线性傅里叶神经算子（CL-FNO），利用分步的线性特性，通过非线性条件调制（如边界几何）实现线性状态映射。3. 引入对称性采样和修正循环两种互补策略，缓解自回归滚动中的误差累积。

Result: 在二维和三维变几何、变初始条件的流体流动实验中，完整模型相比单体全步神经算子，二维平均相对 L^2 滚动误差降低 68%，三维降低 40%。在相近误差水平下，神经算子推理每步速度比蒙特卡洛求解器快约 10^3 倍。

Conclusion: Neural Operator Splitting 框架成功将分步数值格式的物理结构融入神经算子学习，通过分解学习、条件线性建模及误差抑制策略，实现了高精度、高泛化能力和极快的推理速度，为复杂几何流体动力学的数据驱动模拟提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Solving dynamical systems in complex and diverse geometries remains challenging for both numerical solvers and learning-based surrogate models. We propose Neural Operator Splitting, a framework for learning fluid dynamics while preserving the physical structure of fractional-step numerical schemes. Instead of learning the full-step evolution monolithically, we decompose each time step into physically interpretable steps, and learn each operator separately using intermediate states from the underlying numerical solver. Guided by the linearity properties of the fractional steps, we introduce a conditional-linear Fourier neural operator (CL-FNO) whose linear state mappings are modulated by nonlinear conditioning on inputs such as boundary geometry. We further introduce two complementary strategies, symmetry sampling and corrective cycling, to mitigate error accumulation during autoregressive rollout. Experiments on 2D and 3D fluid flows with varying geometries and initial conditions show that our full model reduces mean relative $L^2$ rollout error by 68\% in 2D and 40\% in 3D compared with a single full-step neural operator. At comparable error levels, neural-operator inference is approximately $10^3$ times faster per step than the Monte Carlo solver.

</details>


### [317] [Hybrid Momentum Methods for Optimizing Deep Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27674)
*Haifeng Xu,Yijin Ren,Qi Deng,Chenyu Zhou,Dongdong Ge,Yinyu Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了混合动量，一种结合线性加权平均与几何折扣快速动量的随机优化方法，并结合牛顿-舒尔茨正交化提出了HyMUON优化器，在图像分类、机器翻译和语言建模任务上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典动量方法依赖固定时间尺度的指数移动平均（EMA），会低估仍具信息量的旧梯度，导致长期历史保留与近期变化适应之间的权衡难以平衡。

Method: HyM结合两种动量：线性加权平均（权重与迭代索引成比例，保留增长的历史跨度）和几何折扣快速动量（强调近期梯度）。HyMUON在混合方向上应用牛顿-舒尔茨正交化，模块化设计可与自适应预条件等结合。

Result: 在图像分类、机器翻译、语言建模任务上，HyM和HyMUON匹配或超越强基线。HyMUON在IWSLT14、GPT-2 124M OpenWebText（100k和600k步）上优于Muon。LLaMA-1.3B在C4上预训练，HyMUON最终验证损失2.777，Muon为2.903。

Conclusion: 混合动量通过双时间尺度结合梯度信息有效改进优化，HyMUON作为大模型训练的实用优化器展现出优越性能，为随机优化提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Momentum is central to stochastic optimization in deep learning, including the training of large language models. Classical momentum methods typically rely on a fast exponential moving average (EMA) scheme, which discounts past gradients on a fixed timescale and may underweight older gradients that remain informative. This creates a trade-off between retaining long-term history and adapting to recent changes. We introduce Hybrid Momentum (HyM), which combines a linearly weighted average of past gradients with a geometrically discounted fast momentum. The former assigns weights proportional to the gradients' iteration indices and retains a growing span of history, while the latter emphasizes recent gradients. This hybrid construction is modular and can be combined with adaptive preconditioning and other update transformations. In particular, we propose HyMUON, which applies Newton--Schulz orthogonalization to the hybrid direction. Illustrative examples and controlled experiments demonstrate the benefits of combining gradient information across these two timescales. Across image classification, machine translation, and language modeling, HyM and HyMUON match or improve upon strong baselines. HyMUON improves over Muon on IWSLT14, and in both the 100k- and 600k-step GPT-2 124M OpenWebText regimes. For LLaMA-1.3B pretraining on C4, HyMUON achieves a final validation loss of 2.777, compared with 2.903 for Muon.

</details>


### [318] [Influence Is the Target-Response Jacobian Times Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27689)
*Paul Geertsema,Simon Geertsema*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出目标响应雅可比矩阵 J = [∂ŷ_i/∂y_j]，用于度量模型预测对单个训练目标变化的敏感性。J 与基于影响的训练数据归因方法互补，可无矩阵计算且成本相当。作者证明了样本权重影响 I 与 J 源自同一隐式微分论证，仅差逐样本乘子：对于逐点损失，在平稳点且 Hessian 可逆时 I = J diag(m)；对于规范链接损失，乘子为训练残差 r，即 I = J diag(r)。该恒等式揭示了影响函数在三种场景下的失效模式：插值塌缩、方向冲刷、真实类别塌缩。实验表明 J 的预测 R² 达 0.99–1.00，而影响函数 R² 为负；在 MNIST softmax 分类器上 J 达 0.92，影响函数再次为负。计算上，HIGGS 数据集（10⁶ 样本）上一行 J（即一个预测对所有目标的敏感度）仅需 0.2 秒（RTX 3090），而重新拟合需 10⁶ 次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练数据归因方法主要关注损失中样本权重的敏感性，而非直接针对训练目标的敏感性。作者旨在引入一个直接量化预测对训练目标变化响应的工具，并揭示基于影响函数的方法在替代目标敏感性分析时的根本局限。

Method: 1. 定义目标响应雅可比 J = [∂ŷ_i/∂y_j]。2. 利用隐式微分推导样本权重影响 I 与 J 的关系，证明 I = J diag(m)（逐点损失），并针对规范链接损失（平方误差、逻辑、Poisson）和 softmax 交叉熵给出具体乘子形式。3. 从恒等式推导出影响函数失效的三种模式：插值塌缩、方向冲刷、真实类别塌缩。4. 采用逐预测 R² 作为评价指标，对比 J 与影响函数在反拟合实验中的表现。5. 实现无矩阵计算 J，验证计算效率。

Result: 在 tanh 网络（平方误差、Friedman #1；逻辑损失、MAGIC、HIGGS，样本量 2,000–10⁶）上，J 的 R² 达 0.99–1.00，影响函数 R² 全为负。MNIST softmax 分类器上 J 达 0.92，影响函数仍为负。HIGGS 上计算一行 J（10⁶ 敏感度）仅需 <0.2 秒（一次性设置 <5 分钟），重新拟合需 10⁶ 次。

Conclusion: 目标响应雅可比 J 是一种更准确、高效的工具，可直接量化模型预测对训练目标的敏感性，克服了影响函数在目标扰动分析中的根本缺陷，且具备大规模计算的实用性。

Abstract: We introduce the target-response Jacobian $J = [\partial \hat y_i/\partial y_j]$, which measures how model predictions respond to changes in individual training targets. $J$ complements training-data attribution (TDA) methods, which characterize sensitivity to the weights placed on training observations in the loss rather than to their targets, and can be computed matrix-free at the same cost as influence-based TDA methods. We show that sample-weight influence $\mathcal{I}$ and $J$ follow from the same implicit differentiation argument and differ only by a per-example multiplier: for pointwise losses, $\mathcal{I} = J\operatorname{diag}(m)$ exactly at any stationary point with an invertible Hessian. For canonical-link losses (squared error, logistic, Poisson) the multiplier is the training residual, $\mathcal{I} = J\operatorname{diag}(r)$, and softmax cross-entropy obeys a multiclass version. The identity predicts three ways in which influence fails when used in place of $J$: small residuals shrink its predicted shift towards zero (interpolation collapse), residuals of mixed sign wash out its direction (direction washout), and under softmax cross-entropy it collapses a training point's per-class sensitivities towards its own true class (true-class collapse). We score both operators against refitting with a per-prediction $R^2$ across target perturbations, with no rescaling. On tanh networks trained with squared error (Friedman #1) and logistic loss (MAGIC, HIGGS), with $N$ from 2,000 to $10^6$, $J$ reaches $R^2$ between 0.99 and 1.00, whereas influence's $R^2$ is negative on every dataset. On an MNIST softmax classifier, influence's $R^2$ is again negative, while $J$ reaches 0.92. On HIGGS, one row of $J$, holding all $10^6$ sensitivities of one prediction, takes under 0.2 seconds on an RTX 3090 after a one-time setup of under five minutes, whereas refitting would take $10^6$ refits.

</details>


### [319] [Understanding Data Augmentation under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27701)
*Hong Zheng,Fei Teng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从理论上分析了标签噪声下数据增强的利弊，给出了判断两种机制的充分条件，并揭示了数据增强作为不确定性放大器的反直觉现象，降低了模型稳定性和泛化性能，提供了统一的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数据增强在经验上很成功，但其在标签噪声下的理论影响知之甚少，需要理论解释何时有益、何时有害，并为设计数据增强方法提供统一理论基础。

Method: 通过理论分析，推导学习和泛化界，不依赖特定数据增强技术，仅需温和假设，适用于独立同分布学习和域泛化场景。

Result: 给出了数据增强有益与有害的充分条件，揭示了数据增强本质上作为不确定性放大器，降低模型稳定性和泛化性能的反直觉现象。

Conclusion: 为理解、分析和设计标签噪声下的数据增强方法提供了统一的理论基础，结果适用于广泛的机器学习场景。

Abstract: Data augmentation (DA) has become a fundamental component of modern machine learning systems and is widely regarded as an effective strategy for improving robustness and generalization. Despite its empirical success, the theoretical impact of DA under label noise remains poorly understood. In this work, we first theoretically explain when DA is beneficial and when it becomes detrimental, and provide sufficient conditions for distinguishing between these two regimes under both the i.i.d. learning setting and the more challenging domain generalization scenario. Then, we reveal a counterintuitive phenomenon: DA essentially acts as an uncertainty amplifier in the learning process, decreasing model stability and generalization performance. This finding is supported by the proposed learning and generalization bounds. Our analyses do not depend on specific DA techniques and require only mild assumptions, indicating that the results are applicable to a broad range of machine learning scenarios. Namely, we provide a unified theoretical foundation for understanding, analyzing, and designing DA methods in the presence of label noise.

</details>


### [320] [Multi-Objective Evolutionary Semi-Supervised Learning for Resource-Aware Smart City Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27703)
*Francisco José Sedeño,Jamal Toutouh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOEA-SSL，一种多目标进化方法用于轻量级半监督表格分类，同时优化预测质量和特征使用量，在智慧城市数据集上实现了与现有方法相当的预测性能，同时显著减少特征使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智慧城市应用常面临标注数据稀缺但未标注观测丰富的问题，半监督学习可利用两类数据提升分类性能，但现有方法在特征使用效率方面尚有提升空间。

Method: 采用多目标进化算法（MOEA-SSL），联合优化分类预测质量和特征使用量两个目标，生成帕累托前沿解集，在三个智慧城市分类数据集上以1%、5%、10%标注数据比例进行实验评估。

Result: MOEA-SSL在所有评估条件下均达到与现有轻量级SSL方法相当的预测性能，且相比EA-SSL显著降低特征使用量；帕累托前沿提供了在分类性能轻微下降情况下大幅减少特征使用的备选方案。

Conclusion: MOEA-SSL为智慧城市半监督表格分类提供了有效的多目标优化方案，实现了预测性能与特征效率的良好平衡，帕累托前沿解集为实际部署提供了灵活的权衡选择。

Abstract: Smart-city applications often combine scarce labeled data with abundant unlabeled observations. Semi-supervised learning (SSL) exploits both labeled and unlabeled data to improve classification under limited supervision. This article presents MOEA-SSL, a multi-objective evolutionary approach for lightweight semi-supervised tabular classification that jointly optimizes predictive quality and feature usage. Experimental evaluation on three Smart City classification datasets, considering 1\%, 5\%, and 10\% labeled data, shows that MOEA-SSL achieves competitive predictive performance compared with established lightweight SSL methods while significantly reducing feature usage relative to EA-SSL in all evaluated conditions. The computed Pareto fronts also provide lower-feature-use alternatives with limited degradation in classification performance.

</details>


### [321] [Privacy Accounting for Empirical Denoisers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27710)
*William Cheng,Mijung Park,Dongjun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散模型采样过程中的隐私损失，构建了可计算逐步敏感度的裁剪隐私问责经验去噪器，实现基于高斯差分隐私和f-DP的隐私核算，揭示隐私损失在语义形成关键窗口陡增，提出混合采样器在关键步骤用私有去噪器、后续用公共去噪器，实验表明采样时的设计选择（子采样、停止时间、混合去噪）显著影响隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私训练虽提供正式保证但牺牲样本质量，本文探讨互补问题：采样过程本身会产生多少隐私损失？直接分析神经去噪器敏感度难以处理，需一种可显式计算敏感度的去噪器以实现采样时的隐私核算。

Method: 构建裁剪隐私问责经验去噪器——贝叶斯最优去噪器的无训练估计器，其逐步敏感度可显式计算，使每步去噪成为高斯机制。在此框架下进行高斯DP和f-DP（基于交换函数）的隐私核算，使用隐私损失分布核算器给出经认证的(ε,δ)上界，并用基于累积量的Edgeworth近似作为快速诊断工具。分析去噪轨迹上的隐私损失演变，发现其在语义形成的关键窗口陡增，据此设计混合采样器：前期使用私有经验去噪器，后期切换到公共神经去噪器。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CelebA-64上实验表明：扩散模型的隐私保护不仅取决于训练过程，还取决于采样时的设计选择，包括子采样策略、停止时间、混合去噪策略。混合采样器在保持样本质量的同时显著降低隐私损失。Edgeworth近似在特定条件下可靠，但在某些区域会变得反保守。

Conclusion: 采样过程是扩散模型隐私泄露的重要来源，通过显式敏感度的经验去噪器和混合采样策略，可在不重新训练的情况下实现更精细的隐私-效用权衡。未来工作可进一步优化混合切换点、探索更高效的隐私核算方法及扩展到更大规模模型。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as the foundation of modern generative systems, yet their high memorization capacity raises privacy concerns. While differentially private (DP) training provides formal guarantees, it comes at a cost in sample quality. In this work, we study a complementary question: how much privacy loss is incurred during the sampling process itself? Directly analyzing modern neural denoisers is challenging because their sensitivity to a single training example is generally intractable. To overcome this challenge, we construct and analyze a clipped privacy-accountable empirical denoiser, a training-free estimator of the Bayes-optimal denoiser, whose per-step sensitivity can be computed explicitly, allowing each denoising step to be a Gaussian mechanism. This perspective enables privacy accounting under Gaussian DP when each training example contributes to each denoising step, and under $f$-DP trade-off functions when each generated sample uses a single randomly subsampled block of the training data. We report every $(\varepsilon, δ)$ guarantee as a certified upper bound from a privacy loss distribution accountant, and use a cumulant-based Edgeworth approximation as a fast diagnostic, characterizing where it is reliable and where it becomes anti-conservative. We also analyze the privacy loss along the denoising trajectory and show that it grows steeply near the critical window in which semantic content is formed. This observation motivates hybrid samplers that use the private empirical denoiser through the formative steps where it remains a faithful proxy for the neural denoiser, then switch to a public neural denoiser for the remaining refinement. Our results on CIFAR-10 and CelebA-64 show that the privacy of diffusion models depends not only on the training procedure, but also on sampling-time design choices such as subsampling, stopping time, and hybrid denoising.

</details>


### [322] [EEG-Based Classification of Hypnotic Susceptibility Using Two-Dimensional Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27738)
*Krzysztof Wrobel,Grzegorz M. Wojcik,Filip Postepski,Andrzej Kawiak,Katarzyna Zemla,Grzegorz Sedek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用 EEG 和 2D CNN 分析催眠易感性，在 62 名男性受试者（32 对照，30 高催眠易感性）的 256 导 EEG 数据上，采用滑动窗口、随机切分筛选与 LOSO 交叉验证，发现额前区为最具跨受试者泛化能力的神经标志物，集成 10 个时间窗后前额叶准确率达 0.738，且仅需带通滤波和 z-score 归一化即可达到有竞争力的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引导意象是一种广泛应用于临床的放松技术，但客观评估患者是否进入放松状态仍具挑战。本研究旨在探索基于 EEG 的催眠易感性分类，且不依赖人工特征提取，以识别具有跨受试者泛化能力的神经标志物。

Method: 采集 62 名男性受试者（32 对照组，30 高催眠易感性组）在引导意象或认知任务条件下的 256 导 EEG 数据。使用最小预处理（带通滤波 + z-score 归一化）后的信号，构建滑动窗口框架，在 7 个解剖学定义脑区与 124 个 10 秒时间窗上训练多达 868 个独立的 2D CNN 模型。先进行段级随机切分筛选，再用留一受试者出交叉验证（LOSO）获得受试者无关的性能估计，最后通过集成 K=10 个时间窗的预测提升性能。

Result: 随机切分下最佳单窗口模型准确率达 0.82；LOSO 下前部脑区泛化更好：额区平均准确率 0.646（AUC 0.692），集成 10 窗后前额叶准确率提升至 0.738。枕区在随机切分中表现优异，但在 LOSO 下降至机会水平，揭示其反映的是受试者特异性 EEG 指纹而非真实组间差异。

Conclusion: 仅需带通滤波和 z-score 归一化即可实现无需人工特征提取的有竞争力的 EEG 分类。前部皮层区域（特别是前额叶）被确认为催眠易感性最具泛化能力的神经标志物。

Abstract: Guided Imagery (GI) is a mind-based relaxation technique widely used in clinical and therapeutic settings. Objectively assessing whether a patient has entered a relaxed state remains a challenge for clinicians. This study investigates electroencephalography (EEG)-based classification of hypnotic susceptibility using two-dimensional Convolutional Neural Networks (2D CNNs) applied to minimally preprocessed signals, without manual feature extraction. Data were collected from 62 male participants (32 Control, 30 Hypno) using a 256-channel dense array amplifier during Guided Imagery or cognitive task conditions. A sliding-window framework evaluated up to 868 independent models across seven anatomically defined brain regions and 124 ten-second time windows. Segment-level random-split screening was followed by Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) cross-validation to obtain subject-independent performance estimates, and an ensemble approach combined predictions across K = 10 windows. The best single-window model achieved accuracy of 0.82 on the random split. Under LOSO, anterior regions generalised better across subjects: the frontal region achieved mean accuracy of 0.646 (AUC: 0.692). An ensemble of K = 10 windows further improved the prefrontal region to mean accuracy of 0.738. The occipital region, dominant in random-split screening, collapsed to near-chance under LOSO, revealing subject-specific EEG fingerprinting rather than genuine group differences. Bandpass filtering and z-score normalisation suffice for competitive EEG classification without manual feature extraction. Anterior cortical regions are identified as the most generalisable neural markers of hypnotic susceptibility.

</details>


### [323] [Constant Step Sizes for Muon-Type Spectral Optimizers on Strongly Convex Quadratics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27747)
*Pratik Jawanpuria,Bhabatosh Garai,Bamdev Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了Muon等谱优化器在强凸矩阵二次问题上的行为，提出了增益和长度两个量，将谱更新分为三类：有界增益表现为预条件梯度下降；无界增益但长度消失仍收敛，步长由总曲率决定；长度不消失则几乎对所有初始化都不收敛。实验表明实际Muon变体落入这三类，取决于归一化和缩放方式，而非极分解多项式近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谱优化器（如Muon）使用谱变换梯度更新权重矩阵，但缺乏在理想化设置下的理论分析。作者旨在隔离非凸性、噪声和步长调度等干扰因素，通过强凸矩阵二次问题系统研究谱更新的收敛行为，理解其核心机制。

Method: 在强凸矩阵二次目标上，以常数步长分析谱优化器。引入两个关键量：增益（梯度在各奇异方向上的放大因子）和长度（更新范数）。根据这两个量的性质，将谱更新分为三类 regime 并分析其收敛性。

Result: 三类 regime 的收敛行为：(i) 有界增益：表现为预条件梯度下降，收敛步长由最大曲率决定；(ii) 无界增益但长度消失：仍收敛，容许步长由总曲率决定；(iii) 长度不消失：对几乎所有初始化不收敛。实验验证实际Muon变体落入这三类，仅取决于更新的归一化和缩放方式，而非极分解的多项式近似。

Conclusion: 谱优化器的收敛性由增益和长度两个量决定，而非具体的极分解近似多项式。实际应用中，通过调整归一化和缩放可控制优化器处于哪种 regime，从而影响收敛性和步长选择。这为理解和设计谱优化器提供了统一理论框架。

Abstract: Muon and related optimizers update a weight matrix with a spectrally transformed gradient. In this work, we study such spectral optimizers with a constant step size on strongly convex matrix quadratics, which isolate the effect of the update from the confounds of nonconvexity, noise, and step size schedules. We analyze an update using two quantities: the gain, which is the factor by which it amplifies the gradient along each singular direction, and the length, which is the norm of the update. These two quantities place the popular spectral updates in three regimes with different behavior: (i) with a bounded gain, the iterates behave like preconditioned gradient descent and converge for step sizes set by the largest curvature; (ii) with an unbounded gain but a vanishing length, they still converge, with the admissible step sizes set by the total curvature instead; and (iii) with a non-vanishing length, they do not converge for almost every initialization. Interestingly, the practical variants of Muon fall in all three regimes, depending only on how the update is normalized and scaled and not on the polynomial that approximates the polar factor. Experiments validate these findings.

</details>


### [324] [Detection of Memorization in Diffusion Models Through Thermodynamic Singularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27754)
*Iljung Kim,Kangwoo Park,Yung-Kyun Noh,Daniel D. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析扩散模型中的记忆化现象，提出基于热力学奇点的检测准则，可有效区分正常分布学习与记忆化网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的理想学习是反转底层密度函数上的扩散过程，但关键失败模式是记忆化——反扩散过程直接从个别训练样本学习扩散，导致过拟合。需要对记忆化反过程进行理论表征，并开发可靠的检测方法。

Method: 提供记忆化反扩散过程的解析表征，提出检测相关热力学奇点的准则，并在一系列旨在促进或抑制记忆化的神经网络模型上进行验证。

Result: 所提出的准则能可靠地区分正常学习的网络与记忆化网络，验证了热力学奇点作为记忆化指示器的有效性。

Conclusion: 热力学奇点可作为扩散模型记忆化的可靠理论指示器，为理解、检测和防范记忆化提供了分析工具。

Abstract: In diffusion models, proper distributional learning entails reversing a diffusion process over underlying density functions, whereas a key failure is memorization, in which the reverse process instead learns diffusion from individual training samples. We provide an analytical characterization of such memorized reverse processes, along with a criterion that detects the associated thermodynamic singularity. The proposed criterion reliably distinguishes between properly learned and memorizing networks across a range of neural network models designed to promote or mitigate memorization.

</details>


### [325] [AC/DC on a Budget - Alternating Sparse Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27758)
*Rahul Nittala,Advait Gadhikar,Tom Jacobs,Rebekka Burkholz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ARC (Alternating Rewiring Compression) 方法，通过优化稀疏化训练中的密集-稀疏交替阶段，显著降低计算成本并保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经网络稀疏化方法依赖密集和稀疏训练阶段的交替，但密集阶段计算开销大，作者旨在揭示密集阶段的作用机制并减少其计算成本。

Method: 基于实证分析发现，密集阶段中被掩码参数重新引入时会获得不成比例的大梯度，驱动掩码重连和参数符号翻转。据此提出三项创新：(1) 密集阶段将被掩码参数零初始化以促进有效符号翻转；(2) 缩短密集阶段；(3) 用稀疏重连阶段完全替代密集阶段，仅更新活跃掩码和少量高梯度的掩码外权重。

Result: 在 CIFAR100 和 ImageNet 上，ARC 在极低计算成本下达到基线 AC/DC 性能，建立了优于标准稀疏到稀疏算法的性能-FLOPs 帕累托前沿。在 GPT-2 和 OpenWebText 上，验证困惑度和平均零样本性能均有提升。

Conclusion: ARC 通过优化密集-稀疏交替训练，实现了计算效率和模型性能的更好平衡，为大规模模型稀疏化提供了更实用的方案。

Abstract: State-of-the-art neural network sparsification relies on alternating dense and sparse training phases. While computationally expensive, dense phases are critical for performance. Our empirical analysis reveals the mechanics behind this gain: upon reintroduction, previously masked parameters receive disproportionately large gradients that drive essential mask rewiring and parameter sign-flipping. Building on these insights, we optimize the performance-to-FLOPs Pareto frontier through three innovations: (1) zero-initializing masked parameters during dense phases to promote relevant sign flips, (2) shortening dense phases, and (3) replacing the dense phase entirely with a sparse rewiring phase that updates only the active mask and a small, high-gradient fraction of out-of-mask weights. Evaluated on CIFAR100 and ImageNet, our variant ARC (Alternating Rewiring Compression) matches baseline AC/DC performance at a fraction of the computational cost, establishing a superior performance-to-FLOPs frontier also compared to standard sparse-to-sparse algorithms. Experiments with GPT-2 on OpenWebText further show improvements in validation perplexity and average zero-shot performance

</details>


### [326] [Foundation-Style Pretraining for Scalable and Transferable Distributed PV Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27763)
*Dayin Chen,Xiaodan Shi,Xin Xu,Zhongyue Su,Shibo Zhu,Ruizhe Deng,Dongxiao Zhang,Yuntian Chen,Jinyue Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种领域特定的基础式预训练框架，用于分布式光伏电站的多站点、多时效功率预测，通过混合重构目标实现零样本泛化，并优于现有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式光伏快速增长导致对大量地理分散电站的准确预测需求增加，现有方法多针对单一站点或特定预测时效，需重复训练且难以复用至新站点，存在扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 将数值天气预报（NWP）辅助预测建模为序列补全问题，统一历史光伏功率、气象观测、未来NWP预报和时间编码；设计三种预训练目标（预测对齐重构、通用重构、混合重构），其中混合目标结合光伏预测运行结构与随机序列重构，实现最均衡性能。

Result: 在479个分布式光伏电站实验中（340站开发、139站空间留出零样本评估），多站点预训练的混合重构（MR）模型在6小时至7天多时效上优于单站基线、共享基线及通用时间序列基础模型；预训练模型可直接部署至新站点，后续可利用本地数据适配区域运营目标。

Conclusion: 该框架为大规模分布式光伏机群提供了可扩展的预测范式，通过基础式预训练实现跨站点、跨时效的零样本泛化与快速适配，显著降低模型维护成本并提升预测精度。

Abstract: The rapid growth of distributed photovoltaic (PV) generation is increasing the demand for accurate forecasting across large numbers of geographically dispersed stations. Existing approaches are commonly developed for individual sites or specific forecasting horizons, requiring repeated training and maintenance as new stations are integrated and limiting direct reuse at previously unseen sites. To address this scalability gap, this paper presents a domain-specific foundation-style pretraining framework that learns a shared forecasting model from heterogeneous multi-site and multi-horizon PV data. NWP-assisted forecasting is formulated as a sequence-completion problem that unifies historical PV power, meteorological observations, future numerical weather prediction (NWP) forecasts, and time encodings. Three pretraining objectives are designed and systematically evaluated, including forecast-aligned reconstruction, generic reconstruction, and mixed reconstruction. The mixed objective combines the operational structure of PV forecasting with random sequence reconstruction and provides the most balanced performance across forecasting accuracy, degraded NWP conditions, and downstream adaptation. Experiments on 479 distributed PV stations use 340 stations for model development and 139 spatially held-out stations for zero-shot evaluation. Across horizons from 6 hours to 7 days, the multi-site pretrained MR model outperforms representative station-specific and shared baselines, as well as generalist time-series models, including a recent large-scale pretrained foundation model. The pretrained model can also be directly deployed at new PV stations and subsequently adapted to region-specific operational objectives when local data become available, providing a scalable forecasting paradigm for large distributed PV fleets.

</details>


### [327] [Engram Memory Network: Brain-Inspired Prototype Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27770)
*Hyunjun Kim,Myoung Hoon Ha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Engram Memory Network (EMN)，一种受人类记忆系统和腹侧视觉通路启发的原型解释模型，利用 Hopfield 式记忆模块迭代检索代表性原型，在多个数据集上显著提升感知相似度、子类对齐和信息保留，同时达到最高平均分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有原型解释模型在不同特征数据集上解释质量下降，缺乏标准化评估指标，限制了实际应用。认知神经科学中人类记忆系统将输入相关信息编码为支持识别的表征，为改进提供启示。

Method: 构建模仿腹侧视觉流和颞下皮层的 Engram Memory Network，核心是 Hopfield 启发的记忆模块，迭代检索代表性原型；将记忆细化后的潜变量用于重建和分类，促使选定原型捕捉跨数据集的输入相关信息。评估维度包括感知相似度 (LPIPS, DISTS)、子类对齐和信息保留 (NMI)。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10/100、MNIST、HAM10000、CUB-200 上，EMN 平均使视觉相似度提升 17.06%、子类对齐提升 104.57%、NMI 提升 42.42%，平均分类准确率达 87.62%，均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 神经科学启发的解释机制能有效推进原型模型的可解释性和可靠性，EMN 通过记忆模块实现跨数据集的高质量原型检索，为高风险决策中的 AI 解释提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes decision-making. In response, prototype-based models in explainable AI have attracted growing attention for their ability to retrieve visually similar examples as faithful explanations. Although these models achieve competitive classification accuracy, they often exhibit degraded explanation quality across datasets with varying characteristics, limiting their practical applicability. We draw on insights from cognitive neuroscience, particularly the human memory system, which encodes input-relevant information into representations that support recognition. We propose an Engram Memory Network (EMN), a model that emulates the ventral visual stream and inferior temporal cortex in the human brain. Central to this model is a Hopfield-inspired memory module that iteratively retrieves representative prototypes. These memory-refined latents are used for both reconstruction and classification, encouraging the selected prototypes to capture input-relevant information across diverse datasets. To address the lack of standardized evaluation for prototype explanations, we assess three complementary aspects: perceptual similarity (LPIPS and DISTS) for perceived resemblance, subclass alignment, measuring whether prototypes match the input's fine-grained category beyond supervised labels, and information preservation (NMI), measuring how much input information is captured by selected prototypes. Experiments show that EMN improves visual similarity by 17.06%, subclass alignment by 104.57%, and NMI by 42.42% on average across CIFAR-10/100, MNIST, HAM10000, and CUB-200, while achieving the highest average classification accuracy (87.62%) among all methods. These findings underscore the effectiveness of neuroscience-inspired explanation mechanisms in advancing the interpretability and reliability of prototype-based models.

</details>


### [328] [How Much Do $\ell_1$ Attacks Need Concentrated Perturbations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27773)
*Melika Shirian,Kianoosh Vadaei,Arshia Hemmat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出在l1攻击中添加逐坐标上限约束，通过精确投影在循环内强制执行联合约束，研究扰动集中度对攻击成功率的影响。实验表明，上限≤32/255时保留中位数97%的攻击成功率，峰值幅度下降80%，有效支撑集近三倍。循环内约束优化比事后裁剪多保留88个百分点的攻击成功率。扰动集中度作为l1攻击评估的互补、模型和攻击相关的维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局l1预算限制对抗扰动的总量，但不限制其在各坐标上的分布。研究旨在探究当限制大幅度坐标变化时，l1攻击的观测成功率还能保留多少，即研究扰动集中度这一维度对攻击效果的影响。

Method: 在保持原攻击目标函数不变的前提下，引入逐坐标上限约束，并通过精确的循环内投影强制执行联合约束（全局l1预算+逐坐标上限）。对APGD-l1、SLIDE和FMN-l1三种攻击方法扫描不同上限值，在18个标准模型设置和ImageNet上进行评估。

Result: 上限≤32/255时，相对于无上限基线保留中位数97%的攻击成功率；中位数峰值幅度下降80%，中位数有效支撑集规模近三倍。在相同实现l1范数下，带上限扰动在结构上仍保持差异；ImageNet上选定上限下LPIPS和DISTS更低。循环内约束优化比事后裁剪多保留高达88个百分点的攻击成功率。适用上限随攻击和模型变化，对抗训练分类器表现出更异质的集中度敏感性。

Conclusion: 扰动集中度是l1攻击评估中一个互补的、依赖于模型和攻击方法的重要维度。通过循环内强制执行逐坐标上限约束，可在大幅降低扰动峰值幅度和扩大支撑集的同时，保持极高的攻击成功率，优于事后裁剪。这揭示了l1攻击中扰动分布特性对攻击效果的关键作用。

Abstract: A global $\ell_1$ budget limits how much an adversary may change an image, not how that change is distributed. We ask how much of an $\ell_1$ attack's observed success remains when large coordinate changes are restricted. We add a per-coordinate cap to the budget, enforce the joint constraint by exact in-loop projection, and sweep the cap for APGD-$\ell_1$, SLIDE, and FMN-$\ell_1$ without altering their objectives. Across 18 standard-model settings, caps of at most $32/255$ retain a median $97\%$ of attack success relative to each method's native uncapped baseline, while median peak magnitude falls by $80\%$ and median effective support nearly triples. At matched realized $\ell_1$, capped perturbations remain structurally distinct; separate ImageNet comparisons also show lower LPIPS and DISTS at selected caps. Direct in-loop constrained optimization preserves up to $88$ percentage points more attack success than post-hoc clipping, highlighting the importance of constraint enforcement. Suitable caps nevertheless vary across attacks and models, and adversarially trained classifiers exhibit more heterogeneous concentration sensitivity. These results establish perturbation concentration as a complementary, model- and attack-dependent axis of $\ell_1$ attack evaluation.

</details>


### [329] [Foundations of the Scaling Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27789)
*Tianyu Zhao,Zhixiang Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过几何近似、谱分析和约束优化三大分析框架，理论解释了验证损失与模型规模、训练曝光量及计算量之间的经验缩放定律，并将其应用于视觉和分子预测任务，量化了SE(3)等域特定坐标下的资源权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为经验观测到的缩放定律提供理论基础，阐明模型规模、数据量、计算量之间的定量关系，并分离涌现/钻研现象中的评分阈值与学习动态，以及测试时缩放中的候选覆盖与答案准确性，指导资源分配。

Method: 采用三大关联分析：(1) 几何近似——将空间分辨率、内在维度、平滑度与函数误差联系起来，再由损失函数映射到任务风险；(2) 谱分析——结合特征可见性、协方差特征值、目标对齐度，描述额外数据如何减少未解析的预测质量；(3) 约束优化——推导参数-数据平衡与训练-推理权衡。同时分析涌现/钻研与测试时缩放，并在视觉与分子预测上应用，含SE(3)商空间近似的维度依赖性案例研究。

Result: 给出了参数-数据最优平衡、训练-推理计算前沿，量化了商空间近似增益的维度依赖性，对比了优化后的计算前沿，并分别分析了构象覆盖度和相关样本精度。

Conclusion: 理论框架统一解释了缩放定律、涌现、钻研及测试时缩放，揭示了域特定坐标（如SE(3)）如何改变资源比较，为跨领域的计算资源分配提供了定量指导。

Abstract: Empirical scaling laws relate validation loss to model size, training exposure and compute, providing a quantitative basis for allocating resources. This technical note explains these relationships through three linked analyses. Geometric approximation connects spatial resolution, intrinsic dimension and smoothness to function error; the loss function determines how that error transfers to task risk. Spectral analysis combines feature visibility, covariance eigenvalues and target alignment to describe how additional data reduce unresolved predictive mass. Constrained optimization then gives the parameter-data balance and the training-inference trade-off. We also examine emergence and grokking, separating scoring thresholds from learning dynamics, and test-time scaling, separating candidate coverage from selected-answer accuracy. Applications to vision and molecular prediction show how domain-specific coordinates change the resource comparison. An $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ case study quantifies the dimension dependence of quotient-space approximation gains and compares optimized compute frontiers, alongside separate analyses of conformational coverage and correlated-sample precision.

</details>


### [330] [SliMOO: Interpretable Multi-Objective Evolutionary Search for LLM Depth Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27812)
*Guanchen Li,Yixing Xu,Xuanwu Yin,Dong Li,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SliMOO，一种面向大语言模型深度剪枝的可解释多目标进化搜索框架，通过单层移除探测构建任务-层重要性图谱，并基于 NSGA-II 进行 Pareto 感知的剪枝掩码搜索，在 Llama-3.1 和 Qwen3 系列模型上实现了更优的多任务权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度剪枝方法忽略了 Transformer 层在不同任务上的贡献差异（任务依赖的层贡献异质性），往往针对单一基准优化而牺牲广泛能力，因此应将 LLM 深度剪枝建模为多目标优化问题而非单一评分排序问题。

Method: SliMOO 包含两阶段：1) 单层移除探测，构建任务-层重要性图谱，揭示任务共享层与任务特异层，作为搜索先验；2) Pareto 感知的进化搜索，在任务-层先验指导下生成剪枝掩码候选，以每个任务上与稠密模型的偏差为目标评分，通过 NSGA-II 环境选择保留非支配解。

Result: 在 Llama-3.1-70B 25% 稀疏度下，数学领域平均性能从 24.33% 提升至 34.40%，代码领域从 35.03% 提升至 39.14%；在 Llama-3.1 与 Qwen3 模型族上均持续优于基于规则、贪心及标量化的基线方法。

Conclusion: SliMOO 通过引入任务-层重要性先验与多目标进化搜索，有效解决了深度剪枝中的任务异质性问题，实现了跨任务的更强 Pareto 最优权衡，为大模型高效部署提供了通用且可解释的剪枝范式。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) grow more powerful, their scale makes them costly to serve. Depth pruning offers a practical route to efficiency by directly reducing memory and latency without hardware-specific support. However, we observe that transformer layers exhibit markedly different task-dependent layer contributions, whereas existing depth-pruning methods ignore this heterogeneity and thus tend to optimize for a narrow domain or benchmark rather than preserve broad capability. This suggests that LLM depth pruning should be formulated as a multi-objective optimization problem rather than a single-score ranking problem.
  We propose SliMOO, an interpretable multi-objective evolutionary search framework for LLM depth pruning. SliMOO first uses single-layer removal probing to construct a task-layer importance map, which provides an interpretable view of task-shared and task-specific layers and serves as a search prior. It then performs a Pareto-aware evolutionary search for pruning masks, where candidates are generated under the guidance of the task-layer prior, scored by their deviation from the dense model on each task, and retained through NSGA-II environmental selection. Across the Llama-3.1 and Qwen3 model families, SliMOO consistently achieves stronger multi-task trade-offs than rule-based, greedy, and scalarized baselines. For example, on Llama-3.1-70B at 25% sparsity, it improves the math-domain average from 24.33% to 34.40% and the code-domain average from 35.03% to 39.14%. Code will be made available at https://github.com/AMD-AGI/SliMOO.

</details>


### [331] [From Generic to Dedicated: A Novel Optimizer for Online Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27835)
*Yongyi Wu,Zheng Wang,Sen Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPIN优化器，通过插值聚合Polyak-Ruppert平均（塑性）与Primal平均（稳性），显式解耦在线持续学习中的塑性与稳性，隐式提供逆Hessian近似与Tikhonov正则化，可无缝替换现有OCL方法中的标准优化器，在无重放和严格内存约束等挑战性设置下均显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线持续学习（OCL）面临单遍约束下的非平稳数据流，极易发生灾难性遗忘。现有研究虽从数据、架构、优化等角度探索策略，但优化器常直接沿用SGD、AdamW等通用方法，缺乏针对OCL中稳性-塑性权衡的专用优化动力学设计。

Method: 提出SPIN（Stability-Plasticity INterpolation）优化器：将Polyak-Ruppert平均重构为“塑性”引擎（快速移动序列），将Primal平均重构为“稳性”锚点（自适应序列），通过插值二者显式解耦塑性与稳性。理论证明SPIN隐式采用逆Hessian近似，为噪声单样本Hessian估计提供关键的Tikhonov正则化以缓解遗忘。SPIN仅接收梯度输入，可直接替换现有OCL方法中的标准优化器。

Result: 大量实验表明，在多种OCL方法（包括无重放设置和严格内存约束）中，用SPIN替换标准优化器均能带来持续且显著的性能增益。

Conclusion: SPIN通过优化动力学层面的稳性-塑性显式解耦，为OCL提供了一个即插即用、理论有保证的优化器，有效缓解了单遍约束下的灾难性遗忘，具有广泛的适用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Online Continual Learning (OCL) requires models to learn from non-stationary data streams under a strict single-pass constraint, making them highly susceptible to catastrophic forgetting. While existing studies have explored various strategies from data, architecture, and optimization perspectives, the optimizer often directly uses generic approaches such as SGD and AdamW. In this work, we reveal a new link between optimization dynamics and OCL needs by recasting Polyak-Ruppert averaging as the engine of "plasticity" and Primal averaging as the anchor of "stability". This inspires our novel optimizer, SPIN (Stability-Plasticity INterpolation), which explicitly decouples plasticity and stability by interpolating between a fast-moving "plasticity" sequence and an adaptive "stability" sequence. We theoretically demonstrate that SPIN implicitly employs an inverse Hessian approximation, providing crucial Tikhonov regularization to damp noisy, single-sample Hessian estimates and mitigate forgetting. More importantly, SPIN can be seamlessly integrated into existing OCL methods by taking resulted gradients as input and replacing standard optimizers. Extensive experiments show that using SPIN instead of standard optimizers yields consistent and significant performance gains across multiple OCL approaches, including the challenging rehearsal-free setting and under strict memory constraints.

</details>


### [332] [Elicitation or Expansion? Demystifying Gains from Agent Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27889)
*Younghyun Kim,Wooseok Seo,Minseo Kim,Seunghyuk Oh,Minjae Lee,Donghoon Kim,Hyung Il Koo,Jaehyung Kim,Wonjun Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了语言模型代理中 harness 优化的效果，区分了 elicitation（在已可达任务上提升成功率）与 expansion（扩展可解任务范围）两类增益。通过 pass@k 分析发现，harness 在 pass@1 上的优势在大尝试预算下可能反转，且总体增益掩盖了困难任务上的短板，强调了细粒度诊断的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harness 优化能在不更新模型权重的情况下显著提升性能，但单纯的成功率无法区分优化带来的两类增益：elicitation（在已可解任务上更可靠）与 expansion（扩展可解任务边界）。该区分对递归自我改进和进化搜索至关重要，因为 expansion 能提供更广泛的成功经验以推动能力边界，但现有研究对此区分探讨不足。

Method: 借鉴强化学习文献中的 pass@k 分析方法（对比 RL 训练前后的模型），将优化后的 harness 与优化前的版本进行对比。具体包括：评估可达任务覆盖率的变化；将 harness 优化与模型扩展、增加推理时计算量进行对比；考察增益随任务难度的变化情况。实验涵盖静态预测任务和动态任务。

Result: 在静态预测和动态任务上均发现：harness 优化在 pass@1 上对更强干预（如模型扩展、增加推理计算）的优势，在更大尝试预算（更大 k）下可能发生反转；且聚合增益掩盖了在困难任务上的表现短板。

Conclusion: Harness 优化的效果不能仅用简单的成功率或 pass@1 衡量，必须进行细粒度诊断（如 pass@k 曲线、任务难度分层分析），以区分 elicitation 与 expansion，从而为递归自我改进等场景提供可靠的优化信号。

Abstract: Recent studies in harness optimization have shown substantial performance gains in language model agents without updating model weights. A well-designed harness more effectively leverages the underlying model's capabilities by shaping context, tool use, and execution workflows. However, naïve success rates alone do not fully characterize whether optimized harness yields more reliable success on tasks already within reach (elicitation) or a broader range of solvable tasks (expansion). This distinction is crucial for recursive self-improvement and evolutionary search, as expansion may provide a broader range of successful experiences to push the capability boundaries. Despite the growing significance, this distinction remains underexplored in harness optimization. We conduct a systematic analysis to study the effect of gains from harness optimization. Building on pass@k analyses in the reinforcement learning (RL) literature that compare models before and after RL training, we compare optimized harnesses with their pre-optimization counterparts. Specifically, we assess changes in reachable task coverage, compare harness optimization with model scaling and increased inference-time compute, and examine how gains vary with task difficulty. Across static prediction and dynamic tasks, we find that pass@1 advantages of harness over stronger interventions can reverse at larger attempt budgets and aggregate gains conceal shortfalls on hard tasks, underscoring the need for a fine-grained diagnosis.

</details>


### [333] [Physical-Variable-Informed Deep Learning for Robust Diagnosis in Industrial Fluid Transport Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27891)
*Yu Sha,Junqi Tao,Dixin Zhou,Horst Stoecker,Domagoj Vnucec,Andreas Widl,Kai Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PhyNet提出了一种物理变量引导的深度学习框架，用于异构采样条件下的工业故障诊断，通过物理-响应交叉注意力机制实现无需严格时间对齐的交互，在多个基准测试中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业流体输送系统通过异构传感流监测，但响应信号随工况变化，且与物理变量在采样率、观测时长、数据粒度上存在差异，导致鲁棒故障诊断困难。

Method: 提出PhyNet框架，将物理变量作为全局工况约束，响应信号作为局部诊断证据，设计物理到响应的交叉注意力机制，连接序列级物理变量token与可学习的全局响应token，实现无需严格时间对齐的交互。

Result: 在四个基准测试（三个真实空化数据集和一个公开两相流数据集）上，PhyNet在所有评估指标上均达到最优性能。类别分析、注意力分析和掩码分析显示对模糊状态的判别能力提升，物理变量有效约束了局部响应解释。

Conclusion: 研究展示了一种面向部署的异构工业环境鲁棒诊断方法，证明物理变量可作为全局约束指导局部响应信号解释，提升诊断鲁棒性。

Abstract: Industrial fluid transport systems are monitored through heterogeneous sensing streams, but robust fault diagnosis remains difficult because response signals vary with operating conditions and differ from physical variables in sampling rate, observation duration and data granularity. To address these challenges, we propose a physical-variable-informed deep learning framework (PhyNet) for robust fault diagnosis under real-world sampling heterogeneity. PhyNet treats physical variables as global operating constraints and response signals as local diagnostic evidence. Its physical-to-response cross-attention mechanism connects sequence-level physical-variable tokens with a learnable global response token, enabling interaction without strict temporal alignment. Across four benchmarks, including three real-world cavitation datasets and one public two-phase-flow dataset, PhyNet achieves the best performance across all evaluated metrics. Class-wise analyses, together with attention and masking analyses, show improved discrimination of ambiguous states and indicate that physical variables meaningfully constrain local response interpretation. These findings demonstrate a deployment-oriented approach to robust diagnosis in heterogeneous industrial environments.

</details>


### [334] [The Shape of the DPO Hyperparameter Landscape: What to Measure, How It Moves with Scale, and When Not to Transfer It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27895)
*Hannan Mahadik,David Salinas,Jacek Golebiowski,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPO 超参数（如学习率和 β）对模型性能影响大，需针对特定模型、数据集和评估目标单独调优；验证损失是差代理；短跑训练可降低调优成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO 广泛用于语言模型后训练，但其性能强烈依赖超参数选择，缺乏系统性理解，尤其是不同模型规模和训练步数下的最优超参数演变，以及基准测试对超参数选择的指示作用。

Method: 在同一模型家族的不同规模上进行密集超参数网格实验，分析最优超参数随模型大小和更新步数的变化，利用中间检查点作为廉价信号加速超参数优化，在静态和动态基准上评估，对比多种最先进超参数优化策略。

Result: 最优学习率随模型规模变化；超参数不可跨数据集迁移；验证损失与下游基准最优超参数弱相关，是差代理；短候选训练运行可显著降低选择成本。

Conclusion: DPO 超参数需针对目标模型、数据集和评估目标专门调优；短跑训练可降低调优成本；发布所有训练配置和评估结果以支持可复现分析。

Abstract: Direct preference optimisation (DPO) is widely used for language model post-training, but its performance depends strongly on the choice of its hyperparameters, such as the learning rate and the reference-policy regularisation parameter $β$. To better understand the DPO hyperparameter landscape, we conduct extensive experiments using a dense hyperparameter grid across different model scales from the same family. This allows us to analyse how the optimal DPO hyperparameters evolve with model size and the number of update steps, with intermediate checkpoints providing an inexpensive signal to accelerate the hyperparameter optimisation process. Furthermore, by evaluating our models on both static and dynamic benchmarks, we identify which benchmarks provide the strongest signal for hyperparameter selection. Finally, we investigate how different state-of-the-art hyperparameter optimisation strategies perform in this setting. Our experiments show that the optimal learning rate varies with model size and that hyperparameters are not transferable across training datasets. We also find that validation loss is a poor proxy for hyperparameter optimisation, as hyperparameters that minimize validation loss only weakly correlate with optimal hyperparameters for downstream benchmarks. Therefore, DPO hyperparameters need to be tuned specifically for the target model, dataset, and evaluation objective. We also show that short candidate training runs can substantially reduce the cost of this selection. We release all our training configurations and evaluation results to support reproducible analysis of selection quality and training cost.

</details>


### [335] [Near-Duplicate Image Selection Transfers Preferences in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27943)
*Jaeyoung Kim,Eunseok Kim,Dongsuk Jang,Seunghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究发现，通过在近似重复图像中基于标题似然性进行选择（而不改变原始照片和人工标题），模型可以将其偏好隐性地传递给其他模型，这种“潜意识学习”效应在多个视觉语言模型中稳健存在，并可跨代传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型越来越多地为其他模型策划训练数据，研究者想知道：仅通过在保持源照片和人工标题固定的情况下，在近似重复图像中做选择，是否能引发偏好传递？

Method: 实验设计：对于每张照片，策展模型从8个近似重复变体中基于标题似然性选择一个（无目标特定提示）。学生模型在策展模型选择的数据上训练，然后通过无图像读出测量目标与替代目标的对数似然比。测试目标包括猫头鹰、骆驼、橡树和政治人物，跨LLaVA-1.5、Qwen2.5-VL、Qwen3-VL、Gemma 4等模型，并考察数据集大小、学习率、训练预算的影响，以及跨代传递（二代学生通过标题而无显式目标提及获得偏好）。

Result: 1) 猫头鹰目标在四个模型中均显示定向偏移，目标旋转使偏移转向骆驼、橡树、政治人物。2) 猫头鹰选择的学生在三模型中得分超所有计数匹配的随机重选学生，在LLaVA-1.5中超55/60。3) Qwen3-VL在新源库和新训练策展模型上复现。4) 效应大小取决于数据集大小、学习率、训练预算；贪婪选择使Qwen3-VL向目标移动10.4个百分点，其他模型0.5-1.3点。5) 一代学生在LLaVA-1.5和Qwen3-VL中将猫头鹰偏好传给二代（标题无显式猫头鹰提及）。6) 线性探针分类选择臂准确率达59.7%（随机50%）。

Conclusion: 即使是在保持源照片和标题固定的情况下，仅通过去重风格的近似重复选择，也能改变学生模型的目标相对读出；这种潜意识学习揭示了数据策划中微妙的偏好传递机制，对模型级联训练的安全性和可控性有重要启示。

Abstract: Subliminal learning is the phenomenon in which a model transmits its preferences to another model through data that do not explicitly express them. As models increasingly curate training data for other models, we ask whether this transfer can arise from choosing among near-duplicate images while keeping the source photographs and human-written captions fixed. For each photograph, a curator selects one of eight near-duplicate variants by caption likelihood, without target-specific prompts. Students trained on target-preferring curators' selections show higher mean target-versus-alternatives log-likelihood ratios in an image-free readout than students trained on neutral selections. Under a prespecified decision rule, this directional shift appears for an owl target in LLaVA-1.5, Qwen2.5-VL, Qwen3-VL, and Gemma 4, with curators and students initialized from the same base checkpoint, and target rotations move it toward camels, oak trees, and a political figure. The owl target-selected student also scores above all count-matched random-reselection students in three models and above 55 of 60 in LLaVA-1.5, and Qwen3-VL shows the shift again on a new source bank with newly trained curators. Its size depends on dataset size, learning rate, and training budget; greedy choices among the eight options move toward the target by 10.4 percentage points in Qwen3-VL and 0.5-1.3 points elsewhere. First-generation students also pass the owl preference to a second generation through captions without explicit owl mentions in LLaVA-1.5 and Qwen3-VL. Thus even a deduplication-style choice among near-identical copies can shift students' target-relative readouts with source photographs and captions fixed; the tested linear probes classify the selecting arm with accuracies up to 59.7% (chance 50%).

</details>


### [336] [Unsupervised Head Network Personalization for Split Computing via Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27952)
*Yutaro Horikawa,Takayuki Nishio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于分割计算（SC）的数据高效头部网络训练方法，结合联邦学习和知识蒸馏，利用移动设备上少量非独立同分布（non-IID）且无标签的数据训练头部网络，在保护隐私的前提下实现了与基线相当的精度，并将移动端通信需求降低95%，精度仅下降3-5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分割计算旨在通过将神经网络分割为头部和尾部网络并部署在移动设备与服务器上，平衡计算负载并降低延迟。然而，移动设备算力和带宽有限，头部网络需替换为轻量级模型，但移动端难以获取足量高质量的标注数据，且用户数据含隐私敏感信息，难以聚合训练。

Method: 提出一种数据高效的头部网络训练方法：利用联邦学习框架，在移动设备本地使用少量非IID无标签数据，通过知识蒸馏从预训练的完整模型（教师模型）向轻量级头部网络（学生模型）迁移知识，引入个性化机制提升模型适应性，整个过程不上传原始数据，保障隐私。

Result: 实验表明，在仅对本方法施加数据限制（少量、非IID、无标签）的条件下，其准确率仍与基线方法相当；移动端通信量减少95%，相比原始模型准确率仅下降3-5个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了分割计算中移动端数据稀缺、异构且隐私敏感的问题，通过联邦学习与知识蒸馏的结合实现了高通信效率和隐私保护下的头部网络训练，为资源受限边缘设备上的分布式推理提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Split Computing (SC) is a distributed inference method designed for balancing computational load and reducing latency by partitioning a neural network into head and tail networks and deploying them across both a mobile device and a server, respectively. Due to the limited bandwidth and computational power of mobile devices, the head network is often replaced with a neural network that requires lower computational and communication resources. However, it is challenging to prepare a sufficient quantity and quality of labeled data on the mobile device side for training the replacement head network. Furthermore, the data prepared by mobile users often contains sensitive privacy information, making data aggregation difficult. In this paper, we propose a data-efficient head network training method for SC. This method utilizes federated learning and knowledge distillation to train the head network using a small amount of non-IID and unlabeled data from mobile devices, ensuring that the training datasets are not exposed. Moreover, by introducing personalization, we enhance the accuracy to levels comparable to or exceeding those of existing methods. The experimental evaluations demonstrate that, despite data constraints imposed only on our method, it achieves accuracy comparable to the baselines. Furthermore, it significantly reduces the communication requirements by 95% on mobile devices, with only a 3-5 percentage point drop in accuracy from the original model.

</details>


### [337] [On Johnson-Lindenstrauss Transforms for Byzantine-Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27989)
*Aleksandr Shestakov,Vladimir Aletov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用Johnson-Lindenstrauss变换的几何保证，提出了一种拜占庭鲁棒优化方法，通过根据工人消息与可信参考梯度的对齐程度加权，降低通信成本并理论实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习数据和模型规模增长需要高效优化方法；分布式优化面临通信成本高、拜占庭故障等挑战；现有压缩机制如JL变换可保持几何性质，但未充分用于拜占庭鲁棒场景。

Method: 利用JL变换近似保持内积和距离的性质，设计拜占庭鲁棒优化算法，根据工人梯度与可信参考梯度的对齐度（通过JL投影后的内积）加权聚合，减少通信开销。

Result: 理论证明该方法在拜占庭鲁棒性和收敛性上具有保证；实验结果支持理论发现，展示了在通信受限分布式环境下的有效性。

Conclusion: JL变换可有效用于拜占庭鲁棒分布式优化，通过几何对齐加权实现鲁棒聚合，同时降低通信成本，为大规模分布式训练提供新思路。

Abstract: The growing scale of data and models in machine learning calls for efficient optimization methods. Distributed optimization addresses this challenge, with compression mechanisms helping to reduce communication costs. A classical tool for dimensionality reduction is the Johnson-Lindenstrauss (JL) transform, which is simple to implement and offers strong geometric guarantees. In particular, JL transforms approximately preserve inner products and pairwise distances between vectors with high probability. In this paper, we exploit this property in Byzantine-robust optimization, where worker messages are weighted according to their alignment with a trusted reference gradient. We further support our theoretical findings with experimental results.

</details>


### [338] [When Momentum Helps Polyak--Ruppert Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27990)
*Alexandra Suvroikova,Darina Dvinskikh,Yuriy Dorn,Alexander Gasnikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Polyak-Ruppert平均在SGD和动量方法中的有限样本表现，证明了在线-离线桥接，将误差分解为中心、协方差和形状误差，给出了达到渐近协方差所需的轮数条件和关键批大小，比较了重球法和Nesterov法的协方差误差，并建立了统一的Berry-Esseen界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Polyak-Ruppert平均在有限样本下的收敛速度和可并行化程度，以及不同动量方法之间的差异，为实际应用提供理论指导。

Method: 对于强凸二次函数加加性噪声，证明在线-离线桥接：尾部平均样本上的经验风险最小化估计量与任何在线过程的尾部平均查询点相差一个牛顿步。将该步骤分解为边界项，进而分解为中心误差、协方差误差和形状误差。利用必要充分条件分析最小曲率方向上的中心误差，比较不同方法的协方差误差，并用统一的Berry-Esseen界控制形状误差。

Result: SGD需要m ≫ κ²轮，动量方法只需m ≫ κ轮；关键批大小从Θ(√N/κ)增长到Θ(√(N/κ))。重球法在最硬方向上的相对方差超额为Θ(κ^{3/2}/m)，Nesterov法在所有方向上的相对误差为O(√κ/m)。形状误差与方法无关，当其他两个误差消失时置信区间有效。在重尾噪声、状态相关噪声和裁剪下结论的变化也被分析，并建立了非线性Nesterov平均与ERM的一阶比较。

Conclusion: 本文提供了Polyak-Ruppert平均在有限样本下的精细误差分解，揭示了中心误差主导收敛速度，动量方法显著降低所需轮数，且Nesterov法在协方差误差上优于重球法。形状误差的统一界保证了置信区间的有效性。结果推广到了更一般的噪声设置和非线性情况。

Abstract: Polyak--Ruppert averaging gives SGD and momentum methods the same asymptotic covariance. We characterize how fast finite runs reach it and how far they can be parallelized. For strongly convex quadratics with additive noise, we prove an online--offline bridge: the empirical risk minimizer on the samples of a tail average is one Newton step from the tail-averaged query point of any online procedure. For SGD, heavy ball, and Nesterov's method with constant momentum, this step is a boundary term at the two ends of the tail and splits the gap to the offline estimator into center, covariance, and shape errors. Necessary and sufficient conditions show that, on the least curved direction, the center error sets the pace: SGD needs $m\ggκ^2$ rounds but momentum only $m\ggκ$, where $κ$ is the condition number, so at sample size $N$ the critical batch size grows from $Θ(\sqrt{N}/κ)$ to $Θ(\sqrt{N/κ})$. The covariance error separates the momentum methods: heavy ball has relative variance excess $Θ(κ^{3/2}/m)$ along the stiffest direction, whereas Nesterov has relative error $O(\sqrtκ/m)$ in every direction. The shape error is method-independent by a Berry--Esseen bound uniform in $κ$ and batch size, so confidence intervals are valid when the other two errors vanish. We further examine how the conclusions change under heavy-tailed or state-dependent noise and clipping, and establish a first-order comparison with ERM for nonlinear Nesterov averaging under explicit conditions.

</details>


### [339] [RANGER: Retrieval Agent for Navigating Graphs with Semantic Evaluation Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28010)
*Artem Mikhaylov,Pavel Konoplev,Nikolay Efanov,Artyom Sorokin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RANGER的混合方法，将大语言模型（LLM）作为导师融入强化学习（RL）训练循环，为知识图谱上的多跳问答任务提供语义感知的密集奖励信号，显著提升了检索策略的有效性和语义对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法面临两大挑战：LLM虽具备强语义理解能力，但在结构化环境中难以进行精确的多步导航；RL擅长学习导航策略，但难以设计语义有意义的奖励函数。需要一种深度融合两者优势的方法，解决复杂序列决策任务中的语义对齐与精准检索问题。

Method: 提出RANGER框架：基于Transformer的RL策略网络，采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）训练；LLM作为导师评估智能体遍历路径的语义相关性，生成密集的语义奖励信号；将检索智能体集成到完整的问答流水线中，在开放域问答数据集上进行端到端评估。

Result: 实验表明，RANGER优于基于经典图搜索的检索增强基线和使用启发式奖励训练的RL智能体，在NLP指标和LLM-as-a-Judge评分上均实现显著提升，验证了LLM深度融入RL训练循环的有效性。

Conclusion: 将LLM作为导师嵌入RL训练循环，通过语义感知的奖励信号指导策略学习，能有效解决知识图谱多跳问答中的语义对齐与精准导航难题，为复杂推理任务提供了更有效的检索策略。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models and Reinforcement Learning offers a promising direction for complex sequential decision-making tasks, such as multi-hop question answering over knowledge graphs. While LLMs provide strong semantic understanding and reasoning capabilities, they often struggle with precise, multi-step navigation in structured environments. Conversely, RL agents excel at learning navigation policies but are hindered by the difficulty of designing semantically meaningful reward functions. This paper introduces a novel hybrid approach in which an LLM serves as a mentor to an RL agent during training by providing dense, semantically informed reward signals. The RL agent, built on a Transformer-based policy and trained with Group Relative Policy Optimization, learns to navigate a knowledge graph to retrieve relevant information. The LLM evaluates the semantic relevance of the agent's traversed paths, generating reward signals that align the agent's behavior with the information needs expressed in a query. We integrate this Retrieval Agent for Navigating Graphs with Semantic Evaluation Rewards (RANGER) into a complete question-answering pipeline and evaluate it on the open question answering datasets. Experimental results show that the proposed approach outperforms both a baseline retrieval-augmented system with classical graph search and one using an RL agent trained with heuristic rewards, achieving a significant increase in NLP metrics and LLM-as-a-Judge scores. Our findings demonstrate that deep integration of LLMs into the RL training loop yields more effective and semantically aligned retrieval policies for complex reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [340] [From RL Papers to Framework Code: Inclusion and Review in Seven Open-Source Training Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28027)
*Taiheng Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过追踪7个开源RL后训练框架的提交历史并关联6,939篇arXiv论文，系统分析了学术界提出的RL训练方法被框架采纳的情况。研究发现：仅37/1,598篇通用RL方法论文被至少一个框架采纳；尽管论文数量从72增长到386，但180天内被采纳的数量稳定在5-7篇。被采纳论文更倾向于训练30B+参数模型（41%对10%）且修改现有损失函数的系数/裁剪/掩码/权重（29%对9%）。框架公开讨论中极少拒绝方法，>90%未被采纳方法无任何讨论线索。技术报告显示用户极少公开报告训练失败，且报告中仅覆盖13个广泛采纳方法中的8个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践者通过训练框架使用语言模型的强化学习方法，框架包含的方法仅需一次配置更改即可被用户使用。然而，目前缺乏系统性度量：学术界提出的RL方法究竟有多大概率、以何种模式被主流框架采纳？框架采纳决策的公开讨论过程如何？用户在实际使用中遇到的训练失败是否被记录并关联到框架实现？本文旨在填补这一实证空白。

Method: 作者追踪了7个开源RL后训练框架的Git提交历史，并将其与2025年1月至2026年6月期间6,939篇经过筛选的arXiv论文建立链接。从中识别出1,598篇提出通用RL训练方法的论文，按季度分为5个队列。通过分析框架的Issue和PR记录，考察方法被采纳或被拒绝的公开讨论情况。同时分析13个大型开放权重模型的技术报告，了解用户报告训练失败的情况及其中提及的框架方法。最终构建了一个追踪器，将训练失败关联到框架实现，并列出各框架的贡献规则和早期提案。

Result: 1) 采纳率极低：1,598篇通用RL方法论文中仅37篇（2.3%）被至少一个框架采纳；五个季度队列中，180天内被采纳数量稳定在5-7篇，尽管队列规模从72增至386。2) 被采纳论文特征显著：更常训练≥30B参数策略（41% vs 10%），更常修改现有损失的系数/裁剪/掩码/权重（29% vs 9%）；排除框架维护公司论文后差距依然存在，代码发布比例无显著差异。3) 公开决策极少：>90%未被采纳方法在框架Issue/PR中无任何引用或命名线索；出现的提案常长期开放无回复。4) 训练失败报告稀缺：用户极少公开报告失败；13个模型技术报告中仅提及8个广泛采纳方法。

Conclusion: 学术界提出的RL后训练方法进入主流开源框架的转化率极低，且呈现‘富者越富’模式：大规模训练、微调现有损失组件的方法更易被采纳。框架维护过程缺乏透明的评估与拒绝机制，大量提案沉寂无反馈。用户端训练失败极少公开报告，导致框架实现与实际故障模式脱节。作者发布的追踪器旨在连接训练失败、框架实现与贡献规则，推动更系统的方法采纳与失效知识积累。

Abstract: Practitioners use reinforcement learning methods for language models through training frameworks, so a method that a framework includes is one configuration change away for its users. We measure how often this happens. We trace seven open-source frameworks for RL post-training through their commit histories and link them to 6,939 screened arXiv papers from January 2025 to June 2026. Of 1,598 papers that propose a general RL training method, 37 reached at least one framework. Across five quarterly cohorts of papers, the number included within 180 days stayed between 5 and 7 while the cohorts grew from 72 to 386 papers. Compared with papers from the same quarters, included papers more often trained policies of 30B parameters or more, at 41 against 10 percent, and more often changed a coefficient, clip, mask or weight in the existing loss, at 29 against 9 percent. Both gaps hold without papers from the companies that maintain the frameworks, while code release was equally common in both groups. The frameworks' issues and pull requests show that few methods are turned away in public. More than nine in ten of the general methods no framework included have no thread there that cites or names them, and many proposals that do appear stay open without a reply. Two further records, the frameworks' issues and the technical reports of 13 large open-weight models, show that users rarely report training failures in public and that the reports state 8 of the 13 widely included methods. We release the data as a tracker that links training failures to framework implementations and lists each framework's contribution rules and earlier proposals.

</details>


### [341] [When Does Training on Synthetic Data Provably Work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28036)
*Leroy Odunlami,Tobias Riedlinger,Hanno Gottschalk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在共享条件标签分布下，基于合成数据训练的二分类器向真实分布迁移的条件。给出有限样本超额误差界：当真实协变量分布对合成分布绝对连续且密度比可控时可迁移；当真实分布在合成分布外有质量（奇异情况）时，若假设类足够表达，合成风险无法一致控制真实误差，甚至 Wasserstein 距离任意小也可能迁移失败；但若假设类与条件标签概率的 log-odds 均为 Lipschitz，则可恢复依赖于真实样本落在合成数据覆盖外概率的界，在完全奇异情况下仍提供迁移保证。实验在低维受控设置和 32,200 张 ImageNet 对应合成图像上验证：对抗分类器在合成域误差 0.5% 但在真实域误差 99.25%，常规训练架构的合成与真实测试误差正相关。建议合成数据应大规模预算且模型需在真实数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量训练数据是机器学习的主要瓶颈，推动越来越多地使用人工生成数据。需要理论理解何时合成数据训练的分类器能成功迁移到真实分布，特别是在共享条件标签分布的设定下。

Method: 理论分析分两部分：(1) 真实协变量分布对合成分布绝对连续且密度比受控时，推导有限样本超额误差界。(2) 研究部分/完全奇异情况（真实分布在合成分布支撑集外有质量）：证明对于表达力强的假设类，合成风险无法一致控制真实误差；对于假设与条件标签概率 log-odds 均为 Lipschitz 的函数类，给出依赖于真实样本落在合成数据覆盖外概率的界。实验在低维受控设置和 32,200 张由四个最先进生成器生成的 ImageNet 对应合成图像上进行对抗性评估。

Result: 理论上：绝对连续情况有迁移保证；奇异情况下表达力强的假设类会导致合成域近似最优但真实域趋近最坏误差，即使 Wasserstein 距离任意小；Lipschitz 假设类可恢复迁移保证。实验上：对抗分类器在合成域误差 0.5% 但真实域误差 99.25%，常规训练架构的合成与真实测试误差呈正相关。

Conclusion: 合成数据训练的迁移性取决于假设类复杂度与分布覆盖关系。实践中应大规模预算合成数据样本量，且必须在真实数据上验证模型，不能仅依赖合成域性能。

Abstract: High-quality training data are a major bottleneck in machine learning, motivating increasing use of artificially generated data. We study when a binary classifier trained on synthetic data transfers successfully to a real distribution, under a shared conditional label distribution. First, we derive finite-sample excess-error bounds when the real covariate distribution is absolutely continuous with respect to the synthetic distribution under a controlled density ratio. Second, we study the partially and fully singular case, where the real distribution has mass outside the synthetic one. If the hypothesis class is sufficiently expressive, synthetic risk alone cannot uniformly control real-world error: a classifier can be nearly optimal on synthetic data while approaching worst-case error on the uncovered real region, even when the distributions are arbitrarily close in Wasserstein distance. For function classes where hypotheses and the log-odds of the conditional label probability are Lipschitz, we recover a bound in terms of the probability that a real sample lies outside the reach of the synthetic data, restoring a transfer guarantee even in the fully singular case. Experiments confirm this in controlled low-dimensional settings and on 32,200 images generated as counterparts to real ImageNet images by four state-of-the-art generators: an adversarial classifier reaches 0.5% error on the synthetic domain but 99.25% error on the real domain, while ordinarily trained architectures show positively correlated synthetic and real test errors. Synthetic data should be budgeted at high sample counts and models validated on real data.

</details>


### [342] [Poisoning-Based Backdoors Are Not Cryptographic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28045)
*Masahiro Kaneko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明基于投毒的后门植入无法实现类加密认证的不对称性：攻击者无法植入防御者无法逆向工程的验证器；任何可通过投毒安装的验证器，白盒防御者均可重构。实验验证了这一二分法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究后门攻击中是否存在根本性的不对称性，即攻击者能植入只有其知晓触发器生成规则的验证器，而防御者即使观察模型行为也无法逆向推导验证器，从而使基于学习的防御在原则上失效。

Method: 采用归约论证：将能够从投毒数据安装验证器并保持良性行为的训练过程视为该验证器的学习器；若验证器计算上难学习则无法通过投毒安装，若可安装则白盒防御者可用同量级标注触发样本重构。另辅以推理时秘密密钥对同等防御者可见的互补论证。在指令微调大语言模型上实验验证。

Result: 理论证明不存在仅攻击者可生成触发器而防御者无法逆向验证器的投毒型后门；实验显示无配置能同时使后门对攻击者有效且对防御者不可恢复，验证了理论二分法。

Conclusion: 基于投毒的后门植入天然无法实现加密级不对称性，防御者原则上可重构验证器；后门鲁棒性与可防御性呈不可避免的权衡，为后门防御提供理论基础。

Abstract: To defend against backdoor attacks on neural networks, the defender must identify the verifier function that determines whether an input is a trigger, rather than rejecting individual triggers in isolation, since an attacker can generate new triggers satisfying the same verifier faster than they can be blocked. If a backdoor verifier could be installed with the asymmetry of a cryptographic authentication scheme, in which only the attacker can produce trigger inputs while the defender cannot reverse-engineer the verifier from observations, learning-based defense would be impossible in principle. We show that no such asymmetry can arise from poisoning-based installation, in which the only verifier-dependent input to training is a poisoned dataset. The argument is a reduction. A training procedure that installs a verifier from poisoned data, so that the backdoor fires on unseen triggers while benign behavior is preserved, is itself a learner for that verifier. Any verifier that is computationally hard to learn is therefore impossible to install through poisoning, and any verifier that poisoning can install is reconstructible by a white-box defender holding comparably many labeled trigger observations. A complementary argument shows that a secret key supplied to the model at inference is observable to the same defender. The result takes representative trigger observations as given and concerns when the backdoor fires rather than what it does. Experiments on instruction-tuned large language models confirm the dichotomy, with no configuration yielding a backdoor that is both effective for the attacker and irrecoverable for the defender.

</details>


### [343] [Universal Online Convex Optimization With Delayed Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28058)
*Hao Qiu,Andrew Jacobsen,Emmanuel Esposito,Mengxiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种通用的在线凸优化算法，能够在无需预先知道损失函数曲率类别（一般凸、强凸、exp-concave）或参数的情况下，同时适应这三类损失函数，并实现了最优的延迟反馈依赖性。主要贡献包括：1) 一个具有二阶超额损失保证的延迟元学习器，实现曲率自适应聚合；2) 一个新的exp-concave基础学习器，将延迟依赖从最大延迟改进为最大同时缺失观测数σ_max，同时保留√d_tot依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线凸优化中，反馈延迟是实际应用常见问题。现有工作通常需要预先知道损失函数的曲率类别和参数，或者对不同曲率类别需要不同算法。同时，延迟依赖通常表现为最大延迟d_max，而在许多实际场景中，最大同时缺失观测数σ_max远小于d_max。需要一种通用算法自动适应不同曲率，并实现更紧的延迟依赖。

Method: 论文设计了一个两层结构：基础学习器层和元学习器层。基础学习器针对三种曲率类别分别设计，其中exp-concave基础学习器通过新的二阶剩余形式实现σ_max依赖。元学习器采用延迟版本的二阶超额损失保证，能够聚合延迟反馈下的基础学习器输出，实现曲率自适应。关键技术是构建适合延迟设置的二阶剩余量，并证明元学习器的超额损失界。

Result: 算法同时达到三类损失的最优延迟依赖（至多双对数因子）：一般凸损失为Õ(√(T + d_tot))，强凸和exp-concave损失为Õ(σ_max log T)。其中d_tot为累积总延迟，σ_max为最大同时缺失观测数。exp-concave基础学习器将延迟依赖从d_max改进为σ_max，同时保留√d_tot项。这些界匹配已知最优结果。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了通用在线凸优化在延迟反馈下的曲率自适应，提出的元学习器框架和exp-concave基础学习器为解决延迟在线学习中的自适应问题提供了新思路。将延迟依赖从最坏情况的最大延迟改进为更精细的并发缺失观测数，显著拓展了算法在实际大规模延迟系统中的适用性。

Abstract: We study universal online convex optimization with delayed feedback. We develop a single algorithm that simultaneously adapts to general convex, strongly convex, and exp-concave losses without prior knowledge of the curvature class or parameters. Our regret bounds match the best known dependence on the time horizon $T$ and delays up to doubly logarithmic factors: roughly $\sqrt{T + d_{\mathrm{tot}}}$ for convex losses and $σ_{\max}\log T$ rates with curved losses, where $d_{\mathrm{tot}}$ is the total cumulative delay and $σ_{\max}$ is the maximum number of simultaneously missing observations. Our main technical contribution is a delayed meta-learner with a second-order excess-loss guarantee, which enables curvature-adaptive aggregation of delayed base learners. In addition, our new exp-concave base learner yields a sharper regret bound which improves the state-of-the-art delay dependence by replacing the maximum delay with the potentially much smaller $σ_{\max}$, while retaining the complementary $\sqrt{d_{\mathrm{tot}}}$ dependence.

</details>


### [344] [MuPACE: Learning to Pace Muon for LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28072)
*Jan Kobiolka,Christian Frey,Josif Grabocka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出MuPACE，一种元学习的循环策略网络，用于在LoRA微调过程中自适应调整全局学习率。MuPACE基于Muon优化器（归一化更新固定方向），仅元学习标量步长，在64个微调任务上超越共享固定调度64-78%的情况，并与逐任务调优的峰值学习率持平，且能从16层模型零样本迁移至64层大模型（Qwen3-32B等）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习率调度在训练前固定，但LoRA微调的合理学习率尺度随模型、任务、适配器配置显著变化。实践者需为每个新问题重新调优调度，且固定调度无法响应训练动态。需要一种能跨任务分摊学习率选择、在线自适应且泛化到未见模型/任务的方法。

Method: 保持Muon优化器不变（其归一化更新固定更新方向），仅元学习全局标量步长。MuPACE是一个小型循环策略网络，在微调运行分布上元训练，以观测轨迹（如损失、梯度统计）为条件，输出对可解释解析调度的有界乘性调整。

Result: 在64个微调问题上，MuPACE以强共享默认值超越标准固定调度64-78%的问题，性能与逐任务调优的峰值学习率调度持平。仅在≤16层模型上元训练，MuPACE能一镜到底迁移至64层模型（Qwen3-32B、Qwen3.8-27B）的未见任务，单次运行即可与扫描调度竞争。

Conclusion: 通过元学习仅全局步长标量而非完整调度，MuPACE实现了高效、可迁移的自适应学习率控制，解决了LoRA微调中学习率敏感且难以调优的问题，展示了跨模型规模和任务的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning rate schedules are typically fixed before training begins, yet when fine-tuning large language models with low-rank adapters (LoRA), the appropriate scale varies substantially with the model, task, and adapter configuration. Practitioners re-tune a schedule for every new problem, yet a tuned schedule still cannot react to the training dynamics it encounters. We instead amortize learning rate selection across LoRA fine-tuning problems. We leave the base optimizer, Muon, untouched, so that its normalized update fixes the direction, and meta-learn only the global step size. Our method MuPACE is a small recurrent policy, meta-trained across a distribution of fine-tuning runs, that conditions on the observed trajectory and emits bounded adjustments to an interpretable analytic schedule. On 64 fine-tuning problems, MuPACE outperforms standard fixed schedules at a strong shared default on 64-78% of problems and is on par with the same schedules with per-task tuned peaks. Meta-trained only on models with up to 16 layers, MuPACE transfers one-shot to unseen tasks on models as deep as 64 layers, such as Qwen3-32B and Qwen3.8-27B, where a single run remains competitive with swept schedules.

</details>


### [345] [PULSE: Personalized Opportunistic Learning for Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28081)
*Mattia Giovanni Campana,Dariush Salami,Hüseyin Yiğitler,Stephan Sigg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PULSE，一种面向可穿戴设备的事件驱动个性化机会学习框架，将每次相遇视为在线协作决策，仅在梯度兼容时融合同伴梯度，并利用历史交互指导未来同伴选择。实验表明，PULSE 在保持或提升个性化分类性能的同时，将同伴辅助交换次数减少 80%-94%，且在可穿戴硬件上仅占用 149.8 KiB RAM 和 561.8 KiB Flash，验证了选择性机会协作在资源受限设备上的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备部署后的数据分布常与训练数据差异巨大，纯本地适应受限于单设备数据量；传统联邦/去中心化学习依赖持续通信和稳定连接，现有机会学习方法虽利用相遇机会，但未充分考虑相遇的效用、兼容性及通信开销。因此需要一种能在不稳定、资源受限的机会网络中实现高效个性化适应的方法。

Method: PULSE 采用事件驱动框架，将每次设备相遇建模为在线协作决策：根据同伴梯度与本地学习方向的兼容性决定是否交互，仅融合兼容梯度；同时维护历史交互记录，指导未来同伴选择。算法在合成和真实接触轨迹上评估，涵盖人类活动识别、跌倒检测、压力识别三个任务，并进行实机部署测量资源占用。

Result: 在三个识别任务上，PULSE 保持或提升了个性化分类性能，相比机会模型平均将同伴辅助交换次数降低 80%-94%。实机实验显示同伴辅助操作能耗极低，完整实现仅占用 149.8 KiB RAM 和 561.8 KiB Flash，满足可穿戴设备资源约束。

Conclusion: 选择性机会协作能在有效实现个性化适应的同时，大幅降低通信频率和资源需求，为资源受限的可穿戴设备提供了实用的持续学习范式。

Abstract: Wearable devices increasingly support personalized health and well-being applications, but post-deployment data can differ substantially from those used for training, while purely local adaptation may be limited by the data available on a single device during real-world operation. Conventional federated and decentralized learning rely on recurrent communication and stable connectivity, whereas existing opportunistic approaches often exploit available encounters without adequately considering their utility, compatibility, or communication cost.
  We propose PULSE, an event-driven framework for personalized opportunistic learning that treats each encounter as an online collaboration decision, selecting either no interaction or a promising nearby peer. Peer gradients are incorporated only when compatible with the local learning direction, while past interactions guide future peer selection. Across human activity recognition, fall detection, and stress recognition under synthetic and real contact traces, PULSE preserves or improves personalized classification performance, while reducing peer-assisted exchanges by 80-94% compared with opportunistic model averaging. On-device experiments further demonstrate its feasibility on wearable hardware: peer-assisted operations require limited energy, while the complete implementation occupies 149.8 KiB of RAM and 561.8 KiB of flash. The obtained results show that selective opportunistic collaboration can provide effective personalization while limiting communication and resource demands on constrained wearable devices.

</details>


### [346] [From Complex Histories to Simple Predictors: Predictive Dimension in Dynamic Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28090)
*Hantong Feng,Duxin Chen,Wenwu Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出连续时间图学习应识别用于未来预测的低维预测状态，而非重建完整历史。作者定义并估计了“内在预测维度”来量化任务所需的有效预测自由度，据此提出轻量级模型PreDyG，仅用代表性复杂基线0.07%的参数量即实现了更优的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间图学习方法严重依赖复杂神经架构编码不断扩展的交互历史。然而，由复杂历史诱导的未来条件分布往往可由低维预测状态流形刻画，意味着仅少数预测自由度对未来交互至关重要。因此，学习应聚焦于识别足够的低维状态而非重建全历史。

Method: 1) 定义并独立估计内在预测维度：从历史条件未来分布中移除与历史无关的边缘分布，分解得到的预测残差算子，估计保留全历史预测能力所需的最小有效秩。2) 基于此提出PreDyG，将模型容量与内在预测维度匹配的轻量级模型。

Result: 跨数据集实验表明，模型性能饱和主要由内在预测维度决定。PreDyG在仅使用代表性复杂基线0.07%参数量的情况下，实现了更优的预测性能。

Conclusion: 连续时间图学习的核心应是识别足够用于未来预测的低维预测状态，而非重建完整历史。将模型容量与任务的内在预测维度相匹配，可获得高效且有效的模型。

Abstract: Existing continuous-time graph learning methods rely heavily on complex neural architectures to encode ever-expanding interaction histories. However, the future conditional distribution induced by complex histories can often be characterized by a low-dimensional predictive state manifold, implying that only a few predictive degrees of freedom are essential for future interactions. We therefore argue that continuous-time graph learning should identify low-dimensional states sufficient for future prediction rather than reconstruct the full history. To make this perspective measurable, we define and independently estimate the intrinsic predictive dimension, which quantifies the effective predictive degrees of freedom required by a given task. Specifically, we remove the history-independent marginal from the history-conditioned future distribution, decompose the resulting predictive residual operator, and estimate the minimum effective rank needed to preserve the predictive power of the full history. Experiments across datasets show that model performance saturation is largely determined by the intrinsic predictive dimension. Based on this finding, we propose PreDyG, a lightweight model whose capacity is matched to the intrinsic predictive dimension. PreDyG achieves superior predictive performance with only 0.07\% of the parameters of a representative complex baseline.

</details>


### [347] [Causal Discovery for Linear Models with Latent Confounding via Multi-domain Identifiable Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28134)
*Danru Xu,Aapo Hyvärinen,Sara Magliacane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LinADMG 框架，利用多域设置下的可识别表示学习，在不依赖非高斯性或弓自由假设的情况下，实现了线性因果模型中含混杂因子的 ADMG 结构识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据中潜在混杂普遍存在，现有完全识别 ADMG 的方法通常需要非高斯性或图约束（如弓自由），限制了适用范围。作者旨在开发一种更通用的识别框架，仅利用多域数据即可识别噪声变量进而恢复因果结构。

Method: 提出 Linear ADMG Learning (LinADMG) 框架：1) 利用多域设置通过可识别表示学习识别噪声变量；2) 证明噪声变量可识别性足以恢复所有直接因果关系；3) 在额外的潜在混杂无抵消条件下可识别完整 ADMG；4) 基于文献中四种可识别表示学习方法实现并验证。

Result: 在模拟和半合成数据集上验证了 LinADMG 的有效性，展示了其在不依赖非高斯性或弓自由假设下，能够准确恢复线性因果模型中的直接因果关系及完整 ADMG 结构。

Conclusion: LinADMG 通过多域可识别表示学习实现了含潜在混杂的线性因果模型结构识别，放宽了传统方法的强假设，为实际应用中因果发现提供了更灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Causal discovery with latent confounding is particularly challenging, but a common setting in real-world data. In this work, we focus on linear causal models with latent confounding, represented by acyclic directed mixed graphs (ADMGs). Existing methods that fully identify ADMGs typically assume non-Gaussianity or graphical constraints, such as bow-freeness. In contrast, we propose Linear ADMG Learning (LinADMG), a framework for learning ADMGs for linear causal models through identifiable representation learning. Rather than relying on non-Gaussianity or bow-freeness, LinADMG leverages multi-domain settings to identify the noise variables. We show that the identifiability of the noise variables is sufficient for recovering all direct causal relations, and that, under an additional latent-confounding no-cancellation condition, LinADMG can identify the whole ADMG. We implement LinADMG with four identifiable representation learning approaches from the literature and validate the methods on simulated and semisynthetic datasets.

</details>


### [348] [Curriculum Learning for Generative Flexible Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28145)
*Wenyin Zhou,Hossein Azizpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于课程学习的柔性分子对接方法GCLDock，通过逐步引入大构象变化样本训练，显著提升了对长尾分布 apo-to-holo 构象变化的建模能力，在PDBBind ESMFold apo基准测试中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性对接面临的核心挑战是长尾分布的apo-to-holo构象偏移：少数蛋白发生大幅构象变化。实际观察发现降低大偏移样本权重能改善泛化，且长尾包含真正困难样本和噪声样本的混合，因此需要一种能逐步适应大构象变化的训练策略。

Method: 采用课程学习策略，设计线性调度函数逐步增加大构象变化样本的训练权重。基于现有扩散/流生成模型框架，结合松弛优化和基于置信度的姿态筛选，构建GCLDock模型。

Result: 在PDBBind ESMFold apo基准上，GCLDock实现46.52% Top-1成功率（配体RMSD<2Å），在额外要求PoseBusters有效性条件下达到40.26%，均超越现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 课程学习能有效应对柔性对接中的长尾构象变化分布，通过渐进式训练显著提升大构象变化案例的预测精度，为实际药物发现提供了更可靠的对接工具。

Abstract: Flexible docking is a highly practical task, aiming to predict the joint configuration of a protein-ligand complex from a protein apo structure and a ligand SMILES string. Diffusion and flow-based generative models have made this task tractable by modeling the posterior distribution over the ligand pose and the protein conformation conditioned on the apo input. The central challenge, however, remains the long-tailed apo-to-holo structure shift, where a small fraction of protein undergo large conformational changes. In this work, motivated by the practical observation that downweighting large shift examples improves generalization, and by the mixed nature of the long tail consisting of genuinely difficult examples and noisy ones, we propose to use curriculum learning for this task, gradually incorporating large conformational change examples during training. With a linear pacing function, we show that an existing method can be strongly enhanced.
