On the PDBBind ESMFold apo benchmark, with relaxation and confidence-based pose selection, the resulting model GCLDock achieves a 46.52% top-1 success rate (ligand RMSD less than 2A) and 40.26% when additionally requiring PoseBusters validity, outperforming existing baselines.

</details>


### [349] [Coincidence-Maximizing Customer Clustering Using Smart Meter Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28178)
*Ramiro H. Gálvez,Gabriel Martos Venturini,Iván Mondrzak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种直接最大化加权巧合因子的客户分组方法，证明联合优化为强NP-hard问题，开发了固定峰值细化算法，在四个公开智能电表数据集上验证，显著优于基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力配电规划依赖于客户群内的峰值需求巧合，但基于相似度的聚类无法直接优化这一目标，因此需要直接最大化加权巧合因子的客户分组方法。

Method: 将客户分组形式化为最大化群组巧合因子的加权平均值；证明联合优化成员资格和群组峰值时刻为强NP-hard问题；开发固定峰值细化程序：交替识别群组峰值时刻和在最小群组大小约束下精确重新分配客户，每次多项式时间重分配最大化当前分区下的紧下界，确保加权巧合严格提升；以个体峰值时间分布聚类初始化。

Result: 在四个公开智能电表面板（27,654客户）上，采用时间序列留出和200次配对复制完全重拟合评估。细化过程在拟合阶段持续提升加权巧合，降低对初始化敏感性。在留出测试日，该方法优于随机分组和五个独立基准，比最强基准平均提升1.08-3.48个百分点。要求近似相等群组大小仅轻微降低测试加权巧合。群组随时间保持可识别的峰值时刻模式。

Conclusion: 提出的峰值感知客户聚合方法有效优化加权巧合因子，具有实际应用潜力，可用于峰值感知的客户聚合以支持配电规划。

Abstract: Electricity distribution planning depends on peak-demand coincidence within customer groups, yet similarity-based clustering of smart-meter data does not optimize it directly. We formulate customer grouping to maximize the average of group coincidence factors, weighted by each group's daily share of summed individual peak demand. We prove that jointly optimizing memberships and group peak times is strongly NP-hard even for two groups. We therefore develop a fixed-peak refinement procedure applicable to any feasible partition. It alternates between identifying group peak times and exactly reassigning customers under minimum group-size constraints. Each polynomial-time reassignment maximizes a lower bound tight at the current partition, so accepted updates strictly improve weighted coincidence. We initialize refinement by clustering individual peak-time distributions and evaluate the method on four public smart-meter panels (27,654 customers) using chronological holdouts and 200 paired replicates with complete refitting. Refinement consistently improves weighted coincidence during fitting and reduces sensitivity to initialization. On held-out days, the method outperforms random grouping and five standalone benchmarks, with mean paired gains of 1.08-3.48 percentage points over the strongest benchmark. Requiring nearly equal group sizes lowers test weighted coincidence only modestly. The groups retain recognizable peak-time patterns over time, suggesting applications in peak-aware customer aggregation.

</details>


### [350] [Adaptive Pullback: Reweighting Supervision at Penalty Jumps in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28194)
*Guangyu Liu,Siyuan Gan,Shilong Zhou,Linjian Meng,Boyan Wang,Jing Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出自适应回拉在线策略蒸馏(AP-OPD)，通过检测响应内部的惩罚跳跃并自适应缩放监督系数，无需额外超参数即可改进蒸馏效果，在数学推理和代码生成任务上均取得性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线策略蒸馏(OPD)使用学生-教师对数比作为监督信号，但当前惩罚值无法区分持续高惩罚与突发惩罚增加。研究通过比较响应内连续token块发现“惩罚跳跃”现象：平均惩罚急剧上升且在保留token上持续，同时教师置信度下降，形成惩罚-置信度张力。进入高惩罚块的边界不表现出类似置信度变化，表明局部动态变化提供了超越当前惩罚值的额外信息。

Method: 提出AP-OPD：在检测到惩罚跳跃时，以前一token块作为局部参考，统一缩放后续块所有监督系数，使其加权平均惩罚匹配前块原始均值。该参考自动决定缩放因子，无需额外强度常数。

Result: AP-OPD在数学推理和代码生成两项任务上均显著提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 利用响应内部局部惩罚动态变化(惩罚跳跃)作为自适应调节蒸馏监督强度的信号，AP-OPD有效缓解惩罚-置信度张力，在无需引入额外超参数的情况下提升在线策略蒸馏效果。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) supervises student-generated responses using sampled-token student-teacher log-ratios. A positive log-ratio penalizes a token that the student assigns higher probability to than the teacher, but its current value does not distinguish sustained high penalties from abrupt increases along a response. We examine these local changes by comparing consecutive blocks of tokens within the same response. Our diagnostic identifies penalty jumps, abrupt increases in average penalty that persist on held-out tokens. Across these jumps, penalties rise while teacher confidence decreases. We call this pattern penalty-confidence tension. Boundaries into high-penalty blocks do not exhibit comparable confidence changes, showing that local changes provide information beyond the current penalty value. Motivated by this observation, we propose Adaptive Pullback OPD (AP-OPD). At each detected jump, AP-OPD uses the preceding block as a local reference and uniformly scales all supervision coefficients in the following block so that its weighted average penalty matches the preceding block's original mean. This reference determines the scaling factor without an additional strength constant. AP-OPD improves performance on both mathematical reasoning and code generation.

</details>


### [351] [Reframing Object-Centric Reinforcement Learning: From Absolute to Relative Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28197)
*Cedric Derstroff,Jannis Blüml,Janosch Moos,Michael Girstl,Zihan Ye,Debora Clever,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于锚点的对象相对坐标（ORCA）和对象相对归纳偏置Transformer（ORBiT），将对象表示从绝对全局坐标转换为相对锚点坐标，显著提升了强化学习智能体在平移、旋转和干扰物变化环境下的泛化能力，在Atari游戏和Foosball任务上超越像素级PPO基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对象中心强化学习智能体通常接收固定全局坐标系下的对象位置，但决策往往依赖对象间的相对位置。绝对坐标视角无法捕捉局部上下文，导致智能体需在每个位置重新学习相同行为，且场景平移时性能崩溃。

Method: 提出ORCA框架，以决策相关的锚点为原点建立相对坐标系；设计ORBiT架构，采用Transformer处理锚点相对输入，融入对象相对归纳偏置。实验涵盖Atari 13个游戏、Foosball任务及受控Tag环境，对比MLP、Transformer及像素级PPO基线。

Result: 相对表示提升MLP和Transformer智能体性能；ORBiT在Atari 13游戏上聚合HNS IQM超1.0，击败像素级PPO；在Foosball任务从零学习并碾压所有基线；Tag环境实验证实ORCA可扩展至旋转，ORBiT能泛化至未见数量的干扰物，且增益依赖于决策相关的锚点选择。

Conclusion: 基于锚点的相对坐标表示（ORCA）配合Transformer架构（ORBiT）能有效解决对象中心RL的平移不变性缺失问题，显著增强智能体对空间变换和干扰物的泛化能力，为构建更鲁棒的对象中心RL智能体提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Object-centric reinforcement learning agents typically receive each object's position in a fixed global frame, even though their decisions usually depend on where objects are relative to one another. These absolute views often fail to capture the local context that is most relevant to the agent's behavior. As a result, an agent must relearn the same response at every location where a configuration can occur, and its performance collapses when the scene is merely shifted. In this work, we propose a shift toward Object-Relative Coordinates fixed to Anchor (ORCA), where entities are described in a relative frame of reference. To operationalize this idea, we introduce Object-Relative inductive-Bias Transformers (ORBiT), a transformer-based architecture for processing anchor-relative inputs in reinforcement learning. Our results suggest these relative representations improve performance for both MLP and transformer agents. Furthermore, we demonstrate that ORBiT outperforms the pixel-based PPO baseline, achieving an aggregate HNS IQM greater than 1.0 over 13 Atari games. ORBiT learns the game of Foosball from scratch, dominating all baselines. In our controlled Tag environment, we further show that ORCA extends to rotations, that ORBiT generalizes to unseen numbers of distractors, and that these gains require a decision-relevant anchor.

</details>


### [352] [Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of Time Series Foundation Models for Electrical Load Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28200)
*Aliullah,Julian Oelhaf,Edouard Fouché,Jessica Deuschel,Christian Bergler,Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了预训练推理、全量微调和低秩适应在四个电力负荷聚合层级上的预测精度与适配效率。以MOIRAI 1.0 Large为主干，Chronos-2验证跨模型家族泛化性。实验覆盖分布内和分布外设置。结果显示：预训练基础模型优于季节性朴素法和XGBoost基线；MOIRAI上秩16 LoRA平均降低NMAE 7.5%，仅更新约1%参数（314.6万），保留了全量微调大部分收益；Chronos-2预训练NMAE更低但适配收益较小；分布外场景下适配收益随目标变化，局域序列和部分建筑增益最明显。总体而言，当适配能带来有意义提升时，LoRA提供了良好的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的电力负荷预测支持从国家、地区、城市到建筑各层级的电力系统运行，但针对每个目标重复开发专用模型成本高昂。时间序列基础模型提供可复用的预训练骨干，但多域预训练可能无法完全捕捉负荷特有特征。因此需要系统评估预训练推理、全量微调和参数高效适配在不同聚合层级和分布偏移下的表现，寻找精度与效率的最佳平衡。

Method: 采用MOIRAI 1.0 Large作为主干模型，Chronos-2作为跨家族验证模型。在四个电力负荷聚合层级上分别进行预训练推理、全量微调和秩16 LoRA适配实验。评估指标为归一化平均绝对误差。实验涵盖分布内和分布外两种设置，并与季节性朴素法和XGBoost基线对比。统计适配参数量、NMAE降低幅度及不同目标下的收益差异。

Result: 1. 预训练基础模型在所有聚合层级均优于季节性朴素法和XGBoost基线。2. MOIRAI Large上，秩16 LoRA平均降低NMAE 7.5%，仅更新314.6万参数（约1%），保留了全量微调大部分改进。3. Chronos-2预训练NMAE更低但适配收益较小。4. 分布外结果显示适配收益随目标变化，局域序列和部分建筑增益最明显。5. LoRA在适配带来有意义增益时提供有利的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 低秩适配是适配时间序列基础模型以进行电力负荷预测的高效选择：在MOIRAI上以约1%参数更新获得接近全量微调的精度提升；跨模型家族泛化性有待进一步探索；分布外场景下收益因目标而异，建议根据具体目标决定是否适配。未来工作可研究自动化适配策略选择和更大规模预训练对负荷特征的捕捉。

Abstract: Accurate electrical load forecasting supports power-system operation across countries, regions, cities, and buildings, but repeated development of target-specific models is costly. Time series foundation models (TSFMs) provide reusable pretrained backbones, although multi-domain pretraining may not fully capture load-specific characteristics. This paper compares pretrained inference, full fine-tuning, and low-rank adaptation (LoRA) in terms of forecasting accuracy and adaptation efficiency across four electrical load aggregation levels. MOIRAI 1.0 Large serves as the primary backbone, while Chronos-2 assesses whether the findings transfer across model families. The evaluation covers both in-distribution (ID) and out-of-distribution (OOD) settings and examines whether compact adaptation provides consistent gains over strong pretrained checkpoints. Pretrained foundation models outperform the implemented seasonal naive and XGBoost baselines across the evaluated aggregation levels. On MOIRAI Large, rank-16 LoRA reduces normalized mean absolute error (NMAE) by 7.5% on average while updating 3.146 million parameters, about 1% of the model, and retains most of the improvement from full fine-tuning. Chronos-2 achieves lower pretrained NMAE than MOIRAI but benefits less from adaptation. OOD results show that adaptation benefits vary by target, with the clearest gains for the local-area series and several buildings. Overall, LoRA provides a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off when adaptation yields a meaningful gain over a strong pretrained checkpoint.

</details>


### [353] [JAXbounce: A Continuous-Control Pong-Like Benchmark for Active Inference Beyond Low Dimensional Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28203)
*Chaitanya Kaul,Markus Klar,Sebastian Stein,Fraser C. Paterson,John H. Williamson,Roderick Murray-Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JAXbounce 是一个连续控制的类乒乓球基准，用于评估主动推理在连续、随机环境中的表现，提供可微分、可并行的 JAX 实现，并对比了多种智能体在不同噪声条件下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动推理基准多为离散、低维环境，缺乏连续状态、随机动力学和灵活观测模型的测试平台，需要一个可复现的连续控制基准来评估主动推理在更真实条件下的性能。

Method: 构建 JAXbounce 环境，包含连续状态、离散时间随机动力学、可组合的观测模型（状态向量或像素网格）及丰富噪声族；实现三种智能体：基线确定性解、基于状态的主动推理智能体（无迹卡尔曼滤波感知 + 期望自由能规划）、基于像素的智能体（预训练神经网络编码器）；在三个难度场景和八种噪声条件下运行超 10,000 次模拟对比。

Result: 实验表征了连续状态智能体性能随规划、规划时域、观测模态和噪声过程认知的变化，发现即使在不确定性增加时仍表现出偏好导向行为；像素智能体在噪声下性能下降更明显，状态智能体随规划时域提升显著。

Conclusion: JAXbounce 为连续控制主动推理研究提供了一个可复现、可微分、可并行的基准，支持多种观测模态和噪声条件，促进了对主动推理在连续、随机环境中扩展性和鲁棒性的系统评估。

Abstract: We introduce JAXbounce, a continuous control pong-like benchmark designed to evaluate active inference beyond discrete, low dimensional environments. JAXbounce combines continuous states and stochastic dynamics in discrete time with flexible and composable observation models that allow representing the environment as a state vector or a pixel grid, corruptible through the rich family of noise models we provide. It is fully differentiable and parallelisable in JAX. We run over 10,000 agent simulations on our environment comparing a baseline deterministic solution, to a state-based active inference agent with Unscented Kalman Filter perception and expected free energy planning, and a pixel based agent whose measurement model is a pretrained neural network encoder. All agents are evaluated across three scenarios of increasing difficulty with eight different noise conditions. Our experiments characterise how performance of continuous state agents scales with planning, planning horizons, observation modalities and knowledge of the noise process, exhibiting preference-oriented behaviour even in the presence of increasing uncertainty. We release JAXbounce as a reproducible benchmark for continuous control active inference research.

</details>


### [354] [TPO: Tri-level Distributionally Robust Learning for OOD Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28229)
*Chengtao Jian,Kai Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TPO是一个三层分布鲁棒框架，用于OOD偏好对齐，通过层次化矩形模糊集联合建模群组、提示词和偏好三个层面的分布不确定性，利用Wasserstein距离推导出可处理的目标函数，包含闭式偏好层面边界惩罚、提示词层面对抗嵌入扰动和最坏情况群组重加权，并提供有限样本收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO隐含假设训练偏好分布与部署分布匹配，但实际中存在三层分布偏移：群组级、提示词级和偏好级。现有鲁棒DPO方法要么建模联合分布不确定性，要么仅关注单一来源偏移，未充分探索层次化OOD偏好偏移结构，导致安全部署面临挑战。

Method: 提出TPO框架，构建层次化矩形模糊集，以Wasserstein距离实例化，推导出包含三个独立半径控制的组件：偏好层面闭式边界惩罚、提示词层面对抗嵌入扰动、群组层面最坏情况重加权，形成统一可解释的优化目标。

Result: 建立了有限样本收敛理论保证，实验验证TPO在OOD偏好对齐任务上优于现有鲁棒DPO基线方法。

Conclusion: TPO通过联合建模三层分布不确定性，有效解决了OOD偏好对齐中的层次化分布偏移问题，兼具理论收敛保证与实证有效性，为安全LLM部署提供了更鲁棒的偏好优化范式。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) minimizes empirical risk over observed preference comparisons, implicitly assuming that the training preference distribution matches the deployment preference distribution. In practice, this i.i.d. assumption often fails, as preference distribution shift exhibits a natural hierarchical structure across three levels: group-level shift, prompt-level shift, and preference-level shift. Such multi-level distributional mismatch poses a critical challenge for safe LLM deployment, yet existing robust DPO methods typically either model uncertainty over the joint preference distribution or focus on individual sources of shift, leaving the hierarchical structure of OOD preference shifts underexplored. We propose TPO, a tri-level distributionally robust framework for OOD preference alignment, which jointly models all three levels of distributional uncertainty through a hierarchically structured rectangular ambiguity set. By instantiating it with Wasserstein distance, we derive a tractable and interpretable objective that combines a closed-form preference-level margin penalty, prompt-level adversarial embedding perturbation, and worst-case group reweighting, each governed by an independent radius. We establish finite-sample convergence guarantees and empirically validate the effectiveness of TPO over existing robust DPO methods.

</details>


### [355] [Learning Normal Coordinates on Riemannian Manifolds with Flow Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28302)
*Edmond Cunningham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为“flow chart”的条件可逆神经网络，用于近似黎曼流形上的正规坐标，从而将指数映射和对数映射的计算转化为学习到的坐标空间中的快速欧氏运算，显著加速几何算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼流形是建模非欧几里得结构问题的有力工具，几何深度学习致力于从高维数据中学习黎曼度量。然而，许多几何算法需要反复计算指数映射和对数映射，其高计算成本限制了学习到的黎曼流形在实际中的应用。

Method: 训练一个条件可逆神经网络（flow chart）来近似黎曼流形上各点周围的正规坐标。提出了一种高效的损失函数，该函数非负，且在合适的定义域假设下，当且仅当图表为正规时损失为零。通过学习到的坐标空间中的欧氏运算近似指数映射和对数映射。

Result: 在十六种合成和数据驱动的几何上，flow charts 在对数映射估计上与专用神经测地线插值器具有竞争力，同时也能估计指数映射。预训练后，flow charts 在相当的对数映射估计误差下，相较于数值基线提供了 60-70 倍的中位数加速，且其学习到的坐标提高了数值测地线求解器的精度。

Conclusion: Flow charts 通过学习正规坐标，以极低的计算成本高效近似黎曼流形上的指数映射和对数映射，解决了几何深度学习中计算瓶颈问题，显著提升了学习黎曼流形在实际应用中的实用性。

Abstract: Riemannian manifolds are powerful models for problems with non-Euclidean structure. Recent work in geometric machine learning has focused on training deep neural networks to learn Riemannian metrics from high-dimensional data. However, many geometric algorithms require repeated evaluations of the exponential and logarithmic maps, whose high computational cost restricts the utility of learned Riemannian manifolds in practice. We propose to address this cost by training a conditional invertible neural network, called a flow chart, to approximate normal coordinates around points on a given Riemannian manifold. With this approach, the exponential and logarithmic maps can be approximated by fast Euclidean operations in the model's learned coordinate space. Our main contribution is an efficient loss function for training flow charts that is nonnegative and, under suitable domain assumptions, is zero precisely when the chart is normal. Across sixteen synthetic and data-derived geometries, flow charts are competitive with a specialized neural geodesic interpolator for logarithmic map estimation, while also being able to estimate the exponential map. When pretrained, flow charts provide a median speedup of 60-70x over numerical baselines at comparable logarithmic map estimation error, and their learned coordinates improve the accuracy of a numerical geodesic solver.

</details>


### [356] [Recommending Bacterial Cultivation Conditions from 16S rRNA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28310)
*Juntang Wang,Hao Wu,Yihan Wang,Runkun Guo,Shixin Xu,Dongmian Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对从16S rRNA序列预测细菌培养条件的问题，构建了包含65,023菌株的DSNI基准，并提出了变异性门控编码器（Variability-Gated Encoder），利用16S rRNA的马赛克结构学习位置依赖的重要性权重，仅增加1,280参数即显著提升联合预测准确率，Top-25推荐可覆盖90.2%真实方案，将搜索空间压缩约60倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有细菌培养条件推荐缺乏标准化基准，且未充分利用16S rRNA序列的结构特性（超变区与保守区的功能差异），导致预测性能受限，尤其在低数据量场景下亟需显式先验偏置。

Method: 1. 构建DSNI基准：收集65,023菌株的16S rRNA序列与四维培养条件（培养基、温度、pH、需氧），采用系统发育感知划分避免数据泄露。2. 提出变异性门控编码器：基于区域类型、位置、局部复杂度等生物特征，为序列每个位置学习可变重要性权重，显式建模超变区与保守区的差异贡献。门控模块仅增1,280参数（<1%开销），可即插即用于Transformer或RNABERT等骨干网络。

Result: 在DSNI基准上，门控模块使平凡Transformer联合准确率提升4.7%，配合RNABERT达到44.2%精确匹配联合准确率；Top-25推荐召回率90.2%，将穷举搜索空间压缩约60倍。消融实验证实门控在低数据量下优势显著。

Conclusion: 变异性门控编码器通过注入生物先验知识，以极低参数开销有效利用16S rRNA结构特性，显著提升细菌培养条件多任务预测性能，为序列驱动的微生物表型推断提供了可解释、高效的新范式。

Abstract: Recommending bacterial cultivation conditions from sequence data is a challenging structured problem characterized by missing standardized benchmarks and limited use of sequence structure. To address this, we introduce DSNI, a benchmark of 65,023 bacterial strains with phylogeny-aware splits, pairing 16S rRNA sequences with four cultivation conditions (media, temperature, pH, oxygen). Exploiting the mosaic structure of 16S rRNA, where hypervariable regions support discrimination and conserved regions support primer design, we propose a Variability-Gated Encoder that learns position-dependent importance weights conditioned on biological features (region type, position, and local complexity). The gating module adds minimal overhead (1,280 parameters, <1%) while providing an explicit inductive bias valuable in low-data regimes. Experiments demonstrate that the learnable gate improves flat Transformers by 4.7% in joint accuracy and complements RNABERT, yielding 44.2% exact-match joint accuracy. The model also recovers 90.2% of ground-truth cultivation protocols within its top-25 recommendations, narrowing the exhaustive search space by approximately 60 times.

</details>


### [357] [Manifold-Informed Neural Networks for Low-Label ECG Electrolyte Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28322)
*Juntang Wang,Hao Wu,Yihan Wang,Dongmian Zou,Shixin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了流形信息神经网络（MINN），一种与物理信息神经网络（PINN）平行的范式，将物理算子残差替换为学习潜在空间中k近邻图上的离散泊松方程残差，并引入可学习的、任务感知的源项，打破了经典拉普拉斯正则化的齐次平滑先验。在MIMIC-IV-ECG数据集上进行电解质（钾、钙、镁）三分类实验，MINN在K⁺上达到0.61的Macro-F1（最佳基线0.55），在1%标注数据下相对提升31%，消融实验证实增益源于非齐次泊松公式而非单纯的图平滑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN依赖已知的微分算子，但许多高风险推断问题（如临床信号、行为测量、材料性质）缺乏此类算子。经典流形正则化通过最小化狄利克雷能量施加齐次平滑先验，限制了建模灵活性。需要一种能结合任务感知源项、利用辅助元数据、且不依赖物理算子的流形正则化框架。

Method: MINN在学习的潜在空间构建k近邻图，定义离散泊松方程 L_G H = s_φ(x, m)，其中源项 s_φ 直接接收输入特征和辅助元数据，通过门控逐特征线性调制（FiLM）融合元数据到预测分支。损失函数为算子残差平方与标签监督之和，结构上对标PINN（算子残差加边界数据）。当 s=0 时退化为齐次拉普拉斯方程，但惩罚形式为平方残差而非狄利克雷能量。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV-ECG 12导联心电图电解质三分类任务中，对比12种基线（监督学习、表格方法、心电基础模型、流形/图方法），MINN在K⁺上取得最高Macro-F1 0.61（基线最佳0.55），Ca²⁺和Mg²⁺持续提升，在1%标注数据下相对改进31%。六个针对性消融实验表明，非齐次泊松公式（而非单纯图平滑）是性能提升的关键。

Conclusion: MINN通过将PINN的物理算子残差推广为带有可学习任务感知源项的流形算子残差，成功将流形正则化从齐次平滑先验扩展为非齐次、元数据感知的泊松方程框架。该方法在临床心电图电解质预测任务上显著优于现有基线，尤其在低标注数据场景下表现突出，验证了非齐次泊松公式的有效性。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) supervise on the residual of a known differential operator. Many high-stakes inference problems admit no such operator: clinical signals, behavioral measurements, materials properties. We introduce the Manifold-Informed Neural Network (MINN), a PINN-parallel paradigm that replaces the physics operator residual with a manifold operator residual: a discrete Poisson equation $\mathbf{L}_G \mathbf{H} = s_φ(\mathbf{x}, \mathbf{m})$ on a $k$-nearest-neighbor graph in the learned latent space, with a learned, task-aware source. The construction is a structural parallel to PINNs (squared operator residual plus label data, in place of operator residual plus boundary data); the $s{=}0$ limit recovers the homogeneous Laplace equation $\mathbf{L}_G\mathbf{H}=\mathbf{0}$ (classical manifold regularization solves the same equation but minimizes the Dirichlet energy $\operatorname{tr}(\mathbf{H}^\top\mathbf{L}_G\mathbf{H})$, a different penalty form). The source $s_φ$ takes input features and auxiliary metadata directly, breaking the homogeneous smoothness prior that limits classical Laplacian penalties; gated Feature-wise Linear Modulation separately fuses metadata into the prediction branch. We evaluate MINN on three-class classification of potassium, calcium, and magnesium from 12-lead ECGs in MIMIC-IV-ECG, against twelve baselines spanning supervised and tabular methods, ECG foundation models, and manifold/graph approaches. MINN attains the highest K$^+$ Macro-F1 of 0.61 (best baseline 0.55), with consistent gains on Ca$^{2+}$ and Mg$^{2+}$ and a 31% relative improvement at 1% labeled data. Six targeted ablations show that the inhomogeneous Poisson formulation, not graph smoothing alone, drives the gain.

</details>


### [358] [Observation Assimilation Transformer: Probabilistic Weather Analysis from Sparse Station Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28327)
*Christian Donner,Michele Cattaneo,Maxim Samarin,Francesco Zanetta,Icíar Lloréns Jover,Garbiela Aznar Siguan,Michele Volpi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出观测同化Transformer (OAT)，一种几何感知的神经过程，通过同化稀疏观测将先验数值天气预报(NWP)场修正为高分辨率后验场，实现了任意连续位置的降尺度预测并在区域和大陆尺度数据集上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NWP模型分辨率有限，难以直接利用稀疏地面观测站数据进行高分辨率修正；传统同化方法依赖网格投影且难以处理任意位置预测和部分缺失观测，需要一种可扩展、几何感知的方法实现观测到任意查询位置的概率性降尺度。

Method: 编码器-解码器Transformer架构，采用稀疏注意力机制处理数千站点观测，仅依赖位置间相对坐标而非绝对坐标；输入为观测站位置及其NWP先验场，输出任意查询位置的参数化分布预测；开发站点随机化训练算法并采用CRPS损失函数优化概率预测校准。

Result: 在区域尺度(数百站点)和大陆尺度(数千站点)两个数据集上均优于多个基线模型，预测具有良好校准性；案例研究中将ERA5(~31km)降尺度至COSMO(1km)分辨率，在测试站点上温度和降水的MAE和CRPS均低于COSMO模型。

Conclusion: OAT提供了一种可扩展、几何感知的概率降尺度框架，能有效同化稀疏观测并生成任意分辨率的校准后预测，为高分辨率天气预报修正提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present the Observation Assimilation Transformer (OAT), a geometry-aware neural process that corrects and refines a prior numerical weather prediction (NWP) field into a high-resolution posterior field by assimilating sparse observations. OAT is an encoder-decoder transformer that considers a set of observed locations and their NWP prior to condition predictions at a set of arbitrary unobserved query locations. Its sparse attention mechanism makes it scalable to thousands of station observations and depends on relative positions between locations, never on the absolute coordinates. The model allows for partially missing observations, predictions at arbitrary continuous locations without grid projection, and outputs are parametric distributions. A station-randomized training algorithm is developed with continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) loss. We test OAT on two datasets - one at regional scale with hundreds of stations, and one at continental scale with thousands of stations. On these datasets OAT outperforms several baselines and shows well calibrated predictions. In an illustrative case study, OAT's downscaling capabilities are demonstrated by downscaling ERA5 (~31km) to COSMO (1km), where OAT achieves lower mean absolute error (MAE) and CRPS for temperature and precipitation on test stations compared to COSMO.

</details>


### [359] [Identifying Dependent Components from Multi-domain Linear Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28330)
*Danru Xu,Lauri Parkkonen,Sara Magliacane,Aapo Hyvärinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多域线性混合模型，允许潜在分量之间存在依赖关系，通过域特定的缩放变化实现从二阶统计量中识别潜在变量和混合函数，并提出了MuDo-CoM算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的独立成分分析（ICA）及相关方法通常假设潜在分量相互独立或至少不相关。虽然近期工作允许依赖分量，但往往需要特定假设（如参数化依赖形式、多视图设置、干预数据）或无法恢复单个分量。本文旨在解决这一限制，利用多域数据中域特定的缩放变化来识别依赖潜在分量。

Method: 提出多域协方差匹配算法，将联合对角化方法推广到多域设置。假设各域共享相同的潜在分量分布，但通过域特定的对角缩放矩阵进行变换。利用各域的协方差矩阵差异，从二阶统计量识别混合矩阵和潜在分量。

Result: 理论证明了在充分的域变异性条件下，潜在变量和混合函数可从二阶统计量唯一识别。在仿真数据和真实fMRI数据集上验证了MuDo-CoM算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 多域设置下的域特定缩放提供了识别依赖潜在分量的充分信息，无需独立性假设或高阶统计量。MuDo-CoM算法为多源数据融合和因果表示学习提供了实用工具。

Abstract: We study a linear mixing model with dependent latent components, assuming multiple data domains. Most existing models assume that the components are independent or at least uncorrelated, in line with independent component analysis (ICA). Some recent work allows for dependent components, but then makes specific assumptions such as parametric forms of dependencies, multi-view settings, or interventions, or does not recover the individual components. In contrast, we consider a multi-domain setting in which domains differ through domain-specific scalings of the components, while the distribution of the underlying latent components is the same across domains. This approach can model data collected, for example, from different sensors measuring the same process, different laboratories conducting the same experiment, different experimental conditions, or different subjects that might differ in biological or physiological factors. We show that, under sufficient domain variability, latent variables and mixing functions can be identified from second-order statistics alone. We propose the Multi-Domain Covariance Matching (MuDo-CoM) algorithm that generalizes previous methods of joint diagonalization. MuDo-CoM is validated on simulated data and a real-world fMRI dataset.

</details>


### [360] [Characterizing Cross-Relay Generalization Failure in Machine Learning-Based Power System Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28348)
*Nithin Pradeep Nadayil Kizhakkethil,Julian Oelhaf,Christian Bergler,Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究电力系统中基于机器学习的故障检测器在跨继电器泛化时的表现，发现聚合指标（如F1分数）掩盖了按位置结构化的误报率显著变化，强调需分别评估假阳性与假阴性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的故障检测器常在训练用的继电器位置评估，但局部电压电流测量随电网拓扑变化，需研究跨继电器泛化能力，即仅用局部测量实现全网故障的二分类检测，而非线路选择性跳闸。

Method: 在仿真110 kV输电网络上，用50 ms窗口数据训练多层感知机，覆盖8个继电器、5个随机种子的64种源-目标组合；采用共享仿真级划分与仅源域阈值选择，实现无目标域标定的受控保留评估。

Result: 平均F1分数几乎不变（0.730 vs 0.734），但平均假阳性率（FPR）从0.088升至0.341，平均假阴性率从0.399降至0.318；变化呈强方向性：Bus 2训练的模型在Bus 1/3处FPR达0.670，同母线组内仅0.096。线性探测显示表征保留粗粒度母线组信息，类条件潜在分布分析未发现与迁移误差的单调关联。

Conclusion: 聚合指标会掩盖按位置结构化的误报行为变化，评估跨继电器泛化时必须分别考察假阳性与假阴性，而非仅依赖F1等综合指标。

Abstract: Machine learning-based fault detectors are often evaluated at the relay locations used for training, although local voltage and current measurements vary across power networks. This work investigates cross-relay generalization for binary detection of faults anywhere in the network using local measurements, rather than line-selective tripping. Using 50 ms windows from a simulated 110 kV transmission network, we evaluate a multilayer perceptron across all 64 source target combinations of eight relays and five random seeds. A shared simulation-level split and source-only threshold selection provide a controlled held-out evaluation without target-specific calibration. Despite nearly unchanged mean F1-scores (0.730 within-relay versus 0.734 cross-relay), the mean false-positive rate (FPR) increases from 0.088 to 0.341, while the mean false-negative rate decreases from 0.399 to 0.318. The change is strongly directional: models trained at Bus 2 relays yield a mean FPR of 0.670 at Bus 1 and Bus 3 relays, compared with 0.096 for cross-relay transfers within the same bus group. Linear probing indicates that learned representations retain coarse bus-group information, while exploratory class-conditional latent distribution analysis shows no consistent monotonic association with transfer errors. These findings demonstrate that aggregate metrics can conceal location-structured changes in false-alarm behavior, highlighting the importance of evaluating error types separately when assessing cross-relay generalization.

</details>


### [361] [Beyond the Point Mass: Acceptance-Derived Weights and Distributional Losses for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28355)
*Tianyu Wu,Zhuohan Wang,Jonathan Geuter,Yu Yao,Weifan Jiang,Chengxi Zhang,Ju Li,Minlan Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 D-PAL（动态逐位置接受损失），基于草稿与目标分布重叠度（1 - 全变差）推导出精确的期望接受长度目标，替代 D-PACE 的点质量假设。通过分离位置权重与逐位置损失，发现前向 KL 与 Rényi-1/2 散度优于对数接受损失，且 Rényi-1/2 可证明界定真实接受率。在 Qwen3-4B 实验中，D-PAL 将 T=0 时平均接受长度从 4.50 提升至 4.57，T=1 时从 4.20 提升至 4.28，吞吐率同步提高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D-PACE 以点质量近似目标分布，仅用草稿对目标 token 的概率加权交叉熵，忽略了拒绝采样中接受概率取决于草稿与目标分布的重叠度（1 - TV）这一事实，导致训练目标与真实接受长度不匹配。

Method: 1. 从精确的期望接受长度推导 D-PAL，引入基于重叠度的位置权重与对数接受（LA）逐位置损失。2. 证明权重可分离，允许用任意逐位置损失替代 LA。3. 实验验证前向 KL 与 Rényi-1/2 散度优于 LA，且 Rényi-1/2 可上下界真实接受率。4. 在六个基准上对比 D-PACE 与 D-PAL（含不同损失变体）。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B 目标模型上：T=0 时平均接受长度 4.50→4.57，T=1 时 4.20→4.28；两种温度下吞吐率均稳步提升。Rényi-1/2 损失在 D-PACE 与 D-PAL 权重下均带来增益，且理论上可界定接受率。

Conclusion: D-PAL 通过建模分布重叠而非点质量，得到更准确的训练目标；结合 Rényi-1/2 损失可进一步提升接受长度与吞吐，且具备理论保证。该方法为投机解码草稿模型训练提供了更 principled 的损失设计范式。

Abstract: In speculative decoding, a lightweight draft model proposes tokens that the target model verifies in parallel, speeding up generation without changing the target distribution. D-PACE (Wu et al., 2026) trains such drafters with a loss that weights each position by its contribution to the expected accepted length. Both its weights and its cross-entropy (CE) loss use only the draft's probability of the recorded target token, treating the target as a point mass. Under rejection sampling, however, a sampled draft token is accepted with probability equal to the overlap of the draft and target distributions, one minus their total variation (TV). We derive D-PAL (Dynamic Position-wise Acceptance Loss) from the exact expected accepted length, using this overlap as the acceptance signal. This yields overlap-based position weights with log acceptance (LA) as the per-position loss, and the objective reduces to D-PACE for a point-mass target. Since the weights are detached, any per-position loss can replace LA in practice. We show that forward KL divergence and Rényi divergence of order 1/2 match or exceed LA, and that Rényi-1/2 provably bounds the true acceptance rate from above and below. Replacing CE with Rényi-1/2 improves acceptance length under both D-PACE's weights and ours. On six benchmarks with a Qwen3-4B target, D-PAL raises mean acceptance length over D-PACE from 4.50 to 4.57 at T=0 and from 4.20 to 4.28 at T=1, and consistently achieves higher throughput at both temperatures.

</details>


### [362] [Relative Representations Enable Portable White-Box Monitoring Across Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28366)
*Tim Bakker,Christos Louizos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种全局锚定方法，使独立训练的自回归语言模型之间的内部表示兼容，从而实现可移植的白盒监控：在一个模型上开发的探针可直接应用于另一模型，无需目标标注数据，平均保留85%以上的AUROC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立训练的自回归语言模型虽获相似能力，但其内部表示是否以兼容方式组织行为相关信息尚不清楚。若能实现跨模型探针迁移，将解决模型更新或更换时需重新标注数据的问题，实现可移植的白盒监控。

Method: 引入全局锚定构造：通过共享锚点集合，将每个样本表示为与锚点的角距离，汇总所有Transformer层的信息，消除模型特定的层选择，使同一冻结探针可直接应用于另一模型。使用行为探针在兼容表示上操作。

Result: 在问答、安全监控、数学推理等任务上评估跨模型探针迁移。平均而言，迁移的全局锚定探针保留至少85%的同模型探针AUROC优于随机基线。锚定探针还支持相关数据集迁移，在模型规模和提示词变化下保持鲁棒，且在模型更新后性能保持优于标准探针。但迁移具有任务和目标依赖性，即使目标模型中被监控属性强可解码，迁移仍可能失败。

Conclusion: 全局锚定揭示了独立训练模型间存在实质性的任务相关兼容性，为可移植的白盒监控提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Independently trained autoregressive language models often acquire similar capabilities, but it remains unclear whether their internal representations organize behaviorally relevant information in compatible ways. We show that models do in fact organize information similarly by using specific behavioral probes that operate on compatible representations. This unlocks portable white-box monitoring: a probe developed and validated on one model can be reused when the model changes, without requiring labeled target data. To overcome incompatible native activation coordinates, we express each example through its angular distances to a shared set of anchors. We introduce a global anchored construction that summarizes information from all transformer layers and removes model-specific layer selection, so the same frozen probe can be straightforwardly applied to another model. We evaluate cross-model probe transfer across monitoring tasks on trivia datasets, safety monitoring, and mathematical reasoning. On average, transferred global anchored probes preserve at least 85% of same-model probe AUROC over chance. Anchored probes also support transfer to related datasets, remain robust across model scale and prompt changes, and retain performance better than standard probes after model updates. Transfer is nevertheless task- and target-dependent and can fail even when the monitored property remains strongly decodable in the target model. Overall, global anchoring reveals substantial task-relevant compatibility across independently trained models and provides a practical basis for portable white-box monitoring.

</details>


### [363] [Don't Skip the Baseline: A Controlled Comparison of Dynamic Expert Skipping in MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28369)
*Jakob Steimle,Thomas Elsken,Daniel Ehrhardt,Lukas Rinder,Daniel Cremers,Xi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了动态专家跳过方法在MoE模型中的效果，发现简单的静态减少专家数k的基线难以被持续超越，动态方法往往表现不如基线，且校准阶段的有效k与测试时可能偏离。建议采用免校准的每模态静态k减少策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态专家跳过方法通过重要性评分、专家相似度或学习式分配器来减少激活专家数，但不同校准方案、有效专家数减少幅度和评估套件使得横向比较困难，缺乏统一基准。

Method: 将四种代表性先前方法归类为统一分类体系，并与静态减少k的简单基线在相同专家计算预算下进行公平对比，评估对象包括两个文本MoE（Gemma4-26B-A4B、Qwen3-30B-A3B）和一个视觉语言模型（Qwen3-VL）。

Result: 静态基线在模型、模态、基准和校准设置上难以被持续超越；动态方法常低于基线，增益若有也极小；校准时的有效k与测试时可能偏离，导致推理算力估计不准。

Conclusion: 推荐采用免校准的每模态静态k减少策略，并在保留模型和模态上验证有效；主张以匹配实测算力的静态控制作为动态跳过方法必须击败的基线。

Abstract: A growing body of work proposes dynamic expert-skipping methods for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models that adjust the number of experts activated per token during inference, aiming to preserve model quality while further reducing the number of active parameters. These methods have added calibrated importance scores, inter-expert similarity metrics, or learned per-token allocators in order to identify experts to skip. However, different calibration regimes, different reductions in the effective number of experts, and evaluation suites make comparisons difficult. We classify four representative prior methods into a unified taxonomy and benchmark them against a natural, simple baseline: statically reducing the number of experts $k$ (i.e., same reduced $k$ across all layers and tokens). We provide a fair comparison of these methods at matched expert-compute budget, on two modern MoEs (Gemma4-26B-A4B and Qwen3-30B-A3B) in a text-only setting and a contemporary vision-language model (Qwen3-VL) in a multimodal setting. Surprisingly, we find that the simple baseline is hard to outperform consistently across models, modalities, benchmarks, and calibration settings. The methods we benchmark often underperform the baseline and achieved gains, when present, are minor. We further find that the effective $k$ achieved (or optimized for) in calibration can diverge from the effective $k$ in testing, resulting in over- or under-estimating the compute at inference time. Based on these findings, we recommend a calibration-free per-modality static $k$ reduction and validate this on a held-out model and modality. We further argue that a static control at matched measured compute is the baseline any dynamic skipping method must beat.

</details>


### [364] [Self-Credit: Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Agentic RL via Intra-group Rescaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28388)
*Fangcong Yin,Ming Yin,Tanvi Bhandarkar,Aashu Singh,Eryk Helenowski,Shlok Kumar Mishra,Saghar Hosseini,Karthik Abinav Sankararaman,Greg Durrett,Dinkar Juyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Credit，一种端到端的Agentic RL方法，通过模型自生成的带引用摘要和组内重标度机制，将稀疏的结果奖励转化为稠密的监督信号，解决长时程任务中的信用分配难题。在BrowseComp-Plus、GAIA、xBench-DeepSearch基准上显著超越基线模型和现有最强Agentic RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent常用RLVR训练，但典型RLVR奖励稀疏，导致信用分配困难，尤其在长时程任务中。现有方法难以有效识别关键动作并赋予其应有的信用，限制了策略学习效率。

Method: Self-Credit基于组RL，包含三个核心组件：1) 策略模型生成带引用的摘要，引用早期包含关键证据的轮次，作为自生成信用；2) 组内重标度：对比同一提示下成功与失败轨迹中被引用的动作，利用组级一致性与分歧去除不当信用、强化关键动作信用；3) 优势重加权：利用重标度后的信用权重更新被引用轮次的优势估计。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus上，分别比Qwen3 8B/32B基座提升37.0/36.2分，超越最强Agentic RL基线8.5/5.6分；在GAIA提升4.9分，xBench-DeepSearch提升4.7分。消融实验表明自生成信用能有效识别关键动作，组内重标度（特别是失败轨迹上的）在各模型规模下均至关重要。

Conclusion: Self-Credit通过自生成引用与组内重标度实现了从稀疏奖励到稠密信用的有效转化，显著提升了长时程Agent任务的RL训练效果，且方法具有良好的可扩展性和泛化性。

Abstract: LLM agents are commonly trained with RLVR, but the sparse nature of typical RLVR rewards makes credit assignment challenging, especially for long-horizon tasks. We propose Self-Credit, an end-to-end agentic RL method that creates dense supervision signals from sparse outcome rewards using self-generated credit assignment via intra-group rescaling. Self-Credit builds on group-based RL with three components. First, during its rollouts, the policy model generates summaries with citations to earlier turns containing critical evidence, which serve as self-generated credit. Second, we refine this model-generated credit with intra-group rescaling, which compares cited actions across successful and failed rollouts for the same prompt. This approach uses group-level agreement and disagreement to remove undeserved credit and further emphasize credit assigned to critical actions. Third, advantage reweighting uses the rescaled credit weights to update the advantages of the cited turns. On BrowseComp-Plus, Self-Credit improves over the base models by 37.0 and 36.2 points on Qwen3 8B and 32B respectively, and outperforms the strongest agentic RL baselines by 8.5 and 5.6 points, respectively. Self-Credit also improves over the strongest baselines by 4.9 points on GAIA and 4.7 points on xBench-DeepSearch. Analyses show that self-generated credit provides useful signals for selecting critical actions, and that intra-group rescaling, especially on failed rollouts, is important across model scales.

</details>


### [365] [Rewards for Budget-Aware Multi-Turn Agents That Adapt to Arbitrary Test-Time Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28409)
*Tom A. Lamb,Adam Golinski,Eleonora Gualdoni,Sinead Williamson,Michael Kirchhof*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型智能体在多轮交互中如何学习跨动态指定预算分配推理资源（tokens、API调用、环境步骤等），通过设计11种奖励目标并在AppWorld和BabyAI上训练Qwen3.5模型，发现惩罚超支的奖励显著提升预算内成功率和自然合规性，智能体能零样本泛化到未见预算和新任务分布，无需外部预算控制器即可自适应分配资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体常被给固定推理预算，但如何在多轮交互中自主学习何时及花费多少资源尚不清楚。外部强制预算虽可行，但智能体自身能否学会跨动态预算分配、以及哪种奖励最能教会这种行为，是核心动机。

Method: 将长度惩罚扩展为跨多资源类型的预算约束，设计11种奖励目标（含sigmoid超支惩罚、固定负奖励、频率与幅度组合惩罚等），在共享训练管线中对比。分别在AppWorld（tokens、API调用、环境步预算）和BabyAI（轮次预算）上训练Qwen3.5-4B/9B，每环境每资源类型训练独立策略，并跨多个预算值联合训练。

Result: 惩罚超支的奖励使预算内成功率和自然合规性大幅优于未训练模型及仅优化任务成功的基线。智能体学会根据声明预算和任务难度自适应调整资源使用，增益泛化至未见预算，并零样本迁移到分布偏移任务和新基准。Sigmoid超支惩罚在单一预算下最常达最高预算内成功率；固定负奖励通常产生最强自然合规性；组合频率与幅度惩罚在各预算下平均与最优方法差距最小。

Conclusion: 单一智能体可通过预算反馈学会跨轮次分配资源并适应用户指定预算，无需外部预算分配控制器。奖励目标在预算内成功率与自然合规性间存在权衡，组合惩罚策略提供最稳健的整体表现。

Abstract: Large language model agents are often given an inference budget and asked to maximise success within it. A multi-turn agent has to allocate this budget across many environment interactions. An external harness can enforce the budget, but it remains unclear whether the agent itself can learn when and how much to spend. We ask whether agents can learn this allocation across dynamically specified budgets and which rewards best teach this behaviour. We adapt existing length penalties to budget constraints spanning multiple resource types, introduce new reward formulations, and compare eleven objectives in a shared training pipeline. We train Qwen3.5 models at 4B and 9B on AppWorld with budgets over tokens, API calls, and environment steps, and on the BabyAI gridworld with turn budgets. We train a separate policy for each environment and resource type, with each policy trained across budget values. Training with rewards that penalise overspending substantially improves within-budget success and natural compliance over untrained models and over training on task success alone, with agents learning to adapt their resource use to stated budgets and task difficulty. These gains extend to unseen budgets and, zero-shot, to a shifted task distribution and a new benchmark. Reward objectives trade off success within budget and natural compliance. A sigmoid penalty for overspending most often achieves the highest within-budget success at an individual budget, while a fixed negative reward for overspending generally yields the strongest natural compliance. Combining penalties for overspend frequency and magnitude gives the smallest average gap to the best method at each budget. Overall, we show that a single agent can learn to allocate resources across turns and adapt its behaviour to user-specified budgets using budget feedback without external budget-allocation controllers.

</details>


### [366] [Region-Specific EEG Classification of Hypnotic Susceptibility Using a Sliding-Window 2D CNN: A Subject-Independent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28427)
*Krzysztof Wrobel,Grzegorz M. Wojcik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于最小预处理EEG信号的二维CNN分类催眠易感性，发现随机分割下枕区准确率最高(0.721)，但受试者独立的LOSO交叉验证显示枕区性能降至随机水平(ACC 0.453)，揭示高准确率源于受试者特异性EEG指纹而非真实组间差异；前额叶和前额区在LOSO下仍保持显著高于随机的准确率(0.646和0.624)，证明前部皮层区域是催眠易感性更可靠的神经标记，强调了受试者独立验证在EEG分类研究中的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有催眠易感性EEG分类研究多采用随机分割验证，可能混淆受试者特异性特征与真实组间差异，缺乏受试者独立的泛化能力评估，需要系统考察不同脑区在受试者独立条件下的分类表现，以确定可靠的神经生物学标记。

Method: 招募62名男性参与者(32对照组,30引导意象/催眠组),使用256导EGI系统记录20分钟静息态EEG。采用滑动窗口法将信号切分为10秒窗口,针对预定义的7个脑区(前额叶、额叶、颞叶、中央区、顶叶、枕叶、面部区)分别训练二维CNN模型,共训练多达868个独立模型。先用段级90/10随机分割进行初步筛选,再采用留一受试者出交叉验证(LOSO)进行受试者独立的泛化性能评估。

Result: 随机分割下,5个脑区准确率超过0.67阈值,枕叶区表现最佳(平均ACC 0.721,90.3%窗口达标)。然而LOSO验证显示枕叶区性能崩塌至随机水平(平均ACC 0.453,AUC 0.429),表明随机分割的高准确率源于受试者特异性EEG指纹。相反,前额叶(ACC 0.624)和额叶(ACC 0.646)在LOSO下仍显著高于随机水平,展现真实的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在基于EEG的分类研究中必须采用受试者独立验证(LOSO等)以避免虚假高性能;前部皮层区域(前额叶/额叶)而非枕叶区是催眠易感性更可靠、可泛化的神经标记,为催眠神经机制研究和临床应用提供了重要方法学指导。

Abstract: We investigate whether a 2D Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) trained on minimally preprocessed EEG signals can classify hypnotic susceptibility across distinct brain regions. Sixty-two male participants (32 Control, 30 Guided Imagery / Hipno group) underwent a 20-minute EEG recording using a 256-channel EGI cap. A sliding-window approach evaluates all 10-second windows within the recording for each of seven predefined brain regions (prefrontal, frontal, temporal, central, parietal, occipital, and facial), yielding up to 868 independently trained models. Initial screening with a segment-level 90/10 random split showed that five regions exceeded the 0.67 accuracy threshold, with the occipital region achieving the highest mean accuracy (0.721) across 90.3% of windows. Subject-independent evaluation via Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) cross-validation, however, substantially revised this picture: occipital performance collapsed to near-chance (mean ACC 0.453, AUC 0.429), revealing that high random-split accuracy was driven by subject-specific EEG fingerprints rather than genuine group differences. In contrast, the frontal and prefrontal regions maintained above-chance LOSO accuracy (0.646 and 0.624, respectively), demonstrating genuine generalisability. These results underscore the necessity of subject-independent validation in EEG-based classification studies and highlight anterior cortical regions as more reliable neural markers of hypnotic susceptibility.

</details>


### [367] [Do Neural Models Prefer Their Submodels?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28439)
*Alvaro Ribot,Yulia Alexandr,Anna Seigal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究神经模型的几何性质，探讨局部极小值是否倾向于落在子模型（如失活神经元的子网络）中。结果显示，浅层MLP中失活隐藏神经元的子网络从不被偏好；深层MLP给出不被偏好的条件；Lightning自注意力中低秩子模型通常不被偏好，但存在边界例外。结论：子模型偏好主要源于训练动态而非模型几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络架构的几何结构是否决定了优化过程中对子模型（如稀疏或低秩子网络）的偏好，即局部极小值是否天然落在子模型中。

Method: 引入几何框架分析神经模型及其子模型，利用代数几何和优化理论研究局部极小值与子模型的关系，分别针对浅层MLP、深层MLP和Lightning自注意力进行理论分析。

Result: 1) 浅层MLP：失活隐藏神经元的子网络从不被偏好。2) 深层MLP：给出失活隐藏神经元子网络不被偏好的充分条件。3) Lightning自注意力：低秩子模型通常不被偏好，但存在因位于模型边界而被偏好的例外。

Conclusion: 除少数边界例外，神经模型的几何结构无法解释对子模型的偏好，这种偏好必须源于训练动态（如梯度下降的隐式偏置）等其他因素。

Abstract: A neural model is the set of functions that can be expressed by a given neural network architecture. Neural models contain natural submodels, such as those obtained by setting some neurons to be inactive. We study the geometry of neural models and ask whether they prefer their submodels. By this we mean whether local minimizers of the loss function lie in a given submodel. We introduce a geometric framework to determine if this occurs. For shallow multilayer perceptrons, we show that subnetworks with inactive hidden neurons are never preferred. For deep multilayer perceptrons, we give conditions under which subnetworks with inactive hidden neurons are not preferred. For lightning self-attention, lower-rank submodels are usually not preferred, but we identify examples where they are because they lie on a boundary of the model. Our results show that, aside from a few exceptions, preference for submodels cannot be explained by the geometry of the neural model, and must instead come from other sources such as training dynamics.

</details>


### [368] [CNN-based classifier for subject-independent MBTI Extraversion/Introversion preference classification from short-time EEG signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28442)
*Filip Postepski,Grzegorz M. Wojcik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探究短、极少预处理的EEG片段是否足以区分MBTI外向/内向人格，提出紧凑卷积架构SSCNet，在主体无关嵌套5折交叉验证中优于EEGNet和EEGProgress，准确率0.6481，AUC 0.7063，F1 0.6276，且计算效率最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究MBTI外向-内向偏好是否可从短时域EEG片段中中等程度预测，无需人工频谱特征提取，同时强调主体无关验证和计算高效模型设计的重要性。

Method: 使用50名参与者的EEG记录，切分为4秒片段，采用主体无关嵌套5折交叉验证，对比EEGNet、EEGProgress及新提出的紧凑卷积架构SSCNet。

Result: SSCNet达到最高平均准确率(0.6481)、AUC(0.7063)、F1分数(0.6276)，折间变异性最低；训练周期时间最短、吞吐量最高，参数量与EEGProgress相近。

Conclusion: 短时域EEG片段可中等程度预测MBTI外向-内向偏好，无需人工频谱特征提取；主体无关验证和计算高效模型设计至关重要。

Abstract: This study examines whether short, minimally preprocessed EEG segments contain sufficient information to distinguish between individuals classified as extraverted or introverted according to the Myers--Briggs Type Indicator (MBTI). EEG recordings from 50 participants were divided into 4-s segments and analyzed using a subject-independent nested 5-fold cross-validation procedure to compare EEGNet, EEGProgress, and a newly proposed compact convolutional architecture, SSCNet. SSCNet achieved the highest mean accuracy (0.6481), AUC (0.7063), and F1-score (0.6276). The proposed architecture also showed the lowest variability across folds. SSCNet demonstrated the best computational efficiency, with the shortest median training epoch time and the highest training throughput despite having a similar number of trainable parameters to EEGProgress. The results suggest that MBTI Extraversion--Introversion preferences may be moderately predictable from short time-domain EEG segments without manual spectral feature extraction, while highlighting the importance of subject-independent validation and computationally efficient model design.

</details>


### [369] [LocaLeJEPA: Maximum Entropy Representations under Geometric Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28458)
*Kartik Tandon,Alessio Borgi,Pavel Snopov,Inés García-Redondo,Davide D'Ascenzo,Sebastiano Cultrera di Montesano,Fabrizio Silvestri,Pietro Liò,Francesca Dominici,Randall Balestriero,Claudio Battiloro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LocaLeJEPA 框架，通过流形理论重新表述 LeJEPA 的熵最大化属性，引入局部欧氏分布匹配正则器 LocaLeReg，解决了 LeJEPA 依赖欧氏假设且全局目标不反映领域特定几何先验的问题，在包括双曲 ViT、等变 ResNet 及普通欧氏 ViT 在内的多种骨干网络上均显著优于 LeJEPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) 虽然吸引人但易陷入表征崩溃；LeJEPA 通过分布匹配目标将嵌入推向各向同性高斯分布，但其根本依赖欧氏假设，全局目标无法捕捉非欧氏嵌入几何（如等变 CNN、双曲 ViT）所需的领域特定几何先验。

Method: 基于流形理论重新表述 LeJEPA 的熵最大化性质，提出 LocaLeJEPA 框架及配套的局部欧氏分布匹配正则器 LocaLeReg，实现稳定可扩展的训练，兼容一般嵌入几何（欧氏、双曲、等变等）。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 上大量实验显示，LocaLeJEPA 搭配双曲 ViT-S/8、等变 ResNet-50、欧氏 ViT-S/8 和 ViT-L/14 均显著超越 LeJEPA，线性分类和 k-NN 分类在 ImageNet 及下游任务上均取得更优性能。

Conclusion: LocaLeJEPA 形式化为 JEPA 范式提供了有原则的方法，更好地尊重数据内在几何，同时适配广义嵌入几何，代码已开源。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) offer an appealing approach to self-supervised learning, but are typically prone to representation collapse. LeJEPA resolves this by introducing a theoretically grounded distribution-matching objective that drives the embedding towards an isotropic Gaussian. However, this approach is fundamentally tied to Euclidean assumptions, and its global target does not reflect domain-specific geometric priors, such as the use of non-Euclidean embedding geometries, e.g., equivariant CNNs or hyperbolic ViTs. We thus introduce LocaLeJEPA, a framework motivated by a manifold-theoretic reformulation of LeJEPA's entropy-maximization property. The accompanying LocaLeReg, a novel locally Euclidean distribution-matching regularizer, enables a stable and scalable implementation of the LocaLeJEPA framework. We then perform extensive experiments in the vision setting on models trained on ImageNet-1K. LocaLeJEPA significantly outperforms LeJEPA when paired with hyperbolic ViT-S/8 and equivariant ResNet-50 backbones, and also with ordinary Euclidean ViT-S/8 and ViT-L/14 backbones. This holds both in terms of linear and k-NN classification performance on ImageNet and on a series of downstream tasks tested. We view the LocaLeJEPA formalism as a principled approach to the JEPA paradigm that better respects intrinsic data geometry while also accommodating generalizations to exotic embedding geometries. Code available at https://github.com/alessioborgi/LocaLeJEPA.

</details>


### [370] [Geometry-Agnostic Data-Driven Discovery of Extreme Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28487)
*Angeline Hu,Ava K. Tse,Kaitlyn S. Yasumura,Luciana Sarabia,Malcolm Yeung,Kavin K. Prasanna,Kai Wong,William Hammond,Ioannis G. Keverkidis,Shengkai Li,Trung V. Phan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段数据驱动神经框架，无需预先假设支配方程，直接从观测数据推断动力学约束，再通过物理信息神经搜索发现罕见的高影响相干结构（如孤立子、瞬子等），并实现跨几何、跨维度的动力学迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统极端场景发现依赖已知支配方程，难以应对方程未知或几何复杂的系统。本文旨在仅从观测数据中自动学习有效动力学约束，并利用其几何无关表示，跨维度、跨曲率流形迁移动力学，挖掘原始数据中未出现的罕见极端相干结构，服务于风险分析、非线性预测和异常检测。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段，从观测数据推断有效动力学约束；当系统具备适宜对称性时，采用几何无关神经表示，使学习动力学独立于固定网格、坐标系、维度及内禀曲率。第二阶段，基于学习到的动力学进行物理信息神经搜索，发掘罕见高影响相干结构。通过逐步增加维度与几何复杂度的代表性示例验证：从达菲振子发现回环脉冲，到非线性薛定谔方程的驻孤立子，再经几何无关表示迁移获取汤斯孤子，最后用刘维尔系统演示从(1+1)维洛伦兹拟流形迁移至(2+0)维欧几里得流形识别瞬子。

Result: 成功在无先验方程情况下发现多种极端相干结构：达菲系统的回环脉冲、非线性薛定谔方程的驻孤立子、高维汤斯孤子、跨几何迁移得到的瞬子。验证了框架对维度、曲率、坐标系的不变性，以及动力学跨流形迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该方法可推广至克林克、流氓波、紧孤子、尖孤子甚至奇点等相干时空结构，为风险分析、非线性预测和异常检测提供通用的数据驱动极端场景发现工具。

Abstract: We introduce a two-stage data-driven neural framework for discovering dynamically admissible extreme scenarios. Rather than assuming prior knowledge of the governing equations, the first stage infers the effective dynamical constraints directly from observational data. When the underlying physical system possesses suitable symmetries, the learned dynamics can be represented in a geometry-agnostic form, independent of any fixed grid, coordinate system, dimensionality, and intrinsic curvature. In the second stage, a physics-informed neural-search uses this learned dynamics to uncover rare, high-impact coherent structures that may not have appeared in the original observations. We demonstrate this framework through representative examples of increasing dimensionality and geometric complexity. Starting from a traditionally learned Duffing dynamics, we find a homoclinic pulse as a plausible localized extreme scenario. Next, since the same equation also appears in the stationary nonlinear Schrodinger system, with the coordinate interpreted as space instead of time, this pulse becomes a standing soliton. We then retrain the first stage using a geometry-agnostic neural representation, enabling the learned dynamics to be lifted to higher-dimensional space to obtain higher-dimensional solitary profile, known as Townes soliton. Finally, with a Liouville system, we demonstrate that dynamics learned from data on a (1+1)-dimensional Lorentzian quasi-manifold can be transferred to a (2+0)-dimensional Euclidean manifold to identify an instanton. Our approach can be applied to other coherent spatiotemporal structures, including kinks, rogue waves, compactons, peakons, and potentially even singularities, with applications in risk analysis, nonlinear forecasting, and anomaly detection.

</details>


### [371] [Model- and Layer-Level Closed-Loop Forecasting with SSM-Based Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28495)
*Erik Stolt,Sanja Karilanova,Ayca Ozcelikkale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种闭环预测策略（模型级和层级）解决脉冲神经网络(SNN)在时间序列预测中的自回归依赖问题，在ETT-small基准上与S4零掩码基线和SpaceTime相比表现相当或更优，展示深度状态空间模型在预测任务中的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度状态空间模型(Deep SSMs)在处理复杂时序依赖的时间序列数据方面显示出潜力，脉冲神经网络(SNNs)作为生物启发的深度SSMs通过离散单比特脉冲通信。预测任务需要仅从过去观测预测未来值，但SNNs的标准自回归特性在预测阶段需要不可用的未来值，因此如何用SNNs进行最佳预测是一个开放问题。

Method: 探索两种闭环预测策略：(i) 模型级闭环预测，(ii) 层级闭环预测，将网络自身生成的值反馈作为观测的过去值输入网络。在ETT-small数据集上评估，同时使用脉冲激活函数和连续激活函数，对比S4的零掩码基线和SpaceTime等最先进方法。

Result: 两种闭环方法均与S4零掩码基线表现相当或更优，在大多数情况下匹配SpaceTime性能。结果突显了连续激活与低比特量化激活之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 所提深度状态空间模型架构为时间序列预测提供了一个具有竞争力的框架，验证了闭环策略在SNN预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Deep State-Space Models (Deep SSMs) have shown promising performance and computational benefits in processing of time-series data with complex temporal dependencies. Spiking neural networks (SNNs) constitute a biologically inspired class of deep SSMs, where computing blocks communicate through spikes, discrete single-bit events rather than continuous activation functions. A particularly important task in time-series processing is forecasting, i.e. predicting future values of a sequence from past observations alone. However, how to best perform forecasting with SNNs is an open question, where the standard autoregressive nature of the data processing with SNNs requires values that are not available during the forecasting phase. In this article, we address this challenge. We explore two closed-loop forecasting strategies, in which the network's own generated values are fed back into the network as if they were observed past values: (i) model-level and (ii) layer-level closed-loop forecasting. We evaluate these approaches on the ETT-small datasets, a standard benchmark for time-series forecasting, under both spike-based and continuous activation functions. We compare the proposed closed-loop approaches against state-of-the-art methods such as SpaceTime, as well as the zero-masking baseline in S4. Both of the proposed approaches perform comparably to or better than zero-masking with S4, and in most cases match SpaceTime. These findings highlight the trade-off between continuous and low-bit quantized activations, and demonstrate that the proposed Deep SSM architectures provide a competitive framework for forecasting.

</details>


### [372] [Present but Hard to Read: Probing Latent Reasoning with Minimal Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28498)
*Aur Shalev Merin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了语言模型中潜在推理的探针研究，指出常规探针难以检测输入中被复制的数字，提出了一种包含合成空假设的协议来区分计算值与输入的复制和混合，并在两步推理任务中发现中间值虽可读但未被使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的探针研究通常在答案生成开始时读取模型状态，将探针失败视为值缺失的证据。作者旨在测试这种做法的可靠性，特别是针对从输入中复制的数字是否真正存在于模型的潜在表示中，以及如何区分真正的计算值与单纯的输入混合。

Method: 在循环模型Ouro-1.4B和标准Transformer Qwen2.5-1.5B上，对比仅在一个数字上不同的提示。训练探针在最后一个提示token上读取该数字，并对比单次提示设置下的探测性能。引入合成空假设协议，用于测试计算值与输入副本及混合的区别。在预注册的两步推理任务中，检查未写出的中间值（如a+b）在生成答案最后一位数字前的信号，并通过补丁实验测试该值是否被因果使用。

Result: 数字在最后一个提示token处以共享编码存在（读出准确率约0.9），但常规单次提示探针难以读取（Ouro 0.36，Qwen 0.19，即使经过5249个训练问题）。数字的变化幅度小而非因拥挤：随机噪声落在同样繁忙的方向，而随机词移动状态更大且可读。每个变化的token都留下痕迹，导致成功探针也存在歧义。两步推理中发现中间值a+b有微弱信号且符号跟随运算，但混合本身产生了大部分符号跟随部分。补丁实验显示：保持该值不改变写出的数字，而修补答案则会改变，表明该值可读但未被使用。

Conclusion: 常规探针方法低估了模型中输入副本的存在；提出的合成空假设协议能更好地区分计算值与输入混合。在两步推理中，中间计算值虽可在潜在状态中检测到，但并未因果驱动最终输出，提示潜在推理的探针证据需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Probing studies of latent reasoning often read a model's state where the answer begins and take a failed probe as evidence that a value is absent. We test this with a digit copied from the question, in the looped model Ouro-1.4B and the standard transformer Qwen2.5-1.5B. Comparing prompts that differ only in that digit shows it is present at the last prompt token, in a code shared across questions (readout about 0.9). A probe that sees one prompt at a time, the usual setting, barely reads it: 0.36 in Ouro and 0.19 in Qwen even after 5,249 training questions. The digit's change is small rather than crowded: random noise lands on equally busy directions, while a random word in its place moves the state far more and is readable. Since every varied token leaves a trace, a successful probe is ambiguous too. We give a protocol, including a synthetic null for generic mixing, for testing computed values against copies and mixtures of the inputs. In pre-registered studies of two-step problems it finds a small signal for the unwritten intermediate a+b just before Ouro writes the answer's last digit, and its sign follows the operation, though mixing alone produces much of that sign-following part. Holding that value at another problem's from the first loop on does not move the written digit toward the other problem's, while the same patch of the answer does: the value is readable but not used.

</details>


### [373] [ECG for laboratory values:A comprehensive physiological and technical analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28507)
*Walentin Palanchuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究利用心电图(ECG)和患者元数据通过多模态深度学习预测NT-proBNP，比较单导联(II导联)与12导联配置，发现12导联表现更优，回归任务MAE约514 pg/mL，分类任务AUROC达0.876，但精确回归仍具挑战，ECG预测可作为辅助，需进一步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策依赖实验室检测但需侵入性采血，研究表明ECG可能包含除心律外的实验室指标信息，旨在探索无创ECG预测NT-proBNP的可行性及生理学合理性。

Method: 基于MIMIC-IV数据集，构建多模态深度学习框架融合ECG记录与患者元数据，对比单导联(II)与12导联配置，开展回归、二分类及三分类(基于临床阈值)实验。

Result: 12导联配置持续优于单导联：回归MAE分别为534.98 vs 514.27 pg/mL；二分类AUROC 0.849 vs 0.876；三分类宏平均AUROC 0.738 vs 0.752。精确回归困难，但宽浓度范围分类效果较好。

Conclusion: ECG特征与NT-proBNP浓度反映共享心血管过程，ECG预测可为宽范围浓度分类提供互补信息，但实验室检测仍不可替代，需在独立数据集上进一步验证。

Abstract: Laboratory measurements are essential for clinical decision making but typically require invasive blood sampling. Recent research suggests that ECG recordings may contain information beyond cardiac rhythm and provide information related to laboratory values. This thesis investigates NT-proBNP prediction from ECG recordings and patient metadata using a multimodal deep learning framework, while considering the physiological plausibility of ECG-based prediction. It also compares a 1-lead configuration using lead II with the corresponding 12-lead configuration. Additional experiments on glucose, hemoglobin, and potassium are presented in the appendix.
  Regression, binary classification, and three-class classification experiments were conducted using MIMIC-IV, with the three-class task based on clinically established thresholds. The 12-lead configuration consistently achieved better performance. Regression reached a mean absolute error of 534.98 pg/mL for 1-lead and 514.27 pg/mL for 12-lead. Binary classification achieved AUROCs of 0.849 and 0.876, respectively, while three-class classification achieved macro-averaged AUROCs of 0.738 and 0.752.
  The results indicate that precise NT-proBNP regression remains challenging, whereas broader concentration ranges can be classified more effectively. The findings are consistent with the physiological hypothesis that ECG characteristics and NT-proBNP concentrations reflect shared cardiovascular processes. ECG-based NT-proBNP prediction may therefore provide complementary information, particularly for classifying broader concentration ranges. However, laboratory measurements remain necessary, and validation on independent datasets is required.

</details>


### [374] [GraphCast-Mamba: Learning Non-Markovian Corrections for Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28512)
*Ilya Vilkoviskiy,Liang-Hong Mo,Suozhi Huang,Dmitry A. Abanin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 GraphCast-Mamba，通过冻结预训练的 GraphCast 主干并添加可训练的 Mamba 修正分支，利用图消息传递和 Mamba 层保持时序记忆，在仅训练修正分支的情况下，显著提升了十天天气预报的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有天气预报模型（如 GraphCast）仅使用两帧输入，无法捕捉长时程的历史依赖性，导致预报随时间推移误差累积。需要一种方法在不重新训练整个模型的前提下，引入长时程记忆来修正预报偏差。

Method: 将预训练的 1° GraphCast 主干冻结，附加一个可训练的 Mamba 修正分支，该分支结合图消息传递和 Mamba 层，在连续的 6 小时预报步长中保持时间记忆状态，从而利用超出两帧输入的时序上下文学习修正量。仅使用 ERA5 2015-2021 年数据训练修正分支，共 2500 步优化器更新（含预热）。

Result: 修正模型在十天预报全程将 GraphCast 的归一化加权 MSE 损失降低 14.42%，在第 10 天（240 小时）降低 21.66%。在第 10 天，所有 11 个评估变量组（温度、风、压力、位势、比湿、降水等）的 RMSE 均下降 7.09%–14.60%。

Conclusion: 通过极短的修正分支训练即可大幅提升十天天气预报技巧，且保留了预训练主干的能力，证明了引入长时程记忆修正的有效性和高效性。

Abstract: Unresolved atmospheric processes can introduce history dependence beyond the short input windows used by many weather forecasting models. We introduce GraphCast--Mamba, which augments a frozen, pretrained $1^\circ$ GraphCast backbone with a trainable Mamba correction branch combining graph message passing and Mamba layers. The branch retains a temporal memory state across successive six-hour forecast steps, learning corrections informed by temporal context beyond GraphCast's two-frame input. Training is confined to the correction branch, using ERA5 data from 2015--2021. After 2,500 optimizer updates, including warm-up, the selected model achieves substantial improvements relative to the uncorrected GraphCast backbone. It reduces the GraphCast loss (normalized weighted mean-squared error; MSE) by 14.42% when aggregated over the full ten-day forecast and by 21.66% at day 10 (240 h after forecast initialization). At this final forecast lead time, root-mean-square error (RMSE) decreases by 7.09--14.60% across all 11 evaluated variable groups, spanning temperature, wind, pressure, geopotential, specific humidity, and precipitation. These results demonstrate broad improvements in ten-day weather forecasts through a short correction-training schedule while retaining the pretrained backbone.

</details>


### [375] [Binary Discrete Diffusion: Candidate-Set Diffusion for Logic and Planning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28543)
*Rodrigo Casado Noguerales,Dimitri von Rütte,Bernhard Schölkopf,Thomas Hofmann,Aran Raoufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出二元离散扩散模型，用候选 token 集合（二元成员向量）作为噪声状态，显式建模部分不确定性，实现信息的渐进丢失与恢复，在推理、规划、约束生成等任务上显著优于掩码/均匀扩散基线，且在语言建模上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散（掩码扩散、均匀扩散）在单次破坏事件中即擦除 token 身份，其噪声状态无法显式表示“部分已解不确定性”（即同一位置仍有多个合理候选），这限制了模型在需要迭代推理、规划及约束生成（如编程、数学）任务中的表现。

Method: 构建一族连续时间离散扩散过程——二元离散扩散。其噪声状态为每个位置的候选 token 集合，用二元成员向量表示。前向过程通过候选集的“添加”与“移除”操作逐步改变集合，使信息能渐进丢失并可恢复，且扩散状态本身即显式编码了部分不确定性。反向模型学习从候选集预测原始 token。

Result: 在 Sudoku-Extreme、Boxoban、图着色三大推理/规划基准上，对称形式的二元扩散在架构、训练、采样、调优预算全匹配的受控实验中，持续超越强且经过充分调优的掩码/均匀扩散基线，且在最难实例上增益最显著；在 Text8、TinyStories、OpenWebText 语言建模任务上保持具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 候选集作为中间表示能有效支持迭代推理；破坏核的选择会实质性地改变反向模型所学到的计算过程。二元离散扩散通过显式建模部分不确定性，为离散数据的推理、规划与约束生成提供了更强的生成框架。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models iteratively denoise a noisy sequence of categorical data such as text, enabling any-order generation with self-correction and in-filling capabilities. This makes them promising candidates for reasoning, planning, and constrained-generation tasks like coding and math. Yet masked and uniform diffusion erase a token's identity in a single corruption event, so their noisy states cannot explicitly represent partially resolved uncertainty in which several alternatives remain plausible. We close this gap with Binary Discrete Diffusion, a family of continuous-time discrete-diffusion processes whose noisy state is a set of candidate tokens per position, represented as a binary membership vector. Binary diffusion changes candidate sets through additions and removals, allowing information to be lost and recovered gradually while the diffusion state itself represents partial uncertainty. On Sudoku-Extreme, Boxoban, and graph coloring, the symmetric formulation of binary diffusion consistently outperforms strong, well-tuned masked and uniform baselines under matched architectures and controlled training, sampling, and tuning budgets, with its largest gains on the hardest instances, while remaining competitive on language modeling (Text8, TinyStories, OpenWebText). These results support candidate sets as an effective intermediate representation for iterative reasoning and show that the corruption kernel can materially change the computation learned by the reverse model.

</details>


### [376] [Discount Yourself: Self-inclusive Freshness-weighted Gossip for Decentralised Learning under Client Outages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28545)
*Qiyuan Wang,Ke Xiao,Christos Anagnostopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对去中心化联邦学习（DFL）中设备长期离线导致的模型陈旧问题，提出三个聚合原则：接收者必须像邻居一样被折扣、陈旧度应用继承滞后而非年龄衡量、折扣必须陡峭。由此构建 Self-inclusive Freshness-weighted Gossip (SFG) 系列聚合规则，在保持去中心化联邦平均的通信计算开销下，为陈旧接收者本身提供理论保证。实验表明基于滞后的 SFG-L 和 SFG-Cut 在多基准和真实离线轨迹上持续优于现有方法，且每个原则均不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实设备常离线数小时至数天，重新上线的客户端只能聚合邻域持有的模型。研究观察到这些模型中最陈旧的通常是客户端自身模型，而现有感知陈旧度的规则仅对接收到的模型加权折扣，忽略了接收者自身的陈旧性，导致误差随离线时长无界增长。

Method: 将离线后的聚合形式化为估计全可用网络状态，推导三大原则：1) 接收者必须像邻居一样按相对陈旧度折扣，否则误差无界；2) 陈旧度应用继承滞后而非年龄，因为本地训练重置年龄但不重置从陈旧邻居吸收的滞后，引入有效滞后，仅需每条消息携带一个标量；3) 折扣必须陡峭，因为吸收的滞后会在后续轮次持久化。据此定义 SFG 系列即插即用聚合规则。

Result: 在三个图像基准和八种离线过程（含真实智能手机可用性轨迹）上，基于滞后的 SFG-L 和 SFG-Cut 持续超越去中心化联邦平均和近期信息年龄规则；消融实验证实三个原则各自必要。分析还解释了为何流言学习中的调优限制合并有效。

Conclusion: SFG 通过自包含的新鲜度加权聚合，解决了去中心化联邦学习中接收者自身模型陈旧的核心难题，提供了理论保证并实验验证了优越性，为处理设备间歇性可用性奠定了原则性基础。

Abstract: Decentralised federated learning (DFL) trains personalised models on devices that average with their neighbours rather than with a central server. Real devices, however, are often offline for hours or days, and a client returning from an outage can recover only by aggregating the models its neighbourhood holds. We observe that the stalest of these models is usually the client's own, a case that existing staleness-aware rules, which discount only the models a client receives, do not address. We formalise aggregation after an outage as estimating the state of a fully available network and derive three principles. First, the receiver must be discounted like any neighbour. Every rule that weights it at least as much as each neighbour has an error that grows without bound with the outage, whereas discounting all candidates by relative staleness keeps the error bounded. Second, staleness must be measured as inherited lag rather than age, since local training resets a model's age but not the lag it absorbed from stale neighbours. We introduce the effective lag, which tracks this quantity at the cost of one scalar per message. Third, the discount must be steep, because absorbed lag persists in every later round. These principles define Self-inclusive Freshness-weighted Gossip (SFG), a family of drop-in aggregation rules with the communication and computation of decentralised federated averaging, whose guarantees hold for the stale receiver itself. On three image benchmarks and eight outage processes, including real smartphone availability traces, the lag-based members SFG-L and SFG-Cut consistently outperform decentralised federated averaging and recent age-of-information rules, and ablations show that each principle is necessary. The analysis also explains why tuned limited merging from gossip learning is effective.

</details>


### [377] [Learning Computational Structure for Sample-Efficient Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28555)
*Hangyu Zhou,Constantine Dovrolis,Stephen Mussmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Learned Wiring (LeWi) 方法，通过联合学习神经元嵌入与连接权重，利用类生物神经回路的空间组织原理，在高维统计学习中挖掘隐藏的组合稀疏结构，显著提升样本效率。在合成基准 CSFRBench 与 17 个真实世界模型预测任务上，LeWi 均优于密集网络与稀疏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维统计学习面临维度灾难，但许多实际目标函数具有组合稀疏性（即可分解为仅依赖少数变量的局部函数组合），理论上样本复杂度可仅取决于局部元数而非环境维度。然而，现有梯度训练方法难以自动发现并利用这种隐藏的组合结构，密集网络与稀疏基线均无法充分实现该收益。

Method: 受生物神经回路空间组织启发，提出 Learned Wiring (LeWi)：为每个神经元学习低维嵌入向量，仅允许嵌入空间中邻近的神经元建立连接，嵌入与连接权重通过梯度下降联合端到端训练。同时构建可控基准 CSFRBench，在训练阶段隐藏目标组合计算图，仅保留用于事后诊断。

Result: 在具有层级结构、中间复用或独立模块的目标上，LeWi 样本效率显著提升。在 32 个随机采样计算图目标上，LeWi 为未给定目标图情况下最样本高效方法，平均学习曲线面积比密集网络降低 27%。密集网络仅在单一高元函数目标上更优。LeWi 学到的表征与连接拓扑与目标计算结构对齐。在 17 个跨控制、导航、操作的世界模型预测任务中，LeWi 在 16 个任务击败密集基线，119 次基线对比中获胜 99 次。

Conclusion: 联合学习神经连接拓扑与权重，能有效利用计算结构提升高维学习的样本效率，为克服维度灾难提供了可行的生物启发路径。

Abstract: Statistical learning in high dimensions suffers from the curse of dimensionality. Fortunately, many functions of practical interest are compositionally sparse, meaning they admit a composition of local functions, each depending on only a few variables, allowing sample complexity to depend on local arity rather than ambient dimension. Yet realizing this benefit requires exploiting hidden compositional structure, which theory does not establish for practical gradient-based training. We study this gap with CSFRBench, a controlled benchmark that hides the target's compositional computation during training but retains it for diagnosis, and find that dense networks and sparsity-based baselines leave much of that benefit unrealized. Inspired by the spatial organization of biological neural circuits, we introduce Learned Wiring (LeWi): each neuron has a learned low-dimensional embedding, only nearby neurons connect, and embeddings and weights are trained jointly. LeWi improves sample efficiency on targets with hierarchical structure, intermediate reuse, or independent modules. Across 32 additional targets generated by independently sampled computation graphs, LeWi is the most sample-efficient method not given the target graph, with 27% lower mean learning-curve area than dense networks. Dense networks perform better on targets with a single high-arity function. LeWi's representations and connectivity align with the target computation. On 17 world-model prediction tasks spanning control, navigation, and manipulation, LeWi beats the dense baseline on 16 and wins 99/119 baseline comparisons. These results show that jointly learning connectivity and weights can exploit computational structure to improve sample efficiency.

</details>


### [378] [When Do LLMs Help with Feature Selection? A Systematic Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28556)
*Fateme Memar,Dongjie Wang,Arvin Agah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在18个数据集框架中系统对比了大语言模型（LLM）与经典统计方法在表格特征选择上的表现，发现经典选择器总体预测性能优于7B规模的LLM/强化学习选择器；语义特征名称对零样本Qwen-7B的回归任务有显著提升（+0.1964和+0.1606平均R²），但对分类任务改进较小（+0.0159和+0.0084 ROC-AUC）且依赖数据集。结论：经典选择器仍是表格特征选择的强基线，语义信息在回归任务中更有利于LLM特征选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型利用语义特征名称和摘要统计量进行表格特征选择时，何种情况下优于成熟的统计方法，以及语义信息对LLM特征选择的实际增益。

Method: 构建包含10个GRPO训练集、2个验证集和6个保留评估集的18数据集框架；对比五个经典基线、冻结的Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.3-70B选择器、两个GRPO适配的Qwen2.5-7B模型；分别报告分类ROC-AUC和回归R²，并用数据集级排名进行跨任务比较；另设基数匹配的语义敏感性实验，考察信息量特征名称与匿名/打乱名称的差异。

Result: 经典选择器在五个随机种子评估分区上总体预测性能优于测试的7B LLM/RL选择器（含预设约33%特征预算、严格仅训练集拟合条件）；7B选择器保留更紧凑的特征子集。语义敏感性实验显示：零样本Qwen-7B在回归任务上，信息量特征名称较匿名和打乱名称分别提升平均R² 0.1964和0.1606；分类任务上ROC-AUC提升仅0.0159和0.0084，且呈数据集依赖性。

Conclusion: 语义信息主要在回归任务中显著增强LLM特征选择效果，分类任务增益有限且不稳定；经典统计选择器在预测性能上仍为表格特征选择的强基线，LLM方法在紧凑子集保留上有优势但整体预测性能尚未超越经典方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can leverage semantic feature names alongside summary statistics for tabular feature selection, yet it remains unclear when such information improves subset selection relative to established statistical methods. We investigate this question within an 18-dataset framework comprising 10 datasets for Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) training, 2 for checkpoint validation, and 6 held-out evaluation datasets. We compare five classical baselines, frozen Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.3-70B selectors, and two GRPO-adapted Qwen2.5-7B models, reporting classification ROC-AUC and regression R^2 separately and using dataset-wise ranks for cross-task comparison. Across five seeded evaluation partitions, classical selectors generally achieve higher predictive performance than the tested 7B LLM/RL selectors, including under a pre-specified ~33% feature budget with strict train-only classical fitting, while the 7B selectors retain compact subsets. A cardinality-matched semantic sensitivity experiment shows that, for Zero-shot Qwen-7B, informative feature names improve regression performance over anonymized and shuffled alternatives (+0.1964 and +0.1606 mean R^2), whereas aggregate classification differences are smaller (+0.0159 and +0.0084 ROC-AUC) and dataset-dependent. These results characterize when semantic information benefits LLM-based feature selection and show that classical selectors remain strong baselines for predictive tabular feature selection.

</details>


### [379] [Convergence of stochastic gradient descent for learning linear neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28561)
*Gabin Maxime Nguegnang,Bubacarr Bah,Holger Rauhut,Ulrich Terstiege*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次为深度线性网络中的随机梯度下降（SGD）提供了几乎必然收敛保证，填补了理论优化与实际随机训练之间的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGD 是现代深度学习的基石，但在非凸设置下的理论收敛性质仍知之甚少。为了解决这一差距，需要在保留关键非凸特征且可解析处理的简化模型中研究 SGD 动力学。

Method: 在深度线性神经网络中研究 SGD 动力学，基于梯度下降的近期结果，将收敛保证扩展到正则化平方损失下的 SGD。利用随机逼近理论中的渐近伪轨迹框架进行分析。

Result: 建立了 SGD 迭代的几乎必然有界性；对于确定性和自适应步长选择，在温和条件下（不依赖 Lipschitz 性质或白化数据）证明了 SGD 收敛到临界点集。

Conclusion: 该工作为深度线性网络中的 SGD 提供了首个几乎必然收敛保证，弥合了理论优化与实际随机训练之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is the cornerstone of modern deep learning, yet its theoretical convergence properties remain poorly understood in non-convex settings. To address this gap, we study SGD dynamics in deep linear neural networks - simplified models that retain key non-convex characteristics while remaining analytically tractable. Building on recent results for gradient descent, we extend convergence guarantees to SGD under regularized squared loss. We establish almost sure boundedness of SGD iterates. Using the framework of asymptotic pseudotrajectories from stochastic approximation theory, our analysis establishes then, for both deterministic and adaptive step-size choices, convergence of SGD to the set of critical points under mild conditions (without relying on Lipschitz properties or whitened data). To our knowledge, this is the first work providing almost sure convergence guarantees for SGD in deep linear networks, bridging the gap between theoretical optimization and practical stochastic training.

</details>


### [380] [Asymmetric Bidirectional Predictive Dynamics in Google Search and COVID-19 Time Series: A Multi-Horizon Forecast-Error Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28563)
*Andrei Velichko,Imen Mahmoud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用Google Trends搜索数据与COVID-19指标（病例数、阳性率、封控严格度）配对，采用LightGBM滚动预测框架，结合对数RMSE增益与预测误差分布熵（FEDE），构建方向性预测特征。结果显示“失嗅”→报告病例在准确度上占优（RMSE降低约9.9%，DPRS=100），反向则FEDE主导；“新冠检测”→阳性率为FEDE主导（DPRS=98）尽管RMSE增益为负；“封控”→严格度指数呈一致性预测（DPRS=78）。研究揭示预测准确度与误差分布动态互补，预测效应依赖方向、指标与预测时域，聚合层面模式不应外推至单一时间点或推断因果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将在线搜索活动作为疫情信息寻求的数字足迹，但其预测价值常被简化为相关性、单一滞后或单一预测误差评分，缺乏对方向性、时域依赖性及误差分布动态的系统量化。

Method: 选取八个预设的Google Trends-COVID-19指标配对，双向检验。使用LightGBM在滚动400天窗口上进行1-14天时域预测。引入对数RMSE增益与预测误差分布熵（FEDE）捕捉有符号误差分布的时域演变，构建方向性预测特征（响应类别、RMSE效应、FEDE端点与早期斜率、滚动制度间持久性、时域剖面）。提出相对持久性加权方向性预测响应强度（DPRS），在0-100区间内标度，100代表比较集内最强观测响应而非绝对耦合上限。

Result: 配对呈现一致性、仅RMSE、仅FEDE及混合四类响应。“失嗅”→报告病例为准确度主导（均值对数RMSE增益0.104，约9.9% RMSE降低，DPRS=100），反向为FEDE主导（D_FEDE=0.048，DPRS=42）。“新冠检测”→阳性率为FEDE主导（D_FEDE=0.039，72%窗口为正，DPRS=98）尽管RMSE增益为负。“封控”→严格度指数为最清晰的一致性案例（DPRS=78）。全球层面症状/病例配对持久性较弱。

Conclusion: 预测准确度与误差分布动态互补，预测效应具有方向依赖性、指标依赖性，且预测时域是关系的组成部分。聚合制度层面的模式不应外推至个别时间点，也不应被解释为因果证据。

Abstract: Online search activity is widely used as a digital trace of information seeking during epidemics, but its predictive value is often reduced to correlation, a selected lag, or a single forecast-error score. We examine eight pre-specified Google Trends-COVID-19 pairs in both directions using matched LightGBM forecasts over rolling 400-day windows and horizons of 1-14 days. Log-RMSE gain is combined with forecast-error distribution entropy (FEDE), which captures horizon-dependent changes in the signed error distribution. The resulting directional predictive signature comprises response class, RMSE effect, FEDE endpoint and early-slope responses, persistence across rolling regimes, and horizon profile. For screening candidate directions, we also introduce a relative persistence-weighted directional predictive response strength (DPRS) on a within-study 0-100 scale; 100 denotes the strongest observed response in the comparison set, not an absolute coupling maximum. The pairs show concordant, RMSE-only, FEDE-only, and mixed responses. "loss of smell" -> reported cases is accuracy-dominant, with mean log-RMSE gain 0.104 (about 9.9% equivalent RMSE reduction) and DPRS 100, whereas the reverse direction is FEDE-dominant (D_FEDE=0.048; DPRS=42). "covid test" -> positive rate is FEDE-dominant (D_FEDE=0.039; positive in 72% of windows; DPRS=98) despite negative RMSE gain, while "lockdown" -> stringency is the clearest concordant case (DPRS=78). World-level symptom/case pairs are less persistent. Thus, forecast accuracy and error-distribution dynamics are complementary, predictive effects can be direction- and metric-dependent, and forecast horizon is part of the relationship. Aggregated regime-level patterns should not be extrapolated to individual time points or interpreted as evidence of causality.

</details>


### [381] [The Dynamics of Collapse and Recovery in Clustered Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28565)
*Priyanka Sinha,Snigdhansu Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对簇联邦学习（CFL）中的簇崩溃问题，将其建模为未知动态系统的控制问题，提出基于阻塞EXP3的自主控制器选择机制，通过路由干预逆转崩溃，实验验证了其在不利初始化下恢复可用专家模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 簇联邦学习在不利初始化下会发生簇崩溃：异构客户端群体被错误分配到同一簇模型，导致该模型向混合妥协收敛，而另一模型因无更新而饥饿。现有贪婪路由机制使崩溃自我强化，缺乏自动恢复能力。

Method: 将CFL建模为动态系统控制：簇模型为状态，客户端路由模式为控制输入，路由干预为状态相关控制器。在双群线性模型中识别贪婪路由导致崩溃的初始化区域，推导有限时域误差动态，证明通过反复用兼容群体更新饥饿模型可逆转崩溃。提出每个客户端使用阻塞EXP3算法在贪婪路由与干预路由间自主选择。

Result: 实验显示，自主控制器选择配合更简单的预测器和更快的专家形成，能从困住标准CFL基线的不利初始化中恢复可用专家。在变换FEMNIST上，同步进入配置加持久承诺能在自主控制器配置常失败的设置中恢复可用专家。

Conclusion: 通过将CFL视为动态系统控制并引入基于阻塞EXP3的自主路由干预，可有效解决簇崩溃问题，无需中心化协调即可从不利初始化恢复，为异构联邦学习提供了鲁棒的自适应机制。

Abstract: Clustered federated learning (CFL) can suffer from cluster collapse. In particular, under unfavorable initialization, early client assignments concentrate heterogeneous groups on one cluster model, which is then trained toward a pooled compromise, while another model receives no updates. Since the current models determine client routing and routing determines subsequent model updates, we formulate clustered federated learning as control of an unknown dynamical system, with cluster models as the evolving state, client routing patterns as the control input, and routing interventions as state dependent controllers. In a two group linear model, we identify an initialization region in which greedy routing makes collapse self reinforcing. We then derive finite horizon error dynamics showing how routing interventions can reverse collapse by repeatedly training the starved model, primarily with updates from its compatible group. Motivated by this condition, each client uses blocked EXP3 to choose between greedy routing and interventions that redirect updates toward unused models. Experiments show that with simpler predictors and faster specialist formation, autonomous controller selection recovers usable specialists from unfavorable starts that trap standard CFL baselines.
